Requires two tables: CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_NOTIFY_EMAILS ( EMAIL VARCHAR2(255) PRIMARY KEY, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES ( SCHEDULE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, RUN_AT TIMESTAMP NOT NULL, RECURRENCE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'ONCE' NOT NULL, -- ONCE | DAILY | WEEKLY TEST_ONLY NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, FLYER_MODE VARCHAR2(10) DEFAULT 'WHOLE' NOT NULL, -- WHOLE | VEG — see isWholesaleVegRowServer EMAILS VARCHAR2(1000) NOT NULL, -- comma-separated STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, -- PENDING|RUNNING|DONE|FAILED|CANCELLED LAST_RUN_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT VARCHAR2(4000), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); -- FLYER_MODE was added after WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES already existed in -- production. Run this once against the live table before deploying -- this version — Oracle backfills existing rows with the default -- ('WHOLE') even though the column is NOT NULL, so it's safe to run -- against a table that already has schedules in it: -- ALTER TABLE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES ADD (FLYER_MODE VARCHAR2(10) DEFAULT 'WHOLE' NOT NULL); --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Saved email list ---- app.get('/api/wholesale/schedule/emails', async (req, res) => { try { const rows = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute(`SELECT EMAIL FROM WHOLESALE_NOTIFY_EMAILS ORDER BY EMAIL`); return lower(result.rows).map(r => r.email); }); res.json({ emails: rows }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/schedule/emails', async (req, res) => { const email = String((req.body || {}).email || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!isValidEmail(email)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A valid email address is required' }); try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO WHOLESALE_NOTIFY_EMAILS t USING (SELECT :email AS e FROM dual) d ON (t.EMAIL = d.e) WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (EMAIL) VALUES (:email)`, { email }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/wholesale/schedule/emails/:email', async (req, res) => { // req.params.email is already URL-decoded by Express's router. const email = String(req.params.email).trim().toLowerCase(); try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM WHOLESALE_NOTIFY_EMAILS WHERE EMAIL = :email`, { email }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Schedules: list / create / cancel ---- app.get('/api/wholesale/schedule', async (req, res) => { try { const rows = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SCHEDULE_ID, RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, TEST_ONLY, FLYER_MODE, EMAILS, STATUS, LAST_RUN_AT, LAST_RESULT, CREATED_AT FROM WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES ORDER BY RUN_AT DESC` ); return lower(result.rows); }); res.json({ schedules: rows }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/schedule', async (req, res) => { try { const { run_at, recurrence, test_only, flyer_mode, emails } = req.body || {}; const runAtDate = new Date(run_at); if (!run_at || isNaN(runAtDate.getTime())) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A valid run_at date/time is required' }); } const rec = ['ONCE', 'DAILY', 'WEEKLY'].includes(String(recurrence || '').toUpperCase()) ? String(recurrence).toUpperCase() : 'ONCE'; // Same Whole (every dept except Wholesale Vegetable) / Veg (Wholesale // Vegetable only) choice as the on-screen toggle — see // setWholesaleFlyerMode / isWholesaleVegRowServer. Defaults to WHOLE // for anything unrecognised, matching the column's DB default. const flyerMode = String(flyer_mode || '').toUpperCase() === 'VEG' ? 'VEG' : 'WHOLE'; const emailList = (Array.isArray(emails) ? emails : []).map(e => String(e).trim().toLowerCase()).filter(isValidEmail); if (emailList.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'At least one valid recipient email is required' }); } const scheduleId = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES (RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, TEST_ONLY, FLYER_MODE, EMAILS) VALUES (:runAt, :recurrence, :testOnly, :flyerMode, :emails) RETURNING SCHEDULE_ID INTO :scheduleId`, { runAt: runAtDate, recurrence: rec, testOnly: test_only ? 1 : 0, flyerMode, emails: emailList.join(','), scheduleId: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.scheduleId[0]; }); res.json({ ok: true, schedule_id: scheduleId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/wholesale/schedule/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid schedule id is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'CANCELLED' WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (result.rowsAffected === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Only a pending, not-yet-run schedule can be cancelled' }); } res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "×" on a history entry: permanently removes it from the schedule // list so old Done/Failed/Cancelled runs stop cluttering the list. // Different from the cancel endpoint above, which only ever flips a // still-PENDING row to CANCELLED (it doesn't remove anything) — this // one actually deletes the row and works on any status, including one // that's currently RUNNING (dismissing it from view doesn't affect // the run already in flight, which keeps going and just won't have // anywhere to write its result back to). app.delete('/api/wholesale/schedule/:id/dismiss', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid schedule id is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (result.rowsAffected === 0) { return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Schedule not found' }); } res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* ---- Server-side reproduction of the on-screen "Download CSV" export logic (see downloadWholesaleCSV / wholesaleExportRows / wholesaleCsvSellingPrice in the frontend), so a scheduled run produces byte-for-byte the same kind of file a manual click would — including respecting any Blocked selling prices from WHOLESALE_PRICE_LOCK. Only difference: a scheduled run always covers every row (a manual click's "Test" mode only takes whatever's first in the currently-searched/filtered table on someone's screen, which doesn't apply here). ---- */ function wholesaleIncVatPriceServer(netPrice, isVat) { const STANDARD_VAT_RATE = 0.20; return isVat ? netPrice * (1 + STANDARD_VAT_RATE) : netPrice; } function csvEscapeField(v) { return '"' + String(v === undefined || v === null ? '' : v).replace(/"/g, '""') + '"'; } // Mirrors isWholesaleVegRow on the frontend, same case-insensitive match on // the department name so a scheduled Veg run and a manual Flyer Veg // download always agree on which rows count as "Wholesale Vegetable". function isWholesaleVegRowServer(r) { return /vegetable/i.test(r.department_name || ''); } function buildWholesaleCsvText(pricingRows, locks, testOnly) { const headers = ['Description as per Vector', 'Qty per Box', 'Cost / Unit', 'Selling Price', 'Barcode', 'Department', 'Supplier']; const dataRows = pricingRows.map(r => { const lock = locks[r.stock_name]; // Default (unlocked) selling price is the freshly-synced Trade 3 net // price, exactly like a row nobody has touched on screen yet. A locked // row uses whatever selling price was blocked/last saved for it. const netPrice = lock && lock.price !== null && lock.price !== undefined ? Number(lock.price) : (r.price_net === null || r.price_net === undefined ? null : Number(r.price_net)); const isVat = r.vatid === 1; return { 'Description as per Vector': r.stock_name, 'Qty per Box': r.pack_size === null || r.pack_size === undefined ? '' : r.pack_size, 'Cost / Unit': r.suppcost_net === null || r.suppcost_net === undefined ? '' : Number(r.suppcost_net).toFixed(2), 'Selling Price': netPrice === null ? '' : wholesaleIncVatPriceServer(netPrice, isVat).toFixed(2), 'Barcode': r.barcode || '', 'Department': r.department_name || '', 'Supplier': r.supplier_name || '' }; }); const rows = testOnly ? dataRows.slice(0, 1) : dataRows; const csvLines = [headers.join(',')].concat(rows.map(row => headers.map(h => csvEscapeField(row[h])).join(','))); return { headers, rows, csvText: csvLines.join('\n') }; } // ---- Background runner: checks every WHOLESALE_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS for due // schedules, queues the CSV the same way a manual click does, waits // for the till to process it, then emails the outcome. ---- const WHOLESALE_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS = 60 * 1000; // how often to check for due schedules const WHOLESALE_IMPORT_POLL_MS = 15 * 1000; // how often to check the till's progress on a queued file const WHOLESALE_IMPORT_TIMEOUT_MS = 15 * 60 * 1000; // give up waiting after this long (till may be offline) function nextRecurrenceRunAt(fromDate, recurrence) { const next = new Date(fromDate); const stepMs = recurrence === 'WEEKLY' ? 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000 : 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // Keep stepping forward until it's back in the future — avoids a burst // of catch-up runs if the server was offline when one or more // occurrences were due. while (next.getTime() <= Date.now()) next.setTime(next.getTime() + stepMs); return next; } async function runOneWholesaleSchedule(schedule) { // Safety net: the flyer choice (Whole / Veg) must always come from the saved // schedule row. If the caller didn't load it, read it here rather than // silently defaulting to WHOLE (that default is what caused Veg schedules // to update the normal list). if (schedule.flyer_mode === undefined || schedule.flyer_mode === null) { try { const fm = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute(`SELECT FLYER_MODE FROM WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { id: schedule.schedule_id }); return r.rows.length ? lower(r.rows)[0].flyer_mode : null; }); if (fm) schedule.flyer_mode = fm; } catch (e) { console.error('Could not re-read FLYER_MODE for schedule', schedule.schedule_id, e.message); } } const emails = (schedule.emails || '').split(',').map(e => e.trim()).filter(Boolean); const testOnly = !!schedule.test_only; const flyerMode = String(schedule.flyer_mode || 'WHOLE').toUpperCase() === 'VEG' ? 'VEG' : 'WHOLE'; const flyerModeLabel = flyerMode === 'VEG' ? 'Flyer Veg' : 'Flyer Whole'; let finalStatus = 'DONE'; let resultMessage = ''; try { const allPricingRows = await getWholesalePricingRows(true); // always fresh for a scheduled run // Same Whole/Veg split as the on-screen toggle: Whole = every department // except Wholesale Vegetable, Veg = Wholesale Vegetable only. const pricingRows = allPricingRows.filter(r => flyerMode === 'VEG' ? isWholesaleVegRowServer(r) : !isWholesaleVegRowServer(r)); const locks = await withConnection(conn => getWholesalePriceLocks(conn)); const { rows, csvText } = buildWholesaleCsvText(pricingRows, locks, testOnly); const deptSummary = Array.from(new Set(rows.map(r => r['Department'] || 'Other'))).slice(0, 6).join(', '); console.log(`[wholesale-schedule ${schedule.schedule_id}] mode=${flyerMode} rows=${rows.length} depts=${deptSummary}`); if (rows.length === 0) { finalStatus = 'FAILED'; resultMessage = `Nothing to export — the ${flyerModeLabel} list was empty at run time.`; } else { // Veg runs use their own file name so it's obvious in Vector's File Import // window (and the queue/logs) which list is being imported. The till // scripts take the name from the queue, so nothing there depends on it. const filePrefix = flyerMode === 'VEG' ? 'wholesale-veg-price-update' : 'wholesale-price-update'; const fileName = testOnly ? `${filePrefix}-test.csv` : `${filePrefix}.csv`; const queueId = await withConnection(conn => queueVectorImport(conn, { file_name: fileName, csv_content: csvText, type: 'WHOLESALE' })); // Wait for the till's poller + vector_price_import.ps1 to pick it up // and finish, same states the on-screen status modal watches for. const startedAt = Date.now(); let last = { status: 'QUEUED', status_message: '', error_message: '' }; while (Date.now() - startedAt < WHOLESALE_IMPORT_TIMEOUT_MS) { await sleep(WHOLESALE_IMPORT_POLL_MS); const row = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT STATUS, STATUS_MESSAGE, ERROR_MESSAGE FROM VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE WHERE QUEUE_ID = :id`, { id: queueId } ); return result.rows.length ? lower(result.rows)[0] : null; }); if (!row) break; last = row; if (row.status === 'COMPLETED' || row.status === 'FAILED') break; } if (last.status === 'COMPLETED') { finalStatus = 'DONE'; resultMessage = `Queued ${rows.length} row(s) (${flyerModeLabel}; ${deptSummary}) as ${fileName} and Vector confirmed the import completed. ` + (last.status_message || ''); } else if (last.status === 'FAILED') { finalStatus = 'FAILED'; resultMessage = `Vector import failed: ${last.error_message || last.status_message || 'no further detail given'}`; } else { finalStatus = 'FAILED'; resultMessage = `Queued ${rows.length} row(s) (${flyerModeLabel}; ${deptSummary}) as ${fileName}, but the till had not picked it up/finished within ${Math.round(WHOLESALE_IMPORT_TIMEOUT_MS / 60000)} minutes (last status: ${last.status}). Check that the till's poller and vector_price_import.ps1 are running.`; } } } catch (e) { console.error('Scheduled wholesale export failed', e); finalStatus = 'FAILED'; resultMessage = 'Unexpected error: ' + e.message; } if (emails.length) { const subject = `Wholesale ${testOnly ? 'Test ' : ''}Price Update (${flyerModeLabel}) — ${finalStatus === 'DONE' ? 'Completed' : 'Failed'}`; const bodyText = `Scheduled Wholesale Pricing List ${testOnly ? 'test ' : ''}CSV run: ${finalStatus}\n\n${resultMessage}\n\nRun at: ${new Date().toLocaleString('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Europe/London' })} (UK time)`; try { const mailResult = await sendMail({ to: emails, subject, text: bodyText, html: `

${bodyText.replace(/\n/g, '
')}

` }); // Surfaced in the "Scheduled & recent runs" list in the UI, so a // missing email isn't a silent mystery — if SMTP isn't configured // (or nodemailer isn't installed) this makes that visible right next // to the run instead of only appearing in the server console log. resultMessage += mailResult.sent ? ` (emailed to ${emails.join(', ')})` : ` (email NOT sent — ${mailResult.reason || 'unknown reason'}; check BREVO_API_KEY and MAIL_FROM env vars)`; } catch (e) { console.error('Could not send wholesale schedule result email', e); resultMessage += ` (email NOT sent — ${e.message})`; } } await withConnection(async conn => { if (schedule.recurrence === 'ONCE') { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = :status, LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { status: finalStatus, result: resultMessage.slice(0, 4000), id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { const nextRun = nextRecurrenceRunAt(schedule.run_at, schedule.recurrence); await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'PENDING', RUN_AT = :nextRun, LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { nextRun, result: resultMessage.slice(0, 4000), id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); } let wholesaleSchedulerRunning = false; async function pollWholesaleSchedules() { if (wholesaleSchedulerRunning) return; // don't overlap if a previous tick is still mid-run wholesaleSchedulerRunning = true; try { const due = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SCHEDULE_ID, RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, TEST_ONLY, FLYER_MODE, EMAILS FROM WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND RUN_AT <= SYSTIMESTAMP` ); return lower(result.rows); }); for (const schedule of due) { // Atomically claim it (guards against a second overlapping tick / instance // picking up the same row) before doing the slow queue-and-wait work. const claimed = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'RUNNING' WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.rowsAffected === 1; }); if (!claimed) continue; await runOneWholesaleSchedule(schedule); } } catch (e) { console.error('Wholesale schedule poll failed', e); } finally { wholesaleSchedulerRunning = false; } } // Started from the initPool().then(...) block at the bottom of this file, // once the connection pool actually exists — see STARTUP. /* --------------------------------------------------------- WHOLESALE PRICING — SCHEDULED WHATSAPP FLYER SEND Sits next to the CSV "Schedule" above: lets staff pick a flyer image (Wholesale Colour Flyer / Wholesale Veg Flyer), write the message, tick which contact lists ("groups") it goes to, and choose a date/time — the server then sends it automatically at that time, exactly the way the WhatsApp Broadcast page's Image send does (same approved templates, same env vars, same per-contact results), just without anyone clicking Send. Which image goes out: - If the schedule was created with a custom image, that image is stored on the schedule row and sent as-is. - Otherwise the flyer's saved default image (WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES, the same one the Broadcast page uses) is looked up at SEND time — so if someone updates the default between scheduling and sending, the newer image goes out. Every route below lives under /api/wholesale/, so the existing "wholesale" permission (see resolvePortalModule) is all a staff member needs — they do NOT also need the separate "WhatsApp Broadcast" section ticked. That's also why the contact lists and saved flyer images are exposed through /options below rather than reusing /api/whatsapp/*. Table (created automatically on boot by ensureWholesaleWhatsappScheduleTable — no manual SQL needed; shown here for reference only): CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES ( SCHEDULE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, RUN_AT TIMESTAMP NOT NULL, RECURRENCE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'ONCE' NOT NULL, -- ONCE | DAILY | WEEKLY FLYER_KEY VARCHAR2(20) NOT NULL, -- colour | veg MESSAGE VARCHAR2(4000) NOT NULL, -- fills the template's {{1}} LIST_IDS VARCHAR2(1000) NOT NULL, -- comma-separated WHATSAPP_LISTS.LIST_ID IMAGE_DATA CLOB, -- optional custom image (data URL); NULL = use the flyer's saved default IMAGE_FILENAME VARCHAR2(300), STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, -- PENDING|RUNNING|DONE|FAILED|CANCELLED LAST_RUN_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT VARCHAR2(4000), CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); Depends on the WHATSAPP_LISTS / WHATSAPP_CONTACTS / WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES tables and the WHATSAPP_* env vars already documented under WHATSAPP BROADCAST further down this file. --------------------------------------------------------- */ const WA_SCHEDULE_FLYERS = { colour: { label: 'Wholesale Colour Flyer' }, veg: { label: 'Wholesale Veg Flyer' } }; const WA_SCHEDULE_MAX_IMAGE_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // WhatsApp's own image limit const WA_SCHEDULE_MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS = 1024; // Meta's limit for a template body variable const WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS = 60 * 1000; // how often to check for due WhatsApp schedules // Oracle hands a CLOB back as a Lob stream unless it's read explicitly; this // accepts either shape (same handling as GET /api/whatsapp/flyer-images). async function clobToString(v) { if (v === null || v === undefined) return null; if (typeof v === 'string') return v; return await v.getData(); } // Splits a "data:image/png;base64,...." URL into its parts. Only JPEG/PNG // are accepted because those are the only image types WhatsApp's Cloud API // takes — anything else (WebP, GIF, HEIC…) would only fail later at send // time with nobody watching, so it's rejected up front instead. function parseImageDataUrl(dataUrl) { const s = String(dataUrl || ''); const comma = s.indexOf(','); if (comma < 0) return null; const header = /^data:(image\/(?:jpeg|jpg|png));base64$/i.exec(s.slice(0, comma)); if (!header) return null; const base64 = s.slice(comma + 1); if (!base64) return null; const mime = header[1].toLowerCase(); return { mimeType: mime === 'image/jpg' ? 'image/jpeg' : mime, base64, bytes: Math.floor(base64.length * 3 / 4) }; } // Which approved template (and language) a given flyer goes out under. // Deliberately identical to the resolution inside POST /api/whatsapp/send // for an Image message, so a scheduled send and a manual Broadcast send of // the same flyer always use the same template. Throws with a readable // message if the env var for that flyer isn't set. function whatsappFlyerTemplateFor(flyerKey) { const templateEnvKey = flyerKey === 'veg' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_VEG' : 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_COLOUR'; const templateName = process.env[templateEnvKey] || (flyerKey === 'colour' ? process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE : undefined); if (!templateName) { throw new Error(`No approved template configured for this flyer — set the ${templateEnvKey} environment variable to your approved template name.`); } const languageEnvKey = flyerKey === 'veg' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE_VEG' : 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE_COLOUR'; const languageCode = process.env[languageEnvKey] || (flyerKey === 'colour' ? process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE : undefined) || process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE || 'en_GB'; return { templateName, languageCode }; } function parseWaScheduleListIds(str) { return String(str || '').split(',').map(s => Number(s.trim())).filter(n => Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0); } async function ensureWholesaleWhatsappScheduleTable() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES ( SCHEDULE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, RUN_AT TIMESTAMP NOT NULL, RECURRENCE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'ONCE' NOT NULL, FLYER_KEY VARCHAR2(20) NOT NULL, MESSAGE VARCHAR2(4000) NOT NULL, LIST_IDS VARCHAR2(1000) NOT NULL, IMAGE_DATA CLOB, IMAGE_FILENAME VARCHAR2(300), STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, LAST_RUN_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT VARCHAR2(4000), CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX WHOLESALE_WA_SCHED_IX ON WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES (STATUS, RUN_AT)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[wholesale-wa-schedule] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (made on an earlier boot) — expected. if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[wholesale-wa-schedule] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // A server restart / redeploy in the middle of a send leaves that row stuck // on RUNNING forever. It can't be safely re-run automatically (some contacts // may already have the message — re-sending would double-message them), so // on boot such rows are closed off with an explanatory note instead: a ONCE // schedule becomes FAILED, a repeating one goes back to PENDING for its next // occurrence. async function recoverStaleWholesaleWhatsappRuns() { const note = 'Interrupted by a server restart while sending — some contacts may already have received it, so check before re-scheduling.'; await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SCHEDULE_ID, RUN_AT, RECURRENCE FROM WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES WHERE STATUS = 'RUNNING'` ); for (const s of lower(result.rows)) { if (s.recurrence === 'ONCE') { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'FAILED', LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { result: note, id: s.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'PENDING', RUN_AT = :nextRun, LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { nextRun: nextRecurrenceRunAt(s.run_at, s.recurrence), result: note, id: s.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } console.warn('[wholesale-wa-schedule] recovered stale RUNNING schedule', s.schedule_id); } }); } // ---- Everything the "WhatsApp Schedule" modal needs to draw itself: the // contact lists (with live counts) and each flyer's saved default image. ---- app.get('/api/wholesale/whatsapp-schedule/options', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const listsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT L.LIST_ID, L.NAME, COUNT(C.CONTACT_ID) AS CONTACT_COUNT FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS L LEFT JOIN WHATSAPP_CONTACTS C ON C.LIST_ID = L.LIST_ID GROUP BY L.LIST_ID, L.NAME ORDER BY L.NAME` ); const imgResult = await conn.execute(`SELECT FLYER_KEY, IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME FROM WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES`); const flyers = {}; for (const r of lower(imgResult.rows)) { flyers[r.flyer_key] = { data: await clobToString(r.image_data), filename: r.filename || null }; } return { lists: lower(listsResult.rows), flyers }; }); res.json({ ...out, configured: whatsappConfigured() }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Schedules: list / create / cancel / dismiss ---- app.get('/api/wholesale/whatsapp-schedule', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SCHEDULE_ID, RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, FLYER_KEY, MESSAGE, LIST_IDS, IMAGE_FILENAME, CASE WHEN IMAGE_DATA IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS HAS_CUSTOM_IMAGE, STATUS, LAST_RUN_AT, LAST_RESULT, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT FROM WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES ORDER BY RUN_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 100 ROWS ONLY` ); const listsResult = await conn.execute(`SELECT LIST_ID, NAME FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS`); const nameById = {}; for (const l of lower(listsResult.rows)) nameById[l.list_id] = l.name; return lower(result.rows).map(s => ({ ...s, list_names: parseWaScheduleListIds(s.list_ids).map(id => nameById[id] || '(deleted list)') })); }); res.json({ schedules: out }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/whatsapp-schedule', async (req, res) => { try { if (!whatsappConfigured()) { return res.status(500).json({ error: 'WhatsApp is not configured on the server yet — add WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN and WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID as environment variables.' }); } const { run_at, recurrence, flyer, message, list_ids, image } = req.body || {}; const runAtDate = new Date(run_at); if (!run_at || isNaN(runAtDate.getTime())) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A valid date/time is required' }); } // A minute of grace so "right now" from a slow click isn't rejected. Sending // straight away is what the WhatsApp Broadcast page is for. if (runAtDate.getTime() < Date.now() - 60 * 1000) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'That date/time is in the past — pick a time in the future (to send right now, use the WhatsApp Broadcast page)' }); } const rec = ['ONCE', 'DAILY', 'WEEKLY'].includes(String(recurrence || '').toUpperCase()) ? String(recurrence).toUpperCase() : 'ONCE'; const flyerKey = String(flyer || '').toLowerCase(); if (!WA_SCHEDULE_FLYERS[flyerKey]) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Choose which flyer to send — Wholesale Colour Flyer or Wholesale Veg Flyer' }); } // Fail now (while someone's looking at the screen) rather than at send // time (when nobody is) if this flyer has no approved template set up. try { whatsappFlyerTemplateFor(flyerKey); } catch (e) { return res.status(400).json({ error: e.message }); } const text = String(message || '').trim(); if (!text) { // Same rule as the Broadcast page: the message fills the template's // {{1}} variable and Meta rejects a template send with it left blank. return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Write a message — it fills the template, so it\'s required even when sending an image' }); } if (text.length > WA_SCHEDULE_MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS) { return res.status(400).json({ error: `Message is too long — WhatsApp allows up to ${WA_SCHEDULE_MAX_MESSAGE_CHARS} characters here` }); } const listIds = [...new Set((Array.isArray(list_ids) ? list_ids : []).map(Number).filter(n => Number.isInteger(n) && n > 0))]; if (listIds.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Select at least one contact group to send to' }); } // Optional custom image for this schedule. let imageData = null; let imageFilename = null; if (image && image.data) { const parsed = parseImageDataUrl(image.data); if (!parsed) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The chosen image must be a JPG or PNG — those are the only image types WhatsApp accepts' }); } if (parsed.bytes > WA_SCHEDULE_MAX_IMAGE_BYTES) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The chosen image is too large — WhatsApp allows up to 5MB for an image' }); } imageData = image.data; imageFilename = String(image.filename || '').slice(0, 300) || null; } const createdBy = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) ? String(req.portalUser.name).slice(0, 200) : null; const outcome = await withConnection(async conn => { if (!imageData) { const def = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS N FROM WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES WHERE FLYER_KEY = :k AND IMAGE_DATA IS NOT NULL`, { k: flyerKey } ); if (Number(lower(def.rows)[0].n) === 0) { return { error: `No saved image for the ${WA_SCHEDULE_FLYERS[flyerKey].label} yet — choose an image for this schedule (or save a default one on the WhatsApp Broadcast page)` }; } } const placeholders = listIds.map((_, i) => `:id${i}`).join(','); const binds = {}; listIds.forEach((id, i) => { binds[`id${i}`] = id; }); const cnt = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT PHONE) AS N FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE LIST_ID IN (${placeholders})`, binds ); if (Number(lower(cnt.rows)[0].n) === 0) { return { error: 'The selected group(s) have no saved contacts to send to' }; } const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES (RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, FLYER_KEY, MESSAGE, LIST_IDS, IMAGE_DATA, IMAGE_FILENAME, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:runAt, :recurrence, :flyerKey, :message, :listIds, :imageData, :imageFilename, :createdBy) RETURNING SCHEDULE_ID INTO :scheduleId`, { runAt: runAtDate, recurrence: rec, flyerKey, message: text, listIds: listIds.join(','), imageData: { val: imageData, type: oracledb.CLOB }, imageFilename, createdBy, scheduleId: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return { scheduleId: result.outBinds.scheduleId[0] }; }); if (outcome.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: outcome.error }); res.json({ ok: true, schedule_id: outcome.scheduleId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/wholesale/whatsapp-schedule/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid schedule id is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'CANCELLED' WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (result.rowsAffected === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Only a pending, not-yet-run schedule can be cancelled' }); } res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // "×" on a history entry — permanently removes the row (same idea as // /api/wholesale/schedule/:id/dismiss above). app.delete('/api/wholesale/whatsapp-schedule/:id/dismiss', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid schedule id is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (result.rowsAffected === 0) { return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Schedule not found' }); } res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- The actual send for one due schedule. Mirrors POST /api/whatsapp/send // (Image type): upload the image to Meta once, then one approved-template // message per contact across the chosen lists, de-duplicated by phone so // the same number saved in two lists is only messaged (and billed) once. ---- async function runOneWholesaleWhatsappSchedule(schedule) { let finalStatus = 'DONE'; let resultMessage = ''; try { if (!whatsappConfigured()) { throw new Error('WhatsApp is not configured on the server (WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN / WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID missing).'); } const flyerKey = WA_SCHEDULE_FLYERS[schedule.flyer_key] ? schedule.flyer_key : null; if (!flyerKey) throw new Error(`Unknown flyer "${schedule.flyer_key}".`); const flyerLabel = WA_SCHEDULE_FLYERS[flyerKey].label; const { templateName, languageCode } = whatsappFlyerTemplateFor(flyerKey); const listIds = parseWaScheduleListIds(schedule.list_ids); if (listIds.length === 0) throw new Error('This schedule has no contact groups selected.'); // Load the image (custom one on the schedule, else the flyer's current // saved default) and the recipients in one connection. const { imageDataUrl, imageFilename, contacts, listNames } = await withConnection(async conn => { let dataUrl = null; let filename = null; if (schedule.has_custom_image) { const r = await conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGE_DATA, IMAGE_FILENAME FROM WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { id: schedule.schedule_id } ); const row = lower(r.rows)[0]; if (row) { dataUrl = await clobToString(row.image_data); filename = row.image_filename; } } else { const r = await conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME FROM WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES WHERE FLYER_KEY = :k`, { k: flyerKey } ); const row = lower(r.rows)[0]; if (row) { dataUrl = await clobToString(row.image_data); filename = row.filename; } } const placeholders = listIds.map((_, i) => `:id${i}`).join(','); const binds = {}; listIds.forEach((id, i) => { binds[`id${i}`] = id; }); const contactsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT CONTACT_ID, NAME, PHONE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE LIST_ID IN (${placeholders}) ORDER BY NAME`, binds ); const seenPhones = new Set(); const uniqueContacts = lower(contactsResult.rows).filter(c => { if (seenPhones.has(c.phone)) return false; seenPhones.add(c.phone); return true; }); const namesResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT NAME FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS WHERE LIST_ID IN (${placeholders}) ORDER BY NAME`, binds ); return { imageDataUrl: dataUrl, imageFilename: filename, contacts: uniqueContacts, listNames: lower(namesResult.rows).map(r => r.name) }; }); if (!imageDataUrl) { throw new Error(schedule.has_custom_image ? 'The image saved on this schedule could not be read.' : `No saved default image for the ${flyerLabel} — upload one on the WhatsApp Broadcast page or re-schedule with a custom image.`); } const parsed = parseImageDataUrl(imageDataUrl); if (!parsed) throw new Error('The image for this flyer is not a valid JPG/PNG.'); if (contacts.length === 0) { throw new Error('No saved contacts in the selected group(s) at send time — they may have been emptied or deleted.'); } // Upload once, reuse the same media id for every recipient. const form = new FormData(); form.append('messaging_product', 'whatsapp'); form.append( 'file', new Blob([Buffer.from(parsed.base64, 'base64')], { type: parsed.mimeType }), imageFilename || (flyerKey + '-flyer.' + (parsed.mimeType === 'image/png' ? 'png' : 'jpg')) ); const uploadData = await whatsappApi('/media', { method: 'POST', body: form }); const mediaId = uploadData.id; const components = [ { type: 'header', parameters: [{ type: 'image', image: { id: mediaId } }] }, { type: 'body', parameters: [{ type: 'text', text: schedule.message }] } ]; const failures = []; let okCount = 0; for (const c of contacts) { try { await whatsappApi('/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: c.phone, type: 'template', template: { name: templateName, language: { code: languageCode }, components } }) }); okCount++; } catch (e) { failures.push({ name: c.name, phone: c.phone, error: e.message }); } } resultMessage = `${flyerLabel} sent to ${okCount} of ${contacts.length} contact(s) in: ${listNames.join(', ') || '(no lists)'}.`; if (failures.length) { const shown = failures.slice(0, 5).map(f => `${f.name} (${f.phone}): ${f.error}`).join('; '); resultMessage += ` ${failures.length} failed — ${shown}${failures.length > 5 ? `; …and ${failures.length - 5} more` : ''}.`; } // Only a total failure counts as FAILED; a partial one is still DONE with // the failures spelled out above (e.g. a few numbers not on WhatsApp). if (okCount === 0) finalStatus = 'FAILED'; } catch (e) { console.error('Scheduled wholesale WhatsApp send failed', e); finalStatus = 'FAILED'; resultMessage = 'Could not send: ' + e.message; } await withConnection(async conn => { if (schedule.recurrence === 'ONCE') { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = :status, LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { status: finalStatus, result: resultMessage.slice(0, 4000), id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { const nextRun = nextRecurrenceRunAt(schedule.run_at, schedule.recurrence); await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'PENDING', RUN_AT = :nextRun, LAST_RUN_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_RESULT = :result WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id`, { nextRun, result: resultMessage.slice(0, 4000), id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); } let wholesaleWaSchedulerRunning = false; async function pollWholesaleWhatsappSchedules() { if (wholesaleWaSchedulerRunning) return; // a big send can outlast one tick — don't overlap wholesaleWaSchedulerRunning = true; try { const due = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SCHEDULE_ID, RUN_AT, RECURRENCE, FLYER_KEY, MESSAGE, LIST_IDS, IMAGE_FILENAME, CASE WHEN IMAGE_DATA IS NULL THEN 0 ELSE 1 END AS HAS_CUSTOM_IMAGE FROM WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND RUN_AT <= SYSTIMESTAMP ORDER BY RUN_AT` ); return lower(result.rows); }); for (const schedule of due) { // Atomically claim it so a second overlapping tick/instance can't send // the same flyer twice. const claimed = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULES SET STATUS = 'RUNNING' WHERE SCHEDULE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id: schedule.schedule_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.rowsAffected === 1; }); if (!claimed) continue; await runOneWholesaleWhatsappSchedule(schedule); } } catch (e) { console.error('Wholesale WhatsApp schedule poll failed', e); } finally { wholesaleWaSchedulerRunning = false; } } // Started from the initPool().then(...) block at the bottom of this file, // once the connection pool actually exists — see STARTUP. /* --------------------------------------------------------- WHOLESALE PRICING PASSWORD Lets the password be changed from inside the portal instead of being hardcoded in the frontend, so it stays in sync across every device. The plaintext password is NEVER stored — only a salted scrypt hash (format "salt:hash", both hex) goes in APP_SETTINGS, so nobody reading the database directly — including with full DB access — can see it. Requires an APP_SETTINGS table: CREATE TABLE APP_SETTINGS ( SETTING_KEY VARCHAR2(50) PRIMARY KEY, SETTING_VALUE VARCHAR2(200) ); No seed row is required — if the row is missing, verify/change fall back to comparing against the 'hallan2026' default; the very first successful password change writes the hashed row from then on. --------------------------------------------------------- */ const DEFAULT_WHOLESALE_PASSWORD = process.env.DEFAULT_WHOLESALE_PASSWORD; const DEFAULT_RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD = process.env.DEFAULT_RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD; const SCRYPT_KEYLEN = 64; function hashPassword(password) { const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16).toString('hex'); const hash = crypto.scryptSync(password, salt, SCRYPT_KEYLEN).toString('hex'); return `${salt}:${hash}`; } function verifyPasswordAgainstStored(password, stored, defaultPassword = DEFAULT_WHOLESALE_PASSWORD) { if (!stored) return password === defaultPassword; if (!stored.includes(':')) { // Row predates hashing (e.g. an earlier plaintext seed insert) — compare directly. // It gets upgraded to a hash automatically on the next successful password change. return password === stored; } const hash = crypto.scryptSync(password, stored.split(':')[0], SCRYPT_KEYLEN); const storedHash = Buffer.from(stored.split(':')[1], 'hex'); return hash.length === storedHash.length && crypto.timingSafeEqual(hash, storedHash); } async function getStoredSetting(conn, key) { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT SETTING_VALUE FROM APP_SETTINGS WHERE SETTING_KEY = :key`, { key } ); const rows = lower(result.rows); return rows.length ? rows[0].setting_value : null; } async function getStoredWholesalePassword(conn) { return getStoredSetting(conn, 'WHOLESALE_PASSWORD'); } app.post('/api/wholesale/auth/verify', async (req, res) => { const { password } = req.body || {}; try { const stored = await withConnection(conn => getStoredWholesalePassword(conn)); res.json({ ok: verifyPasswordAgainstStored(password, stored) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/auth/change', async (req, res) => { const { old_password, new_password } = req.body || {}; if (!new_password || !new_password.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'new_password is required' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { const stored = await getStoredWholesalePassword(conn); if (!verifyPasswordAgainstStored(old_password, stored)) { const err = new Error('Current password is incorrect'); err.statusCode = 401; throw err; } await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO APP_SETTINGS s USING (SELECT 'WHOLESALE_PASSWORD' AS k, :new_hash AS v FROM dual) d ON (s.SETTING_KEY = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.SETTING_VALUE = d.v WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SETTING_KEY, SETTING_VALUE) VALUES (d.k, d.v)`, { new_hash: hashPassword(new_password.trim()) }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { if (e.statusCode === 401) { return res.status(401).json({ error: e.message }); } console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- WHOLESALE COLOUR FLYER — saved images Powers the "Upload Images" / "Download Colour Flyer" buttons on the Wholesale Pricing List. Each image (the cover/first page, the closing "thank you"/last page, and one layout image per department) is stored as a full data URL string (e.g. "data:image/jpeg;base64,...") in a CLOB, keyed by a SLOT_KEY: 'FIRST_PAGE' — the cover page background 'LAST_PAGE' — the closing page background 'DEPT::' — that department's page background One row per slot (uploading again overwrites it, same idea as APP_SETTINGS/ROTA_STATE elsewhere in this file), so every device always sees the latest image without piling up old versions. Requires this table: CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES ( SLOT_KEY VARCHAR2(300) PRIMARY KEY, IMAGE_DATA CLOB NOT NULL, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ app.get('/api/wholesale/flyer/images', async (req, res) => { try { const images = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute(`SELECT SLOT_KEY, IMAGE_DATA FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES`); const rows = lower(result.rows); const out = {}; for (const r of rows) { out[r.slot_key] = typeof r.image_data === 'string' ? r.image_data : await r.image_data.getData(); } return out; }); res.json({ images }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/flyer/images', async (req, res) => { const { slot_key, image_data } = req.body || {}; if (!slot_key || !image_data) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'slot_key and image_data are required' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { const existing = await conn.execute( `SELECT SLOT_KEY FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES WHERE SLOT_KEY = :slot_key`, { slot_key } ); if (existing.rows.length === 0) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES (SLOT_KEY, IMAGE_DATA, UPDATED_AT) VALUES (:slot_key, :image_data, SYSTIMESTAMP)`, { slot_key, image_data: { val: image_data, type: oracledb.CLOB } }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES SET IMAGE_DATA = :image_data, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE SLOT_KEY = :slot_key`, { image_data: { val: image_data, type: oracledb.CLOB }, slot_key }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/wholesale/flyer/images/delete', async (req, res) => { // POST rather than a DELETE + path param on purpose — department names // (used as part of the slot key) can contain spaces/slashes that are // awkward to round-trip through a URL segment, so the key travels in the // body instead (same reasoning as the auth endpoints above). const { slot_key } = req.body || {}; if (!slot_key) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'slot_key is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES WHERE SLOT_KEY = :slot_key`, { slot_key }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- PDF LIBRARY — uploadable flyers/documents with a public link Lets staff upload any number of named PDFs (e.g. the wholesale colour flyer, weekly offers) and get back a permanent public URL for each — used as the "Visit Website" button link on WhatsApp templates, which Meta must be able to fetch with no login/session at all. Stored as a BLOB in Oracle rather than on Render's filesystem, because Render's disk is ephemeral — anything written directly to disk is wiped on every redeploy/restart. This follows the exact same "row per slot, re-upload overwrites" pattern as WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES above, just with a BLOB instead of a CLOB since a PDF is binary, not a data-URL string. FILENAME doubles as both the primary key AND the public URL segment (see the serve route below), so it's sanitised on upload to only ever contain characters that are safe in a URL and in most filesystems — this also blocks path-traversal attempts like "../../something". Requires this table: CREATE TABLE WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS ( FILENAME VARCHAR2(300) PRIMARY KEY, PDF_DATA BLOB NOT NULL, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ function sanitisePdfFilename(raw) { const base = String(raw || '').trim() .replace(/\.pdf$/i, '') // strip a trailing .pdf if they typed one — added back below .replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9._-]+/g, '-') // anything not alphanumeric/dot/dash/underscore becomes a dash .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, ''); // trim leading/trailing dashes left over from the replace above return base ? `${base}.pdf` : ''; } // ---- Upload (or overwrite) a PDF. Staff-only — sits behind the normal // portal login + 'wholesale' module permission, same as everything else // under /api/wholesale/. ---- // Body: { filename: 'wholesale-colour-flyer', pdf_base64: '' } app.post('/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs', async (req, res) => { const { filename, pdf_base64 } = req.body || {}; const safeName = sanitisePdfFilename(filename); if (!safeName) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A valid filename is required' }); if (!pdf_base64) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'pdf_base64 is required' }); let buffer; try { buffer = Buffer.from(pdf_base64, 'base64'); if (buffer.length === 0) throw new Error('empty file'); // %PDF is the first 4 bytes of every real PDF — cheap sanity check so // an accidental non-PDF upload fails loudly here instead of silently // serving a broken file to customers later. if (buffer.slice(0, 4).toString('ascii') !== '%PDF') { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'That file does not look like a valid PDF' }); } } catch (e) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'pdf_base64 could not be decoded' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { const existing = await conn.execute( `SELECT FILENAME FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS WHERE FILENAME = :filename`, { filename: safeName } ); if (existing.rows.length === 0) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS (FILENAME, PDF_DATA, UPDATED_AT) VALUES (:filename, :pdf_data, SYSTIMESTAMP)`, { filename: safeName, pdf_data: { val: buffer, type: oracledb.BLOB } }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS SET PDF_DATA = :pdf_data, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE FILENAME = :filename`, { pdf_data: { val: buffer, type: oracledb.BLOB }, filename: safeName }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); const publicUrl = `${PORTAL_PUBLIC_BASE_URL}/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs/file/${encodeURIComponent(safeName)}`; res.json({ ok: true, filename: safeName, url: publicUrl }); } catch (e) { console.error('PDF upload error', e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- List all uploaded PDFs (for the admin management screen). Staff-only. ---- app.get('/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs', async (req, res) => { try { const files = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT FILENAME, UPDATED_AT FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS ORDER BY UPDATED_AT DESC` ); return lower(result.rows).map(r => ({ filename: r.filename, updated_at: r.updated_at, url: `${PORTAL_PUBLIC_BASE_URL}/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs/file/${encodeURIComponent(r.filename)}` })); }); res.json({ files }); } catch (e) { console.error('PDF list error', e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Delete a PDF. Staff-only. POST + body rather than a DELETE verb on // the filename path, for the same reason as the flyer-images delete route // above (keeps this one consistently behind normal auth, unlike the public // serve route below which deliberately lives at a different path). ---- app.post('/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs/delete', async (req, res) => { const { filename } = req.body || {}; const safeName = sanitisePdfFilename(filename); if (!safeName) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A valid filename is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS WHERE FILENAME = :filename`, { filename: safeName }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error('PDF delete error', e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Public serve route — NO auth, NO login, NO IP allowlist. ---- // This is the one customers'/Meta's requests actually hit (via the // WhatsApp template's "Visit Website" button, or any other shared link). // It lives under its own '/file/' segment specifically so it can be // exempted by an exact path-shape check in isPortalFullyExempt() below // without accidentally also exempting the staff-only list/upload/delete // routes above, which all share the '/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs' prefix. app.get('/api/wholesale/flyer/pdfs/file/:filename', async (req, res) => { const safeName = sanitisePdfFilename(req.params.filename); if (!safeName) return res.status(400).send('Invalid filename'); try { const buffer = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT PDF_DATA FROM WHOLESALE_FLYER_PDFS WHERE FILENAME = :filename`, { filename: safeName } ); if (result.rows.length === 0) return null; const lob = result.rows[0][0]; // oracledb gives BLOBs back as a Lob stream by default — read it fully // into a Buffer before sending, same idea as the CLOB .getData() calls // used for the flyer images above, just for binary data instead. const chunks = []; return await new Promise((resolve, reject) => { lob.on('data', chunk => chunks.push(chunk)); lob.on('end', () => resolve(Buffer.concat(chunks))); lob.on('error', reject); }); }); if (!buffer) return res.status(404).send('Not found'); res.set('Content-Type', 'application/pdf'); // 'inline' so it opens in-browser/in-WhatsApp's own PDF viewer rather // than forcing a download prompt — better fit for a "Visit Website" // button someone taps expecting to just see the flyer. res.set('Content-Disposition', `inline; filename="${safeName}"`); // These are public marketing PDFs, safe to let browsers/CDNs cache — // cuts repeat load time for a flyer that gets tapped by many customers. res.set('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=3600'); res.send(buffer); } catch (e) { console.error('PDF serve error', e); res.status(500).send('Server error'); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- RESTAURANT ORDERS: ADMIN PASSWORD Same gate as Wholesale Pricing (a single shared password, hashed the same way, stored under its own APP_SETTINGS key) — protects the "Manage Customers" screen where passcodes are assigned/edited. This is NOT the same thing as a restaurant customer's own passcode (that gates the order sheet itself; see RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS below). --------------------------------------------------------- */ app.post('/api/restaurant/admin/auth/verify', async (req, res) => { const { password } = req.body || {}; try { const stored = await withConnection(conn => getStoredSetting(conn, 'RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD')); res.json({ ok: verifyPasswordAgainstStored(password, stored, DEFAULT_RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/restaurant/admin/auth/change', async (req, res) => { const { old_password, new_password } = req.body || {}; if (!new_password || !new_password.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'new_password is required' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { const stored = await getStoredSetting(conn, 'RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD'); if (!verifyPasswordAgainstStored(old_password, stored, DEFAULT_RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD)) { const err = new Error('Current password is incorrect'); err.statusCode = 401; throw err; } await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO APP_SETTINGS s USING (SELECT 'RESTAURANT_ADMIN_PASSWORD' AS k, :new_hash AS v FROM dual) d ON (s.SETTING_KEY = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.SETTING_VALUE = d.v WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SETTING_KEY, SETTING_VALUE) VALUES (d.k, d.v)`, { new_hash: hashPassword(new_password.trim()) }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { if (e.statusCode === 401) { return res.status(401).json({ error: e.message }); } console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- STORE LOCATIONS: Zone > Shelf > Row > Products Lets staff record exactly where stock physically sits — pick a zone, then a shelf within it, then a row (one row) within that shelf, then the products filed in that row. Requires these tables: CREATE TABLE LOCATION_ZONES ( ZONE_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, ZONE_NAME VARCHAR2(100) NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE LOCATION_SHELVES ( SHELF_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, ZONE_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES LOCATION_ZONES(ZONE_ID), SHELF_NAME VARCHAR2(100) NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE LOCATION_ROWS ( ROW_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, SHELF_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES LOCATION_SHELVES(SHELF_ID), ROW_NAME VARCHAR2(50) NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS ( ITEM_ROW_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, ROW_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES LOCATION_ROWS(ROW_ID), ITEM_NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, STOCKID NUMBER, BARCODE VARCHAR2(100), ADDED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Zones: list (with shelf counts) ---- app.get('/api/location/zones', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT z.ZONE_ID, z.ZONE_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM LOCATION_SHELVES sh WHERE sh.ZONE_ID = z.ZONE_ID) AS SHELF_COUNT FROM LOCATION_ZONES z ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Zones: create ---- app.post('/api/location/zones', async (req, res) => { const { zone_name } = req.body || {}; if (!zone_name || !zone_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'zone_name is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO LOCATION_ZONES (ZONE_NAME) VALUES (:zone_name) RETURNING ZONE_ID INTO :new_id`, { zone_name: zone_name.trim(), new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Zones: rename ---- app.put('/api/location/zones/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { zone_name } = req.body || {}; if (!zone_name || !zone_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'zone_name is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE LOCATION_ZONES SET ZONE_NAME = :zone_name WHERE ZONE_ID = :id`, { zone_name: zone_name.trim(), id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Zones: delete (cascades to shelves, rows, and products under it) ---- app.delete('/api/location/zones/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ROW_ID IN ( SELECT ROW_ID FROM LOCATION_ROWS WHERE SHELF_ID IN ( SELECT SHELF_ID FROM LOCATION_SHELVES WHERE ZONE_ID = :id))`, { id } ); await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROWS WHERE SHELF_ID IN ( SELECT SHELF_ID FROM LOCATION_SHELVES WHERE ZONE_ID = :id)`, { id } ); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_SHELVES WHERE ZONE_ID = :id`, { id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_ZONES WHERE ZONE_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelves: list within a zone (with row counts) ---- app.get('/api/location/zones/:zoneId/shelves', async (req, res) => { const { zoneId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.SHELF_ID, s.ZONE_ID, s.SHELF_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM LOCATION_ROWS a WHERE a.SHELF_ID = s.SHELF_ID) AS ROW_COUNT FROM LOCATION_SHELVES s WHERE s.ZONE_ID = :zoneId ORDER BY s.SHELF_NAME`, { zoneId } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelves: create ---- app.post('/api/location/zones/:zoneId/shelves', async (req, res) => { const { zoneId } = req.params; const { shelf_name } = req.body || {}; if (!shelf_name || !shelf_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'shelf_name is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO LOCATION_SHELVES (ZONE_ID, SHELF_NAME) VALUES (:zoneId, :shelf_name) RETURNING SHELF_ID INTO :new_id`, { zoneId, shelf_name: shelf_name.trim(), new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelves: rename ---- app.put('/api/location/shelves/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { shelf_name } = req.body || {}; if (!shelf_name || !shelf_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'shelf_name is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE LOCATION_SHELVES SET SHELF_NAME = :shelf_name WHERE SHELF_ID = :id`, { shelf_name: shelf_name.trim(), id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelves: delete (cascades to rows and products under it) ---- app.delete('/api/location/shelves/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ROW_ID IN ( SELECT ROW_ID FROM LOCATION_ROWS WHERE SHELF_ID = :id)`, { id } ); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROWS WHERE SHELF_ID = :id`, { id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_SHELVES WHERE SHELF_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Rows: list within a shelf (with product counts) ---- app.get('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/rows', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT a.ROW_ID, a.SHELF_ID, a.ROW_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS i WHERE i.ROW_ID = a.ROW_ID) AS ITEM_COUNT FROM LOCATION_ROWS a WHERE a.SHELF_ID = :shelfId ORDER BY a.ROW_NAME`, { shelfId } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Rows: create ---- app.post('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/rows', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; const { row_name } = req.body || {}; if (!row_name || !row_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'row_name is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO LOCATION_ROWS (SHELF_ID, ROW_NAME) VALUES (:shelfId, :row_name) RETURNING ROW_ID INTO :new_id`, { shelfId, row_name: row_name.trim(), new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Rows: rename ---- app.put('/api/location/rows/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { row_name } = req.body || {}; if (!row_name || !row_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'row_name is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE LOCATION_ROWS SET ROW_NAME = :row_name WHERE ROW_ID = :id`, { row_name: row_name.trim(), id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Rows: delete (cascades to products under it) ---- app.delete('/api/location/rows/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ROW_ID = :id`, { id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROWS WHERE ROW_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Helper: where (if anywhere) is this stockid already filed? Used to stop // the same product being filed at more than one location. ---- async function findExistingLocationForStockid(conn, stockid, excludeItemRowId) { if (!stockid) return null; const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ROW_ID, i.ROW_ID, a.ROW_NAME, sh.SHELF_ID, sh.SHELF_NAME, z.ZONE_ID, z.ZONE_NAME FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS i JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = i.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE i.STOCKID = :stockid ORDER BY i.ITEM_ROW_ID`, { stockid } ); const rows = lower(result.rows).filter(r => r.item_row_id !== Number(excludeItemRowId)); return rows.length ? rows[0] : null; } // ---- Row products: list (with live stock, matched by stockid or, failing that, by name) ---- app.get('/api/location/rows/:rowId/items', async (req, res) => { const { rowId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ROW_ID, i.ROW_ID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.STOCKID, i.BARCODE, s.QUANTITY AS STOCK_AVAILABLE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS i LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = i.STOCKID OR (i.STOCKID IS NULL AND s.NAME = i.ITEM_NAME) WHERE i.ROW_ID = :rid ORDER BY i.ITEM_NAME`, { rid: rowId } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Row products: add (via search or barcode scan). // DUPLICATE-LOCATION CHECK: a product should live at exactly one // location. If this stockid is already filed somewhere else, we // refuse (409) and tell the caller where it already is, instead of // silently creating a second entry. Pass force:true to move it — // the old entry is deleted and the new one inserted in the same // transaction, so the product is never present at two locations // at once. Items with no stockid (name-only entries) can't be // checked this way and are inserted as before. ---- app.post('/api/location/rows/:rowId/items', async (req, res) => { const { rowId } = req.params; const { item_name, stockid, barcode, force } = req.body || {}; if (!item_name || !item_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'item_name is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(async conn => { if (stockid) { const existing = await findExistingLocationForStockid(conn, stockid, null); if (existing) { if (!force) { const err = new Error( `${item_name.trim()} is already filed at ${existing.zone_name} / ${existing.shelf_name} / Row ${existing.row_name}` ); err.statusCode = 409; err.existing = existing; throw err; } // force: move it — remove the old filing so it's never in two places at once await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ROW_ID = :id`, { id: existing.item_row_id } ); } } const insertResult = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS (ROW_ID, ITEM_NAME, STOCKID, BARCODE) VALUES (:rid, :item_name, :stockid, :barcode) RETURNING ITEM_ROW_ID INTO :new_id`, { rid: rowId, item_name: item_name.trim(), stockid: stockid || null, barcode: barcode || null, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } } ); await conn.commit(); return insertResult.outBinds.new_id[0]; }); res.json({ new_id: result }); } catch (e) { if (e.statusCode === 409) { return res.status(409).json({ error: e.message, existing: e.existing }); } console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- One-off cleanup: find stockids filed at more than one location and // collapse each down to a single filing (the earliest one added). // Run this once to clean up any duplicates that already exist from // before the check above was added; safe to call again later since // it's a no-op once everything is unique. ---- app.post('/api/location/dedupe-items', async (req, res) => { try { const removed = await withConnection(async conn => { const dupResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE STOCKID IS NOT NULL GROUP BY STOCKID HAVING COUNT(*) > 1` ); const dupStockIds = lower(dupResult.rows).map(r => r.stockid); const removedItems = []; for (const stockid of dupStockIds) { const rowsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ROW_ID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.ADDED_AT, z.ZONE_NAME, sh.SHELF_NAME, a.ROW_NAME FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS i JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = i.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE i.STOCKID = :stockid ORDER BY i.ADDED_AT ASC, i.ITEM_ROW_ID ASC`, { stockid } ); const rows = lower(rowsResult.rows); // Keep the first (earliest-added) filing, remove the rest const toRemove = rows.slice(1); for (const r of toRemove) { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ROW_ID = :id`, { id: r.item_row_id }); removedItems.push(r); } } await conn.commit(); return removedItems; }); res.json({ ok: true, removed_count: removed.length, removed }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Row products: remove ---- app.delete('/api/location/items/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ROW_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- SHELF REPLENISHMENT A per-shelf "sales since I last checked" counter. Staff hit Begin at a shelf to start the clock; Print List becomes available and shows every sale (from VESALES) of a product filed under that shelf's rows since the clock started; printing resets the clock to that moment so the next print only shows what's sold since then. The last list that was printed is saved too, so it can be reprinted on demand without affecting the clock — useful if the printer jams and the original never came out. MINIMUM PRINT QUANTITY — an item only makes it onto the printed list once its total (this window's sales + anything carried over from before) is at least MIN_PRINT_QTY. Below that, it's saved as a carry-forward amount instead of printed, so a single stray sale doesn't trigger a special trip to restock just one item — it waits, silently accumulating, until there's enough of it (or it's joined by another sale of the same item) to be worth printing. Once an item crosses the threshold it prints in full (window sales + everything carried) and its carry-forward resets to zero. NOTE ON DUPLICATES: a product can now only be filed at one location (see the duplicate-location check on POST /api/location/rows/:rowId/items), so a stockid can no longer show up under two different shelves at once. The DISTINCT below still protects against the same stockid appearing twice within a single shelf's own rows. VESALES.TRANS_DATE (Oracle-side) holds the sale datetime, populated by test_oracle.py joining Firebird's VESALES to its VETRANS header table on TRANSID (VESALES itself has no date column — it's a line-items table). Requires these tables: CREATE TABLE VESALES ( TRANSID NUMBER, STOCKID NUMBER, SELL_QTY NUMBER, TRANS_DATE DATE ); CREATE TABLE SHELF_REPLENISHMENT ( SHELF_ID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES LOCATION_SHELVES(SHELF_ID), STARTED_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_PRINT_ITEMS CLOB, LAST_PRINT_FROM TIMESTAMP, LAST_PRINT_TO TIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE SHELF_REPLEN_CARRY ( SHELF_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES LOCATION_SHELVES(SHELF_ID), STOCKID NUMBER NOT NULL, CARRY_QTY NUMBER NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (SHELF_ID, STOCKID) ); If you already created SHELF_REPLENISHMENT without the last three columns, add them instead of recreating the table: ALTER TABLE SHELF_REPLENISHMENT ADD ( LAST_PRINT_ITEMS CLOB, LAST_PRINT_FROM TIMESTAMP, LAST_PRINT_TO TIMESTAMP ); PERFORMANCE — Print List reads VESALES (an append-only table that only ever grows, never truncated — see test_oracle.py). Without an index, every single Print click does a full scan of that entire table, and it gets slower every day as more sales accumulate. Run this once: CREATE INDEX IDX_VESALES_STOCKID_DATE ON VESALES (STOCKID, TRANS_DATE); This is the fix for Print List being slow — not a cache. The whole point of this screen is "sales since the clock started," so a cache would just serve stale numbers; making the underlying query fast is the correct fix instead. --------------------------------------------------------- */ const REPLEN_MIN_PRINT_QTY = 2; // ---- Shelf replenishment: get current status (started_at or null) ---- app.get('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/replenishment', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STARTED_AT FROM SHELF_REPLENISHMENT WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); res.json({ started_at: rows.length ? rows[0].started_at : null }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelf replenishment: begin (or restart) the clock for a shelf. // Pass started_at (ISO datetime string) to set it manually instead of now — // e.g. after a printer issue, to backdate the clock to when it actually started. // Pass clear_carry: true to also wipe this shelf's carry-forward table — // a genuine restart should zero out everything, not just the clock, or // old carried-over quantities would quietly reappear in the next print. // Both changes happen in one transaction: either the clock resets and the // carry-forward clears together, or neither does. ---- app.post('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/replenishment/begin', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; const { started_at, clear_carry } = req.body || {}; let startedAt = new Date(); if (started_at) { startedAt = new Date(started_at); if (isNaN(startedAt.getTime())) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid date/time' }); } } try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO SHELF_REPLENISHMENT s USING (SELECT :shelfId AS k FROM dual) d ON (s.SHELF_ID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.STARTED_AT = :startedAt WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SHELF_ID, STARTED_AT) VALUES (d.k, :startedAt)`, { shelfId, startedAt } ); if (clear_carry) { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM SHELF_REPLEN_CARRY WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId } ); } await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true, carry_cleared: !!clear_carry }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelf replenishment: print — combines this window's sales with anything // carried over from before; only items whose total reaches // REPLEN_MIN_PRINT_QTY are printed (and have their carry-forward cleared). // Everything else is saved as a carry-forward amount for next time. // Always resets the clock to now, saves the printed snapshot for reprinting. ---- app.post('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/replenishment/print', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; try { const items = await withConnection(async conn => { const stateResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STARTED_AT FROM SHELF_REPLENISHMENT WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId } ); const stateRows = lower(stateResult.rows); if (!stateRows.length || !stateRows[0].started_at) { const err = new Error('Replenishment has not been started for this shelf yet'); err.statusCode = 400; throw err; } const startedAt = stateRows[0].started_at; const printedAt = new Date(); // New sales this window, grouped by stockid (DISTINCT-filtered first so a // product filed in more than one row under this shelf isn't double-counted) const salesResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT v.STOCKID, SUM(v.SELL_QTY) AS QTY_SOLD FROM VESALES v WHERE v.STOCKID IN ( SELECT DISTINCT li.STOCKID FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS li JOIN LOCATION_ROWS r ON r.ROW_ID = li.ROW_ID WHERE r.SHELF_ID = :shelfId AND li.STOCKID IS NOT NULL ) AND v.TRANS_DATE >= :startedAt GROUP BY v.STOCKID`, { shelfId, startedAt } ); const newSales = lower(salesResult.rows); // Whatever was below-threshold and carried over from previous prints const carryResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, CARRY_QTY FROM SHELF_REPLEN_CARRY WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId } ); const carryRows = lower(carryResult.rows); // Merge into one total per stockid const totals = new Map(); newSales.forEach(r => totals.set(r.stockid, (totals.get(r.stockid) || 0) + Number(r.qty_sold))); carryRows.forEach(r => totals.set(r.stockid, (totals.get(r.stockid) || 0) + Number(r.carry_qty))); const toPrint = []; // { stockid, total } const toCarry = []; // { stockid, total } — total is always < REPLEN_MIN_PRINT_QTY here totals.forEach((total, stockid) => { if (total >= REPLEN_MIN_PRINT_QTY) toPrint.push({ stockid, total }); else if (total > 0) toCarry.push({ stockid, total }); }); // Look up name + current stock for whatever's printable let printItems = []; if (toPrint.length) { const idBinds = {}; const placeholders = toPrint.map((r, i) => { idBinds['id' + i] = r.stockid; return ':id' + i; }).join(','); const nameResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, NAME, QUANTITY FROM VESTOCK WHERE STOCKID IN (${placeholders})`, idBinds ); const infoMap = new Map(lower(nameResult.rows).map(r => [r.stockid, r])); printItems = toPrint .map(r => { const info = infoMap.get(r.stockid); return { item_name: info ? info.name : ('Stock #' + r.stockid), qty_sold: r.total, stock_available: info ? info.quantity : null }; }) .sort((a, b) => a.item_name.localeCompare(b.item_name)); // These have now been printed — clear their carry-forward const clearBinds = {}; const clearPlaceholders = toPrint.map((r, i) => { clearBinds['cid' + i] = r.stockid; return ':cid' + i; }).join(','); await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM SHELF_REPLEN_CARRY WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId AND STOCKID IN (${clearPlaceholders})`, { shelfId, ...clearBinds } ); } // Everything still below threshold gets saved (or re-saved) as the new carry amount for (const r of toCarry) { await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO SHELF_REPLEN_CARRY c USING (SELECT :shelfId AS s, :stockid AS k FROM dual) d ON (c.SHELF_ID = d.s AND c.STOCKID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET c.CARRY_QTY = :qty WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SHELF_ID, STOCKID, CARRY_QTY) VALUES (d.s, d.k, :qty)`, { shelfId, stockid: r.stockid, qty: r.total } ); } await conn.execute( `UPDATE SHELF_REPLENISHMENT SET STARTED_AT = :printedAt, LAST_PRINT_ITEMS = :lastPrintItems, LAST_PRINT_FROM = :startedAt, LAST_PRINT_TO = :printedAt WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId, startedAt, printedAt, lastPrintItems: JSON.stringify(printItems) }, { autoCommit: true } ); return printItems; }); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { if (e.statusCode === 400) return res.status(400).json({ error: e.message }); console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shelf replenishment: fetch the last list that was printed, to reprint it // (e.g. the printer jammed) without touching the clock ---- app.get('/api/location/shelves/:shelfId/replenishment/last-print', async (req, res) => { const { shelfId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT LAST_PRINT_ITEMS, LAST_PRINT_FROM, LAST_PRINT_TO FROM SHELF_REPLENISHMENT WHERE SHELF_ID = :shelfId`, { shelfId } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); if (!rows.length || !rows[0].last_print_items) { return res.json({ items: null }); } res.json({ items: JSON.parse(rows[0].last_print_items), from: rows[0].last_print_from, to: rows[0].last_print_to }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Categories: list ---- app.get('/api/categories', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT CATEGORY_ID, CATEGORY_NAME, LIST_TYPE, SORT_ORDER FROM PICK_CATEGORIES ORDER BY LIST_TYPE, CATEGORY_NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Categories: create ---- app.post('/api/categories', async (req, res) => { const { category_name, list_type } = req.body;
