try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO PICK_CATEGORIES (CATEGORY_NAME, LIST_TYPE) VALUES (:category_name, :list_type) RETURNING CATEGORY_ID INTO :new_id`, { category_name, list_type: list_type || 'WAREHOUSE', new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Categories: rename ---- app.put('/api/categories/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { category_name } = req.body; if (!category_name || !category_name.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'category_name is required' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE PICK_CATEGORIES SET CATEGORY_NAME = :category_name WHERE CATEGORY_ID = :id`, { category_name: category_name.trim(), id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Categories: delete (also removes every item filed under it) ---- app.delete('/api/categories/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS WHERE CATEGORY_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } ); await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM PICK_CATEGORIES WHERE CATEGORY_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Categories: reset every WAREHOUSE item's quantity to 0 (used by // "Create New List" on the Warehouse List hub). This does NOT delete // the item rows themselves — the item list, its categories, and its // alphabetical order all stay exactly as they are. Only QTY_REQUIRED // goes back to 0, so staff aren't re-adding every block from scratch // for each new list — they just start counting again from zero. ---- app.post('/api/categories/clear-warehouse-items', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS SET QTY_REQUIRED = 0 WHERE CATEGORY_ID IN (SELECT CATEGORY_ID FROM PICK_CATEGORIES WHERE LIST_TYPE = 'WAREHOUSE')`, {}, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Items: list ---- app.get('/api/items', async (req, res) => { try { // LOCATION mirrors the same lookup used in Stock Info: match by STOCKID // first, fall back to a name match for items filed without one. Left as // a COALESCE of two correlated subqueries rather than a JOIN so an item // with no location on file just comes back NULL (blank on the picking // sheet) instead of being dropped from the list entirely. const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ROW_ID, i.CATEGORY_ID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.QTY_REQUIRED, s.QUANTITY AS STOCK_AVAILABLE, COALESCE( (SELECT LISTAGG(z.ZONE_NAME || ' / ' || sh.SHELF_NAME || ' / Row ' || a.ROW_NAME, '; ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME, sh.SHELF_NAME, a.ROW_NAME) FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS li JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = li.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE li.STOCKID = s.STOCKID), (SELECT LISTAGG(z.ZONE_NAME || ' / ' || sh.SHELF_NAME || ' / Row ' || a.ROW_NAME, '; ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME, sh.SHELF_NAME, a.ROW_NAME) FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS li JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = li.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE li.STOCKID IS NULL AND li.ITEM_NAME = i.ITEM_NAME) ) AS LOCATION FROM PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS i LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.NAME = i.ITEM_NAME ORDER BY i.CATEGORY_ID, i.ITEM_NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Items: add ---- app.post('/api/items', async (req, res) => { const { category_id, item_name, qty_required } = req.body; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS (CATEGORY_ID, ITEM_NAME, QTY_REQUIRED) VALUES (:category_id, :item_name, :qty_required) RETURNING ITEM_ROW_ID INTO :new_id`, { category_id, item_name, qty_required: qty_required || 1, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Items: update quantity ---- app.put('/api/items/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { qty_required } = req.body; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS SET QTY_REQUIRED = :qty_required WHERE ITEM_ROW_ID = :id`, { qty_required, id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Items: delete ---- app.delete('/api/items/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM PICK_CATEGORY_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ROW_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- SUPPLIER ORDERS Search a supplier, then: - Basic List: items that supplier supplies, ranked by sales over a chosen number of weeks (from VESALES, joined via SUPPLIER_STOCK). - Advanced List: a reorder suggestion per item — NOT real machine learning, to be upfront about it. It's a recency-weighted average of historical weekly sales (60% weight on the more recent half of the chosen lookback window, 40% on the older half), projected across the order cycle (time since last delivery, or 4 weeks if that isn't set yet). Also shows a plain flat-average estimate alongside it for comparison, current stock, and actual demand since the last delivery. - Compare: predicted vs previous-period vs current-period actual sales. Last delivery date is set manually per supplier (no delivery-records table identified yet) — same manual-time pattern as Shelf Replenishment. Requires these tables: CREATE TABLE SUPPLIERS ( SUPPID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, NAME VARCHAR2(200) ); CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_STOCK ( STOCKID NUMBER NOT NULL, SUPPID NUMBER NOT NULL, COST_NET NUMBER, PACKSIZE NUMBER, CATNO VARCHAR2(100), -- catalogue number, from VESUPPSTOCK.CATNO PREFERRED NUMBER(1), -- 1 = preferred supplier for this item, else not STOCKNO VARCHAR2(100), -- product code, from VESTOCKNOS.STOCKNO PRIMARY KEY (STOCKID, SUPPID) ); -- If SUPPLIER_STOCK already exists from before, run: -- ALTER TABLE SUPPLIER_STOCK ADD (CATNO VARCHAR2(100)); -- ALTER TABLE SUPPLIER_STOCK ADD (PREFERRED NUMBER(1)); -- ALTER TABLE SUPPLIER_STOCK ADD (STOCKNO VARCHAR2(100)); CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_LAST_DELIVERY ( SUPPID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES SUPPLIERS(SUPPID), LAST_DELIVERY_AT TIMESTAMP ); Place Order — reorder level / max stock level overrides: VESTOCK.REORDERLEVEL / VESTOCK.MAXSTOCKLEVEL come from Vector and get overwritten by the CSV sync every ~5 minutes (and are frequently blank in Vector to begin with), so they can't be edited in place. Edits made on the Place Order screen are instead stored in this separate table, which the sync job never touches, and are joined back on top of the VESTOCK values (override wins when present, otherwise fall back to whatever VESTOCK currently has): CREATE TABLE STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, REORDER_LEVEL NUMBER, MAX_STOCK_LEVEL NUMBER, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200) ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ /* --------------------------------------------------------- SUPPLIERS > CREDIT NOTES (The old "Supplier Orders password" prompt and its /auth/verify and /auth/change routes have been removed — access to this whole section is already controlled by the 'supplier' module permission in Manage Users.) A credit note has a header row plus one or more lines (description, amount ex-VAT, VAT amount). Totals are stored on the header so lists don't have to add the lines up every time. Status flow: PENDING --(Completed button)--> COMPLETED Only PENDING credit notes can be edited. Nothing is ever deleted. Tables (created automatically on boot by ensureSupplierCreditNoteTables): CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES ( CREDIT_NOTE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(200), REFERENCE VARCHAR2(100), STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), COMPLETED_AT TIMESTAMP, COMPLETED_BY VARCHAR2(200), NET_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, VAT_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, GROSS_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0 ); CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES ( LINE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, CREDIT_NOTE_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES(CREDIT_NOTE_ID), LINE_NO NUMBER, DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500) NOT NULL, AMOUNT NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, VAT_AMOUNT NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0 ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ async function ensureSupplierCreditNoteTables() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES ( CREDIT_NOTE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(200), REFERENCE VARCHAR2(100), STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), COMPLETED_AT TIMESTAMP, COMPLETED_BY VARCHAR2(200), NET_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, VAT_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, GROSS_TOTAL NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0 )`, `CREATE TABLE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES ( LINE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, CREDIT_NOTE_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES(CREDIT_NOTE_ID), LINE_NO NUMBER, DESCRIPTION VARCHAR2(500) NOT NULL, AMOUNT NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0, VAT_AMOUNT NUMBER(12,2) DEFAULT 0 )`, `CREATE INDEX SUPP_CN_STATUS_IX ON SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES (STATUS, CREDIT_NOTE_ID)`, `CREATE INDEX SUPP_CN_LINES_IX ON SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES (CREDIT_NOTE_ID)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[supplier-credit-notes] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (made on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[supplier-credit-notes] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } function cnRound2(v) { const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.round(n * 100) / 100 : NaN; } function cnUserName(req) { return (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) ? String(req.portalUser.name).slice(0, 200) : null; } // Validates the body of a create/edit request. Completely blank lines are // ignored; everything else must have a description and a valid amount. // VAT may be left blank (treated as 0). Returns { error } or { header, lines }. function parseCreditNoteBody(body) { body = body || {}; const supplierName = String(body.supplier_name || '').trim().slice(0, 200); const reference = String(body.reference || '').trim().slice(0, 100); const rawLines = Array.isArray(body.lines) ? body.lines : []; if (!supplierName) return { error: 'Choose a supplier' }; if (!reference) return { error: 'Enter a reference number' }; const lines = []; for (const l of rawLines) { const description = String((l && l.description) || '').trim().slice(0, 500); const amountBlank = !l || l.amount === '' || l.amount === null || l.amount === undefined; const vatBlank = !l || l.vat_amount === '' || l.vat_amount === null || l.vat_amount === undefined; if (!description && amountBlank && vatBlank) continue; if (!description) return { error: 'Every line needs a description' }; if (amountBlank) return { error: 'Every line needs an amount' }; const amount = cnRound2(l.amount); const vat = vatBlank ? 0 : cnRound2(l.vat_amount); if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || !Number.isFinite(vat)) return { error: 'Amount and VAT must be numbers' }; if (amount < 0 || vat < 0) return { error: 'Amount and VAT cannot be negative' }; lines.push({ description, amount, vat }); } if (!lines.length) return { error: 'Add at least one line' }; let netP = 0, vatP = 0; lines.forEach(l => { netP += Math.round(l.amount * 100); vatP += Math.round(l.vat * 100); }); return { header: { supplierName, reference, net: netP / 100, vat: vatP / 100, gross: (netP + vatP) / 100 }, lines }; } async function insertCreditNoteLines(conn, creditNoteId, lines) { for (let i = 0; i < lines.length; i++) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES (CREDIT_NOTE_ID, LINE_NO, DESCRIPTION, AMOUNT, VAT_AMOUNT) VALUES (:id, :line_no, :description, :amount, :vat)`, { id: creditNoteId, line_no: i + 1, description: lines[i].description, amount: lines[i].amount, vat: lines[i].vat }, { autoCommit: false } ); } } // ---- Counts for the Credit Notes menu ---- app.get('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes/summary', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS = 'PENDING' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS PENDING_COUNT, SUM(CASE WHEN STATUS = 'COMPLETED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS COMPLETED_COUNT FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES` )); const row = lower(result.rows)[0] || {}; res.json({ pending: Number(row.pending_count) || 0, completed: Number(row.completed_count) || 0 }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- List by status (?status=PENDING | COMPLETED), newest first ---- app.get('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes', async (req, res) => { const status = String(req.query.status || 'PENDING').toUpperCase(); if (status !== 'PENDING' && status !== 'COMPLETED') return res.status(400).json({ error: 'status must be PENDING or COMPLETED' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT c.CREDIT_NOTE_ID, c.SUPPLIER_NAME, c.REFERENCE, c.STATUS, c.CREATED_BY, c.CREATED_AT, c.UPDATED_AT, c.COMPLETED_AT, c.COMPLETED_BY, c.NET_TOTAL, c.VAT_TOTAL, c.GROSS_TOTAL, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES l WHERE l.CREDIT_NOTE_ID = c.CREDIT_NOTE_ID) AS LINE_COUNT FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES c WHERE c.STATUS = :status ORDER BY c.CREDIT_NOTE_ID DESC FETCH FIRST 500 ROWS ONLY`, { status } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- One credit note with its lines (used by Edit and Download) ---- app.get('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes/:id', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const h = await conn.execute( `SELECT CREDIT_NOTE_ID, SUPPLIER_NAME, REFERENCE, STATUS, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT, UPDATED_AT, COMPLETED_AT, COMPLETED_BY, NET_TOTAL, VAT_TOTAL, GROSS_TOTAL FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES WHERE CREDIT_NOTE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id } ); const header = lower(h.rows)[0]; if (!header) return null; const l = await conn.execute( `SELECT LINE_NO, DESCRIPTION, AMOUNT, VAT_AMOUNT FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES WHERE CREDIT_NOTE_ID = :id ORDER BY LINE_NO`, { id: req.params.id } ); return { ...header, lines: lower(l.rows) }; }); if (!out) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Credit note not found' }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Create: always starts as PENDING ---- app.post('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes', async (req, res) => { const parsed = parseCreditNoteBody(req.body); if (parsed.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error }); const by = cnUserName(req); try { const newId = await withConnection(async conn => { try { const r = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES (SUPPLIER_NAME, REFERENCE, STATUS, CREATED_BY, NET_TOTAL, VAT_TOTAL, GROSS_TOTAL) VALUES (:supplier_name, :reference, 'PENDING', :created_by, :net, :vat, :gross) RETURNING CREDIT_NOTE_ID INTO :new_id`, { supplier_name: { val: parsed.header.supplierName || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, reference: { val: parsed.header.reference || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, created_by: { val: by, type: oracledb.STRING }, net: parsed.header.net, vat: parsed.header.vat, gross: parsed.header.gross, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: false } ); const id = r.outBinds.new_id[0]; await insertCreditNoteLines(conn, id, parsed.lines); await conn.commit(); return id; } catch (e) { try { await conn.rollback(); } catch (_) {} throw e; } }); res.json({ ok: true, credit_note_id: newId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Edit: only while PENDING; replaces the header details and all lines ---- app.put('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes/:id', async (req, res) => { const parsed = parseCreditNoteBody(req.body); if (parsed.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: parsed.error }); const by = cnUserName(req); try { const outcome = await withConnection(async conn => { try { const u = await conn.execute( `UPDATE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES SET SUPPLIER_NAME = :supplier_name, REFERENCE = :reference, NET_TOTAL = :net, VAT_TOTAL = :vat, GROSS_TOTAL = :gross, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY = :updated_by WHERE CREDIT_NOTE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { supplier_name: { val: parsed.header.supplierName || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, reference: { val: parsed.header.reference || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, net: parsed.header.net, vat: parsed.header.vat, gross: parsed.header.gross, updated_by: { val: by, type: oracledb.STRING }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: false } ); if (!u.rowsAffected) { await conn.rollback(); return 'not-editable'; } await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTE_LINES WHERE CREDIT_NOTE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: false }); await insertCreditNoteLines(conn, req.params.id, parsed.lines); await conn.commit(); return 'ok'; } catch (e) { try { await conn.rollback(); } catch (_) {} throw e; } }); if (outcome === 'not-editable') return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Only a pending credit note can be edited' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Completed": moves a PENDING credit note to the Completed list ---- app.post('/api/supplier-orders/credit-notes/:id/complete', async (req, res) => { const by = cnUserName(req); try { const r = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE SUPPLIER_CREDIT_NOTES SET STATUS = 'COMPLETED', COMPLETED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, COMPLETED_BY = :completed_by WHERE CREDIT_NOTE_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { completed_by: { val: by, type: oracledb.STRING }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (!r.rowsAffected) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'This credit note is not pending (it may already be completed)' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- STOCK INFO > ONLINE DATA > SUPPLIER Tracks, per product, whether the three pieces of online-listing data are done: Images, Description, Other Details. A product is "complete" once all three are ticked; it can be sent back to "incomplete" (which clears the three ticks so it genuinely needs doing again). Products are grouped Preferred / Non-Preferred per supplier using the same SUPPLIER_STOCK.PREFERRED flag the Supplier Orders screens use. Completion is stored per PRODUCT (STOCKID), not per supplier-product, because images/description/details belong to the product — if the same product sits under two suppliers it only needs doing once. Routes live under /api/stock/ on purpose, so anyone with Stock Info access can use this without also needing the Supplier Orders module. Table (created automatically on boot by ensureOnlineDataChecksTable): CREATE TABLE ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, IMAGES_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, DESCRIPTION_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, OTHER_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, IS_COMPLETE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, COMPLETED_AT TIMESTAMP, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200) ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ async function ensureOnlineDataChecksTable() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, IMAGES_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, DESCRIPTION_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, OTHER_DONE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, IS_COMPLETE NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, COMPLETED_AT TIMESTAMP, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200) )`, `CREATE INDEX ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS_IX ON ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS (IS_COMPLETE)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[online-data] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (made on an earlier boot) — expected. if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[online-data] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // ---- Supplier search for the Online Data screen (same matching as // /api/suppliers/search, but under /api/stock/ so the Stock Info // permission is enough). ---- app.get('/api/stock/online-data/suppliers', async (req, res) => { const q = (req.query.q || '').trim(); if (!q) return res.json({ items: [] }); try { const words = q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const binds = {}; const matchExprs = words.map((w, i) => { binds['w' + i] = w; return `CASE WHEN UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`; }); const orClauses = words.map((w, i) => `UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%'`).join(' OR '); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT SUPPID, NAME FROM ( SELECT SUPPID, NAME, (${matchExprs.join(' + ')}) AS MATCH_COUNT FROM SUPPLIERS WHERE ${orClauses} ) ORDER BY MATCH_COUNT DESC, NAME FETCH FIRST 25 ROWS ONLY`, binds )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Suppliers that already have Online Data progress (at least one product // with a tick, or completed), most recently worked on first — so the // Select Supplier screen can offer "continue where you left off". ---- app.get('/api/stock/online-data/started', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT SUPPID, NAME, TOTAL, DONE, PARTIAL, LAST_UPDATED FROM ( SELECT sup.SUPPID, sup.NAME, COUNT(DISTINCT ss.STOCKID) AS TOTAL, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN c.IS_COMPLETE = 1 THEN ss.STOCKID END) AS DONE, COUNT(DISTINCT CASE WHEN NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) = 0 AND NVL(c.IMAGES_DONE, 0) + NVL(c.DESCRIPTION_DONE, 0) + NVL(c.OTHER_DONE, 0) > 0 THEN ss.STOCKID END) AS PARTIAL, MAX(c.UPDATED_AT) AS LAST_UPDATED FROM SUPPLIERS sup JOIN SUPPLIER_STOCK ss ON ss.SUPPID = sup.SUPPID LEFT JOIN ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS c ON c.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID GROUP BY sup.SUPPID, sup.NAME ) WHERE DONE + PARTIAL > 0 ORDER BY LAST_UPDATED DESC NULLS LAST, NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows).map(r => ({ suppid: r.suppid, name: r.name, total: Number(r.total) || 0, complete: Number(r.done) || 0, partial: Number(r.partial) || 0 })) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ==================================================================== // WOOCOMMERCE WEBSITE STATUS (Online Data > Supplier > product lists) // Reads every product from the WooCommerce REST API (read-only calls) and // matches it to our products so each row can show Published / Draft / etc. // // Render environment variables (Environment tab of THIS backend service): // WC_URL e.g. https://www.yourshop.co.uk (no trailing slash needed) // WC_KEY ck_xxxxxxxx (WooCommerce > Settings > Advanced > REST API) // WC_SECRET cs_xxxxxxxx // WC_CACHE_MINUTES optional, default 10 — how long the product list is cached // // Matching: a WooCommerce product matches when its SKU (or its GTIN/UPC/EAN // "global unique ID" field) equals one of our barcodes, or our supplier // catalogue no. / stock no. Leading zeros and spaces are ignored. // Anything with no match is reported as "not_found" (not on the website). // ==================================================================== const WC_SITE_URL = (process.env.WC_URL || process.env.WC_SITE_URL || process.env.WOOCOMMERCE_URL || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, ''); const WC_KEY = (process.env.WC_KEY || process.env.WC_CONSUMER_KEY || process.env.WOOCOMMERCE_CONSUMER_KEY || '').trim(); const WC_SECRET = (process.env.WC_SECRET || process.env.WC_CONSUMER_SECRET || process.env.WOOCOMMERCE_CONSUMER_SECRET || '').trim(); const WC_EXTRA_HEADER = (process.env.WC_EXTRA_HEADER || '').trim(); // This is the User-Agent DCS Digital's Cloudflare rule looks for. // It is the default, so the env vars no longer have to carry it. // Override with WC_USER_AGENT on Render only if Scott changes the rule. const WC_USER_AGENT = (process.env.WC_USER_AGENT || 'X-Hallan-Sync').trim(); // WC_SOURCE = 'oracle' (default): website status is read from the WOO_PRODUCTS table that the PowerShell // sync (Hallan-WooSync.ps1) keeps up to date, so this server never calls the website (no Cloudflare 403s). // WC_SOURCE = 'direct': old behaviour, call the WooCommerce REST API from here (needs WC_URL/WC_KEY/WC_SECRET). const WC_SOURCE = String(process.env.WC_SOURCE || 'oracle').trim().toLowerCase() === 'direct' ? 'direct' : 'oracle'; const WOO_STALE_MIN = Math.max(5, Number(process.env.WOO_STALE_MINUTES) || 30); const WC_CACHE_MS = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.WC_CACHE_MINUTES) || (WC_SOURCE === 'oracle' ? 1 : 10)) * 60 * 1000; const WC_PAGE_CONCURRENCY = 2; // pages fetched at once (gentle on Cloudflare / the host) const WC_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 4; // tries per page before giving up const wcCache = { fetchedAt: 0, syncedAt: 0, map: null, total: 0, error: null, inflight: null }; function wcConfigured() { return WC_SOURCE === 'oracle' ? true : !!(WC_SITE_URL && WC_KEY && WC_SECRET); } function wcNormKey(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ''); } function wcAltKey(k) { return k.replace(/^0+/, ''); } function wcBuildHeaders() { const auth = Buffer.from(WC_KEY + ':' + WC_SECRET).toString('base64'); const headers = { Authorization: 'Basic ' + auth, Accept: 'application/json', 'User-Agent': WC_USER_AGENT }; // Optional extra header, format Header-Name:value // (If it is User-Agent:something it overrides the default above.) if (WC_EXTRA_HEADER.includes(':')) { const i = WC_EXTRA_HEADER.indexOf(':'); const name = WC_EXTRA_HEADER.slice(0, i).trim(); const value = WC_EXTRA_HEADER.slice(i + 1).trim(); if (name && value) headers[name] = value; } return headers; } async function wcFetchPage(page, attempt = 1) { const url = WC_SITE_URL + '/wp-json/wc/v3/products' + '?per_page=100&page=' + page + '&status=any' + '&_fields=id,name,sku,status,permalink,global_unique_id'; const headers = wcBuildHeaders(); let r; try { r = await fetch(url, { headers, signal: AbortSignal.timeout(45000) }); } catch (netErr) { // timeout / connection reset: retry if (attempt < WC_MAX_ATTEMPTS) { await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, attempt * 1500)); return wcFetchPage(page, attempt + 1); } throw new Error('WooCommerce page ' + page + ' network error: ' + netErr.message); } if (!r.ok) { // Transient block, rate limit or server error: wait and retry if ([403, 429, 500, 502, 503, 504].includes(r.status) && attempt < WC_MAX_ATTEMPTS) { await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, attempt * 1500)); return wcFetchPage(page, attempt + 1); } const t = await r.text().catch(() => ''); const cfMitigated = r.headers.get('cf-mitigated') || 'none'; const looksLikeCloudflare = cfMitigated !== 'none' || /just a moment|cf-chl|attention required/i.test(t); throw new Error( 'WooCommerce API ' + r.status + ' on page ' + page + (looksLikeCloudflare ? ' (Cloudflare challenge)' : '') + ' | UA sent: ' + headers['User-Agent'] + ' | cf-mitigated: ' + cfMitigated + ' | cf-ray: ' + (r.headers.get('cf-ray') || 'none') + ' | ' + t.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 150) ); } const totalPages = Number(r.headers.get('x-wp-totalpages')) || 1; const total = Number(r.headers.get('x-wp-total')) || 0; const rows = await r.json(); return { rows: Array.isArray(rows) ? rows : [], totalPages, total }; } // Reads the website copy that Hallan-WooSync.ps1 keeps in Oracle (WOO_PRODUCTS). async function wcLoadFromOracle() { const rows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT WOO_ID, SKU, GTIN, NAME, STATUS, PERMALINK FROM WOO_PRODUCTS`, [], { maxRows: 0 }))).rows); const state = {}; lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(`SELECT STATE_KEY, STATE_VALUE FROM WOO_SYNC_STATE`))).rows) .forEach(r => { state[r.state_key] = r.state_value; }); if (!rows.length) throw new Error('No website products in Oracle yet - the PowerShell sync (Hallan-WooSync.ps1) has not loaded any. ' + (state.LAST_PULL_ERROR ? 'Last sync error: ' + state.LAST_PULL_ERROR : '')); const map = new Map(); const put = (key, p) => { if (!key) return; const entry = { id: Number(p.woo_id), status: p.status || 'unknown', permalink: p.permalink || '', name: p.name || '' }; if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, entry); const alt = wcAltKey(key); if (alt && !map.has(alt)) map.set(alt, entry); }; rows.forEach(p => { put(wcNormKey(p.sku), p); put(wcNormKey(p.gtin), p); }); const syncedAt = state.LAST_PULL_OK_AT ? Date.parse(state.LAST_PULL_OK_AT) || 0 : 0; let warning = null; if (state.LAST_PULL_ERROR) warning = 'The last website sync failed: ' + state.LAST_PULL_ERROR; else if (!syncedAt || (Date.now() - syncedAt) > WOO_STALE_MIN * 60 * 1000) { warning = 'The website sync has not run since ' + (syncedAt ? new Date(syncedAt).toISOString() : 'ever') + ' - is the PowerShell scheduled task running?'; } return { map, total: rows.length, syncedAt, warning }; } async function wcLoadAllProducts() { if (WC_SOURCE === 'oracle') return wcLoadFromOracle(); const first = await wcFetchPage(1); let all = first.rows; const pages = []; for (let p = 2; p <= first.totalPages; p++) pages.push(p); for (let i = 0; i < pages.length; i += WC_PAGE_CONCURRENCY) { const batch = await Promise.all(pages.slice(i, i + WC_PAGE_CONCURRENCY).map(p => wcFetchPage(p))); batch.forEach(b => { all = all.concat(b.rows); }); await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, 150)); // short breather between batches } const map = new Map(); const put = (key, p) => { if (!key) return; const entry = { id: p.id, status: p.status || 'unknown', permalink: p.permalink || '', name: p.name || '' }; if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, entry); const alt = wcAltKey(key); if (alt && !map.has(alt)) map.set(alt, entry); }; all.forEach(p => { put(wcNormKey(p.sku), p); put(wcNormKey(p.global_unique_id), p); }); return { map, total: all.length }; } // Returns { map, configured, fetchedAt, total, error }. Never throws: if the // website can't be reached the (possibly stale) cache is returned with the // error alongside, so the page keeps working. async function wcGetProducts(force) { if (!wcConfigured()) return { map: null, configured: false, fetchedAt: 0, total: 0, error: null }; const fresh = wcCache.map && (Date.now() - wcCache.fetchedAt) < WC_CACHE_MS; if (fresh && !force) { return { map: wcCache.map, configured: true, fetchedAt: wcCache.syncedAt || wcCache.fetchedAt, total: wcCache.total, error: wcCache.error }; } if (!wcCache.inflight) { wcCache.inflight = wcLoadAllProducts() .then(r => { wcCache.map = r.map; wcCache.total = r.total; wcCache.fetchedAt = Date.now(); wcCache.syncedAt = r.syncedAt || 0; wcCache.error = r.warning || null; }) .catch(e => { wcCache.error = e.message; console.error('[woocommerce] fetch failed:', e.message); }) .finally(() => { wcCache.inflight = null; }); } if (wcCache.map && !force) { // stale copy: answer now, refresh in the background return { map: wcCache.map, configured: true, fetchedAt: wcCache.syncedAt || wcCache.fetchedAt, total: wcCache.total, error: wcCache.error }; } await wcCache.inflight; return { map: wcCache.map, configured: true, fetchedAt: wcCache.syncedAt || wcCache.fetchedAt, total: wcCache.total, error: wcCache.error }; } // Works out the website status for one of our products. function wcStatusFor(map, candidates) { if (!map) return { web_status: 'unknown', web_id: null, web_url: '' }; for (const c of candidates) { const k = wcNormKey(c); if (!k) continue; const hit = map.get(k) || map.get(wcAltKey(k)); if (hit) return { web_status: hit.status, web_id: hit.id, web_url: hit.permalink }; } return { web_status: 'not_found', web_id: null, web_url: '' }; } // ---- WordPress admin "Edit product" link -------------------------------------------------- // In WooCommerce a product IS a WordPress post (post type "product"), so the product id the REST API // returns (WOO_PRODUCTS.WOO_ID, saved by Hallan-WooSync.ps1) is exactly the number in // https://hallans.co.uk/wp-admin/post.php?post=47794&action=edit (47794 = the product's id) // Site address: WP_ADMIN_URL env var if set, otherwise taken from the product's own permalink // (so it always matches the live site, www or not), otherwise WC_URL. const WP_ADMIN_URL = (process.env.WP_ADMIN_URL || '').trim().replace(/\/+$/, '').replace(/\/wp-admin$/i, ''); function wpEditUrl(wooId, permalink) { const id = Number(wooId); if (!Number.isFinite(id) || id <= 0) return ''; let base = WP_ADMIN_URL; if (!base && permalink) { try { base = new URL(permalink).origin; } catch (e) { base = ''; } } if (!base) base = WC_SITE_URL; return base ? (base + '/wp-admin/post.php?post=' + Math.trunc(id) + '&action=edit') : ''; } // stock id -> the website product it was pushed to (WOO_SYNC), with that product's current status. async function wooLinkMap() { const m = new Map(); try { const rows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.STOCKID, l.WOO_PRODUCT_ID, w.STATUS, w.PERMALINK FROM WOO_SYNC l JOIN WOO_PRODUCTS w ON w.WOO_ID = l.WOO_PRODUCT_ID`, [], { maxRows: 0 }))).rows); rows.forEach(r => m.set(Number(r.stockid), { id: Number(r.woo_product_id), status: r.status || 'unknown', permalink: r.permalink || '' })); } catch (e) { console.error('[woo] link map failed:', e.message); } return m; } // stock id -> state of its latest website send, only while it is waiting / running / failed. async function wooQueueMap() { const m = new Map(); try { const rows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT q.STOCKID, q.STATUS, q.MESSAGE FROM WOO_QUEUE q WHERE q.ID = (SELECT MAX(q2.ID) FROM WOO_QUEUE q2 WHERE q2.STOCKID = q.STOCKID AND q2.STATUS <> 'SUPERSEDED') AND q.STATUS IN ('PENDING', 'PROCESSING', 'ERROR')`, [], { maxRows: 0 }))).rows); rows.forEach(r => m.set(Number(r.stockid), { status: r.status, message: r.message || '' })); } catch (e) { console.error('[woo] queue map failed:', e.message); } return m; } // Which website product is this stock item? Same order Hallan-WooSync.ps1 uses: // 1) the product we pushed it to (WOO_SYNC) 2) website SKU = stock id 3) website GTIN = one of its barcodes. // Catalogue / supplier numbers are deliberately NOT used: they could hit a different product. function wooResolve(stockid, barcodes, linkMap, wcMap) { const link = linkMap && linkMap.get(Number(stockid)); if (link) return { web_status: link.status, web_id: link.id, web_url: link.permalink, web_edit_url: wpEditUrl(link.id, link.permalink) }; if (!wcMap) return { web_status: 'unknown', web_id: null, web_url: '', web_edit_url: '' }; const w = wcStatusFor(wcMap, [String(stockid), ...(barcodes || [])]); return { web_status: w.web_status, web_id: w.web_id, web_url: w.web_url, web_edit_url: w.web_id ? wpEditUrl(w.web_id, w.web_url) : '' }; } // ---- Quick check that the WooCommerce connection works (handy when setting up) app.get('/api/stock/online-data/woo-status', async (req, res) => { try { const w = await wcGetProducts(req.query.refresh === '1'); res.json({ configured: w.configured, ok: w.configured && !w.error && !!w.map, source: WC_SOURCE, products_on_website: w.total, fetched_at: w.fetchedAt ? new Date(w.fetchedAt).toISOString() : null, error: w.error }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Counts for one supplier: Preferred / Non-Preferred x Incomplete / Complete ---- app.get('/api/stock/online-data/summary', async (req, res) => { const suppId = Number(req.query.suppid); if (!Number.isFinite(suppId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'suppid is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT CASE WHEN NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS PREF, CASE WHEN NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS DONE, COUNT(*) AS CNT FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS c ON c.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId GROUP BY CASE WHEN NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END, CASE WHEN NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) = 1 THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`, { suppId } )); const out = { preferred: { incomplete: 0, complete: 0 }, non_preferred: { incomplete: 0, complete: 0 } }; lower(result.rows).forEach(r => { const group = r.pref === 1 ? out.preferred : out.non_preferred; group[r.done === 1 ? 'complete' : 'incomplete'] = Number(r.cnt) || 0; }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- The product list itself. // Query params: // suppid - supplier // preferred - 'yes' | 'no' // status - 'incomplete' | 'complete' ---- app.get('/api/stock/online-data/items', async (req, res) => { const suppId = Number(req.query.suppid); const preferred = (req.query.preferred || '').trim().toLowerCase(); const status = (req.query.status || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!Number.isFinite(suppId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'suppid is required' }); if (preferred !== 'yes' && preferred !== 'no') return res.status(400).json({ error: "preferred must be 'yes' or 'no'" }); if (status !== 'incomplete' && status !== 'complete') return res.status(400).json({ error: "status must be 'incomplete' or 'complete'" }); try { const preferredClause = preferred === 'yes' ? 'AND ss.PREFERRED = 1' : 'AND NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) != 1'; const statusClause = status === 'complete' ? 'AND NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) = 1' : 'AND NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) = 0'; const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, (SELECT LISTAGG(b.BARCODE, ', ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY b.BARCODE) FROM VEBARCODES b WHERE b.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) AS BARCODES, NVL(c.IMAGES_DONE, 0) AS IMAGES_DONE, NVL(c.DESCRIPTION_DONE, 0) AS DESCRIPTION_DONE, NVL(c.OTHER_DONE, 0) AS OTHER_DONE, NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) AS IS_COMPLETE, ss.CATNO, ss.STOCKNO FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS c ON c.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId ${preferredClause} ${statusClause} ORDER BY s.NAME`, { suppId } )); // Website (WooCommerce) status — cached, so this is instant after the first load. // ?refresh=1 forces a fresh pull from the website. const web = await wcGetProducts(req.query.refresh === '1'); const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => { const barcodeList = (r.barcodes || '').split(',').map(b => b.trim()).filter(Boolean); const w = wcStatusFor(web.map, [String(r.stockid), ...barcodeList, r.catno, r.stockno]); // the website SKU is the stock id return { stockid: r.stockid, name: r.name, barcodes: r.barcodes || '', images_done: r.images_done === 1, description_done: r.description_done === 1, other_done: r.other_done === 1, is_complete: r.is_complete === 1, web_status: w.web_status, // publish | draft | pending | private | future | not_found | unknown web_id: w.web_id, web_url: w.web_url }; }); res.json({ items, web: { configured: web.configured, ok: web.configured && !web.error && !!web.map, error: web.error, total: web.total, fetched_at: web.fetchedAt ? new Date(web.fetchedAt).toISOString() : null } }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Save ticks for one product. Send any of images_done / // description_done / other_done (booleans); anything left out keeps its // current value. When all three end up ticked the product becomes // complete, i.e. moves from the Incomplete List to the Complete List. ---- app.put('/api/stock/online-data/items/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const stockid = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(stockid)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid stock id' }); const body = req.body || {}; const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const saved = await withConnection(async conn => { const cur = lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGES_DONE, DESCRIPTION_DONE, OTHER_DONE FROM ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { id: stockid } )).rows)[0] || {}; const pick = (key, current) => (body[key] === undefined ? (current === 1 ? 1 : 0) : (body[key] ? 1 : 0)); const images = pick('images_done', cur.images_done); const description = pick('description_done', cur.description_done); const other = pick('other_done', cur.other_done); const complete = (images && description && other) ? 1 : 0; await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS t USING (SELECT :id AS STOCKID FROM DUAL) src ON (t.STOCKID = src.STOCKID) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t.IMAGES_DONE = :img, t.DESCRIPTION_DONE = :descr, t.OTHER_DONE = :oth, t.IS_COMPLETE = :comp, t.COMPLETED_AT = CASE WHEN :comp2 = 1 THEN SYSTIMESTAMP ELSE NULL END, t.UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, t.UPDATED_BY = :updby WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (STOCKID, IMAGES_DONE, DESCRIPTION_DONE, OTHER_DONE, IS_COMPLETE, COMPLETED_AT, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (:id2, :img2, :descr2, :oth2, :comp3, CASE WHEN :comp4 = 1 THEN SYSTIMESTAMP ELSE NULL END, SYSTIMESTAMP, :updby2)`, { id: stockid, img: images, descr: description, oth: other, comp: complete, comp2: complete, updby: by, id2: stockid, img2: images, descr2: description, oth2: other, comp3: complete, comp4: complete, updby2: by }, { autoCommit: true } ); return { stockid, images_done: !!images, description_done: !!description, other_done: !!other, is_complete: !!complete }; }); res.json({ item: saved }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Send a completed product back to the Incomplete List. Clears all // three ticks so it genuinely needs going through again (otherwise it // would instantly count as complete again). ---- app.post('/api/stock/online-data/items/:stockid/reopen', async (req, res) => { const stockid = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(stockid)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid stock id' }); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS SET IMAGES_DONE = 0, DESCRIPTION_DONE = 0, OTHER_DONE = 0, IS_COMPLETE = 0, COMPLETED_AT = NULL, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY = :updby WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { id: stockid, updby: by }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ item: { stockid, images_done: false, description_done: false, other_done: false, is_complete: false } }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ==================================================================== // WOOCOMMERCE <-> ORACLE SYNC (the PowerShell side is Hallan-WooSync.ps1) // // Portal -> website: Generate / Save / choosing a picture adds a row to WOO_QUEUE. // Hallan-WooSync.ps1 (Task Scheduler, every 2 min) reads the queue, loads the // product + SHOPIFY_CONTENT from Oracle and creates/updates the WooCommerce product. // Website -> portal: the same script copies every WooCommerce product into WOO_PRODUCTS; the Online Data // "Website status" column reads that table (WC_SOURCE=oracle, the default). // // Tables (created on boot): // WOO_PRODUCTS copy of the website catalogue (id, sku, status, link, price, stock, image count ...) // WOO_SYNC stock id <-> WooCommerce product id, last push result, uploaded picture id/hash // WOO_QUEUE outbox: PENDING -> PROCESSING -> DONE / ERROR (PUSH_MODE FILL = only fill blanks on // existing products, FULL = replace our fields with the portal's content) // WOO_SYNC_STATE heartbeat + last-pull markers written by the script // // Optional env vars: WOO_AUTO_QUEUE (default 1) WOO_GENERATE_MODE (FULL|FILL, default FULL) // WOO_STALE_MINUTES (default 30) WC_SOURCE (oracle|direct) // SH_IMAGE_SIZE (default 1200) SH_IMAGE_FILL (default 0.85) // ==================================================================== const WOO_AUTO_QUEUE = !['0', 'false', 'no', 'off'].includes(String(process.env.WOO_AUTO_QUEUE || '1').trim().toLowerCase()); const WOO_QUEUE_MAX_PER_REQUEST = 50; // What Generate sends to the website. FULL (default) = the portal's text/picture replaces the website's, so the // website matches what you checked here. Set WOO_GENERATE_MODE=FILL to only fill blanks on products already live. const WOO_GENERATE_MODE = String(process.env.WOO_GENERATE_MODE || 'FULL').trim().toUpperCase() === 'FILL' ? 'FILL' : 'FULL'; async function ensureWooTables() { const ddls = [ `CREATE TABLE WOO_PRODUCTS ( WOO_ID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, SKU VARCHAR2(200), GTIN VARCHAR2(100), NAME VARCHAR2(500), STATUS VARCHAR2(30), PRODUCT_TYPE VARCHAR2(30), PERMALINK VARCHAR2(1000), STOCK_STATUS VARCHAR2(30), MANAGE_STOCK NUMBER(1), STOCK_QTY NUMBER, PRICE NUMBER(12,2), REGULAR_PRICE NUMBER(12,2), SALE_PRICE NUMBER(12,2), IMAGE_COUNT NUMBER, IMAGE_URL VARCHAR2(1000), HAS_DESCRIPTION NUMBER(1), HAS_SHORT_DESC NUMBER(1), CATEGORIES VARCHAR2(1000), DATE_MODIFIED_GMT TIMESTAMP, SEEN_RUN NUMBER, SYNCED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP)`, `CREATE INDEX WOO_PRODUCTS_SKU_IX ON WOO_PRODUCTS (SKU)`, `CREATE INDEX WOO_PRODUCTS_GTIN_IX ON WOO_PRODUCTS (GTIN)`, `CREATE TABLE WOO_SYNC ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, WOO_PRODUCT_ID NUMBER, SKU VARCHAR2(200), IMAGE_HASH VARCHAR2(64), WOO_MEDIA_ID NUMBER, LAST_PUSHED_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_ACTION VARCHAR2(30), LAST_ERROR VARCHAR2(2000))`, `CREATE TABLE WOO_QUEUE ( ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, STOCKID NUMBER NOT NULL, PUSH_MODE VARCHAR2(10) DEFAULT 'FILL' NOT NULL, STATUS VARCHAR2(12) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, ATTEMPTS NUMBER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, REASON VARCHAR2(40), REQUESTED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, REQUESTED_BY VARCHAR2(200), STARTED_AT TIMESTAMP, FINISHED_AT TIMESTAMP, RESULT_ACTION VARCHAR2(30), WOO_PRODUCT_ID NUMBER, MESSAGE VARCHAR2(2000))`, `CREATE INDEX WOO_QUEUE_ST_IX ON WOO_QUEUE (STATUS, STOCKID)`, `CREATE TABLE WOO_SYNC_STATE ( STATE_KEY VARCHAR2(60) PRIMARY KEY, STATE_VALUE VARCHAR2(500), UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP)`, // Website pictures -> portal photos (front / back). One row per product: PENDING = the "Pull from website" // button was pressed; DONE / NONE / ERROR = what Hallan-WooSync.ps1 did the last time it looked at the product. `CREATE TABLE WOO_PHOTO_IMPORT ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, STATUS VARCHAR2(12) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, REQUESTED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, REQUESTED_BY VARCHAR2(200), FINISHED_AT TIMESTAMP, IMPORTED NUMBER DEFAULT 0, MESSAGE VARCHAR2(500))` ]; for (const sql of ddls) { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(sql, [], { autoCommit: true })); console.log('[woo] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955 && e.errorNum !== 1408) console.error('[woo] schema error:', e.message); // 955/1408 = already there } } } // Adds products to the outbox. One PENDING row per product: a second request upgrades FILL -> FULL, never duplicates. async function wooQueueProducts(stockids, by, mode, reason) { mode = mode === 'FULL' ? 'FULL' : 'FILL'; const out = { queued: 0, upgraded: 0, already: 0 }; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const id of stockids) { const ex = lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT ID, PUSH_MODE FROM WOO_QUEUE WHERE STOCKID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id })).rows); if (ex.length) { if (mode === 'FULL' && ex.some(r => r.push_mode !== 'FULL')) { await conn.execute(`UPDATE WOO_QUEUE SET PUSH_MODE = 'FULL' WHERE STOCKID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id }, { autoCommit: true }); out.upgraded++; } else out.already++; continue; } // a failed earlier attempt is replaced by this fresh request await conn.execute(`UPDATE WOO_QUEUE SET STATUS = 'SUPERSEDED', FINISHED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE STOCKID = :id AND STATUS = 'ERROR'`, { id }, { autoCommit: true }); await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WOO_QUEUE (STOCKID, PUSH_MODE, STATUS, REASON, REQUESTED_BY) VALUES (:id, :pm, 'PENDING', :why, :byu)`, { id, pm: mode, why: reason || null, byu: by || null }, { autoCommit: true }); out.queued++; } }); return out; } // Never throws: a problem with the website queue must not break Generate / Save. async function wooAutoQueue(stockid, by, mode, reason) { if (!WOO_AUTO_QUEUE) return false; try { const has = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT 1 AS X FROM SHOPIFY_CONTENT WHERE STOCKID = :id AND DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(DESCRIPTION) > 0`, { id: stockid }))).rows); if (!has.length) return false; await wooQueueProducts([stockid], by, mode, reason); return true; } catch (e) { console.error('[woo] could not queue stock ' + stockid + ' for the website:', e.message); return false; } } async function wooSyncState() { const st = {}; lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(`SELECT STATE_KEY, STATE_VALUE FROM WOO_SYNC_STATE`))).rows) .forEach(r => { st[r.state_key] = r.state_value; }); const ts = st.LAST_RUN_AT ? Date.parse(st.LAST_RUN_AT) || 0 : 0; return { last_run_at: ts ? new Date(ts).toISOString() : null, last_run_ok: st.LAST_RUN_OK === '1', last_run_message: st.LAST_RUN_MSG || '', last_pull_at: st.LAST_PULL_OK_AT || null, last_pull_error: st.LAST_PULL_ERROR || '', stale: !ts || (Date.now() - ts) > WOO_STALE_MIN * 60 * 1000 }; } // Send products to the website queue now. Body: { stockids:[..], mode?:'FILL'|'FULL' (default FULL) } app.post('/api/stock/woo/queue', async (req, res) => { const ids = [...new Set(((req.body && req.body.stockids) || []).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite))]; if (!ids.length) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'stockids is required' }); if (ids.length > WOO_QUEUE_MAX_PER_REQUEST) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Queue at most ' + WOO_QUEUE_MAX_PER_REQUEST + ' products per request' }); const mode = (req.body && req.body.mode === 'FILL') ? 'FILL' : 'FULL'; const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const binds = {}; const inList = ids.map((id, i) => { binds['i' + i] = id; return ':i' + i; }).join(','); const ready = new Set(lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID FROM SHOPIFY_CONTENT WHERE STOCKID IN (${inList}) AND DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(DESCRIPTION) > 0`, binds))).rows).map(r => Number(r.stockid))); const todo = ids.filter(id => ready.has(id)); const out = await wooQueueProducts(todo, by, mode, 'manual'); res.json({ ok: true, ...out, skipped_no_content: ids.filter(id => !ready.has(id)) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Everything the portal needs to show "what happened on the website" for one product app.get('/api/stock/woo/product/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); try { const STOCKID = :id ORDER BY ID DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { id })).rows)[0] || null; const link = lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT l.WOO_PRODUCT_ID, l.LAST_PUSHED_AT, l.LAST_ACTION, l.LAST_ERROR, w.STATUS AS WOO_STATUS, w.PERMALINK FROM WOO_SYNC l LEFT JOIN WOO_PRODUCTS w ON w.WOO_ID = l.WOO_PRODUCT_ID WHERE l.STOCKID = :id`, { id })).rows)[0] || null; const stock = lower((await Number(data.link.woo_product_id) }; } else if (data.stock) { const w = await wcGetProducts(false); const barcodes = String(data.stock.barcodes || '').split(',').map(b => b.trim()).filter(Boolean); }); } }); // Health of the whole website sync: queue counts, recent failures, heartbeat of the PowerShell task app.get('/api/stock/woo/queue/status', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await counts[r.status] = Number(r.n); }); const errors = lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT q.STOCKID, s.NAME, q.ATTEMPTS, q.FINISHED_AT, q.MESSAGE FROM WOO_QUEUE q LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = q.STOCKID WHERE q.STATUS = 'ERROR' ORDER BY q.ID DESC FETCH FIRST 20 ROWS ONLY`)).rows); const mirror = lower((await conn.execute(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS N FROM WOO_PRODUCTS`)).rows)[0]; return { counts, errors, products_on_website: Number(mirror.n) }; }); res.json({ ...out, sync: await wooSyncState() }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ==================================================================== // SHOPIFY (Stock Info > Shopify) // Sends product details to the Shopify store through the Admin GraphQL API // and shows each product's Shopify status (Active / Draft / Archived / // Not on Shopify) — the same idea as the WooCommerce status above, but // this one can also WRITE: it creates or updates products. // // Render environment variables (Environment tab of THIS backend service): // SHOPIFY_STORE yourshop.myshopify.com (the myshopify.com address, NOT the public domain) // // -- Authentication: use ONE of these two options -- // SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID Client ID } Dev Dashboard app (recommended, new apps) // SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET Client secret } the server fetches + refreshes the 24h token itself // SHOPIFY_ACCESS_TOKEN shpat_xxx (only if you already have a permanent token, // e.g. a legacy custom app made before 1 Jan 2026) // // -- Optional -- // SHOPIFY_API_VERSION default 2026-04 // SHOPIFY_NEW_PRODUCT_STATUS DRAFT (default) | ACTIVE | ARCHIVED — status given to NEW products // SHOPIFY_PRICE_MODE gross (default: adds VAT to VAT-rated items) | net (send price as stored) // SHOPIFY_VAT_RATE default 0.2 // SHOPIFY_VENDOR vendor name to put on new products (blank = Shopify's default) // SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK 1 = also push stock quantity (needs SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID + write_inventory scope) // SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID numeric location id (Shopify admin > Settings > Locations > click the location > number in the URL) // SHOPIFY_CACHE_MINUTES default 10 — how long the Shopify product list is cached // SHOPIFY_STOCK_SYNC_MINUTES default 5 — how often changed stock is pushed to Shopify in the // background (only runs when SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK=1 and SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID is set). // 0 = background sync off (stock then only goes out when a product is pushed) // SHOPIFY_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_HOURS default 6 — every this many hours ALL linked products are re-sent, not just // the changed ones, to correct any drift (0 = never) // // CONTINUOUS STOCK SYNC: every SHOPIFY_STOCK_SYNC_MINUTES the server compares each linked product's EPOS // quantity (VESTOCK.QUANTITY) with the quantity last sent (SHOPIFY_SYNC.LAST_QTY) and sends only the // differences to Shopify. Products not yet sent to Shopify (no row in SHOPIFY_SYNC) are not touched. // Status + manual trigger: GET /api/stock/shopify/stock-sync/status, POST /api/stock/shopify/stock-sync/run (admin). // // What gets sent: title (new products only), SKU, barcode, price, product type // (= our department), optionally stock quantity. On an UPDATE only price, // barcode, SKU (and stock if enabled) are changed — titles, descriptions, images // and anything else edited in Shopify are never overwritten. // // SKU used = the product's longest barcode, else stock no., else catalogue no. // Every product we send is remembered in SHOPIFY_SYNC (our stock id <-> Shopify // product/variant id), so the same product is never created twice. // // Table (created automatically on boot by ensureShopifySyncTable): // CREATE TABLE SHOPIFY_SYNC ( // STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, SHOPIFY_PRODUCT_ID VARCHAR2(100), SHOPIFY_VARIANT_ID VARCHAR2(100), // SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(100), SKU VARCHAR2(200), // LAST_PUSHED_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_PUSHED_BY VARCHAR2(200), LAST_ACTION VARCHAR2(20) // ); // ==================================================================== const SHOPIFY_STORE = (() => { let s = (process.env.SHOPIFY_STORE || process.env.SHOPIFY_SHOP || '').trim() .replace(/^https?:\/\//i, '').replace(/\/.*$/, '').toLowerCase(); if (s && !s.includes('.')) s += '.myshopify.com'; return s; })(); const SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID = (process.env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID || '').trim(); const SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET = (process.env.SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET || '').trim(); const SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN = (process.env.SHOPIFY_ACCESS_TOKEN || '').trim(); const SHOPIFY_API_VERSION = (process.env.SHOPIFY_API_VERSION || '2026-04').trim(); const SHOPIFY_NEW_STATUS = (() => { const v = String(process.env.SHOPIFY_NEW_PRODUCT_STATUS || 'DRAFT').trim().toUpperCase(); return ['ACTIVE', 'DRAFT', 'ARCHIVED'].includes(v) ? v : 'DRAFT'; })(); const SHOPIFY_PRICE_MODE = String(process.env.SHOPIFY_PRICE_MODE || 'gross').trim().toLowerCase() === 'net' ? 'net' : 'gross'; const SHOPIFY_VAT_RATE = (() => { const n = Number(process.env.SHOPIFY_VAT_RATE); return process.env.SHOPIFY_VAT_RATE && isFinite(n) && n >= 0 ? n : 0.2; })(); const SHOPIFY_VENDOR = (process.env.SHOPIFY_VENDOR || '').trim(); const SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK = ['1', 'true', 'yes'].includes(String(process.env.SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK || '').trim().toLowerCase()); const SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID = (() => { const v = (process.env.SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID || '').trim(); if (!v) return ''; return v.startsWith('gid://') ? v : 'gid://shopify/Location/' + v.replace(/\D/g, ''); })(); const SH_CACHE_MS = Math.max(1, Number(process.env.SHOPIFY_CACHE_MINUTES) || 10) * 60 * 1000; const SH_MAX_PUSH_PER_REQUEST = 25; const SH_STOCK_SYNC_MS = (() => { const raw = process.env.SHOPIFY_STOCK_SYNC_MINUTES; const n = (raw === undefined || raw === '') ? 5 : Number(raw); return isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? Math.max(1, n) * 60 * 1000 : 0; })(); const SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS = (() => { const raw = process.env.SHOPIFY_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_HOURS; const n = (raw === undefined || raw === '') ? 6 : Number(raw); return isFinite(n) && n > 0 ? n * 3600 * 1000 : 0; })(); const SH_STOCK_BATCH = 100; // inventorySetQuantities accepts up to 250 per call const shToken = { value: '', expiresAt: 0, inflight: null }; const shCache = { fetchedAt: 0, map: null, byVariant: null, total: 0, error: null, inflight: null }; const shSleep = ms => new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, ms)); function shConfigured() { return !!(SHOPIFY_STORE && (SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN || (SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID && SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET))); } function shNormKey(v) { return String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ''); } function shAltKey(k) { return k.replace(/^0+/, ''); } function shNumericId(gid) { return String(gid || '').split('/').pop(); } function shAdminUrl(productGid) { return 'https://admin.shopify.com/store/' + SHOPIFY_STORE.replace(/\.myshopify\.com$/, '') + '/products/' + shNumericId(productGid); } async function ensureShopifySyncTable() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE SHOPIFY_SYNC ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, SHOPIFY_PRODUCT_ID VARCHAR2(100), SHOPIFY_VARIANT_ID VARCHAR2(100), SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID VARCHAR2(100), SKU VARCHAR2(200), LAST_PUSHED_AT TIMESTAMP, LAST_PUSHED_BY VARCHAR2(200), LAST_ACTION VARCHAR2(20) )` ]; // Added for the continuous stock sync: the quantity last sent to Shopify, and when. const columns = [ `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_SYNC ADD (LAST_QTY NUMBER)`, `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_SYNC ADD (LAST_QTY_AT TIMESTAMP)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[shopify] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (made on an earlier boot) — expected. if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[shopify] schema error:', e.message); } } for (const sql of columns) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[shopify] applied:', sql.trim()); } catch (e) { // 1430 = column already exists (made on an earlier boot) — expected. if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[shopify] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // ---- Access token. Static token if you have one; otherwise the Dev Dashboard // "client credentials" grant: POST client id + secret, get a token that // lasts 24 hours. It is cached and renewed automatically (5 min early). ---- async function shGetToken(force) { if (SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN) return SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN; if (!force && shToken.value && Date.now() < shToken.expiresAt) return shToken.value; if (!shToken.inflight) { shToken.inflight = (async () => { const r = await fetch('https://' + SHOPIFY_STORE + '/admin/oauth/access_token', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/x-www-form-urlencoded', Accept: 'application/json' }, body: new URLSearchParams({ client_id: SHOPIFY_CLIENT_ID, client_secret: SHOPIFY_CLIENT_SECRET, grant_type: 'client_credentials' }).toString(), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(30000) }); const text = await r.text(); let j = {}; try { j = JSON.parse(text); } catch (_) { /* not JSON */ } if (!r.ok || !j.access_token) { throw new Error('Shopify token request failed (' + r.status + '): ' + (j.error_description || j.error || text.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 200))); } shToken.value = j.access_token; const ttl = Number(j.expires_in) || 86399; shToken.expiresAt = Date.now() + Math.max(60, ttl - 300) * 1000; })().finally(() => { shToken.inflight = null; }); } await shToken.inflight; return shToken.value; } // ---- One GraphQL call, with retries for throttling / transient errors / expired token ---- async function shGraphQL(query, variables, attempt = 1) { const token = await shGetToken(false); const url = 'https://' + SHOPIFY_STORE + '/admin/api/' + SHOPIFY_API_VERSION + '/graphql.json'; let r; try { r = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', Accept: 'application/json', 'X-Shopify-Access-Token': token }, body: JSON.stringify({ query, variables: variables || {} }), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(45000) }); } catch (netErr) { if (attempt < 4) { await shSleep(attempt * 1500); return shGraphQL(query, variables, attempt + 1); } throw new Error('Shopify network error: ' + netErr.message); } if (r.status === 401 && !SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN && attempt < 3) { await shGetToken(true); // token expired/revoked: get a fresh one and retry return shGraphQL(query, variables, attempt + 1); } if ([429, 500, 502, 503, 504].includes(r.status) && attempt < 4) { await shSleep(attempt * 2000); return shGraphQL(query, variables, attempt + 1); } const text = await r.text(); let j; try { j = JSON.parse(text); } catch (_) { throw new Error('Shopify returned a non-JSON reply (' + r.status + '): ' + text.replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, 200)); } if (!r.ok) throw new Error('Shopify API ' + r.status + ': ' + JSON.stringify(j.errors || j).slice(0, 300)); if (j.errors && j.errors.length) { const throttled = j.errors.some(e => e.extensions && e.extensions.code === 'THROTTLED'); if (throttled && attempt < 6) { await shSleep(attempt * 1500); return shGraphQL(query, variables, attempt + 1); } throw new Error('Shopify: ' + j.errors.map(e => e.message).join('; ')); } return j.data; } function shUserErrors(errs) { return (errs || []).map(e => (e.field && e.field.length ? e.field.join('.') + ': ' : '') + e.message).join('; '); } // ---- Reads the whole Shopify catalogue (variants + their product's status) into a lookup // keyed by SKU and barcode. Cached; never throws — returns the error alongside. ---- const SH_VARIANTS_QUERY = `query($first:Int!,$after:String){ productVariants(first:$first, after:$after){ pageInfo{ hasNextPage endCursor } nodes{ id sku barcode product{ id status handle onlineStoreUrl } } } }`; function shEntryFromNode(n) { const p = n.product || {}; return { productId: p.id, variantId: n.id, status: String(p.status || 'unknown').toLowerCase(), url: p.onlineStoreUrl || (p.id ? shAdminUrl(p.id) : ''), adminUrl: p.id ? shAdminUrl(p.id) : '' }; } async function shLoadAllVariants() { const map = new Map(); const byVariant = new Map(); let after = null, total = 0; for (let page = 0; page < 400; page++) { const data = await shGraphQL(SH_VARIANTS_QUERY, { first: 250, after }); const conn = data.productVariants; (conn.nodes || []).forEach(n => { total++; const entry = shEntryFromNode(n); byVariant.set(n.id, entry); [n.sku, n.barcode].forEach(raw => { const key = shNormKey(raw); if (!key) return; if (!map.has(key)) map.set(key, entry); const alt = shAltKey(key); if (alt && !map.has(alt)) map.set(alt, entry); }); }); if (!conn.pageInfo.hasNextPage) break; after = conn.pageInfo.endCursor; } return { map, byVariant, total }; } async function shGetCatalogue(force) { if (!shConfigured()) return { configured: false, map: null, byVariant: null, fetchedAt: 0, total: 0, error: null }; const fresh = shCache.map && (Date.now() - shCache.fetchedAt) < SH_CACHE_MS; if (fresh && !force) { return { configured: true, map: shCache.map, byVariant: shCache.byVariant, fetchedAt: shCache.fetchedAt, total: shCache.total, error: null }; } if (!shCache.inflight) { shCache.inflight = shLoadAllVariants() .then(r => { shCache.map = r.map; shCache.byVariant = r.byVariant; shCache.total = r.total; shCache.fetchedAt = Date.now(); shCache.error = null; }) .catch(e => { shCache.error = e.message; console.error('[shopify] catalogue fetch failed:', e.message); }) .finally(() => { shCache.inflight = null; }); } // Out of date but we have a copy: answer straight away with it and refresh in the background, so reloading the page // never waits for the whole Shopify catalogue to be downloaded again (only the very first load after a restart does). if (shCache.map && !force) { return { configured: true, map: shCache.map, byVariant: shCache.byVariant, fetchedAt: shCache.fetchedAt, total: shCache.total, error: shCache.error }; } await shCache.inflight; return { configured: true, map: shCache.map, byVariant: shCache.byVariant, fetchedAt: shCache.fetchedAt, total: shCache.total, error: shCache.error }; } // Keep the cache correct straight after a push, without re-reading the whole shop. function shCachePut(skuKeys, entry) { if (!shCache.map) return; skuKeys.forEach(raw => { const key = shNormKey(raw); if (!key) return; shCache.map.set(key, entry); const alt = shAltKey(key); if (alt) shCache.map.set(alt, entry); }); if (shCache.byVariant) shCache.byVariant.set(entry.variantId, entry); } // ---- Product row from our database, with everything we send to Shopify ---- const SH_STOCK_SELECT = ` SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.QUANTITY, s.VATID, d.NAME AS DEPARTMENT, (SELECT LISTAGG(b.BARCODE, ',') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY b.BARCODE) FROM VEBARCODES b WHERE b.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) AS BARCODES, (SELECT MAX(ss.CATNO) FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss WHERE ss.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) AS CATNO, (SELECT MAX(ss.STOCKNO) FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss WHERE ss.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) AS STOCKNO, NVL(NULLIF(p3.PRICE_NET, 0), p1.PRICE_NET) AS PRICE_NET, NVL(c.IS_COMPLETE, 0) AS ONLINE_COMPLETE, NVL(ct.COMPLETED, 0) AS SH_COMPLETED, CASE WHEN NVL(DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(ct.DESCRIPTION), 0) > 0 AND NVL(DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(ct.IMAGE_DATA), 0) > 0 AND ct.IMAGE_SOURCE IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS CONTENT_READY, NVL(ct.NEEDS_REVIEW, 0) AS CONTENT_REVIEW, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS ph WHERE ph.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) AS PHOTO_COUNT, l.SHOPIFY_PRODUCT_ID, l.SHOPIFY_VARIANT_ID, l.SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID, l.LAST_PUSHED_AT FROM VESTOCK s LEFT JOIN VEDEPTS d ON d.DEPTID = s.DEPTID LEFT JOIN STOCK_PRICES p3 ON p3.STOCKID = s.STOCKID AND p3.PRICEID = 3 LEFT JOIN STOCK_PRICES p1 ON p1.STOCKID = s.STOCKID AND p1.PRICEID = 1 LEFT JOIN ONLINE_DATA_CHECKS c ON c.STOCKID = s.STOCKID LEFT JOIN SHOPIFY_CONTENT ct ON ct.STOCKID = s.STOCKID LEFT JOIN SHOPIFY_SYNC l ON l.STOCKID = s.STOCKID`; function shBuildPayload(r) { const barcodes = String(r.barcodes || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean) .sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length); const barcode = barcodes[0] || ''; const stockno = String(r.stockno || '').trim(); const catno = String(r.catno || '').trim(); const sku = barcode || stockno || catno; let price = (r.price_net === null || r.price_net === undefined) ? null : Number(r.price_net); if (price !== null && isFinite(price) && SHOPIFY_PRICE_MODE === 'gross' && Number(r.vatid) === 1) { price = price * (1 + SHOPIFY_VAT_RATE); // VESTOCK.VATID 1 = VAT-rated (see returnsDefaultVatFromVatid) } const priceStr = (price === null || !isFinite(price) || price <= 0) ? null : (Math.round(price * 100) / 100).toFixed(2); return { stockid: r.stockid, title: String(r.name || '').trim(), sku, barcode, price: priceStr, qty: Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(r.quantity) || 0)), productType: String(r.department || '').trim(), candidates: [...barcodes, stockno, catno].filter(Boolean) }; } // Display price for the list (same number that would be sent) function shDisplayPrice(r) { return shBuildPayload(r).price; } // ---- Where does this product sit on Shopify? (linked variant first, then SKU/barcode) ---- function shStatusFor(cat, r) { if (!cat.map) return { shopify_status: 'unknown', shopify_url: '' }; let hit = (r.shopify_variant_id && cat.byVariant && cat.byVariant.get(r.shopify_variant_id)) || null; if (!hit) { for (const c of shBuildPayload(r).candidates) { const k = shNormKey(c); if (!k) continue; hit = cat.map.get(k) || cat.map.get(shAltKey(k)); if (hit) break; } } return hit ? { shopify_status: hit.status, shopify_url: hit.url } : { shopify_status: 'not_found', shopify_url: '' }; } // ---- Live lookups used while pushing (so we never rely on a stale cache to decide create vs update) ---- const SH_VARIANT_FIELDS = `id sku barcode inventoryItem{ id } product{ id status handle onlineStoreUrl }`; async function shGetVariantById(gid) { const data = await shGraphQL(`query($id:ID!){ productVariant(id:$id){ ${SH_VARIANT_FIELDS} } }`, { id: gid }); return data.productVariant || null; } async function shFindVariantLive(p) { const keys = []; [p.sku, ...p.candidates].forEach(k => { if (k && !keys.includes(k)) keys.push(k); }); for (const key of keys.slice(0, 3)) { const esc = key.replace(/\\/g, '\\\\').replace(/"/g, '\\"'); const data = await shGraphQL( `query($q:String!){ productVariants(first:10, query:$q){ nodes{ ${SH_VARIANT_FIELDS} } } }`, { q: `sku:"${esc}" OR barcode:"${esc}"` } ); const want = shNormKey(key); const hit = ((data.productVariants && data.productVariants.nodes) || []).find(n => shNormKey(n.sku) === want || shNormKey(n.barcode) === want || shAltKey(shNormKey(n.sku)) === shAltKey(want) || shAltKey(shNormKey(n.barcode)) === shAltKey(want)); if (hit) return hit; } return null; } const SH_PRODUCT_CREATE = `mutation($product:ProductCreateInput!,$media:[CreateMediaInput!]){ productCreate(product:$product, media:$media){ product{ id status handle onlineStoreUrl variants(first:1){ nodes{ id inventoryItem{ id } } } } userErrors{ field message } } }`; const SH_VARIANTS_BULK_UPDATE = `mutation($productId:ID!,$variants:[ProductVariantsBulkInput!]!){ productVariantsBulkUpdate(productId:$productId, variants:$variants){ productVariants{ id sku barcode price } userErrors{ field message } } }`; const SH_INVENTORY_SET = `mutation($input:InventorySetQuantitiesInput!){ inventorySetQuantities(input:$input){ inventoryAdjustmentGroup{ id } userErrors{ field message } } }`; async function shSaveLink(stockid, ids, by, action) { await withConnection(async conn => { const up = await conn.execute( `UPDATE SHOPIFY_SYNC SET SHOPIFY_PRODUCT_ID = :pid, SHOPIFY_VARIANT_ID = :vid, SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID = :iid, SKU = :sku, LAST_PUSHED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, LAST_PUSHED_BY = :byu, LAST_ACTION = :act, LAST_QTY = NVL(:qty, LAST_QTY), LAST_QTY_AT = CASE WHEN :qty2 IS NULL THEN LAST_QTY_AT ELSE SYSTIMESTAMP END WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { pid: ids.productId, vid: ids.variantId, iid: ids.inventoryItemId || null, sku: ids.sku || null, byu: by, act: action, qty: ids.qty === undefined ? null : ids.qty, qty2: ids.qty === undefined ? null : ids.qty, id: stockid }, { autoCommit: true } ); if (!up.rowsAffected) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO SHOPIFY_SYNC (STOCKID, SHOPIFY_PRODUCT_ID, SHOPIFY_VARIANT_ID, SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID, SKU, LAST_PUSHED_AT, LAST_PUSHED_BY, LAST_ACTION, LAST_QTY, LAST_QTY_AT) VALUES (:id, :pid, :vid, :iid, :sku, SYSTIMESTAMP, :byu, :act, :qty, CASE WHEN :qty2 IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE SYSTIMESTAMP END)`, { id: stockid, pid: ids.productId, vid: ids.variantId, iid: ids.inventoryItemId || null, sku: ids.sku || null, byu: by, act: action, qty: ids.qty === undefined ? null : ids.qty, qty2: ids.qty === undefined ? null : ids.qty }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); } // ---- Create or update ONE product on Shopify. Throws on failure. ---- async function shPushOne(r, by, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const p = shBuildPayload(r); if (!p.title) throw new Error('Product has no name'); if (!p.sku) throw new Error('No barcode, stock no. or catalogue no. to use as the SKU'); // Generated content (description, ingredients, nutrition, picture) saved by "Generate", if any const content = await shLoadContent(p.stockid, true); if (opts.requireContent && !(content && content.description_html && content.image)) { throw new Error('Content is not generated yet (description and picture are needed) — press Generate first'); } const contentSent = { description: false, image: false }; // 1) Is it already on Shopify? (saved link first, then a live SKU/barcode search) let found = null; if (r.shopify_variant_id) found = await shGetVariantById(r.shopify_variant_id); if (!found) found = await shFindVariantLive(p); let action, productId, variantId, inventoryItemId, status, url, warning = ''; if (found) { action = 'updated'; productId = found.product.id; variantId = found.id; inventoryItemId = found.inventoryItem && found.inventoryItem.id; status = String(found.product.status || '').toLowerCase(); url = found.product.onlineStoreUrl || shAdminUrl(productId); } else { if (!p.price) throw new Error('No price on file (online or retail) — cannot create it on Shopify'); action = 'created'; const productInput = { title: p.title, status: SHOPIFY_NEW_STATUS, tags: ['hallan-portal'] }; if (p.productType) productInput.productType = p.productType; if (SHOPIFY_VENDOR) productInput.vendor = SHOPIFY_VENDOR; let createMedia; if (content && content.description_html) { const f = shContentProductFields(content, true); Object.assign(productInput, f); productInput.tags = ['hallan-portal', ...(content.tags || [])].filter((t, i, a) => t && a.indexOf(t) === i).slice(0, 250); contentSent.description = true; } if (content && content.image) { createMedia = [await shMediaInput(content, p)]; contentSent.image = true; } const created = (await shGraphQL(SH_PRODUCT_CREATE, createMedia ? { product: productInput, media: createMedia } : { product: productInput })).productCreate; if (created.userErrors && created.userErrors.length) throw new Error(shUserErrors(created.userErrors)); const prod = created.product; const v = prod.variants.nodes[0]; productId = prod.id; variantId = v.id; inventoryItemId = v.inventoryItem && v.inventoryItem.id; status = String(prod.status || '').toLowerCase(); url = prod.onlineStoreUrl || shAdminUrl(productId); // Save the link NOW: if the variant update below fails, the next attempt finds this // product by its saved id instead of creating a duplicate. await shSaveLink(p.stockid, { productId, variantId, inventoryItemId, sku: p.sku }, by, 'created'); } // 2) Price / barcode / SKU on the variant (price skipped if we have none on an update) const variantInput = { id: variantId, inventoryItem: { sku: p.sku } }; if (p.price) variantInput.price = p.price; if (p.barcode) variantInput.barcode = p.barcode; if (SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK) variantInput.inventoryItem.tracked = true; const upd = (await shGraphQL(SH_VARIANTS_BULK_UPDATE, { productId, variants: [variantInput] })).productVariantsBulkUpdate; if (upd.userErrors && upd.userErrors.length) throw new Error(shUserErrors(upd.userErrors)); // 3) Stock quantity (optional) let qtySent; // stays undefined unless Shopify accepted the quantity, so LAST_QTY is only set for real if (SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK) { if (!SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID) warning = 'Stock not sent: SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID is not set'; else if (!inventoryItemId) warning = 'Stock not sent: Shopify returned no inventory item'; else { try { const inv = (await shGraphQL(SH_INVENTORY_SET, { input: { name: 'available', reason: 'correction', ignoreCompareQuantity: true, quantities: [{ inventoryItemId, locationId: SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID, quantity: p.qty }] } })).inventorySetQuantities; if (inv.userErrors && inv.userErrors.length) warning = 'Stock not sent: ' + shUserErrors(inv.userErrors); else qtySent = p.qty; } catch (e) { warning = 'Stock not sent: ' + e.message; } } } // 4) Existing Shopify products: only FILL IN what is empty (description / picture) — // anything already written or uploaded on Shopify is never overwritten. if (found && content) { try { const applied = await shFillExistingProduct(productId, content, p); contentSent.description = applied.description; contentSent.image = applied.image; } catch (e) { warning = (warning ? warning + ' · ' : '') + 'Content not added: ' + e.message; } } await shSaveLink(p.stockid, { productId, variantId, inventoryItemId, sku: p.sku, qty: qtySent }, by, action); shCachePut([p.sku, ...p.candidates], { productId, variantId, status, url, adminUrl: shAdminUrl(productId) }); return { action, status, url, price: p.price, warning, content: contentSent }; } // ==================================================================== // CONTINUOUS STOCK SYNC (EPOS quantity -> Shopify available quantity) // Runs in the background every SHOPIFY_STOCK_SYNC_MINUTES. Only products that // have already been sent to Shopify (rows in SHOPIFY_SYNC) are touched, and // only those whose quantity differs from LAST_QTY (the last quantity we sent) // — plus everything every SHOPIFY_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_HOURS to correct drift. // ==================================================================== const shStockSync = { enabled: false, running: false, lastStartedAt: null, lastFinishedAt: null, lastDurationMs: null, lastMode: null, lastChecked: 0, lastChanged: 0, lastSent: 0, lastFailed: 0, lastError: null, lastFailures: [], lastFullAt: 0, totalSent: 0, failedUntil: new Map() // stockid -> time before which we don't retry a failing product }; const SH_STOCK_RETRY_FAILED_MS = 60 * 60 * 1000; const SH_INVENTORY_ITEM_TRACK = `mutation($id:ID!,$input:InventoryItemInput!){ inventoryItemUpdate(id:$id, input:$input){ inventoryItem{ id tracked } userErrors{ field message } } }`; const SH_INVENTORY_ACTIVATE = `mutation($itemId:ID!,$locationId:ID!){ inventoryActivate(inventoryItemId:$itemId, locationId:$locationId){ inventoryLevel{ id } userErrors{ field message } } }`; function shSetQuantitiesInput(items) { return { name: 'available', reason: 'correction', ignoreCompareQuantity: true, quantities: items.map(i => ({ inventoryItemId: i.iid, locationId: SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID, quantity: i.qty })) }; } // Sends one batch. Returns { ok: [items], failed: [{item, error}] }.
