If a batch is rejected as a whole // (one bad product spoils it), each product is retried on its own so the good ones still go through. async function shSendStockBatch(items) { try { const inv = (await shGraphQL(SH_INVENTORY_SET, { input: shSetQuantitiesInput(items) })).inventorySetQuantities; if (!(inv.userErrors && inv.userErrors.length)) return { ok: items, failed: [] }; if (items.length === 1) return { ok: [], failed: [{ item: items[0], error: shUserErrors(inv.userErrors), fixable: true }] }; } catch (e) { if (items.length === 1) return { ok: [], failed: [{ item: items[0], error: e.message, fixable: false }] }; } const ok = [], failed = []; for (const it of items) { const r = await shSendStockBatch([it]); ok.push(...r.ok); failed.push(...r.failed); } return { ok, failed }; } // A product that Shopify refuses to take stock for is usually "not tracked" or "not stocked at this // location" (e.g. created before stock sync was switched on). Fix both once, then try again. async function shMakeStockable(item) { const t = (await shGraphQL(SH_INVENTORY_ITEM_TRACK, { id: item.iid, input: { tracked: true } })).inventoryItemUpdate; if (t.userErrors && t.userErrors.length) throw new Error(shUserErrors(t.userErrors)); const a = (await shGraphQL(SH_INVENTORY_ACTIVATE, { itemId: item.iid, locationId: SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID })).inventoryActivate; if (a.userErrors && a.userErrors.length) throw new Error(shUserErrors(a.userErrors)); } async function shSyncStockOnce(opts) { opts = opts || {}; if (!SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK || !SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID || !shConfigured()) return { skipped: 'not enabled' }; if (shStockSync.running) return { skipped: 'already running' }; shStockSync.running = true; const started = Date.now(); shStockSync.lastStartedAt = new Date(started).toISOString(); try { const full = !!opts.full || (SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS > 0 && started - shStockSync.lastFullAt >= SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS); const rows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.STOCKID, l.SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID, l.LAST_QTY, s.QUANTITY FROM SHOPIFY_SYNC l JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = l.STOCKID WHERE l.SHOPIFY_INVENTORY_ITEM_ID IS NOT NULL` ))).rows); const todo = []; for (const r of rows) { const qty = Math.max(0, Math.floor(Number(r.quantity) || 0)); const last = (r.last_qty === null || r.last_qty === undefined) ? null : Number(r.last_qty); if (!full && last !== null && last === qty) continue; const blockedUntil = shStockSync.failedUntil.get(Number(r.stockid)); if (blockedUntil && blockedUntil > started && !full) continue; todo.push({ stockid: Number(r.stockid), iid: r.shopify_inventory_item_id, qty }); } const okItems = [], failures = []; for (let i = 0; i < todo.length; i += SH_STOCK_BATCH) { const res = await shSendStockBatch(todo.slice(i, i + SH_STOCK_BATCH)); okItems.push(...res.ok); for (const f of res.failed) { let err = f.error; if (f.fixable) { // one repair attempt: track + activate at the location, then resend try { await shMakeStockable(f.item); const retry = await shSendStockBatch([f.item]); if (retry.ok.length) { okItems.push(f.item); continue; } err = retry.failed[0] ? retry.failed[0].error : err; } catch (e) { err = err + ' (repair failed: ' + e.message + ')'; } } failures.push({ stockid: f.item.stockid, error: err }); shStockSync.failedUntil.set(f.item.stockid, Date.now() + SH_STOCK_RETRY_FAILED_MS); } } if (okItems.length) { await withConnection(conn => conn.executeMany( `UPDATE SHOPIFY_SYNC SET LAST_QTY = :q, LAST_QTY_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE STOCKID = :id`, okItems.map(i => ({ q: i.qty, id: i.stockid })), { autoCommit: true } )); okItems.forEach(i => shStockSync.failedUntil.delete(i.stockid)); } if (full) shStockSync.lastFullAt = started; shStockSync.lastMode = full ? 'full' : 'changes'; shStockSync.lastChecked = rows.length; shStockSync.lastChanged = todo.length; shStockSync.lastSent = okItems.length; shStockSync.lastFailed = failures.length; shStockSync.lastFailures = failures.slice(0, 20); shStockSync.lastError = null; shStockSync.totalSent += okItems.length; if (todo.length || failures.length) { console.log('[shopify] stock sync (' + shStockSync.lastMode + '): checked ' + rows.length + ', sent ' + okItems.length + ', failed ' + failures.length); failures.slice(0, 5).forEach(f => console.error('[shopify] stock sync failed for stock ' + f.stockid + ':', f.error)); } return { mode: shStockSync.lastMode, checked: rows.length, sent: okItems.length, failed: failures.length }; } catch (e) { shStockSync.lastError = e.message; console.error('[shopify] stock sync error:', e.message); return { error: e.message }; } finally { shStockSync.running = false; shStockSync.lastFinishedAt = new Date().toISOString(); shStockSync.lastDurationMs = Date.now() - started; } } // Called once from the boot sequence. Never throws; never blocks startup. function startShopifyStockSync() { if (!shConfigured()) return console.log('[shopify] stock sync not started: Shopify is not configured'); if (!SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK) return console.log('[shopify] stock sync not started: SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK is not 1'); if (!SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID) return console.log('[shopify] stock sync not started: SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID is not set'); if (!SH_STOCK_SYNC_MS) return console.log('[shopify] stock sync not started: SHOPIFY_STOCK_SYNC_MINUTES is 0'); shStockSync.enabled = true; console.log('[shopify] stock sync every ' + (SH_STOCK_SYNC_MS / 60000) + ' min' + (SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS ? ', full re-send every ' + (SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS / 3600000) + ' h' : '')); setTimeout(() => shSyncStockOnce().catch(() => {}), 30 * 1000); // first pass shortly after boot setInterval(() => shSyncStockOnce().catch(() => {}), SH_STOCK_SYNC_MS); } app.get('/api/stock/shopify/stock-sync/status', (req, res) => { res.json({ enabled: shStockSync.enabled, running: shStockSync.running, every_minutes: SH_STOCK_SYNC_MS / 60000, full_resync_hours: SH_STOCK_FULL_SYNC_MS / 3600000, last_started_at: shStockSync.lastStartedAt, last_finished_at: shStockSync.lastFinishedAt, last_duration_ms: shStockSync.lastDurationMs, last_mode: shStockSync.lastMode, last_checked: shStockSync.lastChecked, last_changed: shStockSync.lastChanged, last_sent: shStockSync.lastSent, last_failed: shStockSync.lastFailed, last_failures: shStockSync.lastFailures, last_error: shStockSync.lastError, total_sent_since_boot: shStockSync.totalSent, blocked_products: shStockSync.failedUntil.size }); }); // Run a pass right now. Body: { full: true } re-sends every linked product, not just the changed ones. app.post('/api/stock/shopify/stock-sync/run', requirePortalAdmin, async (req, res) => { if (!shConfigured()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Shopify is not set up yet' }); if (!SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK || !SHOPIFY_LOCATION_GID) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Set SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK=1 and SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID first' }); res.json(await shSyncStockOnce({ full: !!(req.body && req.body.full) })); }); // ---- Connection check (handy when setting up): who are we connected to, and what are we allowed to do? ---- app.get('/api/stock/shopify/status', async (req, res) => { const base = { configured: shConfigured(), store: SHOPIFY_STORE, api_version: SHOPIFY_API_VERSION, auth: SHOPIFY_STATIC_TOKEN ? 'access token' : 'client credentials', new_product_status: SHOPIFY_NEW_STATUS, price_mode: SHOPIFY_PRICE_MODE, sync_stock: SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK }; if (!base.configured) return res.json({ ...base, ok: false, error: null }); try { const data = await shGraphQL(`{ shop{ name myshopifyDomain currencyCode } currentAppInstallation{ accessScopes{ handle } } }`); const scopes = ((data.currentAppInstallation && data.currentAppInstallation.accessScopes) || []).map(s => s.handle); res.json({ ...base, ok: true, shop_name: data.shop.name, shop_domain: data.shop.myshopifyDomain, currency: data.shop.currencyCode, scopes, can_write_products: scopes.includes('write_products'), can_write_inventory: scopes.includes('write_inventory'), error: null }); } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] status check failed:', e.message); res.json({ ...base, ok: false, error: e.message }); } }); // ---- The product list for one supplier, each row with its Shopify status. // Query params: suppid, preferred ('yes'|'no'), refresh ('1' = re-read Shopify) ---- app.get('/api/stock/shopify/items', async (req, res) => { const suppId = Number(req.query.suppid); const preferred = (req.query.preferred || '').trim().toLowerCase(); if (!Number.isFinite(suppId)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'suppid is required' }); if (preferred !== 'yes' && preferred !== 'no') return res.status(400).json({ error: "preferred must be 'yes' or 'no'" }); try { const preferredClause = preferred === 'yes' ? 'AND ss.PREFERRED = 1' : 'AND NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) != 1'; // The five pieces do not depend on each other, so they run at the same time (the page waits for the slowest, not the sum). // The timings go to the log: if the page is slow, the "[items]" line shows which piece it was. const t0 = Date.now(), tm = {}; const timed = (k, p) => p.then(v => { tm[k] = Date.now() - t0; return v; }); const [result, cat, web, wooLinks, wooQueue] = await Promise.all([ timed('stock', withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `${SH_STOCK_SELECT} WHERE s.STOCKID IN (SELECT ss.STOCKID FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId ${preferredClause}) ORDER BY s.NAME`, { suppId } ))), timed('shopify', shGetCatalogue(req.query.refresh === '1')), // WordPress / WooCommerce side (read from Oracle, so this never calls the website) timed('website', wcGetProducts(req.query.refresh === '1')), timed('links', wooLinkMap()), timed('queue', wooQueueMap()) ]); console.log('[items] supplier ' + suppId + ': ' + result.rows.length + ' rows, ms until each piece was ready ' + JSON.stringify(tm) + ', total ' + (Date.now() - t0)); const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => { const st = shStatusFor(cat, r); const p = shBuildPayload(r); const wb = wooResolve(r.stockid, String(r.barcodes || '').split(',').map(b => b.trim()).filter(Boolean), wooLinks, web.map); const wq = wooQueue.get(Number(r.stockid)) || null; return { stockid: r.stockid, name: r.name, barcodes: r.barcodes || '', sku: p.sku, price: p.price, qty: p.qty, department: r.department || '', online_complete: Number(r.online_complete) === 1, content_ready: Number(r.content_ready) === 1, content_review: Number(r.content_review) === 1, photo_count: Number(r.photo_count) || 0, sh_completed: Number(r.sh_completed) === 1, linked: !!r.shopify_variant_id, last_pushed_at: r.last_pushed_at, shopify_status: st.shopify_status, shopify_url: st.shopify_url, web_status: wb.web_status, // publish | draft | pending | private | future | not_found | unknown web_id: wb.web_id, // WooCommerce product id = WordPress post id (the post=... in the admin link) web_url: wb.web_url, // public page on the website web_edit_url: wb.web_edit_url, // wp-admin > Edit product web_queue: wq ? wq.status : '', // PENDING | PROCESSING | ERROR while a send to the website is waiting / failed web_queue_msg: wq ? wq.message : '' }; }); res.json({ items, shopify: { configured: cat.configured, ok: cat.configured && !cat.error && !!cat.map, error: cat.error, total: cat.total, fetched_at: cat.fetchedAt ? new Date(cat.fetchedAt).toISOString() : null, new_product_status: SHOPIFY_NEW_STATUS }, website: { configured: web.configured, ok: !!web.map || wooLinks.size > 0, warning: web.error || null, total: web.total, fetched_at: web.fetchedAt ? new Date(web.fetchedAt).toISOString() : null } }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Send products to Shopify. Body: { stockids: [1,2,3] } (max 25 per request). // Always answers 200 with one result per product, so one bad product // never stops the rest. ---- app.post('/api/stock/shopify/push', async (req, res) => { if (!shConfigured()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Shopify is not set up yet — add the SHOPIFY_ variables on Render.' }); const ids = [...new Set(((req.body && req.body.stockids) || []).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite))]; if (ids.length === 0) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'stockids is required' }); if (ids.length > SH_MAX_PUSH_PER_REQUEST) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Send at most ' + SH_MAX_PUSH_PER_REQUEST + ' products per request' }); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const binds = {}; const inList = ids.map((id, i) => { binds['i' + i] = id; return ':i' + i; }).join(','); const dbRows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `${SH_STOCK_SELECT} WHERE s.STOCKID IN (${inList})`, binds ))).rows); const byId = new Map(dbRows.map(r => [Number(r.stockid), r])); const results = []; for (const id of ids) { const r = byId.get(id); if (!r) { results.push({ stockid: id, ok: false, error: 'Product not found' }); continue; } try { const out = await shPushOne(r, by, { requireContent: !!(req.body && req.body.require_content) }); results.push({ stockid: id, ok: true, action: out.action, shopify_status: out.status, shopify_url: out.url, price: out.price, warning: out.warning || '', content: out.content || null }); } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] push failed for stock ' + id + ':', e.message); results.push({ stockid: id, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } res.json({ results }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ==================================================================== // SHOPIFY PRODUCT CONTENT (Stock Info > Shopify > "Generate") // Uses the Gemini API (GEMINI_API_KEY, already set up for invoice reading) to write // - short description, full description (SEO-minded), ingredients, nutritional info // - a product picture // Everything generated is saved in SHOPIFY_CONTENT, so a product is only ever // generated ONCE (press Generate again on a ready product = no new cost). It can be // viewed / edited / regenerated from the page, and is sent to Shopify with the product. // // Optional env vars (all have sensible defaults): // GEMINI_CONTENT_MODEL default gemini-3.5-flash (text — the "medium" Flash tier) // GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL default gemini-3.1-flash-image (picture — "Nano Banana 2", medium tier) // GEMINI_CONTENT_GROUNDING default 1 — lets Gemini look the product up with Google Search so // ingredients / nutrition come from the real pack, not a guess. // Set 0 to switch it off (cheaper, but less accurate). // ==================================================================== const GEMINI_TEXT_MODEL = (process.env.GEMINI_CONTENT_MODEL || 'gemini-3.5-flash').trim(); // Picture model: Nano Banana Pro (gemini-3-pro-image) has the most accurate text rendering and follows long instructions best. // If it is unavailable the picture falls back to Nano Banana 2.1 (gemini-nano-banana-2.1). gemini-3.1-flash-image is deprecated. // Set GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL / GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK on Render to change them (an old GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL value there overrides this default). const GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL = (process.env.GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL || 'gemini-3-pro-image').trim(); const GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK = (process.env.GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK || '').trim(); // empty = only GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL is ever used const GEMINI_IMAGE_REQUEST_SIZE = (process.env.SH_IMAGE_REQUEST_SIZE || '2K').trim(); // size Gemini draws at (1K / 2K / 4K); it is scaled down to the final size afterwards const GEMINI_CONTENT_GROUNDING = !['0', 'false', 'no', 'off'].includes(String(process.env.GEMINI_CONTENT_GROUNDING || '1').trim().toLowerCase()); const SH_GEN_MAX_PER_REQUEST = 2; const shGenInFlight = new Set(); async function ensureShopifyContentTable() { const ddl = `CREATE TABLE SHOPIFY_CONTENT ( STOCKID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, SHORT_DESC VARCHAR2(2000), DESCRIPTION CLOB, INGREDIENTS CLOB, NUTRITION CLOB, STORAGE VARCHAR2(1000), SEO_TITLE VARCHAR2(300), SEO_DESC VARCHAR2(600), TAGS VARCHAR2(1500), PACK_DESC VARCHAR2(1000), NEEDS_REVIEW NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, REVIEW_NOTE VARCHAR2(1000), IMAGE_MIME VARCHAR2(60), IMAGE_DATA BLOB, TEXT_MODEL VARCHAR2(100), IMAGE_MODEL VARCHAR2(100), GENERATED_AT TIMESTAMP, GENERATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), COMPLETED NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, COMPLETED_AT TIMESTAMP, COMPLETED_BY VARCHAR2(200) )`; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(ddl, [], { autoCommit: true })); console.log('[shopify] created table SHOPIFY_CONTENT'); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[shopify] SHOPIFY_CONTENT schema error:', e.message); // 955 = already exists } // Columns added later (safe to run every boot: 1430 = column already there) for (const sql of [ `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_CONTENT ADD (IMAGE_SOURCE VARCHAR2(20))`, `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_CONTENT ADD (IMAGE_EDITED BLOB)`, `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_CONTENT ADD (IMAGE_EDITED_MIME VARCHAR2(60))`, `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_CONTENT ADD (ALLERGENS VARCHAR2(1000))` ]) { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(sql, [], { autoCommit: true })); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[shopify] SHOPIFY_CONTENT alter error:', e.message); } } // The photos taken with the camera button (front / back / nutrition panel ...) try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `CREATE TABLE SHOPIFY_PHOTOS ( ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, STOCKID NUMBER NOT NULL, ROLE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'other' NOT NULL, MIME VARCHAR2(60), IMAGE_DATA BLOB, THUMB_DATA BLOB, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200) )`, [], { autoCommit: true })); console.log('[shopify] created table SHOPIFY_PHOTOS'); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[shopify] SHOPIFY_PHOTOS schema error:', e.message); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(`CREATE INDEX SHOPIFY_PHOTOS_IX ON SHOPIFY_PHOTOS (STOCKID)`, [], { autoCommit: true })); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955 && e.errorNum !== 1408) console.error('[shopify] SHOPIFY_PHOTOS index error:', e.message); } // Where a photo came from: 'camera' (or empty) = taken / uploaded in the portal, 'website' = copied from the WordPress product for (const sql of [ `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_PHOTOS ADD (SOURCE VARCHAR2(20))`, `ALTER TABLE SHOPIFY_PHOTOS ADD (SRC_URL VARCHAR2(1000))` ]) { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(sql, [], { autoCommit: true })); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[shopify] SHOPIFY_PHOTOS alter error:', e.message); } } } // ---------- small helpers ---------- function shStr(v, max) { let t = (v === null || v === undefined) ? '' : String(v).replace(/\r\n/g, '\n').trim(); if (max && t.length > max) t = t.slice(0, max).trim(); return t; } function shEsc(t) { return String(t === null || t === undefined ? '' : t).replace(/&/g, '&').replace(//g, '>').replace(/"/g, '"'); } // Keep only simple formatting tags (no attributes, no scripts) in the description function shCleanHtml(html) { let t = String(html || ''); t = t.replace(/<(script|style|iframe|object|embed)[\s\S]*?<\/\1>/gi, ''); t = t.replace(//g, ''); t = t.replace(/<\/?([a-z0-9]+)([^>]*)>/gi, (m, tag) => { tag = tag.toLowerCase(); if (!['p', 'ul', 'ol', 'li', 'strong', 'em', 'br', 'h3', 'h4'].includes(tag)) return ''; return m.startsWith('' : (tag === 'br' ? '
' : '<' + tag + '>'); }); return t.trim(); } const SH_NUTRI_KEYS = ['energy_kj', 'energy_kcal', 'fat', 'saturates', 'carbohydrate', 'sugars', 'fibre', 'protein', 'salt']; const SH_NUTRI_LABELS = { energy_kj: 'Energy (kJ)', energy_kcal: 'Energy (kcal)', fat: 'Fat', saturates: 'of which saturates', carbohydrate: 'Carbohydrate', sugars: 'of which sugars', fibre: 'Fibre', protein: 'Protein', salt: 'Salt' }; function shNormNutrition(n) { if (!n || typeof n !== 'object') return null; const out = { per: shStr(n.per, 40) || 'per 100g' }; let any = false; for (const k of SH_NUTRI_KEYS) { let v = n[k]; if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') { out[k] = ''; continue; } if (typeof v === 'number') v = (k === 'energy_kj' ? v + ' kJ' : k === 'energy_kcal' ? v + ' kcal' : v + ' g'); v = shStr(v, 40); if (/^(n\/?a|unknown|not available|null|-+)$/i.test(v)) v = ''; out[k] = v; if (v) any = true; } return any ? out : null; } function shNutritionRows(n) { if (!n) return []; const rows = []; const energy = [n.energy_kj, n.energy_kcal].filter(Boolean).join(' / '); if (energy) rows.push(['Energy', energy]); [['fat', 'Fat'], ['saturates', 'of which saturates'], ['carbohydrate', 'Carbohydrate'], ['sugars', 'of which sugars'], ['fibre', 'Fibre'], ['protein', 'Protein'], ['salt', 'Salt']].forEach(([k, label]) => { if (n[k]) rows.push([label, n[k]]); }); return rows; } function shNutritionText(n) { const rows = shNutritionRows(n); if (!rows.length) return ''; return (n.per ? 'Typical values ' + n.per + '\n' : '') + rows.map(r => r[0] + ': ' + r[1]).join('\n'); } // The whole product page body: description + ingredients + nutrition (+ storage). Works on any Shopify theme. function shBuildBodyHtml(c) { let html = c.description_html || ''; if (c.ingredients) html += '

Ingredients

' + shEsc(c.ingredients).replace(/\n/g, '
') + '

'; const rows = shNutritionRows(c.nutrition); if (rows.length) { html += '

Nutritional Information

' + rows.map(r => '').join('') + '
Typical values' + shEsc((c.nutrition && c.nutrition.per) || 'per 100g') + '
' + shEsc(r[0]) + '' + shEsc(r[1]) + '
'; } if (c.storage) html += '

Storage

' + shEsc(c.storage) + '

'; return html; } // Fields for productCreate / productUpdate function shContentProductFields(c, withMetafields) { const f = { descriptionHtml: shBuildBodyHtml(c) }; if (c.seo_title || c.seo_description) f.seo = { title: c.seo_title || undefined, description: c.seo_description || undefined }; if (withMetafields) { const mf = []; const add = (key, value) => { if (value) mf.push({ namespace: 'custom', key, type: 'multi_line_text_field', value }); }; add('short_description', c.short_description); add('ingredients', c.ingredients); add('nutritional_information', shNutritionText(c.nutrition)); if (mf.length) f.metafields = mf; } return f; } // ---------- reading / writing SHOPIFY_CONTENT ---------- async function shLoadContent(stockid, withImage) { return withConnection(async conn => { const res = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, SHORT_DESC, DESCRIPTION, INGREDIENTS, NUTRITION, STORAGE, ALLERGENS, SEO_TITLE, SEO_DESC, TAGS, PACK_DESC, NEEDS_REVIEW, REVIEW_NOTE, IMAGE_MIME, TEXT_MODEL, IMAGE_MODEL, GENERATED_AT, GENERATED_BY, COMPLETED, IMAGE_SOURCE, DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(IMAGE_EDITED) AS ED_LEN, IMAGE_EDITED_MIME, DBMS_LOB.GETLENGTH(IMAGE_DATA) AS IMG_LEN${withImage ? ', IMAGE_DATA, IMAGE_EDITED' : ''} FROM SHOPIFY_CONTENT WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { id: stockid }, { fetchInfo: { DESCRIPTION: { type: oracledb.STRING }, INGREDIENTS: { type: oracledb.STRING }, NUTRITION: { type: oracledb.STRING } } } ); const r = lower(res.rows)[0]; if (!r) return null; let nutrition = null; if (r.nutrition) { try { nutrition = shNormNutrition(JSON.parse(r.nutrition)); } catch (_) { nutrition = null; } } const hasImage = Number(r.img_len) > 0 && !!r.image_source; // a picture with no photo behind it (old AI drawing) is ignored return { stockid: Number(r.stockid), short_description: r.short_desc || '', description_html: r.description || '', ingredients: r.ingredients || '', nutrition, storage: r.storage || '', allergens: r.allergens || '', seo_title: r.seo_title || '', seo_description: r.seo_desc || '', tags: String(r.tags || '').split(',').map(t => t.trim()).filter(Boolean), pack_description: r.pack_desc || '', needs_review: Number(r.needs_review) === 1, review_note: r.review_note || '', text_model: r.text_model || '', image_model: r.image_model || '', generated_at: r.generated_at, generated_by: r.generated_by || '', completed: Number(r.completed) === 1, has_image: hasImage, image_source: r.image_source || '', has_edited: Number(r.ed_len) > 0, edited: (withImage && Number(r.ed_len) > 0 && r.image_edited) ? { buffer: r.image_edited, mime: r.image_edited_mime || 'image/png' } : null, image: (withImage && hasImage && r.image_data) ? { buffer: r.image_data, mime: r.image_mime || 'image/png' } : null }; }); } const SH_CONTENT_COLS = { short_desc: 'v', description: 'c', ingredients: 'c', nutrition: 'c', storage: 'v', seo_title: 'v', seo_desc: 'v', tags: 'v', pack_desc: 'v', needs_review: 'n', review_note: 'v', image_mime: 'v', image_data: 'b', image_source: 'v', image_edited: 'b', image_edited_mime: 'v', allergens: 'v', text_model: 'v', image_model: 'v', generated_by: 'v' }; // Insert-or-update only the fields given. opts.stamp = also set GENERATED_AT. async function shSaveContent(stockid, fields, opts) { opts = opts || {}; const keys = Object.keys(fields).filter(k => SH_CONTENT_COLS[k]); const binds = {}; keys.forEach(k => { const kind = SH_CONTENT_COLS[k], v = fields[k]; if (kind === 'c') binds[k] = { val: v === null || v === undefined ? null : String(v), type: oracledb.CLOB }; else if (kind === 'b') binds[k] = { val: v, type: oracledb.BLOB }; else binds[k] = v === undefined ? null : v; }); await withConnection(async conn => { if (keys.length || opts.stamp) { const sets = keys.map(k => `${k.toUpperCase()} = :${k}`); if (opts.stamp) sets.push('GENERATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP'); const up = await conn.execute(`UPDATE SHOPIFY_CONTENT SET ${sets.join(', ')} WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { ...binds, id: stockid }, { autoCommit: true }); if (up.rowsAffected) return; const cols = ['STOCKID', ...keys.map(k => k.toUpperCase())]; const vals = [':id', ...keys.map(k => ':' + k)]; if (opts.stamp) { cols.push('GENERATED_AT'); vals.push('SYSTIMESTAMP'); } await conn.execute(`INSERT INTO SHOPIFY_CONTENT (${cols.join(', ')}) VALUES (${vals.join(', ')})`, { ...binds, id: stockid }, { autoCommit: true }); } }); } // ---------- Photos (camera) ---------- const SH_PHOTO_ROLES = ['front', 'back', 'nutrition', 'side', 'other']; const SH_PHOTO_MAX_PER_PRODUCT = 12; const SH_PHOTO_MAX_BYTES = 9 * 1024 * 1024; const SH_PHOTOS_TO_GEMINI = 6; // All photos of a product, front first (full size — for Gemini). withData=false = ids/roles only. async function shLoadPhotos(stockid, withData) { return withConnection(async conn => { const res = await conn.execute( `SELECT ID, ROLE, MIME, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY${withData ? ', IMAGE_DATA' : ''} FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS WHERE STOCKID = :id ORDER BY CASE ROLE WHEN 'front' THEN 0 WHEN 'back' THEN 1 WHEN 'nutrition' THEN 2 WHEN 'side' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END, ID`, { id: stockid }); return lower(res.rows).map(r => ({ id: Number(r.id), role: r.role, mime: r.mime || 'image/jpeg', created_at: r.created_at, created_by: r.created_by, buffer: withData ? r.image_data : null })); }); } function shPhotoRolePhrase(role) { return ({ front: 'FRONT of the pack', back: 'BACK of the pack', nutrition: 'NUTRITION / INGREDIENTS panel', side: 'SIDE of the pack', other: 'extra photo' })[role] || 'extra photo'; } // ---------- Gemini ---------- async function shGeminiCall(model, body, label, timeoutMs, maxAttempts) { const key = (process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY || '').trim(); if (!key) throw new Error('GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured on the server'); const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent`; let lastErr = null; for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= (maxAttempts || 3); attempt++) { let r, text; try { r = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json', 'x-goog-api-key': key }, body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: AbortSignal.timeout(timeoutMs || 90000) }); text = await r.text(); } catch (e) { lastErr = new Error(label + ': could not reach Gemini (' + (e.name === 'TimeoutError' ? 'timed out' : e.message) + ')'); console.error('[shopify] gemini', label, 'attempt', attempt, e.message); await shSleep(1500 * attempt); continue; } if (r.ok) { try { return JSON.parse(text); } catch (_) { throw new Error(label + ': Gemini sent back something unreadable'); } } console.error('[shopify] gemini', label, model, 'status', r.status, text.slice(0, 400)); if (r.status === 429) throw new Error(label + ': Gemini quota / rate limit reached (429) — wait a minute and try again, or check billing on the Gemini API key'); if (r.status === 400 || r.status === 401 || r.status === 403 || r.status === 404) { let msg = ''; try { msg = JSON.parse(text).error.message; } catch (_) { msg = text.slice(0, 200); } throw new Error(label + ' failed (' + r.status + '): ' + msg); } lastErr = new Error(label + ': Gemini is busy (' + r.status + ')'); await shSleep(2000 * attempt); } throw lastErr || new Error(label + ' failed'); } function shGeminiText(data) { const parts = (data && data.candidates && data.candidates[0] && data.candidates[0].content && data.candidates[0].content.parts) || []; return parts.filter(x => typeof x.text === 'string' && !x.thought).map(x => x.text).join(''); } function shParseJsonLoose(text) { let t = String(text || '').trim().replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*/i, '').replace(/```\s*$/i, ''); const a = t.indexOf('{'), b = t.lastIndexOf('}'); if (a < 0 || b <= a) throw new Error('Gemini did not return the product details in the expected format'); t = t.slice(a, b + 1); try { return JSON.parse(t); } catch (_) { throw new Error('Gemini returned product details that could not be read — please try Generate again'); } } function shContentPrompt(p, r, photos) { photos = photos || []; const photoBlock = photos.length ? ` PHOTOS OF THE REAL PACK (attached after this text, in this order) ${photos.map((ph, i) => '- Photo ' + (i + 1) + ': ' + shPhotoRolePhrase(ph.role)).join('\n')} These photos are the SOURCE OF TRUTH. Read the brand, variant, pack size, claims (for example Vegan, Vegetarian, No Added MSG), ingredients, allergens, nutrition table, storage and origin EXACTLY as printed on them. Transcribe the ingredients word for word. If a value is not clearly legible in the photos, leave it empty - never guess it. Use web search only to fill gaps, and never to contradict what is printed on the pack. Describe the pack in pack_description exactly as it appears in the photos. ` : ''; const barcodes = String(r.barcodes || '').split(',').map(x => x.trim()).filter(Boolean); return `You are a senior UK e-commerce copywriter and food-data specialist for Hallan Cash & Carry (hallans.co.uk), an online grocery and wholesale store in the UK. Write the product page content for ONE product. PRODUCT - Name in our system: ${p.title} - Barcode(s) / EAN: ${barcodes.length ? barcodes.join(', ') : 'none on file'} - Department: ${p.productType || 'unknown'} ${photoBlock} STEP 1 - IDENTIFY THE REAL PRODUCT Work out the exact brand, variant and pack size. ${GEMINI_CONTENT_GROUNDING ? 'Use Google Search (search the barcode first, then the name) to find the real product and the manufacturer / retailer product page.' : 'Use the name and barcode.'} STEP 2 - FACTS (strict accuracy rules) - Ingredients, allergens and nutrition are legal food information. Only report them if you actually found them for THIS exact product (same brand, flavour and size). NEVER guess, average, or copy from a similar product. If you cannot verify a fact, leave it empty ("" or null) and say so in review_note. - Ingredients: copy the wording as printed on the pack, UK style, with allergens in UPPERCASE (e.g. WHEAT, MILK, SOYA). - Allergens: the allergen statement printed on the pack, in UK style, e.g. "Contains: MILK, SOYA. May contain: NUTS." Use the 14 UK allergen names (celery, cereals containing gluten, crustaceans, eggs, fish, lupin, milk, molluscs, mustard, peanuts, sesame, soybeans, sulphur dioxide / sulphites, tree nuts). Copy only what the pack states. If you cannot read or verify it leave "" - never write "none", "allergen free" or "no allergens" unless the pack prints exactly that. - Nutrition: typical values per 100g (or per 100ml for drinks), as strings with units (e.g. "1.2 g", "1250 kJ", "295 kcal"). Use "" for any value you cannot verify. - If the product is NOT food or drink (household, health & beauty, etc.), set is_food to false and leave ingredients and nutrition empty unless the pack genuinely lists ingredients (for example cosmetics INCI). STEP 3 - WRITE (UK English, SEO-minded but natural, no keyword stuffing) - short_description: plain text, 140-200 characters, 1-2 sentences, starts with the main search phrase (brand + product + size). - description_html: 120-220 words using ONLY these HTML tags:

,

    ,
  • , . Open with a sentence containing brand + product name + pack size. Then: what it is and how it is used, taste / texture / quality points that are true for this product, and a short bullet list (
      ) of key features. Work in the phrases UK shoppers actually search for (for example "buy ... online UK", the product type, the main use) once or twice each, naturally. Never invent claims (no "organic", "halal", "vegan", "gluten free" or health claims unless verified on the pack). No prices, no offers, no competitor names, no superlatives like "best in the world". - storage: storage and use-by advice from the pack, or "". - seo_title: max 60 characters, brand + product + size + a short benefit, e.g. "Brand Product 500g | Buy Online UK | Hallan". - seo_description: max 155 characters, a clear sentence with a reason to click. - tags: 5-8 lowercase search tags (brand, product type, category, key use). - pack_description: one sentence describing what the real pack looks like (container type - bag, tin, box, bottle, jar, tub, sachet - and its main colours / label style), used to draw a picture. - brand: the brand name. OUTPUT: reply with ONE JSON object and nothing else (no markdown fences), exactly these keys: {"is_food": true|false, "verified": true|false, "confidence": "high"|"medium"|"low", "review_note": "", "brand": "", "short_description": "", "description_html": "", "ingredients": "", "allergens": "", "nutrition": {"per": "per 100g", "energy_kj": "", "energy_kcal": "", "fat": "", "saturates": "", "carbohydrate": "", "sugars": "", "fibre": "", "protein": "", "salt": ""}, "storage": "", "seo_title": "", "seo_description": "", "tags": [], "pack_description": ""} "verified" = true only if the exact product was identified and the ingredients / nutrition (where food) were read from the photos of its real pack or from its manufacturer data.`; } async function shGenText(p, r, photos) { photos = (photos || []).slice(0, SH_PHOTOS_TO_GEMINI); const parts = [{ text: shContentPrompt(p, r, photos) }]; photos.forEach(ph => parts.push({ inlineData: { mimeType: ph.mime || 'image/jpeg', data: Buffer.from(ph.buffer).toString('base64') } })); const body = { contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }], generationConfig: { temperature: 0.3, maxOutputTokens: 8192 } }; if (GEMINI_CONTENT_GROUNDING) body.tools = [{ google_search: {} }]; else body.generationConfig.responseMimeType = 'application/json'; const data = await shGeminiCall(GEMINI_TEXT_MODEL, body, 'Writing the description', 120000); const text = shGeminiText(data); if (!text) { const why = data && data.candidates && data.candidates[0] && data.candidates[0].finishReason; throw new Error('Gemini returned no text' + (why ? ' (' + why + ')' : '')); } const j = shParseJsonLoose(text); const out = { short_description: shStr(j.short_description, 400), description_html: shCleanHtml(j.description_html), ingredients: shStr(j.ingredients, 4000), allergens: shStr(j.allergens, 900), nutrition: shNormNutrition(j.nutrition), storage: shStr(j.storage, 600), seo_title: shStr(j.seo_title, 70), seo_description: shStr(j.seo_description, 170), tags: (Array.isArray(j.tags) ? j.tags : String(j.tags || '').split(',')) .map(t => shStr(t, 40).toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean).filter((t, i, a) => a.indexOf(t) === i).slice(0, 8), pack_description: shStr(j.pack_description, 400), brand: shStr(j.brand, 80) }; if (!out.short_description || !out.description_html) throw new Error('Gemini returned an incomplete description — please try Generate again'); // Anything we could not confirm is flagged for a human check before it is trusted const isFood = j.is_food !== false; const notes = []; if (shStr(j.review_note, 400)) notes.push(shStr(j.review_note, 400)); let review = false; if (j.verified !== true) { review = true; if (!notes.length) notes.push('Product could not be fully verified — check the details against the pack.'); } if (isFood && !out.ingredients) { review = true; notes.push('Ingredients not found — add them from the pack.'); } if (isFood && !out.nutrition) { review = true; notes.push('Nutrition values not found — add them from the pack.'); } if (String(j.confidence || '').toLowerCase() === 'low') review = true; out.needs_review = review; out.review_note = shStr(notes.join(' '), 900); return out; } // ---- Same picture size for EVERY product ------------------------------------------------------------ // Gemini cannot be trusted to size the pack identically each time, so after it returns the picture we // measure the pack ourselves and re-place it: every product picture is exactly SH_IMG_CANVAS x SH_IMG_CANVAS // on pure white, the pack centred, its longest side exactly SH_IMG_FILL of the canvas. // Needs the 'sharp' package (npm install sharp). Without it pictures are kept as Gemini made them. const SH_IMG_CANVAS = Math.max(600, Number(process.env.SH_IMAGE_SIZE) || 1200); const SH_IMG_FILL = Math.min(0.95, Math.max(0.5, Number(process.env.SH_IMAGE_FILL) || 0.85)); let sharpLib = null; try { sharpLib = require('sharp'); } catch (e) { console.warn('[shopify] the "sharp" package is not installed, so product pictures are NOT size-normalised. Run: npm install sharp'); } // Whitens a grey band that floats BELOW the pack with white space between (the "ground shadow" that AI models like to add). // A shadow that touches the pack is left to the model and the automatic check. async function shRemoveFloatingShadow(flat) { try { const meta = await sharpLib(flat).metadata(); const W = 400, scale = W / meta.width; const { data, info } = await sharpLib(flat).resize({ width: W }).removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const rows = info.height, ch = info.channels; const mass = new Array(rows).fill(0), sat = new Array(rows).fill(0); for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) { for (let x = 0; x < W; x++) { const i = (y * W + x) * ch, r = data[i], g = data[i + 1], b = data[i + 2]; if ((r + g + b) / 3 < 238) { mass[y]++; const mx = Math.max(r, g, b); sat[y] += mx ? (mx - Math.min(r, g, b)) / mx : 0; } } } const gap = Math.max(4, Math.round(rows * 0.015)), segs = []; let cur = null, empty = 0; for (let y = 0; y < rows; y++) { if (mass[y] >= 2) { if (cur && empty >= gap) { segs.push(cur); cur = null; } if (!cur) cur = { start: y, end: y, px: 0, sat: 0 }; cur.end = y; cur.px += mass[y]; cur.sat += sat[y]; empty = 0; } else if (cur) empty++; } if (cur) segs.push(cur); if (segs.length < 2) return flat; const main = segs.reduce((m, x) => (x.px > m.px ? x : m), segs[0]); const overlays = []; for (const sg of segs) { if (sg === main || sg.start <= main.end) continue; // only bands below the pack if (sg.px < main.px * 0.15 && sg.sat / sg.px < 0.15) { // small and grey = a shadow const top = Math.max(0, Math.floor(sg.start / scale) - 3), bottom = Math.min(meta.height, Math.ceil((sg.end + 1) / scale) + 3); overlays.push({ input: { create: { width: meta.width, height: bottom - top, channels: 3, background: '#ffffff' } }, top, left: 0 }); } } if (!overlays.length) return flat; console.log('[shopify] removed a floating shadow below the pack (' + overlays.length + ' band)'); return await sharpLib(flat).composite(overlays).toBuffer(); } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] shadow clean-up skipped:', e.message); return flat; } } async function shNormalizePack(buffer) { if (!sharpLib) return null; try { let flat = await sharpLib(buffer).flatten({ background: '#ffffff' }).toBuffer(); flat = await shRemoveFloatingShadow(flat); const meta = await sharpLib(flat).metadata(); const { data, info } = await sharpLib(flat).trim({ background: '#ffffff', threshold: 18 }).toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const big = Math.max(meta.width, meta.height), found = Math.max(info.width, info.height); if (info.width < 50 || info.height < 50 || found < big * 0.5) { // nothing sensible found (e.g. a white pack on white): leave it alone console.warn('[shopify] picture not size-normalised: could not find the pack edges'); return null; } const target = Math.round(SH_IMG_CANVAS * SH_IMG_FILL); const scaled = await sharpLib(data).resize({ width: target, height: target, fit: 'inside', kernel: 'lanczos3' }).toBuffer(); const out = await sharpLib({ create: { width: SH_IMG_CANVAS, height: SH_IMG_CANVAS, channels: 3, background: '#ffffff' } }) .composite([{ input: scaled, gravity: 'centre' }]).jpeg({ quality: 92, mozjpeg: true }).toBuffer(); return { buffer: out, mime: 'image/jpeg' }; } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] picture size-normalising failed, keeping the original:', e.message); return null; } } // ---- Picture clean-up with an automatic check -------------------------------------------------------- // 1) Nano Banana Pro tidies the REAL front photo: no price, no expiry date, no website watermark, no shadow, every word perfect. // 2) The tidied picture is re-placed at the standard size (shNormalizePack). // 3) A second, cheap text-model call READS the result and lists anything still wrong. // 4) If something is wrong the picture is edited ONE more time with exact instructions. Whatever is best after that is the FINAL // picture (SH_IMAGE_MAX_TRIES = 2 tries, never more than SH_IMAGE_MAX_REQUESTS = 2 picture requests per product in total, // refusals included). // 5) The Hallan logo (turban + namaste, silver, 5% opacity) is laid over the middle of the final picture. // Env vars (all optional): SH_IMAGE_MAX_TRIES (2) SH_IMAGE_MAX_REQUESTS (2) SH_IMAGE_BUDGET_SEC (240) SH_IMAGE_CALL_SEC (110) // SH_IMAGE_HOLD_FAILED=1 (hold a picture that still has problems back from the website; default: it is used as the final picture) // SH_IMAGE_KEEP_SMALL_PRINT=0 (remove all small print instead of keeping it) SH_IMAGE_SAFE_MODE=1 (second try removes all small print) // SH_WATERMARK=off SH_WATERMARK_SIZE (0.14 = logo height as a fraction of the picture) SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY (7 = x the built-in 5%) SH_WATERMARK_GREY (70) SH_WATERMARK_EMBOSS=off const SH_IMG_MAX_TRIES = 1; // one picture per product: no automatic checker and no automatic redo (the person compares it with the real pack) const SH_IMG_MAX_REQUESTS = Math.min(6, Math.max(1, Number(process.env.SH_IMAGE_MAX_REQUESTS) || 2)); const SH_IMG_BUDGET_MS = Math.max(60, Number(process.env.SH_IMAGE_BUDGET_SEC) || 240) * 1000; const SH_PIC_NOTE_TAG = 'Picture check: '; // Only the SHORT prompt exists now (the long prompt was removed). const SH_IMG_CHECK_ON = false; // the Gemini picture checker is switched off const shEnvOn = (v, dflt) => { const x = String(v == null ? '' : v).trim().toLowerCase(); return x === '' ? dflt : ['1', 'true', 'yes', 'on'].includes(x); }; // Small print is KEPT when the model can draw it correctly; only a word / line it cannot draw correctly is removed. const SH_KEEP_SMALL_PRINT = shEnvOn(process.env.SH_IMAGE_KEEP_SMALL_PRINT, true); const SH_IMG_HOLD_FAILED = shEnvOn(process.env.SH_IMAGE_HOLD_FAILED, false); // One picture request is never retried on a timeout inside the call: after SH_IMAGE_CALL_SEC (default 110) it is given up. const SH_IMG_CALL_MS = Math.max(40, Number(process.env.SH_IMAGE_CALL_SEC) || 110) * 1000; // A short, plain version of the instructions, used only when the full prompt is refused (IMAGE_SAFETY false alarms happen). function shEditPromptLite(p, fixes) { const extra = (fixes && fixes.length) ? '\nAlso fix these problems found in an earlier attempt:\n' + fixes.map((f, i) => (i + 1) + '. ' + f).join('\n') : ''; return `Edit this product photo of "${p.title}" into a clean e-commerce image of the same pack, front view only. - Keep the pack exactly as it is: same artwork, colours, shape, brand logo and ALL the text and lines printed on it. Do not remove or shorten any text, barcode or artwork that you can draw correctly; change only what is listed here. - Remove: any price label or sticker; any date or batch code; any shadow or reflection; hands, objects and the background. - If a word would come out garbled or unreadable, delete that word, and the whole line it belongs to; never invent words. - ALSO REMOVE EVERY WATERMARK: a faint, ghost-like, semi-transparent logo, icon or symbol drawn over the picture (for example a palm-tree, lamp or hands icon, or a shop / website mark). It is often in the MIDDLE of the pack over the flat colour areas, but may be in a corner or along an edge. It is not part of the pack: erase it completely and rebuild the colours and artwork underneath so the area looks clean and solid. Keep only the manufacturer's own brand logo that is part of the pack design. - Show the whole pack upright and facing the camera, centred in a square frame on a pure white background (#FFFFFF), longest side about 85% of the frame, with no shadow at all.${extra} Output one square 1:1 image.`; } async function shGeminiImageOnce(prompt, images, label, altPrompts, budget) { const imgParts = images.map(im => ({ inlineData: { mimeType: im.mime || 'image/jpeg', data: Buffer.from(im.buffer).toString('base64') } })); // Nano Banana Pro only. A further request is made ONLY when the one before returned no picture (a safety refusal or an // error), alternating the full and the short wording, and never more than budget.left requests for the whole product. const alts = (altPrompts || []).filter(Boolean); const cycle = [prompt].concat(alts); const t00 = Date.now(); const tag = (budget && budget.tag) || label; let lastWhy = '', lastErr = null, asked = 0, withSize = !!GEMINI_IMAGE_REQUEST_SIZE, i = 0; while (budget.left > 0) { const model = (i >= cycle.length && GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK && budget.left === 1) ? GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK : GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL; if (asked > 0 && Date.now() - t00 > SH_IMG_BUDGET_MS) { console.warn('[shopify] picture request stopped: time used up'); break; } const imageConfig = { aspectRatio: '1:1' }; if (withSize) imageConfig.imageSize = GEMINI_IMAGE_REQUEST_SIZE; const body = { contents: [{ role: 'user', parts: [{ text: cycle[i % cycle.length] }].concat(imgParts) }], generationConfig: { responseModalities: ['TEXT', 'IMAGE'], imageConfig } }; const promptKind = (budget.kinds && budget.kinds[i % cycle.length]) || ((i % cycle.length) === 0 ? 'FULL (long) prompt' : 'SHORT (lite) prompt'); const tReq = Date.now(); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + tag + ' >> REQUEST to Gemini: model=' + model + ' | ' + promptKind + ' (' + String(cycle[i % cycle.length]).length + ' chars) | input images=' + images.length + ' | size=' + (withSize ? GEMINI_IMAGE_REQUEST_SIZE : 'default') + ' | requests left in budget=' + budget.left); let data; try { data = await shGeminiCall(model, body, label, SH_IMG_CALL_MS, 1); budget.left--; asked++; i++; } catch (e) { lastErr = e; console.error('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + tag + ' << ERROR from Gemini after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - tReq) / 1000) + 's: model=' + model + ' | ' + promptKind + ' | ' + String(e.message).slice(0, 300)); if (withSize && /imageSize|image_size|image size/i.test(e.message)) { withSize = false; continue; } // this model does not take a size: ask without it (not counted) budget.left--; asked++; i++; console.error('[shopify] picture model ' + model + ' failed:', e.message); if (/\((400|401|403|404)\)|429|quota/i.test(e.message)) break; // asking again cannot help continue; } const cand = data && data.candidates && data.candidates[0]; const rp = (cand && cand.content && cand.content.parts) || []; for (const part of rp) { const inl = part.inlineData || part.inline_data; if (inl && inl.data) { const buffer = Buffer.from(inl.data, 'base64'); if (buffer.length > 2000) { console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + tag + ' << PICTURE RECEIVED in ' + Math.round((Date.now() - tReq) / 1000) + 's: model=' + model + ' | ' + promptKind + ' | ' + Math.round(buffer.length / 1024) + ' KB | finishReason=' + ((cand && cand.finishReason) || '-')); return { buffer, mime: inl.mimeType || inl.mime_type || 'image/png', model, promptKind, requestsUsed: asked }; } } } const sayText = rp.filter(x => x && x.text).map(x => x.text).join(' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').slice(0, 300); console.error('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + tag + ' << NO PICTURE after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - tReq) / 1000) + 's: model=' + model + ' | ' + promptKind + ' | finishReason=' + ((cand && cand.finishReason) || '-') + ' | blockReason=' + ((data && data.promptFeedback && data.promptFeedback.blockReason) || '-') + (sayText ? ' | model said: ' + sayText : '')); lastWhy = (cand && cand.finishReason) || (data && data.promptFeedback && data.promptFeedback.blockReason) || 'no image in the reply'; console.error('[shopify] gemini photo edit', model, 'request ' + asked + ' gave no image:', lastWhy); // A safety refusal is the same every time: asking the same model again only wastes a request. Go on only if a DIFFERENT model is still to try. if (/SAFETY|PROHIBITED|BLOCK|RECITATION/i.test(lastWhy) && (!GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK || model === GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK)) { console.warn('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + tag + ' -- refused (' + lastWhy + ') and no other model left to try, so no second request is made'); break; } await shSleep(1200); } const blocked = /SAFETY|PROHIBITED|BLOCK|RECITATION/i.test(lastWhy); throw new Error(blocked ? GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL + ' refused to edit this photo (' + lastWhy + ') after ' + asked + ' request' + (asked === 1 ? '' : 's') : 'Gemini could not tidy the photo (' + (lastWhy || (lastErr && lastErr.message) || 'no request left') + ')'); } // Zoomed crops of the pack (needs sharp) so the checker can read tiny print that the full picture hides. async function shQaTiles(buffer) { if (!sharpLib) return []; try { const flat = await sharpLib(buffer).flatten({ background: '#ffffff' }).toBuffer(); const t = await sharpLib(flat).trim({ background: '#ffffff', threshold: 18 }).toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const w = t.info.width, h = t.info.height; if (w < 200 || h < 200) return []; const cols = 2, rows = h > w * 1.3 ? 3 : 2; const tw = Math.min(w, Math.round(w / cols * 1.2)), th = Math.min(h, Math.round(h / rows * 1.2)); const names = rows === 3 ? ['top', 'middle', 'bottom'] : ['top', 'bottom'], sides = ['left', 'right']; const out = []; for (let r = 0; r < rows; r++) for (let c = 0; c < cols; c++) { const left = Math.round((w - tw) * c / (cols - 1)), top = Math.round((h - th) * r / (rows - 1)); const buf = await sharpLib(t.data).extract({ left, top, width: tw, height: th }) .resize({ width: 1024, height: 1024, fit: 'inside', kernel: 'lanczos3' }).jpeg({ quality: 92 }).toBuffer(); out.push({ label: names[r] + '-' + sides[c], buffer: buf, mime: 'image/jpeg' }); } return out; } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] zoom crops for the check skipped:', e.message); return []; } } // Second Gemini call: READ the tidied picture and report what is still wrong. Anything not positively OK counts as a problem. async function shCheckPicture(original, edited, title) { const tiles = await shQaTiles(edited.buffer); const prompt = `You are a strict quality inspector for e-commerce product pictures. Product: "${title}". IMAGE 1 is the ORIGINAL photo. IMAGE 2 is the EDITED picture that will be published.${tiles.length ? ' After them come ' + tiles.length + ' ZOOMED CROPS of IMAGE 2 (labelled) so you can read the tiny print. Use them: read every line in them.' : ''} Inspect IMAGE 2 very closely. First transcribe ALL the text you can see in IMAGE 2, line by line, exactly as it appears (including mistakes). Then judge it. Rules the edited picture must meet: - NO price of any kind (price stickers, "£" amounts, PMP, offer or promo labels added by a shop). - NO date or batch code of any kind (best before, use by, expiry, EXP, MFD, lot / batch numbers, printed codes). - NO semi-transparent logo, watermark or symbol laid over the picture (for example a hands-together icon, a palm-tree or lamp symbol, a Somaya's mark, a "Hallan" mark). These can be very FAINT and ghost-like: look specifically at the MIDDLE of the pack and at the big flat colour areas in IMAGE 2 and in every zoomed crop, and compare with IMAGE 1. The manufacturer's own printed brand logo is fine. - NO shadow or reflection of any kind: no grey shadow under, behind or beside the pack, no floating-above-a-shadow look, no grey smudge on the white background. - EVERY word must be a real, correctly spelled word that is also on the original pack AND must be sharp enough to read with certainty. Any word or line that is gibberish, misspelled, smeared, soft, blurry, too small to read with certainty, or invented (like "kjjs" or "ndds", or a barcode whose digits are not crisp) is a fault. For each fault give the bad word and the COMPLETE printed line or sentence it sits in (for a barcode give the digits line). - The pack artwork, colours and shape must match the original (apart from the removed items above). Text or artwork that exists only in IMAGE 2 is invented. - The whole pack must be visible (nothing cropped), upright and square to the camera (no tilt or perspective), alone (no hands or objects), centred on a plain white background. Reply with ONE JSON object only, no markdown fences, exactly these keys: {"all_text": ["line 1", "line 2"], "price_or_offer_visible": false, "price_details": "", "date_or_batch_code_visible": false, "date_details": "", "overlay_logo_or_watermark_visible": false, "overlay_details": "", "shadow_or_reflection_visible": false, "shadow_details": "", "garbled_text": [{"text": "bad word", "line": "the complete line or sentence containing it"}], "invented_or_changed_text": [{"text": "word", "line": "the complete line or sentence"}], "pack_artwork_changed": false, "pack_cropped_or_cut_off": false, "pack_tilted_or_distorted": false, "hands_or_other_objects_visible": false, "more_than_one_pack": false, "background_not_plain_white": false, "summary": "one short sentence"}`; const inline = im => ({ inlineData: { mimeType: im.mime || 'image/jpeg', data: Buffer.from(im.buffer).toString('base64') } }); const parts = [{ text: prompt }, { text: 'IMAGE 1 (ORIGINAL):' }, inline(original), { text: 'IMAGE 2 (EDITED, to be checked):' }, inline(edited)]; tiles.forEach(t => { parts.push({ text: 'ZOOMED CROP of IMAGE 2, ' + t.label + ' part of the pack:' }); parts.push(inline(t)); }); const body = { contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }], generationConfig: { temperature: 0, maxOutputTokens: 8192, responseMimeType: 'application/json' } }; const data = await shGeminiCall(GEMINI_TEXT_MODEL, body, 'Checking the picture', 90000, 2); const text = shGeminiText(data); if (!text) throw new Error('the checker returned nothing'); const j = shParseJsonLoose(text); const yes = v => v === true || String(v).toLowerCase() === 'true'; const list = v => (Array.isArray(v) ? v : []).map(x => (typeof x === 'string' ? { text: x, line: x } : { text: shStr(x && x.text, 80), line: shStr(x && (x.line || x.sentence), 220) })).filter(x => x.text || x.line); const issues = [], fixes = []; if (yes(j.price_or_offer_visible)) { issues.push('a price / offer is still visible' + (j.price_details ? ' (' + shStr(j.price_details, 120) + ')' : '')); fixes.push('Remove EVERY price, price sticker, price marker and shop offer label' + (j.price_details ? ' (seen: ' + shStr(j.price_details, 120) + ')' : '') + ' and restore the pack artwork underneath.'); } if (yes(j.date_or_batch_code_visible)) { issues.push('a date / batch code is still visible' + (j.date_details ? ' (' + shStr(j.date_details, 120) + ')' : '')); fixes.push('Remove EVERY best-before / expiry / production date and batch or lot code' + (j.date_details ? ' (seen: ' + shStr(j.date_details, 120) + ')' : '') + ' including the stamped patch, and restore the pack surface underneath.'); } if (yes(j.overlay_logo_or_watermark_visible)) { issues.push('a transparent logo / watermark is still visible' + (j.overlay_details ? ' (' + shStr(j.overlay_details, 120) + ')' : '')); fixes.push('Remove the semi-transparent logo / watermark / symbol laid over the picture' + (j.overlay_details ? ' (seen: ' + shStr(j.overlay_details, 120) + ')' : '') + ' completely and rebuild the pack artwork beneath it. Keep only the manufacturer\'s own printed brand logo.'); } if (yes(j.shadow_or_reflection_visible)) { issues.push('a shadow / reflection is visible' + (j.shadow_details ? ' (' + shStr(j.shadow_details, 120) + ')' : '')); fixes.push('Remove every shadow and reflection' + (j.shadow_details ? ' (seen: ' + shStr(j.shadow_details, 120) + ')' : '') + ': the pack is a flat-on cut-out on perfectly flat pure white with NO shadow under, behind or beside it.'); } const garbled = list(j.garbled_text), changed = list(j.invented_or_changed_text); garbled.forEach(g => { issues.push('unreadable text "' + (g.text || g.line) + '"'); fixes.push('Delete the garbled / blurry word "' + (g.text || g.line) + '"' + (g.line && g.line !== g.text ? ' AND delete the WHOLE line / sentence it belongs to: "' + g.line + '"' : '') + ', and fill the space naturally with the pack artwork or background colour.'); }); changed.forEach(g => { issues.push('text that is not on the original pack "' + (g.text || g.line) + '"'); fixes.push('The text "' + (g.line || g.text) + '" does not match the original pack: delete that whole line / sentence (do not rewrite it) and fill the space naturally.'); }); if (yes(j.pack_artwork_changed)) { issues.push('the pack artwork / colours differ from the original'); fixes.push('Keep the pack artwork, colours and shape exactly as in IMAGE 1; do not redraw or restyle anything.'); } if (yes(j.pack_cropped_or_cut_off)) { issues.push('the pack is cropped or cut off'); fixes.push('Show the whole pack inside the frame with equal white margins; nothing may be cut off.'); } if (yes(j.pack_tilted_or_distorted)) { issues.push('the pack is tilted or distorted'); fixes.push('Straighten the pack completely: no tilt, no perspective, no rotation, edges perfectly vertical and horizontal.'); } if (yes(j.hands_or_other_objects_visible)) { issues.push('a hand or another object is visible'); fixes.push('Remove every hand, finger and other object; show the single pack only.'); } if (yes(j.more_than_one_pack)) { issues.push('more than one pack is shown'); fixes.push('Show exactly ONE pack.'); } if (yes(j.background_not_plain_white)) { issues.push('the background is not plain white'); fixes.push('Make the whole background pure white (#FFFFFF), seamless, with no frame or border.'); } return { ok: issues.length === 0, issues, fixes, textFault: garbled.length + changed.length > 0, summary: shStr(j.summary, 200) }; } // Measures the final picture itself (needs sharp): exact size, white corners, pack size and centring. async function shCheckLayout(buffer) { if (!sharpLib) return { ok: true, skipped: true, issues: [] }; const issues = []; try { const meta = await sharpLib(buffer).metadata(); if (meta.width !== SH_IMG_CANVAS || meta.height !== SH_IMG_CANVAS) issues.push('picture size is ' + meta.width + 'x' + meta.height + ', not ' + SH_IMG_CANVAS + 'x' + SH_IMG_CANVAS); const { data, info } = await sharpLib(buffer).removeAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const lum = (x, y) => { const i = (y * info.width + x) * info.channels; return (data[i] + data[i + 1] + data[i + 2]) / 3; }; const m = 3, w = info.width - 1 - m, h = info.height - 1 - m; if ([lum(m, m), lum(w, m), lum(m, h), lum(w, h)].some(v => v < 245)) issues.push('the picture corners are not pure white'); const t = await sharpLib(buffer).trim({ background: '#ffffff', threshold: 18 }).toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); const bw = t.info.width, bh = t.info.height; const fill = Math.max(bw, bh) / SH_IMG_CANVAS; if (Math.abs(fill - SH_IMG_FILL) > 0.03) issues.push('the pack fills ' + Math.round(fill * 100) + '% of the frame instead of ' + Math.round(SH_IMG_FILL * 100) + '%'); if (typeof t.info.trimOffsetLeft === 'number' && typeof t.info.trimOffsetTop === 'number') { const cx = (-t.info.trimOffsetLeft + bw / 2) / SH_IMG_CANVAS, cy = (-t.info.trimOffsetTop + bh / 2) / SH_IMG_CANVAS; if (Math.abs(cx - 0.5) > 0.02 || Math.abs(cy - 0.5) > 0.02) issues.push('the pack is not centred in the frame'); } } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] layout check could not run:', e.message); return { ok: true, skipped: true, issues: [] }; } return { ok: issues.length === 0, issues }; } // ---- The Hallan logo (turban + namaste, silver, 5% opacity), laid over the middle of every tidied picture ----------------- // Embedded here (a lossless 27 KB PNG, only the cropped logo) so nothing extra has to be uploaded next to server.js. const SH_WATERMARK_ON = shEnvOn(process.env.SH_WATERMARK, true); const SH_WATERMARK_SIZE = Math.min(1, Math.max(0.04, Number(process.env.SH_WATERMARK_SIZE) || 0.14)); const SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY = Math.min(19, Math.max(0.1, Number(process.env.SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY) || 7)); // x the built-in ~5% opacity (7 = about 35%) const SH_WATERMARK_GREY = (() => { const v = (process.env.SH_WATERMARK_GREY || '').trim(); const n = Number(v); return v !== '' && Number.isFinite(n) ? Math.min(255, Math.max(0, n)) : 70; })(); // 0 black .. 255 white const SH_WATERMARK_EMBOSS = shEnvOn(process.env.SH_WATERMARK_EMBOSS, true); // a light copy 2-3 px behind the dark one, so the symbol shows on dark AND light packs let shLogoRaw = null; async function shLogoLayer(heightPx, mult, grey) { if (!shLogoRaw) { const { data, info } = await sharpLib(Buffer.from(SH_WATERMARK_B64, 'base64')).ensureAlpha().raw().toBuffer({ resolveWithObject: true }); shLogoRaw = { data, width: info.width, height: info.height }; } const out = Buffer.from(shLogoRaw.data); for (let i = 0; i < out.length; i += 4) { const al = Math.min(255, Math.round(out[i + 3] * mult)); out[i] = grey; out[i + 1] = grey; out[i + 2] = grey; out[i + 3] = al; } return sharpLib(out, { raw: { width: shLogoRaw.width, height: shLogoRaw.height, channels: 4 } }) .resize({ height: heightPx, fit: 'inside', kernel: 'lanczos3' }).png().toBuffer(); } const SH_WATERMARK_B64 = 'iVBORw0KGgoAAAANSUhEUgAAAocAAASXCAMAAACkzCVxAAADAFBMVEXAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMDAwMBw5kZ8AAAADnRSTlMAAQIDBAUGBwgJCgsMDRK8QVgAAGV2SURBVHja7V2JgqS4jkxzH+L/f7eKM4EEWzY+ZCzvvpme6u6qBAIdoZD0+fB59xHbufwq3yA+7jH4OaEvm444AJJvEx93+Nv9R15UddO0Xdf1y+m6tqnrqsizg4XkG8fHsiteEFjWzT/4AIbfA//nH5HtPxyzzVXzveNjF4p59Y/ASwCOGDwCsu+auhAMRT7WDOH0r7xu/43g1+zB8qtBgsoRjCVjkY+NqPD/ZGXT7SEICxBhgFvruP4G9O1iF/lm8nloCGfrNuNuD0TlgcUuNgXDkM+WMuiwe9MfHEG498STFcRB8OjBoWsKfgZ8TE5WrZZw9cPrvwaNs9nONVjkk6QJXH+d5Xkxn/xrEy8N5PQlUf6DcB8I/uTEsnz5DMP59H3FDyVdtqWsmrbd+OaJcu7apioXju+cQcz+uAP4emBY/wewhYkn+P184QeH/z93aPh5pGUHFwj+I/BIOR9+PTLOZS4O8eNnMoWwT3l/DN8+SJyQ+f3TP39nB8NhaDJ+OMmRLVXbbVg5n6/VmhjnagbjBMPRFO7/jiTsO3HX14Ti9xt1nDSndv7B1O8xeKZczuTLxDhnqyk8koRw7W5/I0JphDhAycYwLVN4JFtg2LPOPxj8GrCRcW73pvDoxPWy5B/T2eQf5rJTAmI5gXCF4DmZuCOiv/nwxW/DMyBOxpAhmBQKq25n5S6hs2cCLyO54WQJr1KcmyLetYf+jwxzfjQpnQmFO1RcJcprgnEBqwWF39LJsM9nZPZQ/ls1R4YpnUOOsY8Ph+E2RYFLi7jh7lpYo+Og//9syY8mofSkaOHSEy+YHG4yZYVGAZBShnsL2XOCkhJR06gKvwBHWyl1rnDHzVz9Jbhgurfv37JPjtr async function shApplyWatermark(buffer, mime) { if (!SH_WATERMARK_ON || !sharpLib) return { buffer, mime, applied: false }; try { const meta = await sharpLib(buffer).metadata(); const hPx = Math.max(24, Math.round(meta.height * SH_WATERMARK_SIZE)); const dark = await shLogoLayer(hPx, SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY, SH_WATERMARK_GREY); const lm = await sharpLib(dark).metadata(); const left = Math.round((meta.width - lm.width) / 2), top = Math.round((meta.height - lm.height) / 2); const layers = []; if (SH_WATERMARK_EMBOSS) { const off = Math.max(1, Math.round(hPx * 0.018)); layers.push({ input: await shLogoLayer(hPx, SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY, 255), left: left + off, top: top + off, blend: 'over' }); } layers.push({ input: dark, left, top, blend: 'over' }); const out = await sharpLib(buffer).flatten({ background: '#ffffff' }) .composite(layers) .jpeg({ quality: 94, mozjpeg: true }).toBuffer(); return { buffer: out, mime: 'image/jpeg', applied: true }; } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] logo could not be added:', e.message); return { buffer, mime, applied: false }; } } // Cleans up the REAL front photo and CHECKS the result. Returns { buffer, mime, model, qa:{ ok, attempts, issues[], warnings[] } }. // The pack artwork is kept as photographed - nothing is drawn from scratch. At most SH_IMG_MAX_TRIES tries (2) and // SH_IMG_MAX_REQUESTS picture requests (2) per product; the best picture after that is the final one, with the logo added. async function shEditFrontPhoto(p, front) { const started = Date.now(); const budget = { left: SH_IMG_MAX_REQUESTS }; budget.tag = 'stock ' + p.stockid + ' "' + String(p.title || '').slice(0, 40) + '"'; const frontHash = (() => { try { return crypto.createHash('md5').update(Buffer.from(front.buffer)).digest('hex').slice(0, 10); } catch (_) { return '?'; } })(); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' == PICTURE RUN START | front photo id=' + front.id + ' role=' + front.role + ' ' + Math.round(Buffer.from(front.buffer).length / 1024) + ' KB md5=' + frontHash + ' | image model=' + GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL + ' | max tries=' + SH_IMG_MAX_TRIES + ' max requests=' + SH_IMG_MAX_REQUESTS + ' | image fallback model=' + (GEMINI_IMAGE_FALLBACK || 'none')); let best = null, prev = null, fixes = [], lastMs = 0, lastErr = null, tries = 0, textFault = false; for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= SH_IMG_MAX_TRIES; attempt++) { if (budget.left <= 0) break; if (attempt > 1 && (Date.now() - started) + lastMs > SH_IMG_BUDGET_MS) { console.warn('[shopify] second picture try skipped: time budget used for stock ' + p.stockid); break; } const t0 = Date.now(); tries = attempt; let final = null, warnings = [], layout = { ok: true, issues: [] }, qa, model = GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL, cur_prompt_kind = '?'; try { const retry = !!(prev && fixes.length); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- TRY ' + attempt + ' of ' + SH_IMG_MAX_TRIES + ': ' + (retry ? 'RETRY (sends the ORIGINAL photo + the previous rejected picture + ' + fixes.length + ' fixes)' : 'FIRST try (sends the original photo only)') + ' | requests left=' + budget.left); const shortP = shEditPromptLite(p, retry ? fixes : []); budget.kinds = ['SHORT (lite) prompt']; const raw = await shGeminiImageOnce(shortP, [front], retry ? 'Tidying the front photo (second try)' : 'Tidying the front photo', [], budget); model = raw.model || model; cur_prompt_kind = raw.promptKind || '?'; console.log('[shopify] picture stock ' + p.stockid + ' try ' + attempt + ': ' + model + ' answered in ' + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000) + 's'); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- TRY ' + attempt + ' picture came from: ' + model + ' using the ' + cur_prompt_kind + ' (picture requests used so far in this run: ' + (SH_IMG_MAX_REQUESTS - budget.left) + ')'); const norm = await shNormalizePack(raw.buffer); final = norm || raw; if (!norm && sharpLib) warnings.push('the picture could not be set to the standard size (pack edges not found)'); } catch (e) { lastErr = e; console.error('[shopify] picture try ' + attempt + ' for stock ' + p.stockid + ' failed:', e.message); lastMs = Date.now() - t0; continue; } let unverified = false; try { qa = SH_IMG_CHECK_ON ? await shCheckPicture(front, final, p.title) : { ok: true, issues: [], fixes: [], textFault: false, summary: 'check switched off' }; } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] picture check failed for stock ' + p.stockid + ':', e.message); unverified = true; qa = { ok: false, issues: ['the automatic picture check could not run (' + e.message + ')'], fixes: [], textFault: false }; } const issues = qa.issues.concat(layout.issues); const ok = qa.ok && layout.ok; console.log('[shopify] picture stock ' + p.stockid + ' try ' + attempt + ' checked in ' + Math.round((Date.now() - t0) / 1000) + 's total: ' + (ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL - ' + issues.join('; ').slice(0, 300))); const HEAVY = /background|artwork|colours? differ|shadow|price|date|batch|watermark|logo|cropped|tilt|not centred|standard size/i; const score = unverified ? 50 : issues.reduce((n, t) => n + (HEAVY.test(String(t)) ? 10 : 1), 0); // a wrong background must never beat a few doubtful words const cand = { img: final, model, score, result: { ok, attempts: attempt, issues, warnings }, promptKind: cur_prompt_kind }; console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- TRY ' + attempt + ' RESULT: ' + (ok ? 'PASS' : 'FAIL') + ' | score=' + score + ' (fewer problems = better)' + (issues.length ? ' | problems: ' + issues.join(' ;; ').slice(0, 900) : '')); if (!best || score < best.score) { best = cand; console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- TRY ' + attempt + ' is now the BEST picture so far'); } else console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- TRY ' + attempt + ' is WORSE than the best so far (score ' + best.score + ' from try ' + best.result.attempts + '), so it is thrown away'); if (ok) { best = cand; break; } if (unverified) break; // another edit cannot fix a checker that did not run fixes = qa.fixes.slice(0, 12); textFault = !!qa.textFault; prev = final; lastMs = Date.now() - t0; } if (!best) { console.error('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' == PICTURE RUN FAILED after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - started) / 1000) + 's: ' + (lastErr && lastErr.message)); throw (lastErr || new Error('Gemini could not tidy the photo')); } console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' == PICTURE RUN END after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - started) / 1000) + 's | FINAL picture = try ' + best.result.attempts + ' | model=' + best.model + ' | ' + (best.promptKind || '?') + ' | ' + (best.result.ok ? 'PASSED the check' : 'did NOT pass: ' + best.result.issues.join(' ;; ').slice(0, 600))); best.result.attempts = tries; const wm = await shApplyWatermark(best.img.buffer, best.img.mime); if (!wm.applied && SH_WATERMARK_ON) best.result.warnings.push('the Hallan logo could not be added'); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] ' + budget.tag + ' -- LOGO: ' + (wm.applied ? 'added (small centre symbol: size ' + SH_WATERMARK_SIZE + ', opacity x' + SH_WATERMARK_OPACITY + ', grey ' + SH_WATERMARK_GREY + ', emboss ' + (SH_WATERMARK_EMBOSS ? 'on' : 'off') + ')' : (SH_WATERMARK_ON ? 'NOT added (sharp ' + (sharpLib ? 'is loaded, see the error above' : 'is NOT installed') + ')' : 'switched off by SH_WATERMARK'))); return { buffer: wm.buffer, mime: wm.mime, model: best.model, qa: best.result }; } // Generate whatever is missing for one product (or redo a part when forced). // part: 'all' | 'text' | 'image'. Needs at least a FRONT photo taken with the camera button. async function shGenerateOne(r, by, force, part) { const p = shBuildPayload(r); if (shGenInFlight.has(p.stockid)) throw new Error('This product is already being generated'); shGenInFlight.add(p.stockid); try { const existing = await shLoadContent(p.stockid, false); const legacy = !!(existing && existing.description_html && !existing.has_image && !existing.image_source); // made before photos were used: redo it from the photos const haveText = !!(existing && existing.description_html) && !legacy; const haveImage = !!(existing && existing.has_image); // part says which piece this call is for: 'all' (default), 'text' or 'image'. The portal asks for the text and the // picture in two separate calls so that neither request runs for too long. const wantText = part === 'all' || part === 'text', wantImage = part === 'all' || part === 'image'; const doText = wantText && (force || !haveText); const doImage = wantImage && (force || !haveImage); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] stock ' + p.stockid + ' shGenerateOne: part=' + part + ' force=' + !!force + ' | already has text=' + haveText + ' picture=' + haveImage + ' | will do text=' + doText + ' picture=' + doImage); if (!doText && !doImage) return { status: 'cached', needs_review: !!(existing && existing.needs_review) }; const photos = await shLoadPhotos(p.stockid, true); const front = photos.find(x => x.role === 'front') || photos[0]; if (!front) throw new Error('No photos yet — open the camera button on this product: press "Pull from website" to copy its pictures from the website, or take the FRONT (and the back / ingredients) of the pack first'); let needsReview = !!(existing && existing.needs_review); if (doText) { const t = await shGenText(p, r, photos); await shSaveContent(p.stockid, { short_desc: t.short_description, description: t.description_html, ingredients: t.ingredients, allergens: t.allergens, nutrition: t.nutrition ? JSON.stringify(t.nutrition) : null, storage: t.storage, seo_title: t.seo_title, seo_desc: t.seo_description, tags: t.tags.join(', '), pack_desc: t.pack_description, needs_review: t.needs_review ? 1 : 0, review_note: t.review_note, text_model: GEMINI_TEXT_MODEL, generated_by: by || null }, { stamp: true }); needsReview = t.needs_review; } if (!doImage) return { status: 'generated', needs_review: needsReview }; // Records the result of the picture check in the review note (and the review flag) so the preview window shows it. const noteAfterPicture = async (issuesText) => { const cur = await shLoadContent(p.stockid, false); const base = String((cur && cur.review_note) || '').replace(/\s*Picture check:[\s\S]*$/, '').trim(); const hadTag = /Picture check:/.test(String((cur && cur.review_note) || '')); if (issuesText) { await shSaveContent(p.stockid, { needs_review: 1, review_note: shStr((base ? base + ' ' : '') + SH_PIC_NOTE_TAG + issuesText, 900) }); return true; } if (hadTag) { await shSaveContent(p.stockid, { needs_review: base ? 1 : 0, review_note: base || null }); return !!base; } return !!(cur && cur.needs_review); }; let img; try { img = await shEditFrontPhoto(p, front); } catch (e) { // Could not tidy it: the real photo is kept so the product still has a true picture, but it may show a price / date / watermark, so it is held back console.error('[shopify] photo tidy failed for stock ' + p.stockid + ':', e.message); // A picture is already chosen for this product (for example the one made earlier): leave it exactly as it is, only say that this try failed. if (existing && existing.has_image) { console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] stock ' + p.stockid + ' tidy failed, the picture that was already chosen (' + existing.image_source + ') is KEPT unchanged'); const keptWhy = 'a new picture could not be made this time (' + e.message + '). The picture you had before was kept unchanged.'; const nrKept = await noteAfterPicture(keptWhy); return { status: 'generated', needs_review: nrKept, hold_website: existing.image_source !== 'edited', note: 'Picture not changed — ' + e.message + '. The previous picture was kept.' }; } let std = null; try { std = await shNormalizePack(front.buffer); } catch (_) { std = null; } // no AI: only centre it at the standard size on white and drop a floating shadow await shSaveContent(p.stockid, { image_mime: std ? std.mime : front.mime, image_data: std ? std.buffer : front.buffer, image_source: 'original', image_model: null }, { stamp: true }); const why = 'the picture could not be tidied (' + e.message + '). ' + (std ? 'Your photo was only resized, centred on white and cleared of any floating shadow' : 'Your original photo is in use') + ' - it may still show a price, date, watermark or small print.'; const nr = await noteAfterPicture(why); return { status: 'generated', needs_review: nr, hold_website: true, note: 'Picture not tidied — ' + e.message + '. Held back from the website until you check it.' }; } await shSaveContent(p.stockid, { image_mime: img.mime, image_data: img.buffer, image_source: 'edited', image_edited: img.buffer, image_edited_mime: img.mime, image_model: img.model || GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL }, { stamp: true }); if (img.qa.ok) { needsReview = await noteAfterPicture(''); return { status: 'generated', needs_review: needsReview, picture_checked: true, note: img.qa.attempts > 1 ? 'Picture checked and corrected (attempt ' + img.qa.attempts + ')' : '' }; } const why = img.qa.issues.join('; ') + ' (still there after ' + img.qa.attempts + ' try' + (img.qa.attempts === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' - this is the best picture that could be made). Compare it with your photo; use your own photo or tidy it again if needed.'; needsReview = await noteAfterPicture(why); return { status: 'generated', needs_review: needsReview, hold_website: SH_IMG_HOLD_FAILED, note: 'Picture may need a check: ' + img.qa.issues.slice(0, 3).join('; ') + (SH_IMG_HOLD_FAILED ? '. Held back from the website until you check it.' : '. It is used as the final picture.') }; } finally { shGenInFlight.delete(p.stockid); } } // ---------- Shopify: picture upload + filling existing products ---------- async function shUploadImage(buffer, mime, filename) { const data = await shGraphQL( `mutation($input:[StagedUploadInput!]!){ stagedUploadsCreate(input:$input){ stagedTargets{ url resourceUrl parameters{ name value } } userErrors{ field message } } }`, { input: [{ resource: 'IMAGE', filename, mimeType: mime, httpMethod: 'POST' }] } ); const res = data.stagedUploadsCreate; if (res.userErrors && res.userErrors.length) throw new Error('Picture upload: ' + shUserErrors(res.userErrors)); const target = res.stagedTargets && res.stagedTargets[0]; if (!target) throw new Error('Picture upload: Shopify gave no upload address'); const form = new FormData(); (target.parameters || []).forEach(pm => form.append(pm.name, pm.value)); form.append('file', new Blob([buffer], { type: mime }), filename); const up = await fetch(target.url, { method: 'POST', body: form }); if (!up.ok) throw new Error('Picture upload failed (' + up.status + '): ' + (await up.text()).replace(/<[^>]+>/g, ' ').slice(0, 160)); return target.resourceUrl; } async function shMediaInput(content, p) { const mime = content.image.mime || 'image/png'; const ext = /jpe?g/i.test(mime) ? 'jpg' : /webp/i.test(mime) ?
