'webp' : 'png'; const safe = String(p.sku || p.stockid).replace(/[^A-Za-z0-9_-]+/g, '-').slice(0, 60) || 'product'; const resourceUrl = await shUploadImage(content.image.buffer, mime, safe + '.' + ext); return { originalSource: resourceUrl, mediaContentType: 'IMAGE', alt: p.title }; } async function shFillExistingProduct(productId, content, p) { const cur = (await shGraphQL( `query($id:ID!){ product(id:$id){ descriptionHtml media(first:1){ nodes{ id } } } }`, { id: productId } )).product; const applied = { description: false, image: false }; if (!cur) return applied; const input = { id: productId }; let media; const hasDesc = String(cur.descriptionHtml || '').replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '').trim().length > 0; if (!hasDesc && content.description_html) { Object.assign(input, shContentProductFields(content, true)); applied.description = true; } const hasMedia = !!(cur.media && cur.media.nodes && cur.media.nodes.length); if (!hasMedia && content.image) { media = [await shMediaInput(content, p)]; applied.image = true; } if (applied.description || applied.image) { const upd = (await shGraphQL( `mutation($product:ProductUpdateInput!,$media:[CreateMediaInput!]){ productUpdate(product:$product, media:$media){ product{ id } userErrors{ field message } } }`, media ? { product: input, media } : { product: input } )).productUpdate; if (upd.userErrors && upd.userErrors.length) throw new Error(shUserErrors(upd.userErrors)); } return applied; } // ---------- Routes ---------- // Generate content. Body: { stockids:[..] (max 2), force?:bool, part?:'all'|'text'|'image' } // Products that already have everything are skipped for free ("cached"), unless force is set. app.post('/api/stock/shopify/generate', async (req, res) => { if (!process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'GEMINI_API_KEY is not set on the server (Render environment variables).' }); const ids = [...new Set(((req.body && req.body.stockids) || []).map(Number).filter(Number.isFinite))]; if (!ids.length) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'stockids is required' }); if (ids.length > SH_GEN_MAX_PER_REQUEST) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Generate at most ' + SH_GEN_MAX_PER_REQUEST + ' products per request' }); const force = !!(req.body && req.body.force); const part = ['text', 'image'].includes(req.body && req.body.part) ? req.body.part : 'all'; // defer_website: do not queue for the website after this call (the picture call that follows does it). // queue_website: queue for the website after this call even when nothing new was generated (used by the picture call). const deferWebsite = !!(req.body && req.body.defer_website), queueWebsite = !!(req.body && req.body.queue_website); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; const genT0 = Date.now(); const genWho = (part === 'image' && force) ? 'TIDY button (force, picture only)' : (part === 'image' && queueWebsite) ? 'GENERATE button, picture step' : (part === 'text' && deferWebsite) ? 'GENERATE button, text step' : (force ? 'forced regenerate' : 'generate'); console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] REQUEST /generate from ' + genWho + ' | stock ids=' + ids.join(',') + ' | part=' + part + ' force=' + force + ' defer_website=' + deferWebsite + ' queue_website=' + queueWebsite + ' | by=' + (by || '-')); res.on('close', () => { if (!res.writableEnded) console.error('[shopify][PICLOG] /generate (' + genWho + ', stock ' + ids.join(',') + '): the BROWSER/PROXY CONNECTION WAS CLOSED after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - genT0) / 1000) + 's before the server finished - the page will show "failed" but the server may still finish and save the picture'); }); try { const binds = {}; const inList = ids.map((id, i) => { binds['i' + i] = id; return ':i' + i; }).join(','); const dbRows = lower((await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(`${SH_STOCK_SELECT} WHERE s.STOCKID IN (${inList})`, binds))).rows); const byId = new Map(dbRows.map(r => [Number(r.stockid), r])); const results = []; for (const id of ids) { const r = byId.get(id); if (!r) { results.push({ stockid: id, ok: false, error: 'Product not found' }); continue; } try { const out = await shGenerateOne(r, by, force, part); // Newly generated text/picture -> queue it for the website (PowerShell picks it up within ~2 min). // WOO_GENERATE_MODE: FULL (default) replaces the website's text/picture, FILL only fills blanks. // A picture that failed its check is held back: it is not sent to the website until someone looks at it. const wantQueue = !out.hold_website && !deferWebsite && (out.status === 'generated' || queueWebsite); const queued = wantQueue ? await wooAutoQueue(id, by, WOO_GENERATE_MODE, 'generated') : false; results.push({ stockid: id, ok: true, status: out.status, needs_review: !!out.needs_review, note: out.note || '', website_queued: queued, held: !!out.hold_website, picture_checked: !!out.picture_checked }); } catch (e) { console.error('[shopify] generate failed for stock ' + id + ':', e.message); results.push({ stockid: id, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } console.log('[shopify][PICLOG] /generate (' + genWho + ') FINISHED after ' + Math.round((Date.now() - genT0) / 1000) + 's | browser still connected: ' + (res.writableEnded || res.socket && !res.socket.destroyed ? 'YES' : 'NO') + ' | results: ' + JSON.stringify(results.map(x => ({ id: x.stockid, ok: x.ok, status: x.status, err: x.error || undefined, note: (x.note || '').slice(0, 120) })))); res.json({ results, models: { text: GEMINI_TEXT_MODEL, image: GEMINI_IMAGE_MODEL } }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Everything generated for one product (the picture comes back as a data: URL for the preview window) app.get('/api/stock/shopify/content/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); try { const c = await shLoadContent(id, true); if (!c) return res.json({ exists: false }); const url = im => im ? 'data:' + im.mime + ';base64,' + Buffer.from(im.buffer).toString('base64') : null; const out = { ...c, exists: true, image_data_url: url(c.image), edited_data_url: url(c.edited) }; // the real front photo (small version) so the page can show "your photo" next to the tidied picture const front = await withConnection(async conn => { const rr = await conn.execute( `SELECT THUMB_DATA, MIME FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS WHERE STOCKID = :id ORDER BY CASE ROLE WHEN 'front' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END, ID FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { id }); return lower(rr.rows)[0] || null; }); out.front_photo_data_url = front && front.thumb_data ? 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + Buffer.from(front.thumb_data).toString('base64') : null; delete out.image; delete out.edited; res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Save edits made in the preview window. Saving counts as "reviewed" (clears the review flag). app.put('/api/stock/shopify/content/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); const b = req.body || {}; const f = {}; if (b.short_description !== undefined) f.short_desc = shStr(b.short_description, 2000); if (b.description_html !== undefined) f.description = shCleanHtml(b.description_html); if (b.ingredients !== undefined) f.ingredients = shStr(b.ingredients, 4000); if (b.nutrition !== undefined) { const n = shNormNutrition(b.nutrition); f.nutrition = n ? JSON.stringify(n) : null; } if (b.storage !== undefined) f.storage = shStr(b.storage, 1000); if (b.allergens !== undefined) f.allergens = shStr(b.allergens, 1000); if (b.seo_title !== undefined) f.seo_title = shStr(b.seo_title, 300); if (b.seo_description !== undefined) f.seo_desc = shStr(b.seo_description, 600); if (b.tags !== undefined) { const arr = Array.isArray(b.tags) ? b.tags : String(b.tags || '').split(','); f.tags = arr.map(t => shStr(t, 40).toLowerCase()).filter(Boolean).filter((t, i, a) => a.indexOf(t) === i).slice(0, 12).join(', '); } if (b.reviewed) { f.needs_review = 0; f.review_note = null; } try { await shSaveContent(id, f); // Saved edits are the checked version: send them to the website, replacing the website's own text (FULL). const queued = await wooAutoQueue(id, (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null, 'FULL', 'content-saved'); res.json({ ok: true, website_queued: queued }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Photos taken with the camera button ---- // List (small versions only) app.get('/api/stock/shopify/photos/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); try { const rows = await withConnection(async conn => lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT ID, ROLE, MIME, THUMB_DATA, CREATED_AT, CREATED_BY, SOURCE FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS WHERE STOCKID = :id ORDER BY CASE ROLE WHEN 'front' THEN 0 WHEN 'back' THEN 1 WHEN 'nutrition' THEN 2 WHEN 'side' THEN 3 ELSE 4 END, ID`, { id })).rows)); // what the website import did for this product (the PowerShell sync writes it) let imp = null; try { imp = await withConnection(async conn => lower((await conn.execute( `SELECT STATUS, MESSAGE, IMPORTED, FINISHED_AT FROM WOO_PHOTO_IMPORT WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { id })).rows)[0] || null); } catch (_) { imp = null; } res.json({ photos: rows.map(r => ({ id: Number(r.id), role: r.role, created_at: r.created_at, created_by: r.created_by || '', source: r.source === 'website' ? 'website' : 'camera', thumb: r.thumb_data ? 'data:image/jpeg;base64,' + Buffer.from(r.thumb_data).toString('base64') : null })), import: imp ? { status: imp.status, message: imp.message || '', imported: Number(imp.imported) || 0, finished_at: imp.finished_at } : null }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Add one photo. Body: { role?, mime, data (base64 of the picture), thumb (base64 of a small version) } app.post('/api/stock/shopify/photos/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); const b = req.body || {}; const strip = v => String(v || '').replace(/^data:[^;]+;base64,/, ''); const mime = ['image/jpeg', 'image/png', 'image/webp'].includes(b.mime) ? b.mime : 'image/jpeg'; const buf = Buffer.from(strip(b.data), 'base64'); if (buf.length < 1000) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'That photo is empty or unreadable' }); if (buf.length > SH_PHOTO_MAX_BYTES) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'That photo is too large (max 9 MB)' }); let thumb = b.thumb ? Buffer.from(strip(b.thumb), 'base64') : null; if (!thumb || thumb.length < 500) thumb = buf; const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const cnt = lower((await conn.execute(`SELECT COUNT(*) AS N, SUM(CASE WHEN ROLE = 'front' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS F FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { id })).rows)[0]; if (Number(cnt.n) >= SH_PHOTO_MAX_PER_PRODUCT) return { error: 'Up to ' + SH_PHOTO_MAX_PER_PRODUCT + ' photos per product — delete one first' }; const role = SH_PHOTO_ROLES.includes(b.role) ? b.role : (Number(cnt.f) > 0 ? 'back' : 'front'); const ins = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO SHOPIFY_PHOTOS (STOCKID, ROLE, MIME, IMAGE_DATA, THUMB_DATA, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:id, :role, :mime, :img, :thumb, :byu) RETURNING ID INTO :oid`, { id, role, mime, img: { val: buf, type: oracledb.BLOB }, thumb: { val: thumb, type: oracledb.BLOB }, byu: by, oid: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true }); return { id: Number(ins.outBinds.oid[0]), role }; }); if (out.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: out.error }); res.json({ ok: true, ...out }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // "Pull from website" button: adds this product to the pull list so its website (WordPress) pictures are copied in as FRONT (1st picture) // and BACK (2nd picture). Nothing is ever pulled automatically. Hallan-WooSync.ps1 does the copying within about a minute; pictures already here (or whose role is already filled) are left alone. app.post('/api/stock/shopify/photos/:stockid/import-website', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad stock id' }); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO WOO_PHOTO_IMPORT t USING (SELECT :id AS STOCKID FROM dual) s ON (t.STOCKID = s.STOCKID) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t.STATUS = 'PENDING', t.REQUESTED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, t.REQUESTED_BY = :byu, t.FINISHED_AT = NULL, t.MESSAGE = NULL WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (STOCKID, STATUS, REQUESTED_BY) VALUES (s.STOCKID, 'PENDING', :byu2)`, { id, byu: by, byu2: by }, { autoCommit: true })); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Change what a photo shows (front / back / nutrition / side / other) app.put('/api/stock/shopify/photos/:stockid/:photoId', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid), pid = Number(req.params.photoId); const role = req.body && req.body.role; if (!Number.isFinite(id) || !Number.isFinite(pid) || !SH_PHOTO_ROLES.includes(role)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad request' }); try { await withConnection(async conn => { // only one FRONT per product: the photo that was the front becomes the back (same commit) if (role === 'front') await conn.execute(`UPDATE SHOPIFY_PHOTOS SET ROLE = 'back' WHERE STOCKID = :id AND ROLE = 'front' AND ID <> :pid`, { id, pid }); await conn.execute(`UPDATE SHOPIFY_PHOTOS SET ROLE = :role WHERE ID = :pid AND STOCKID = :id`, { role, pid, id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/stock/shopify/photos/:stockid/:photoId', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.stockid), pid = Number(req.params.photoId); if (!Number.isFinite(id) || !Number.isFinite(pid)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad request' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(`DELETE FROM SHOPIFY_PHOTOS WHERE ID = :pid AND STOCKID = :id`, { pid, id }, { autoCommit: true })); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Choose which picture goes on Shopify: your own front photo, or the tidied-up one. Body: { stockid, source:'original'|'edited' } app.post('/api/stock/shopify/picture', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.body && req.body.stockid); const source = req.body && req.body.source; if (!Number.isFinite(id) || !['original', 'edited'].includes(source)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad request' }); try { if (source === 'original') { const photos = await shLoadPhotos(id, true); const front = photos.find(x => x.role === 'front') || photos[0]; if (!front) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'There is no photo for this product' }); await shSaveContent(id, { image_mime: front.mime, image_data: front.buffer, image_source: 'original' }); } else { const c = await shLoadContent(id, true); if (!c || !c.edited) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'There is no tidied-up picture yet — press Regenerate picture' }); await shSaveContent(id, { image_mime: c.edited.mime, image_data: c.edited.buffer, image_source: 'edited' }); } await wooAutoQueue(id, (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null, 'FULL', 'picture-chosen'); res.json({ ok: true, source }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Tick / untick "Completed" for a product (completed products move to the Completed list) app.post('/api/stock/shopify/completed', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.body && req.body.stockid); if (!Number.isFinite(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'stockid is required' }); const done = (req.body && req.body.completed) ? 1 : 0; const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { await withConnection(async conn => { const up = await conn.execute( `UPDATE SHOPIFY_CONTENT SET COMPLETED = :c, COMPLETED_AT = CASE WHEN :c2 = 1 THEN SYSTIMESTAMP ELSE NULL END, COMPLETED_BY = CASE WHEN :c3 = 1 THEN :byu ELSE NULL END WHERE STOCKID = :id`, { c: done, c2: done, c3: done, byu: by, id }, { autoCommit: true }); if (!up.rowsAffected) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO SHOPIFY_CONTENT (STOCKID, COMPLETED, COMPLETED_AT, COMPLETED_BY) VALUES (:id, :c, CASE WHEN :c2 = 1 THEN SYSTIMESTAMP ELSE NULL END, CASE WHEN :c3 = 1 THEN :byu ELSE NULL END)`, { id, c: done, c2: done, c3: done, byu: by }, { autoCommit: true }); } }); res.json({ ok: true, completed: done === 1 }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Supplier search ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/search', async (req, res) => { const q = (req.query.q || '').trim(); if (!q) return res.json({ items: [] }); try { const words = q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const binds = {}; const matchExprs = words.map((w, i) => { binds['w' + i] = w; return `CASE WHEN UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`; }); const orClauses = words.map((w, i) => `UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%'`).join(' OR '); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT SUPPID, NAME FROM ( SELECT SUPPID, NAME, (${matchExprs.join(' + ')}) AS MATCH_COUNT FROM SUPPLIERS WHERE ${orClauses} ) ORDER BY MATCH_COUNT DESC, NAME FETCH FIRST 25 ROWS ONLY`, binds )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- SCAN GOODS (Goods In scanning) Requires GOODS_IN_SESSIONS + GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS — see goods_in_schema.sql. GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS is a permanent record (expiry dates included) — never truncated/deleted in bulk anywhere in this app; only single-row edits via the PUT endpoint below. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- List sessions (open ones for "Continue Existing Scan", or all) ---- app.get('/api/goods-in/sessions', async (req, res) => { const status = (req.query.status || '').trim().toUpperCase(); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.SESSION_ID, s.SUPPID, s.SUPPLIER_NAME, s.SCANNED_BY, s.STARTED_AT, s.STATUS, s.COMPLETED_AT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS i WHERE i.SESSION_ID = s.SESSION_ID) AS ITEM_COUNT FROM GOODS_IN_SESSIONS s ${status ? 'WHERE s.STATUS = :status' : ''} ORDER BY s.STARTED_AT DESC`, status ? { status } : {} )); res.json({ sessions: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Start a new Goods In session ---- app.post('/api/goods-in/sessions', async (req, res) => { const { suppid, supplier_name, scanned_by } = req.body || {}; if (!supplier_name || !scanned_by) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'supplier_name and scanned_by are required' }); } try { const sessionId = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO GOODS_IN_SESSIONS (SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, SCANNED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :supplier_name, :scanned_by) RETURNING SESSION_ID INTO :out_id`, { suppid: suppid || null, supplier_name, scanned_by, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.out_id[0]; }); res.json({ session_id: sessionId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Get one session + all its scanned items (used both to open a fresh // session and to resume an existing one — same shape either way) ---- app.get('/api/goods-in/sessions/:id', async (req, res) => { try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const sessionResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT SESSION_ID, SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, SCANNED_BY, STARTED_AT, STATUS, COMPLETED_AT FROM GOODS_IN_SESSIONS WHERE SESSION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id } ); const sessions = lower(sessionResult.rows); if (sessions.length === 0) return null; const itemsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT ITEM_ID, STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, BARCODE, PACK_SIZE, EXPIRY_DATE, QUANTITY, SCANNED_AT FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS WHERE SESSION_ID = :id ORDER BY ITEM_ID`, { id: req.params.id } ); return { session: sessions[0], items: lower(itemsResult.rows) }; }); if (!data) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Session not found' }); res.json(data); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Barcode lookup for Goods In scanning: same VEBARCODES/VESTOCK join // as /api/stock/barcode, plus pack size for the chosen supplier from // SUPPLIER_STOCK (pack size is supplier-specific, so it needs suppid). ---- app.get('/api/goods-in/scan-lookup/:code', async (req, res) => { const code = (req.params.code || '').trim(); const suppid = req.query.suppid || null; if (!code) return res.json({ item: null }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, b.BARCODE, s.QUANTITY AS CURRENT_STOCK, ss.PACKSIZE AS PACK_SIZE FROM VEBARCODES b JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = b.STOCKID LEFT JOIN SUPPLIER_STOCK ss ON ss.STOCKID = s.STOCKID AND ss.SUPPID = :suppid WHERE b.BARCODE = :code`, { code, suppid } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); res.json({ item: rows[0] || null }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Add one scanned item to a session. This is the permanent write — // it happens the instant the person taps "Add" on a scanned item, not // batched at the end, so nothing is lost if the session is interrupted. ---- app.post('/api/goods-in/sessions/:id/items', async (req, res) => { const { stockid, item_name, barcode, pack_size, expiry_date, quantity } = req.body || {}; if (!item_name || !quantity) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'item_name and quantity are required' }); } try { const itemId = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS (SESSION_ID, STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, BARCODE, PACK_SIZE, EXPIRY_DATE, QUANTITY) VALUES (:session_id, :stockid, :item_name, :barcode, :pack_size, TO_DATE(:expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), :quantity) RETURNING ITEM_ID INTO :out_id`, { session_id: req.params.id, stockid: stockid || null, item_name, barcode: barcode || null, pack_size: pack_size || null, expiry_date: expiry_date || null, quantity, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.out_id[0]; }); res.json({ item_id: itemId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Edit a scanned item (correct expiry date, quantity, or pack size // after it's already been added) ---- app.put('/api/goods-in/items/:id', async (req, res) => { const { pack_size, expiry_date, quantity } = req.body || {}; if (!quantity) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'quantity is required' }); try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `UPDATE GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS SET PACK_SIZE = :pack_size, EXPIRY_DATE = TO_DATE(:expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), QUANTITY = :quantity WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { pack_size: pack_size || null, expiry_date: expiry_date || null, quantity, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Remove a mistakenly-added item (e.g. wrong barcode scanned twice). // Only ever a single-row delete triggered by the person, never a bulk // or automatic cleanup — the table itself is otherwise append-only. ---- app.delete('/api/goods-in/items/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Mark a session finished once all items are scanned and the CSV has // been downloaded. The rows in GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS stay exactly as // they are — completing a session never deletes anything. ---- app.post('/api/goods-in/sessions/:id/complete', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `UPDATE GOODS_IN_SESSIONS SET STATUS = 'COMPLETED', COMPLETED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE SESSION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Restore a finished session back to OPEN — undoes /complete. Items are // never touched by complete/restore either way, so nothing is lost. ---- app.post('/api/goods-in/sessions/:id/restore', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `UPDATE GOODS_IN_SESSIONS SET STATUS = 'OPEN', COMPLETED_AT = NULL WHERE SESSION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Delete an entire scan session (e.g. started by mistake, wrong // supplier, duplicate). Removes its scanned items first since there's // no ON DELETE CASCADE on GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS.SESSION_ID. ---- app.delete('/api/goods-in/sessions/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS WHERE SESSION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM GOODS_IN_SESSIONS WHERE SESSION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true }); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Vector import queue --------------------------------------------- // Bridges the gap between "someone clicked Retail CSV on any computer" and // "the Vector terminal's local FileSystemWatcher only sees files that land // in its own Downloads folder". The browser still downloads the CSV locally // as before; it also POSTs the same content here. A small poller script // running on the Vector machine calls GET /next every 30-60s, and when it // gets a file back, writes it straight into the watched Downloads folder — // the existing watcher + vector_price_import.ps1 then run completely // unchanged. Requires one table: // CREATE TABLE VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE ( // QUEUE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, // FILE_NAME VARCHAR2(255) NOT NULL, // CSV_CONTENT CLOB NOT NULL, // IMPORT_TYPE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'RETAIL' NOT NULL, -- RETAIL (price update) or RECEIPT (goods-in receipt note) // SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(255), -- only used by RECEIPT imports, to search the Supplier List picker // CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, // CONSUMED_AT TIMESTAMP, // STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'QUEUED' NOT NULL, // STATUS_MESSAGE VARCHAR2(255), // ERROR_MESSAGE VARCHAR2(4000), // UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP // ); // If this table already exists from before RECEIPT imports existed, run: // ALTER TABLE VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE ADD (IMPORT_TYPE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'RETAIL' NOT NULL); // ALTER TABLE VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE ADD (SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(255)); // STATUS moves QUEUED -> PULLED -> IMPORTING -> COMPLETED / FAILED, so the // person who clicked "Retail CSV" / "Quantity CSV" can see it happen instead // of just staring at a downloaded file with no idea whether the till has // even noticed it yet. // Extracted so the Wholesale Pricing schedule runner (see further down) // can queue a CSV the exact same way a manual "Download CSV" click does, // without duplicating the insert. Behaviour is unchanged for the existing // caller below. async function queueVectorImport(conn, { file_name, csv_content, type, supplier_name }) { const importType = (type || 'RETAIL').toUpperCase(); const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE (FILE_NAME, CSV_CONTENT, IMPORT_TYPE, SUPPLIER_NAME) VALUES (:fileName, :csvContent, :importType, :supplierName) RETURNING QUEUE_ID INTO :queueId`, { fileName: file_name, csvContent: { val: csv_content, type: oracledb.CLOB }, importType, supplierName: supplier_name || null, queueId: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.queueId[0]; } app.post('/api/vector-import/queue', async (req, res) => { try { const { file_name, csv_content, type, supplier_name } = req.body; if (!file_name || !csv_content) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'file_name and csv_content are required' }); const importType = (type || 'RETAIL').toUpperCase(); if (importType === 'RECEIPT' && !supplier_name) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'supplier_name is required for RECEIPT imports' }); } const queueId = await withConnection(conn => queueVectorImport(conn, { file_name, csv_content, type, supplier_name })); res.json({ ok: true, queue_id: queueId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Called by the Vector machine's poller. Returns the oldest not-yet- // picked-up file as a plain CSV download, and marks it PULLED in the // same request (also carried in the X-Queue-Id header so the poller // knows which row to report status against). 204 (no body) when the // queue is empty. ---- app.get('/api/vector-import/next', requireLocalSyncSecret, async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT QUEUE_ID, FILE_NAME, CSV_CONTENT, IMPORT_TYPE, SUPPLIER_NAME FROM VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE WHERE CONSUMED_AT IS NULL ORDER BY CREATED_AT FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY` ); if (result.rows.length === 0) { res.status(204).end(); return; } const row = lower(result.rows)[0]; const csvText = typeof row.csv_content === 'string' ? row.csv_content : await row.csv_content.getData(); await conn.execute( `UPDATE VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE SET CONSUMED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, STATUS = 'PULLED', STATUS_MESSAGE = 'Picked up by the till', UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE QUEUE_ID = :id`, { id: row.queue_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); res.setHeader('Content-Type', 'text/csv'); res.setHeader('Content-Disposition', `attachment; filename="${row.file_name}"`); res.setHeader('X-Queue-Id', String(row.queue_id)); res.setHeader('X-Import-Type', row.import_type || 'RETAIL'); if (row.supplier_name) res.setHeader('X-Supplier-Name', encodeURIComponent(row.supplier_name)); res.send(csvText); }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Reported by the till scripts (poller + vector_price_import.ps1) as // the import actually progresses. Kept deliberately permissive on // `status` (just an upper-cased string) rather than an enum check, so // a till-side script tweak never needs a server redeploy to match. ---- app.post('/api/vector-import/:id/status', requireLocalSyncSecret, async (req, res) => { const queueId = Number(req.params.id); if (!queueId) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid queue id is required' }); const { status, message, error } = req.body; if (!status) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'status is required' }); try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `UPDATE VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE SET STATUS = :status, STATUS_MESSAGE = :message, ERROR_MESSAGE = :error, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE QUEUE_ID = :id`, { status: status.toUpperCase(), message: message || null, error: error || null, id: queueId }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Polled by the browser after queueing a Retail CSV, so the person who // clicked the button sees the till actually working through it instead // of a silent gap between "downloaded" and "done". ---- app.get('/api/vector-import/:id/status', async (req, res) => { const queueId = Number(req.params.id); if (!queueId) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid queue id is required' }); try { const row = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT QUEUE_ID, FILE_NAME, IMPORT_TYPE, SUPPLIER_NAME, STATUS, STATUS_MESSAGE, ERROR_MESSAGE, CREATED_AT, UPDATED_AT FROM VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE WHERE QUEUE_ID = :id`, { id: queueId } ); return result.rows.length ? lower(result.rows)[0] : null; }); if (!row) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Queued import not found' }); res.json(row); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Expiry list for Stock Info > Date: every scanned item with an expiry // date, across every supplier, soonest first. Pass ?within_days=N to // only return items expiring within that many days from today (used by // the "Soon Expiry" 1 week / 1 month / 6 month filters) — items already // past their expiry date are still included either way, since knowing // what's already expired matters at least as much as what's coming up. ---- app.get('/api/goods-in/expiry-list', async (req, res) => { const withinDays = req.query.within_days ? Number(req.query.within_days) : null; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ID, i.STOCKID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.BARCODE, i.EXPIRY_DATE, i.QUANTITY, s.SUPPID, s.SUPPLIER_NAME FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS i JOIN GOODS_IN_SESSIONS s ON s.SESSION_ID = i.SESSION_ID WHERE i.EXPIRY_DATE IS NOT NULL ${withinDays !== null ? 'AND i.EXPIRY_DATE <= SYSDATE + :withinDays' : ''} ORDER BY i.EXPIRY_DATE ASC`, withinDays !== null ? { withinDays } : {} )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Best-guess supplier for a stock item, used by the Add Product & // Expiry quick-add flow so the person scanning doesn't need to know or // pick the correct supplier themselves. Two signals, in priority order: // 1) the supplier from the most recent prior Goods In scan of this // exact stock item (the strongest signal — it's what actually // happened last time), 2) any supplier this stock item is linked to // in SUPPLIER_STOCK. If neither exists, comes back unassigned and // the item is still saved, just flagged for manual follow-up. ---- async function detectSupplierForStock(conn, stockid) { const recent = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.SUPPID, s.SUPPLIER_NAME FROM GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS i JOIN GOODS_IN_SESSIONS s ON s.SESSION_ID = i.SESSION_ID WHERE i.STOCKID = :stockid AND s.SUPPID IS NOT NULL ORDER BY i.SCANNED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY`, { stockid } ); const recentRows = lower(recent.rows); if (recentRows.length) return { suppid: recentRows[0].suppid, supplier_name: recentRows[0].supplier_name, source: 'recent_scan' }; const linked = await conn.execute( `SELECT ss.SUPPID, sup.NAME AS SUPPLIER_NAME FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN SUPPLIERS sup ON sup.SUPPID = ss.SUPPID WHERE ss.STOCKID = :stockid FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY`, { stockid } ); const linkedRows = lower(linked.rows); if (linkedRows.length) return { suppid: linkedRows[0].suppid, supplier_name: linkedRows[0].supplier_name, source: 'supplier_stock' }; return { suppid: null, supplier_name: 'Unknown Supplier', source: 'none' }; } // ---- Quick add: scan a barcode + expiry date without picking a session or // supplier first. Finds (or creates) a standing "Quick Add" session per // detected supplier and appends the item to it, so this still goes // through the exact same permanent GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS table as a // normal Goods In scan — nothing special about how it's stored, only // how it's entered. ---- app.post('/api/goods-in/quick-add', async (req, res) => { const { barcode, expiry_date, quantity } = req.body || {}; if (!barcode || !quantity) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'barcode and quantity are required' }); try { const outcome = await withConnection(async conn => { const stockResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME FROM VEBARCODES b JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = b.STOCKID WHERE b.BARCODE = :barcode`, { barcode } ); const stockRows = lower(stockResult.rows); if (!stockRows.length) return { notFound: true }; const stock = stockRows[0]; const supplier = await detectSupplierForStock(conn, stock.stockid); const sessionResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT SESSION_ID FROM GOODS_IN_SESSIONS WHERE SCANNED_BY = 'Quick Add' AND STATUS = 'OPEN' AND ${supplier.suppid !== null ? 'SUPPID = :suppid' : 'SUPPID IS NULL'} FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY`, supplier.suppid !== null ? { suppid: supplier.suppid } : {} ); let sessionRows = lower(sessionResult.rows); let sessionId; if (sessionRows.length) { sessionId = sessionRows[0].session_id; } else { const created = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO GOODS_IN_SESSIONS (SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, SCANNED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :supplier_name, 'Quick Add') RETURNING SESSION_ID INTO :out_id`, { suppid: supplier.suppid, supplier_name: supplier.supplier_name, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } } ); sessionId = created.outBinds.out_id[0]; } const itemResult = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO GOODS_IN_SCANNED_ITEMS (SESSION_ID, STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, BARCODE, EXPIRY_DATE, QUANTITY) VALUES (:session_id, :stockid, :item_name, :barcode, TO_DATE(:expiry_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD'), :quantity) RETURNING ITEM_ID INTO :out_id`, { session_id: sessionId, stockid: stock.stockid, item_name: stock.name, barcode, expiry_date: expiry_date || null, quantity, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } } ); await conn.commit(); return { notFound: false, item_id: itemResult.outBinds.out_id[0], item_name: stock.name, supplier_name: supplier.supplier_name, supplier_source: supplier.source }; }); if (outcome.notFound) return res.json({ item: null }); res.json({ item: outcome }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Last delivery: get / set manually ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/:suppId/last-delivery', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT LAST_DELIVERY_AT FROM SUPPLIER_LAST_DELIVERY WHERE SUPPID = :suppId`, { suppId } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); res.json({ last_delivery_at: rows.length ? rows[0].last_delivery_at : null }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/suppliers/:suppId/last-delivery', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; const { last_delivery_at } = req.body || {}; const parsed = last_delivery_at ? new Date(last_delivery_at) : new Date(); if (isNaN(parsed.getTime())) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Invalid date/time' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO SUPPLIER_LAST_DELIVERY s USING (SELECT :suppId AS k FROM dual) d ON (s.SUPPID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.LAST_DELIVERY_AT = :dt WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SUPPID, LAST_DELIVERY_AT) VALUES (d.k, :dt)`, { suppId, dt: parsed }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Basic List: items this supplier supplies, ranked by sales over N weeks ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/:suppId/basic-list', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; const weeks = Number(req.query.weeks) || 4; try { const since = new Date(Date.now() - weeks * 7 * 86400000); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, NVL(SUM(v.SELL_QTY), 0) AS QTY_SOLD FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VESALES v ON v.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID AND v.TRANS_DATE >= :since WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId GROUP BY s.STOCKID, s.NAME ORDER BY QTY_SOLD DESC, s.NAME`, { suppId, since } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows), weeks }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Place Order: name, sold, in stock, pack size, product code, catalogue // number, preferred flag, reorder/max stock levels, and a set of // order-quantity suggestions (one per forecasting method) per item — // feeds the tick/cross "add to order" screen. // Query params: // weeks - sales history window used to work out demand (default 4) // predict_weeks - how many weeks ahead we're ordering cover for // (default = weeks, so behaviour is unchanged unless // the "Predicted for" dropdown is set differently) // preferred - 'yes' | 'no' | omitted (both) // All four methods are computed server-side and returned together // (in `suggestions`) so the client can switch the method dropdown // instantly without an extra round trip; weeks / predict_weeks // changes still require a refetch since they change the underlying // sales data window. ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/:suppId/place-order-list', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; const weeks = Number(req.query.weeks) || 4; const predictWeeks = Number(req.query.predict_weeks) || weeks; const preferred = (req.query.preferred || '').trim().toLowerCase(); // 'yes' | 'no' | '' try { const now = Date.now(); const since = new Date(now - weeks * 7 * 86400000); // Split the lookback window in half so the "Trend Weighted" method can // tell whether sales are speeding up or slowing down within it — same // 60/40 recent/older weighting used by the Advanced List. const midSince = new Date(now - (weeks / 2) * 7 * 86400000); let preferredClause = ''; if (preferred === 'yes') preferredClause = 'AND ss.PREFERRED = 1'; else if (preferred === 'no') preferredClause = 'AND NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) != 1'; const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.QUANTITY AS IN_STOCK, ss.PACKSIZE, ss.CATNO, ss.STOCKNO, NVL(ss.PREFERRED, 0) AS PREFERRED, s.REORDERLEVEL AS VESTOCK_REORDER_LEVEL, s.MAXSTOCKLEVEL AS VESTOCK_MAX_STOCK_LEVEL, ov.REORDER_LEVEL AS OVERRIDE_REORDER_LEVEL, ov.MAX_STOCK_LEVEL AS OVERRIDE_MAX_STOCK_LEVEL, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :midSince THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS RECENT_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :since AND v.TRANS_DATE < :midSince THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS OLDER_QTY, NVL(SUM(v.SELL_QTY), 0) AS SOLD FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VESALES v ON v.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID AND v.TRANS_DATE >= :since LEFT JOIN STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES ov ON ov.STOCKID = s.STOCKID WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId ${preferredClause} GROUP BY s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.QUANTITY, ss.PACKSIZE, ss.CATNO, ss.STOCKNO, ss.PREFERRED, s.REORDERLEVEL, s.MAXSTOCKLEVEL, ov.REORDER_LEVEL, ov.MAX_STOCK_LEVEL ORDER BY s.NAME`, { suppId, since, midSince } )); const halfWeeks = weeks / 2; const packsFor = (packSize, units) => { if (!packSize || packSize <= 0) return 0; return Math.max(0, Math.ceil(units / packSize)); }; const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => { const packSize = r.packsize; const inStock = Number(r.in_stock) || 0; const sold = Number(r.sold) || 0; const hasReorderOverride = r.override_reorder_level !== null && r.override_reorder_level !== undefined; const hasMaxOverride = r.override_max_stock_level !== null && r.override_max_stock_level !== undefined; const reorderLevel = hasReorderOverride ? r.override_reorder_level : r.vestock_reorder_level; const maxStockLevel = hasMaxOverride ? r.override_max_stock_level : r.vestock_max_stock_level; const flatRatePerWeek = weeks > 0 ? sold / weeks : 0; const recentRatePerWeek = halfWeeks > 0 ? (Number(r.recent_qty) || 0) / halfWeeks : 0; const olderRatePerWeek = halfWeeks > 0 ? (Number(r.older_qty) || 0) / halfWeeks : 0; const trendRatePerWeek = 0.6 * recentRatePerWeek + 0.4 * olderRatePerWeek; // Method 1 — Simple: flat sales rate over the lookback window, // scaled up (or down) to the number of weeks being predicted for. // This replaces the old "just divide sold by pack size" behaviour, // which silently assumed predict_weeks === weeks. const simplePacks = packsFor(packSize, flatRatePerWeek * predictWeeks); // Method 2 — Min / Max: the classic reorder-point method. Only // triggers an order once stock is at/below REORDERLEVEL, and then // tops back up to MAXSTOCKLEVEL. Needs both levels to be set // (via VESTOCK or the edited override) — otherwise there's nothing // sensible to suggest, so it's left null rather than guessing. let minmaxPacks = 0, minmaxUnits = 0, minmaxTriggered = null; const hasReorderLevel = reorderLevel !== null && reorderLevel !== undefined; const levelsAvailable = hasReorderLevel && maxStockLevel !== null && maxStockLevel !== undefined; if (levelsAvailable) { minmaxTriggered = inStock <= reorderLevel; minmaxUnits = minmaxTriggered ? Math.max(0, maxStockLevel - inStock) : 0; minmaxPacks = packsFor(packSize, minmaxUnits); } // Method 3 — Trend Weighted: 60% weight on the more recent half of // the lookback window, 40% on the older half (same weighting as the // Advanced List), projected across the predicted window. const trendPacks = packsFor(packSize, trendRatePerWeek * predictWeeks); // Method 4 — Stock-Aware Velocity: like Simple, but nets off what's // already sitting in stock so it doesn't over-order. // // BUGFIX #1: this used to net predicted demand straight against In // Stock with nothing else in the equation — (flatRatePerWeek * // predictWeeks) - inStock. For a slow mover that's nearly always <= 0, // so it never fired until stock was basically already gone. // // BUGFIX #2 (this round): the first fix added a "safety stock" // cushion, but for any item with a Reorder Level set, it used the // *entire* Reorder Level as that cushion. Reorder Level is an // absolute stock number (e.g. "19"), not a small buffer — adding the // whole thing on top of demand and then subtracting In Stock meant // the Reorder Level was being counted twice (once implicitly, once // explicitly), which produced numbers that swamped the sales-driven // part of the formula and made "Predicted for" look like it did // nothing. That's the "sold 8 in 4 weeks, ordering 13 for 1 week" // case — the 13 was almost entirely the Reorder Level gap, not sales. // // Fix: always use a small, pack-based cushion (half a pack) — same // one used when there's no Reorder Level at all — regardless of // whether a Reorder Level happens to be configured. Reorder Level is // used elsewhere (Min/Max, and the Smart Reorder trigger below) to // decide *whether* to reorder; it no longer gets folded into *how // much* here as well. const velocitySafetyStock = packSize > 0 ? packSize / 2 : 0; const velocityUnits = Math.max(0, (flatRatePerWeek * predictWeeks) + velocitySafetyStock - inStock); const velocityPacks = packsFor(packSize, velocityUnits); // Method 5 — Smart Reorder (Velocity + Reorder Point): the method // worth defaulting to going forward. // // BUGFIX #3 (this round): the gate that decided whether to bother // calculating anything at all was purely `in_stock <= Reorder Level` // — nothing else. That's fine when Reorder Level is set sensibly, but // if it's set too LOW for how fast an item is actually selling (e.g. // Reorder Level 18 on something selling 30 units/week), the gate // never fires, and a genuinely urgent shortage gets silently reported // as "0, above reorder point" — even with under a week of stock left. // Reorder Level being too HIGH caused the opposite bug (fixed above); // this is the same root problem from the other direction. // // Fix: the gate now fires if EITHER signal says so — stock is below // the configured Reorder Level (or half a pack, if none is set), OR // the sales-driven maths (same calculation as Stock-Aware Velocity) // says demand isn't covered. Reorder Level still matters — it's still // checked, and still shown via the "above reorder point" note — it // just can no longer be the ONLY thing standing between a real // shortage and a silent 0. const smartTriggerLevel = hasReorderLevel ? reorderLevel : (packSize > 0 ? Math.ceil(packSize / 2) : 0); const smartBelowReorderLevel = inStock <= smartTriggerLevel; // Reuse the exact same demand-vs-stock maths as Stock-Aware Velocity // for the quantity — Reorder Level's role here is only to decide // whether the item counts as "below its policy level" (for the note), // never to inflate or replace the sales-driven number. // // Dormant items: nothing in stock AND nothing sold across the whole // sales-history window means the item has effectively been unavailable // for that long (e.g. 0 stock, 0 sold for 4 weeks). The safety-stock // cushion above would otherwise make these look like they need a pack, // so skip them entirely — no trigger, no suggested quantity. const smartDormant = inStock <= 0 && sold <= 0; const smartUnits = smartDormant ? 0 : velocityUnits; const smartTriggered = !smartDormant && (smartBelowReorderLevel || smartUnits > 0); const smartPacks = smartDormant ? 0 : packsFor(packSize, smartUnits); return { stockid: r.stockid, name: r.name, in_stock: r.in_stock, pack_size: packSize, product_code: r.stockno, catalogue_number: r.catno, preferred: r.preferred === 1, sold, reorder_level: reorderLevel === null || reorderLevel === undefined ? null : reorderLevel, max_stock_level: maxStockLevel === null || maxStockLevel === undefined ? null : maxStockLevel, reorder_level_override: hasReorderOverride, max_stock_level_override: hasMaxOverride, reorder_level_vestock: r.vestock_reorder_level === null || r.vestock_reorder_level === undefined ? null : r.vestock_reorder_level, max_stock_level_vestock: r.vestock_max_stock_level === null || r.vestock_max_stock_level === undefined ? null : r.vestock_max_stock_level, minmax_triggered: minmaxTriggered, smart_triggered: smartTriggered, smart_dormant: smartDormant, suggestions: { simple: simplePacks, minmax: minmaxPacks, trend: trendPacks, velocity: velocityPacks, smart: smartPacks } }; }); res.json({ items, weeks, predict_weeks: predictWeeks }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Reorder level / max stock level overrides (Place Order screen) ---- // GET is not strictly needed since place-order-list already returns the // effective values, but is provided for completeness / other callers. app.get('/api/stock/:stockid/level-override', async (req, res) => { const { stockid } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, REORDER_LEVEL, MAX_STOCK_LEVEL, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY FROM STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES WHERE STOCKID = :stockid`, { stockid } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); res.json({ override: rows.length ? rows[0] : null }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/stock/:stockid/level-override', async (req, res) => { const { stockid } = req.params; let { reorder_level, max_stock_level, updated_by } = req.body || {}; reorder_level = reorder_level === '' || reorder_level === undefined ? null : Number(reorder_level); max_stock_level = max_stock_level === '' || max_stock_level === undefined ? null : Number(max_stock_level); if ((reorder_level !== null && Number.isNaN(reorder_level)) || (max_stock_level !== null && Number.isNaN(max_stock_level))) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'reorder_level and max_stock_level must be numbers' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES t USING (SELECT :stockid AS k FROM dual) d ON (t.STOCKID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t.REORDER_LEVEL = :reorder_level, t.MAX_STOCK_LEVEL = :max_stock_level, t.UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, t.UPDATED_BY = :updated_by WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (STOCKID, REORDER_LEVEL, MAX_STOCK_LEVEL, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (d.k, :reorder_level, :max_stock_level, SYSTIMESTAMP, :updated_by)`, { stockid, reorder_level, max_stock_level, updated_by: updated_by || null }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true, stockid: Number(stockid), reorder_level, max_stock_level }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Bulk version of the above, for the "Edit Reorder & Max Levels" list on // the Place Order screen, where staff can edit many items in one sitting — // sent as a single request/transaction rather than one PUT per row. app.put('/api/stock/level-overrides/bulk', async (req, res) => { const { updates, updated_by } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(updates) || updates.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'updates must be a non-empty array' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { for (const u of updates) { const stockid = Number(u.stockid); if (!stockid) continue; let reorder_level = u.reorder_level === '' || u.reorder_level === undefined || u.reorder_level === null ? null : Number(u.reorder_level); let max_stock_level = u.max_stock_level === '' || u.max_stock_level === undefined || u.max_stock_level === null ? null : Number(u.max_stock_level); if ((reorder_level !== null && Number.isNaN(reorder_level)) || (max_stock_level !== null && Number.isNaN(max_stock_level))) continue; await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES t USING (SELECT :stockid AS k FROM dual) d ON (t.STOCKID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t.REORDER_LEVEL = :reorder_level, t.MAX_STOCK_LEVEL = :max_stock_level, t.UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, t.UPDATED_BY = :updated_by WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (STOCKID, REORDER_LEVEL, MAX_STOCK_LEVEL, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (d.k, :reorder_level, :max_stock_level, SYSTIMESTAMP, :updated_by)`, { stockid, reorder_level, max_stock_level, updated_by: updated_by || null } ); } await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true, count: updates.length }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Revert an item back to whatever VESTOCK (Vector) currently has, by // dropping its override row. app.delete('/api/stock/:stockid/level-override', async (req, res) => { const { stockid } = req.params; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM STOCK_LEVEL_OVERRIDES WHERE STOCKID = :stockid`, { stockid }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Advanced List: reorder suggestion per item ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/:suppId/advanced-list', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; const months = Number(req.query.months) || 6; try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const deliveryResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT LAST_DELIVERY_AT FROM SUPPLIER_LAST_DELIVERY WHERE SUPPID = :suppId`, { suppId } ); const deliveryRows = lower(deliveryResult.rows); const lastDeliveryAt = deliveryRows.length ? deliveryRows[0].last_delivery_at : null; const now = new Date(); const lookbackDays = months * 30; const startDate = new Date(now - lookbackDays * 86400000); const midDate = new Date(now - (lookbackDays / 2) * 86400000); const sinceDeliveryDate = lastDeliveryAt || new Date(now - 36500 * 86400000); // effectively "never" if unset const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.QUANTITY AS CURRENT_STOCK, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :midDate THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS RECENT_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :startDate AND v.TRANS_DATE < :midDate THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS OLDER_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :sinceDeliveryDate THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS SINCE_DELIVERY_QTY FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VESALES v ON v.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID AND v.TRANS_DATE >= :startDate WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId GROUP BY s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.QUANTITY ORDER BY s.NAME`, { suppId, startDate, midDate, sinceDeliveryDate } ); const halfWeeks = lookbackDays / 2 / 7; const orderCycleWeeks = lastDeliveryAt ? Math.max(1, (now - new Date(lastDeliveryAt)) / (7 * 86400000)) : 4; const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => { const recentRate = r.recent_qty / halfWeeks; const olderRate = r.older_qty / halfWeeks; const trendWeightedRate = 0.6 * recentRate + 0.4 * olderRate; const flatRate = (r.recent_qty + r.older_qty) / (halfWeeks * 2); return { item_name: r.name, current_stock: r.current_stock, basic_avg_estimate: Math.round(flatRate * orderCycleWeeks), predicted_reorder_qty: Math.round(trendWeightedRate * orderCycleWeeks), actual_since_last_delivery: lastDeliveryAt ? r.since_delivery_qty : null }; }); return { items, lastDeliveryAt, orderCycleWeeks }; }); res.json({ items: data.items, months, last_delivery_at: data.lastDeliveryAt, order_cycle_weeks: Math.round(data.orderCycleWeeks * 10) / 10 }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Compare: predicted vs previous-period actual vs current-period actual ---- app.get('/api/suppliers/:suppId/compare', async (req, res) => { const { suppId } = req.params; const weeks = Number(req.query.weeks) || 4; try { const now = new Date(); const currentStart = new Date(now - weeks * 7 * 86400000); const previousStart = new Date(now - weeks * 2 * 7 * 86400000); // Trend rate is always based on a fixed 6-month base, regardless of the // comparison window length, so short windows don't produce a noisy trend const trendBaseDays = 180; const trendStart = new Date(now - trendBaseDays * 86400000); const trendMid = new Date(now - (trendBaseDays / 2) * 86400000); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :currentStart THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS CURRENT_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :previousStart AND v.TRANS_DATE < :currentStart THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS PREVIOUS_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :trendMid THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS TREND_RECENT_QTY, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN v.TRANS_DATE >= :trendStart AND v.TRANS_DATE < :trendMid THEN v.SELL_QTY ELSE 0 END), 0) AS TREND_OLDER_QTY FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VESALES v ON v.STOCKID = ss.STOCKID AND v.TRANS_DATE >= :trendStart WHERE ss.SUPPID = :suppId GROUP BY s.STOCKID, s.NAME ORDER BY s.NAME`, { suppId, currentStart, previousStart, trendStart, trendMid } )); const trendHalfWeeks = trendBaseDays / 2 / 7; const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => { const recentRate = r.trend_recent_qty / trendHalfWeeks; const olderRate = r.trend_older_qty / trendHalfWeeks; const trendWeightedRate = 0.6 * recentRate + 0.4 * olderRate; return { item_name: r.name, predicted_qty: Math.round(trendWeightedRate * weeks), previous_period_qty: r.previous_qty, current_period_qty: r.current_qty }; }); res.json({ items, weeks }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- INVOICES (Goods Received Notes) Pending vs Received is purely POSTDATE: NULL = pending, not-null = received. Uses the existing GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES table (already created and synced). NOTE: Cost Price is computed here as NET_VALUE / QTY. Retail Price (PRICEID 1) and Online Price (PRICEID 3) come from VESTOCKPRICES via the sync in test_oracle.py. Pack Size and VAT code (VATID) come from VESUPPSTOCK, matched on both STOCKID and SUPPID since they can differ by supplier for the same stock item. MARGIN CHECK: each line's Department + minimum margin come from a new MIN_MARGIN_PCT column on VEDEPTS (department is looked up from GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES.STOCKID -> VESTOCK.DEPTID -> VEDEPTS). Run this once against Oracle before using the margin features below: ALTER TABLE VEDEPTS ADD (MIN_MARGIN_PCT NUMBER DEFAULT 38); UPDATE VEDEPTS SET MIN_MARGIN_PCT = 38 WHERE MIN_MARGIN_PCT IS NULL; COMMIT; That seeds every department at a 38% minimum margin; the "Edit Minimum Margins" button in the Invoices screen (GET/PUT /api/departments/margins below) lets staff change any department's threshold afterwards. The frontend computes each line's actual margin as (Retail Price − Cost Price) / Retail Price × 100 and flags it red when it's below its department's MIN_MARGIN_PCT. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Notes list: one row per note (grouped by supplier + reference), pending or received ---- app.get('/api/invoices/notes', async (req, res) => { const status = req.query.status === 'received' ? 'received' : 'pending'; const postDateClause = status === 'pending' ? 'POSTDATE IS NULL' : 'POSTDATE IS NOT NULL'; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT SUPPID, RECNOTEID, RECNOTEREF, SUPPLIER_NAME, RECDATE, POSTDATE, COUNT(*) AS ITEM_COUNT, SUM(NET_VALUE) AS TOTAL_NET, SUM(VAT_VALUE) AS TOTAL_VAT FROM GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES WHERE ${postDateClause} GROUP BY SUPPID, RECNOTEID, RECNOTEREF, SUPPLIER_NAME, RECDATE, POSTDATE ORDER BY RECDATE DESC` )); res.json({ notes: lower(result.rows), status }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Note detail: line items for one note ---- // Joins GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES -> VESTOCK -> VEDEPTS to also bring back which // department each line belongs to and that department's minimum margin, so // the frontend can flag under-margin lines without a separate round trip. app.get('/api/invoices/notes/:suppid/:recnoteid/items', async (req, res) => { const { suppid, recnoteid } = req.params; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT g.STOCKID, g.STOCK_NAME, g.QTY, g.NET_VALUE, g.VAT_VALUE, g.RETAIL_PRICE, g.ONLINE_PRICE, g.PACK_SIZE, g.VATID, d.NAME AS DEPARTMENT_NAME, d.MIN_MARGIN_PCT AS DEPT_MIN_MARGIN_PCT, (SELECT MAX(ss.CATNO) FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss WHERE ss.STOCKID = g.STOCKID AND ss.SUPPID = g.SUPPID) AS CATNO, (SELECT LISTAGG(b.BARCODE, ', ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY b.BARCODE) FROM VEBARCODES b WHERE b.STOCKID = g.STOCKID) AS BARCODE FROM GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES g LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = g.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VEDEPTS d ON d.DEPTID = s.DEPTID WHERE g.SUPPID = :suppid AND g.RECNOTEID = :recnoteid ORDER BY g.STOCK_NAME`, { suppid, recnoteid } )); const items = lower(result.rows).map(r => ({ stockid: r.stockid, stock_name: r.stock_name, qty: r.qty, net_value: r.net_value, vat_value: r.vat_value, cost_price: (r.qty && r.qty !== 0) ? r.net_value / r.qty : null, retail_price: r.retail_price, online_price: r.online_price, pack_size: r.pack_size, vatid: r.vatid, catno: r.catno || '', barcode: r.barcode || '', department_name: r.department_name || 'Other', min_margin_pct: r.dept_min_margin_pct !== null && r.dept_min_margin_pct !== undefined ? Number(r.dept_min_margin_pct) : 38 })); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- DEPARTMENT MINIMUM MARGINS Backs the "Edit Minimum Margins" panel inside Invoices. Reads/writes VEDEPTS.MIN_MARGIN_PCT directly (see the ALTER TABLE note above) — every department shares the same table Invoices uses to flag lines. --------------------------------------------------------- */ app.get('/api/departments/margins', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT DEPTID, NAME, NVL(MIN_MARGIN_PCT, 38) AS MIN_MARGIN_PCT FROM VEDEPTS ORDER BY NAME` )); res.json({ departments: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/departments/margins', async (req, res) => { const { departments } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(departments) || departments.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'departments array is required' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { for (const d of departments) { const margin = Number(d.min_margin_pct); if (d.deptid === undefined || d.deptid === null || isNaN(margin)) continue; await conn.execute( `UPDATE VEDEPTS SET MIN_MARGIN_PCT = :margin WHERE DEPTID = :deptid`, { margin, deptid: d.deptid }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- AUTOMATED INVOICE CHECK — extracts product lines from photos of a physical invoice using Google's Gemini API (vision), instead of the old in-browser Tesseract OCR + regex guessing (which struggled on real-world creased/annotated photos). Requires a GEMINI_API_KEY env var — get a free key at https://aistudio.google.com/apikey (free tier: no card required, rate-limited per minute/day; see Google's docs for current limits). Accepts one or more images (multiple images = multiple pages of the SAME invoice) as { images: [{ mimeType, data (base64) }, ...] } and returns { items: [{ product_name, qty, unit_price, net_value }, ...] }. --------------------------------------------------------- */ app.post('/api/invoices/ocr-extract', async (req, res) => { const { images } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(images) || images.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Send at least one image' }); } if (!process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY) { return res.status(500).json({ error: 'GEMINI_API_KEY is not configured on the server' }); } try { const parts = [ { text: 'You are reading one or more photos of the SAME physical supplier document ' + '(multiple photos = multiple pages of one document). It may be a formal printed invoice ' + 'with a QTY column and a pack notation like "Cs1x6" in the product description, OR a ' + 'handwritten/printed pick list where the case size is embedded directly in the product ' + 'name (e.g. "Haribo x30 Happy Cherries" means 30 units per case, or "Coriander 20\'s" means ' + '20 units per case) and the quantity ordered is a handwritten number/tally mark to the left ' + 'of each line. Handle both formats. ' + 'Extract every product line item across all the images and return ONLY a JSON array (no ' + 'markdown, no code fences, no commentary) where each item is: {"product_name": string, ' + '"case_qty": number, "units_per_case": number, "qty": number, "case_price": number|null, ' + '"unit_price": number|null}. ' + '"case_qty" is the number of cases/cartons ordered — the printed QTY column value on a ' + 'formal invoice, or the handwritten count to the left of the line on a pick list. ' + '"units_per_case" is the case/pack multiplier for that product — from notation like ' + '"Cs1x6", "1x6", "Case of 6", "Cs 12" in the description, an "x" prefix directly in ' + 'the product name on a pick list (e.g. "x30" means 30), or a "\'s" suffix in the ' + 'name (e.g. "20\'s" means 20); if truly no multiplier is shown anywhere, use 1. Read this ' + "multiplier carefully and independently for each line — do not reuse the previous line's value. " + '"qty" MUST equal case_qty × units_per_case — the total individual units received, which is ' + 'what stock records track, NOT just the case count. ' + '"case_price" is the price printed per case/carton (the "Price" or "Net Price" column, or ' + 'the price figure on a pick list row), if shown. ' + '"unit_price" MUST equal case_price ÷ units_per_case — the price per individual unit, so it ' + 'is comparable to a per-unit stock cost, not a per-case price. ' + 'Never pull qty or price from a product code or barcode digit. If a value isn\'t printed, ' + "isn't handwritten anywhere on the line, or is genuinely illegible, use null for it rather " + 'than guessing — do not invent a plausible-looking number. Skip header/footer/bank-detail/' + 'totals lines — only individual product rows.' }, ...images.map(img => ({ inline_data: { mime_type: (img && img.mimeType) || 'image/jpeg', data: (img && img.data) || '' } })) ]; // Model list is env-configurable so a retired/over-quota model can be // swapped without a code change. Comma-separated, tried in order. // e.g. GEMINI_MODELS=gemini-3.5-flash,gemini-3.5-flash-lite // Free-tier quota is tracked PER MODEL, so falling through to a second // model gives extra daily requests. Check // https://ai.google.dev/gemini-api/docs/models for current model names. const GEMINI_MODELS = (process.env.GEMINI_MODELS || 'gemini-3.5-flash') .split(',').map(m => m.trim()).filter(Boolean); const geminiBody = JSON.stringify({ contents: [{ role: 'user', parts }], generationConfig: { temperature: 0, responseMimeType: 'application/json' } }); // 503 (model overloaded): retry the same model with short backoff. // 429 (quota): a per-DAY quota will not clear by waiting 40s, so move on // to the next model in the list instead of retrying. Anything else // (400/404 etc.) will not be fixed by retrying, so fail straight away. let geminiRes, quotaHit = false, lastStatus = 0; modelLoop: for (const model of GEMINI_MODELS) { const url = `https://generativelanguage.googleapis.com/v1beta/models/${model}:generateContent?key=${process.env.GEMINI_API_KEY}`; for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= 3; attempt++) { geminiRes = await fetch(url, { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: geminiBody }); if (geminiRes.ok) break modelLoop; const errText = await geminiRes.text(); lastStatus = geminiRes.status; console.error(`Gemini API error (${model}, attempt ${attempt}/3):`, geminiRes.status, errText); if (geminiRes.status === 429) { quotaHit = true; continue modelLoop; } if (geminiRes.status !== 503 || attempt === 3) { if (geminiRes.status === 503) continue modelLoop; return res.status(502).json({ error: 'Gemini API request failed (' + geminiRes.status + ')' }); } await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, attempt * 3000)); // 3s, then 6s } } if (!geminiRes || !geminiRes.ok) { if (quotaHit) { return res.status(429).json({ error: 'Invoice reading is temporarily unavailable: the Gemini API daily quota has been used up. Try again tomorrow or ask an admin to enable billing on the Gemini API key.' }); } return res.status(502).json({ error: 'Gemini API request failed (' + lastStatus + ')' }); } const geminiData = await geminiRes.json(); const rawText = (geminiData?.candidates?.[0]?.content?.parts || []) .map(p => p.text || '') .join(''); let items; try { items = JSON.parse(rawText); } catch (e) { // Fallback in case the model wrapped the JSON in code fences anyway const cleaned = rawText.replace(/```json|```/g, '').trim(); items = JSON.parse(cleaned); } if (!Array.isArray(items)) items = []; res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error('invoice ocr-extract error', e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- CREATE INVOICE — a third Invoices category alongside Pending/Received. Staff upload a PDF of a real invoice, it's OCR'd (reuses ocr-extract above — Gemini accepts a PDF directly via inline_data, same as an image, so no separate extraction path is needed), each line is auto-matched to a real product where possible, quantities are entered by hand (deliberately left blank — OCR quantities are not trustworthy enough to pre-fill, especially for by-weight items), and finally each line is priced: previous cost/selling price come from THIS SAME SUPPLIER's most recent past goods-received line for that product (different suppliers charge different prices for the same item, so a global "last price" would be misleading) — see PREVIOUS_COST_PRICE / PREVIOUS_SELLING_PRICE below. Margin compares the NEW cost being entered against the EXISTING selling price, i.e. "if we don't change what we charge, does this new cost still clear our margin target?" — flagged red client-side using the same VEDEPTS.MIN_MARGIN_PCT threshold as the Pending/Received margin check. Requires CREATED_INVOICES and CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS — see created_invoices_schema.sql. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- List / create draft invoices ---- app.get('/api/invoices/created', async (req, res) => { try { // FIX: was a per-row correlated subquery with no cap, so the list got // slower as invoices piled up over time. Rewritten as a single join+group // by, plus a 200-row cap — plenty for browsing recent invoices, and this // is a list screen, not the system of record (old invoices aren't lost, // just not shown here past 200). Bump the number, or ask for pagination // instead, if 200 turns out to be too few in practice. const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT h.INVOICE_ID, h.SUPPID, h.SUPPLIER_NAME, h.REFERENCE, h.STATUS, h.CREATED_BY, h.CREATED_AT, NVL(c.ITEM_COUNT, 0) AS ITEM_COUNT FROM CREATED_INVOICES h LEFT JOIN ( SELECT INVOICE_ID, COUNT(*) AS ITEM_COUNT FROM CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS GROUP BY INVOICE_ID ) c ON c.INVOICE_ID = h.INVOICE_ID ORDER BY h.CREATED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 200 ROWS ONLY` )); res.json({ invoices: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/invoices/created', async (req, res) => { const { suppid, supplier_name, reference, created_by } = req.body || {}; if (!suppid || !supplier_name) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'suppid and supplier_name are required — pick a supplier first' }); } try { const invoiceId = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO CREATED_INVOICES (SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, REFERENCE, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :supplierName, :reference, :createdBy) RETURNING INVOICE_ID INTO :new_id`, { suppid, supplierName: supplier_name, reference: reference || null, createdBy: created_by || null, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.new_id[0]; }); res.json({ ok: true, invoice_id: invoiceId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/invoices/created/:id', async (req, res) => { const { status, supplier_name, reference } = req.body || {}; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE CREATED_INVOICES SET STATUS = NVL(:status, STATUS), SUPPLIER_NAME = NVL(:supplierName, SUPPLIER_NAME), REFERENCE = NVL(:reference, REFERENCE), UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { status: status || null, supplierName: supplier_name || null, reference: reference || null, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/invoices/created/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CREATED_INVOICES WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Detail: header + items, enriched with this-supplier's previous cost/selling // price, live margin inputs, department + min margin, and barcode ---- app.get('/api/invoices/created/:id', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const headerResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT INVOICE_ID, SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, REFERENCE, STATUS, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT FROM CREATED_INVOICES WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { id } ); const header = lower(headerResult.rows)[0]; if (!header) return null; // PREVIOUS_COST_PRICE and PACK_SIZE_PREVIOUS come from SUPPLIER_STOCK for // THIS SAME SUPPLIER (ss.SUPPID = header.suppid) — that table holds one // row per stockid+supplier, so this is "what we have on file for this // exact supplier's cost and pack size", not a guess from whoever last // happened to deliver it. // RETAIL_PRICE defaults from STOCK_PRICES (PRICEID = 1 = Retail) via // sp.PRICE_NET the first time this item is loaded — staff can still // override it, and once i.RETAIL_PRICE has been saved (non-null) that // saved value always wins over the live Vector price. const itemsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ID, i.OCR_PRODUCT_NAME, i.STOCKID, i.MATCHED_NAME, i.LINE_ORDER, i.QTY_BOX, i.QTY_PER_BOX, i.BOX_PRICE, NVL(i.RETAIL_PRICE, sp.PRICE_NET) AS RETAIL_PRICE, ss.COST_NET AS PREVIOUS_COST_PRICE, ss.PACKSIZE AS PACK_SIZE_PREVIOUS, (SELECT LISTAGG(b.BARCODE, ', ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY b.BARCODE) FROM VEBARCODES b WHERE b.STOCKID = i.STOCKID) AS BARCODE, d.NAME AS DEPARTMENT_NAME, NVL(d.MIN_MARGIN_PCT, 38) AS MIN_MARGIN_PCT FROM CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS i LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = i.STOCKID LEFT JOIN VEDEPTS d ON d.DEPTID = s.DEPTID LEFT JOIN SUPPLIER_STOCK ss ON ss.STOCKID = i.STOCKID AND ss.SUPPID = :suppid LEFT JOIN STOCK_PRICES sp ON sp.STOCKID = i.STOCKID AND sp.PRICEID = 1 WHERE i.INVOICE_ID = :id ORDER BY i.LINE_ORDER`, { id, suppid: header.suppid } ); const items = lower(itemsResult.rows).map(r => ({ item_id: r.item_id, ocr_product_name: r.ocr_product_name, stockid: r.stockid, matched_name: r.matched_name, qty_box: r.qty_box, qty_per_box: r.qty_per_box, box_price: r.box_price, retail_price: r.retail_price, previous_cost_price: r.previous_cost_price, pack_size_previous: r.pack_size_previous, barcode: r.barcode || '', department_name: r.department_name || 'Other', min_margin_pct: r.min_margin_pct !== null && r.min_margin_pct !== undefined ? Number(r.min_margin_pct) : 38 })); return { header, items }; }); if (!data) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'Invoice not found' }); res.json(data); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Replace the full item list — used after OCR+matching, again after // quantities are entered, and again after prices are edited. Simplest // safe approach for a list that can grow/shrink as unmatched lines get // resolved via search: delete and reinsert rather than diff/upsert. ---- app.put('/api/invoices/created/:id/items', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { items } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(items)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'items array is required' }); try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { id }); if (items.length > 0) { const num = v => (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') ? null : v; const binds = items.map((it, i) => ({ id, ocrName: it.ocr_product_name || null, stockid: it.stockid || null, matchedName: it.matched_name || null, qtyBox: num(it.qty_box), qtyPerBox: num(it.qty_per_box), boxPrice: num(it.box_price), retailPrice: num(it.retail_price), lineOrder: i + 1 })); // FIX: this used to loop conn.execute() once per line — a 30-line // invoice meant 30 separate round-trips to Oracle on every autosave. // executeMany() sends the whole batch as ONE round-trip, same fix // already used above for the Wholesale "Block" button. await conn.executeMany( `INSERT INTO CREATED_INVOICE_ITEMS (INVOICE_ID, OCR_PRODUCT_NAME, STOCKID, MATCHED_NAME, QTY_BOX, QTY_PER_BOX, BOX_PRICE, RETAIL_PRICE, LINE_ORDER) VALUES (:id, :ocrName, :stockid, :matchedName, :qtyBox, :qtyPerBox, :boxPrice, :retailPrice, :lineOrder)`, binds ); } await conn.execute(`UPDATE CREATED_INVOICES SET UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE INVOICE_ID = :id`, { id }); await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Batch-match OCR product names to real products via VESTOCK.NOTES — // no more fuzzy guessing. Staff maintain NOTES as a comma-separated // list of that product's known invoice names across suppliers (e.g. // "Coke Can 330ml, CocaCola 330ml, Coca-Cola Can"), so a match here // means the invoice name matches one of those exactly — not a // best-effort word overlap. Whitespace around commas and case are // both ignored, but each comma-separated piece must match in full; // a name that's merely a substring of one alias no longer counts // (that was the old fuzzy behaviour, which is exactly what "no more // guessing" was about).
