Anything with no NOTES match comes back // unmatched for manual search — same red-flag-and-search flow as // before, just without ever silently guessing wrong. ---- app.post('/api/invoices/match-items', async (req, res) => { const { names } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(names) || names.length === 0) return res.json({ matches: [] }); try { const matches = await withConnection(async conn => { const out = []; for (const rawName of names) { const q = (rawName || '').trim(); if (!q) { out.push({ query: rawName, stockid: null, item_name: null, matched_via: null }); continue; } // Normalise NOTES to ",alias1,alias2,alias3," (no stray whitespace around // commas) and check whether ",THIS NAME," appears as a whole segment — // that's what makes this an exact-segment match rather than a substring // match that could accidentally hit part of a different alias. // FETCH FIRST 2: if more than one product shares the same alias, treat // it as ambiguous/unmatched rather than silently picking one at random. const notesResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, NAME FROM VESTOCK WHERE NOTES IS NOT NULL AND UPPER(',' || REGEXP_REPLACE(TRIM(NOTES), '\\s*,\\s*', ',') || ',') LIKE '%,' || UPPER(TRIM(:q)) || ',%' FETCH FIRST 2 ROWS ONLY`, { q } ); const notesMatches = lower(notesResult.rows); out.push(notesMatches.length === 1 ? { query: rawName, stockid: notesMatches[0].stockid, item_name: notesMatches[0].name, matched_via: 'notes_exact' } : { query: rawName, stockid: null, item_name: null, matched_via: null }); } return out; }); res.json({ matches }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- SUPPLIER FILE CHECK — SAVED STATE Backs the Save / reopen / Refresh buttons on the Received-note "Supplier Check" screen (Automated Check -> CSV / Excel). One saved check per note (SUPPID + RECNOTEID); the whole screen state (parsed invoice lines, tick/cross decisions, edited prices, manual entries) is stored as JSON. Table is created automatically on boot by ensureInvoiceSupplierChecksTable: CREATE TABLE INVOICE_SUPPLIER_CHECKS ( SUPPID NUMBER NOT NULL, RECNOTEID VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, STATE_JSON CLOB NOT NULL, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), PRIMARY KEY (SUPPID, RECNOTEID)) --------------------------------------------------------- */ async function ensureInvoiceSupplierChecksTable() { await withConnection(async conn => { try { await conn.execute( `CREATE TABLE INVOICE_SUPPLIER_CHECKS ( SUPPID NUMBER NOT NULL, RECNOTEID VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, STATE_JSON CLOB NOT NULL, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CONSTRAINT INVOICE_SUPPLIER_CHECKS_PK PRIMARY KEY (SUPPID, RECNOTEID) )`, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[supplier-check] table created'); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[supplier-check] schema error:', e.message); } }); } app.get('/api/invoices/supplier-check/:suppid/:recnoteid', async (req, res) => { try { const row = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute( `SELECT STATE_JSON, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY FROM INVOICE_SUPPLIER_CHECKS WHERE SUPPID = :suppid AND RECNOTEID = :recnoteid`, { suppid: req.params.suppid, recnoteid: String(req.params.recnoteid) } ); return lower(r.rows)[0] || null; }); if (!row) return res.json({ saved: false }); const json = await clobToString(row.state_json); res.json({ saved: true, state: JSON.parse(json), updated_at: row.updated_at, updated_by: row.updated_by }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/invoices/supplier-check/:suppid/:recnoteid', async (req, res) => { const { state, updated_by } = req.body || {}; if (!state || typeof state !== 'object') return res.status(400).json({ error: 'state is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO INVOICE_SUPPLIER_CHECKS t USING (SELECT :suppid AS SUPPID, :recnoteid AS RECNOTEID FROM DUAL) s ON (t.SUPPID = s.SUPPID AND t.RECNOTEID = s.RECNOTEID) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET t.STATE_JSON = :json, t.UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, t.UPDATED_BY = :by WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SUPPID, RECNOTEID, STATE_JSON, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :recnoteid, :json, :by)`, { suppid: req.params.suppid, recnoteid: String(req.params.recnoteid), json: { val: JSON.stringify(state), type: oracledb.CLOB }, by: updated_by || null }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- NEW ITEMS (Invoices > New Items) — saved lists + send to Vector. Each list is a named bundle of brand-new products (not yet in Vector). "Update" validates every item against the live Vector-synced data, builds ONE CSV in a fixed column order and queues it as IMPORT_TYPE 'NEWITEM' through the same VECTOR_IMPORT_QUEUE the other imports use, so the till's poller + watcher pick it up exactly like Create Invoice. NEWITEM CSV COLUMN ORDER (this is what File Import Settings must be set to on the till, for this import only — columns 1-7 are identical to the existing RETAIL/WHOLESALE layout, so nothing existing moves): 1 Stock Description 2 Pack Size 3 Cost Price (NET) 4 Retail Price (inc VAT) 5 EAN Barcode 6 Department Name 7 Supplier Name 8 Catalogue No. 9 Size 10 VAT Rate End Field or Column = 11 Requires NEW_ITEM_LISTS (created automatically on boot). --------------------------------------------------------- */ const NEW_ITEM_IMPORT_FILE_NAME = 'hallan_new_items_import.csv'; // fixed name — the till's watcher looks for this exact filename const NEW_ITEM_STANDARD_VAT = 0.20; const NEW_ITEM_VAT_NAMES = ['Zero Rate', 'Standard Rate']; async function ensureNewItemListsTable() { await withConnection(async conn => { try { await conn.execute( `CREATE TABLE NEW_ITEM_LISTS ( LIST_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, LIST_NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, ITEMS_JSON CLOB, ITEM_COUNT NUMBER DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, STATUS VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'OPEN' NOT NULL, QUEUE_ID NUMBER, SENT_AT TIMESTAMP, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[new-items] table created'); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[new-items] schema error:', e.message); } }); } function newItemText(v, max) { return String(v === undefined || v === null ? '' : v).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').trim().slice(0, max); } function newItemNum(v) { if (v === undefined || v === null || v === '') return null; const n = Number(v); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null; } function newItemCleanItems(raw) { if (!Array.isArray(raw)) return []; return raw.slice(0, 500).map((r, i) => { r = r || {}; return { uid: newItemText(r.uid, 40) || ('n' + i), description: newItemText(r.description, 200), barcode: newItemText(r.barcode, 30).replace(/\s+/g, ''), size: newItemText(r.size, 50), department: newItemText(r.department, 200), vat: newItemText(r.vat, 20), supplier: newItemText(r.supplier, 200), catalogue_no: newItemText(r.catalogue_no, 60), pack_size: newItemNum(r.pack_size), cost_net: newItemNum(r.cost_net), retail_inc: newItemNum(r.retail_inc), margin: newItemNum(r.margin), markup: newItemNum(r.markup), quantity: newItemNum(r.quantity), driver: ['retail', 'margin', 'markup'].includes(r.driver) ? r.driver : 'retail' }; }); } function newItemMoney(n) { const r2 = Math.round(n * 100) / 100; return Math.abs(r2 - n) < 1e-9 ? r2.toFixed(2) : String(Number(n.toFixed(4))); } function newItemInClause(prefix, values, binds) { return values.map((v, i) => { binds[prefix + i] = v; return ':' + prefix + i; }).join(','); } app.get('/api/invoices/new-item-lists', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT LIST_ID, LIST_NAME, ITEM_COUNT, STATUS, SENT_AT, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY FROM NEW_ITEM_LISTS ORDER BY UPDATED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 200 ROWS ONLY`)); res.json({ lists: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.get('/api/invoices/new-item-lists/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid list id is required' }); try { const row = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute( `SELECT LIST_ID, LIST_NAME, ITEMS_JSON, STATUS, SENT_AT, UPDATED_AT FROM NEW_ITEM_LISTS WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { id }); return lower(r.rows)[0] || null; }); if (!row) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'List not found' }); const json = row.items_json ? await clobToString(row.items_json) : '[]'; let items = []; try { items = JSON.parse(json || '[]'); } catch (e) { items = []; } res.json({ list_id: row.list_id, list_name: row.list_name, status: row.status, sent_at: row.sent_at, updated_at: row.updated_at, items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/invoices/new-item-lists', async (req, res) => { const name = newItemText((req.body || {}).name, 200); if (!name) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A list name is required' }); const items = newItemCleanItems((req.body || {}).items); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const id = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO NEW_ITEM_LISTS (LIST_NAME, ITEMS_JSON, ITEM_COUNT, CREATED_BY, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (:name, :json, :cnt, :by, :by) RETURNING LIST_ID INTO :id`, { name, json: { val: JSON.stringify(items), type: oracledb.CLOB }, cnt: items.length, by, id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true }); return r.outBinds.id[0]; }); res.json({ ok: true, list_id: id, item_count: items.length }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/invoices/new-item-lists/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'valid list id is required' }); const name = newItemText((req.body || {}).name, 200); if (!name) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A list name is required' }); const items = newItemCleanItems((req.body || {}).items); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE NEW_ITEM_LISTS SET LIST_NAME = :name, ITEMS_JSON = :json, ITEM_COUNT = :cnt, UPDATED_BY = :by, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { name, json: { val: JSON.stringify(items), type: oracledb.CLOB }, cnt: items.length, by, id }, { autoCommit: true })); } let items = []; try { items = newItemCleanItems(JSON.parse(await clobToString(row.items_json) || '[]')); } catch (e) { items = []; } if (items.length === 0) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The list has no items' }); const [deptRows, suppRows] = await withConnection(async conn => { const d = await conn.execute(`SELECT NAME FROM VEDEPTS`); const s = await conn.execute(`SELECT NAME FROM SUPPLIERS`); return [d, s]; }); const deptMap = new Map(lower(deptRows.rows).map(d => [String(d.name).trim().toUpperCase(), String(d.name).trim()])); const suppMap = new Map(lower(suppRows.rows).map(s => [String(s.name).trim().toUpperCase(), String(s.name).trim()])); // Barcodes / names that already exist in Vector (as last synced to Oracle) const barcodes = [...new Set(items.map(i => i.barcode).filter(Boolean))]; const names = [...new Set(items.map(i => i.description.toUpperCase()).filter(Boolean))]; const existingBarcodes = new Set(); const existingNames = new Set(); await withConnection(async conn => { for (let i = 0; i < barcodes.length; i += 500) { const binds = {}; const r = await conn.execute(`SELECT BARCODE FROM VEBARCODES WHERE BARCODE IN (${newItemInClause('b', barcodes.slice(i, i + 500), binds)})`, binds); lower(r.rows).forEach(x => existingNames.add(String(x.nkey))); } }); const seenBarcodes = new Map(); const seenNames = new Map(); const problems = []; const csvRows = []; items.forEach((it, idx) => { const msgs = []; if (!it.description) msgs.push('Description is required'); if (!it.barcode) msgs.push('Barcode is required'); else if (!/^\d{4,14}$/.test(it.barcode)) msgs.push('Barcode must be digits only (4-14 digits)'); const dept = deptMap.get(it.department.toUpperCase()); if (!it.department) msgs.push('Department is required'); else if (!dept) msgs.push('Department "' + it.department + '" does not exist in Vector — pick one from the list'); const supp = suppMap.get(it.supplier.toUpperCase()); if (!it.supplier) msgs.push('Supplier is required'); else if (!supp) msgs.push('Supplier "' + it.supplier + '" does not exist in Vector — pick one from the list'); if (!NEW_ITEM_VAT_NAMES.includes(it.vat)) msgs.push('VAT Rate must be Zero Rate or Standard Rate'); if (!(it.pack_size > 0)) msgs.push('Pack Size must be more than 0'); if (it.cost_net === null || it.cost_net < 0) msgs.push('Item Cost NET is required'); if (it.retail_inc === null || !(it.retail_inc > 0)) msgs.push('Retail Price inc VAT is required'); if (it.barcode) { if (existingBarcodes.has(it.barcode)) msgs.push('Barcode ' + it.barcode + ' already exists in Vector'); if (seenBarcodes.has(it.barcode)) msgs.push('Barcode ' + it.barcode + ' is used again on row ' + seenBarcodes.get(it.barcode)); else seenBarcodes.set(it.barcode, idx + 1); } if (it.description) { const nk = it.description.toUpperCase(); if (existingNames.has(nk)) msgs.push('An item called "' + it.description + '" already exists in Vector'); if (seenNames.has(nk)) msgs.push('Same description as row ' + seenNames.get(nk)); else seenNames.set(nk, idx + 1); } if (msgs.length) { problems.push({ row: idx + 1, uid: it.uid, name: it.description, messages: msgs }); return; } csvRows.push([ it.description, String(Number(it.pack_size)), newItemMoney(it.cost_net), newItemMoney(it.retail_inc), it.barcode, dept, supp, it.catalogue_no, it.size, it.vat ]); }); if (problems.length) { return res.status(422).json({ error: problems.length + ' item(s) need fixing before they can go to Vector', problems }); } const headers = ['Stock Description', 'Pack Size', 'Cost Price', 'Retail Price', 'EAN Barcode', 'Department Name', 'Supplier Name', 'Catalogue No', 'Size', 'VAT Rate']; const csvText = [headers.join(',')].concat(csvRows.map(r => r.map(csvEscapeField).join(','))).join('\n'); const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) || null; const queueId = await withConnection(async conn => { const qid = await queueVectorImport(conn, { file_name: NEW_ITEM_IMPORT_FILE_NAME, csv_content: csvText, type: 'NEWITEM' }); await conn.execute( `UPDATE NEW_ITEM_LISTS SET STATUS = 'SENT', QUEUE_ID = :qid, SENT_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY = :by, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { qid, by, id }, { autoCommit: true }); return qid; }); res.json({ ok: true, queue_id: queueId, count: csvRows.length }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- RETAIL LISTS (Making List + Picking List workflow) Requires RETAIL_LISTS and RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS tables — see retail_schema.sql RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS now also needs a QTY_PICKED column — see the ALTER TABLE statement noted above this file. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Lists: list all (with item + fully-picked counts) ---- app.get('/api/retail/lists', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.LIST_ID, l.LIST_NAME, l.STATUS, l.CREATED_BY, l.PICKED_BY, l.STARTED_AT, l.FINALIZED_AT, l.PICK_STARTED_AT, l.CLOSED_AT, NVL(l.MAKING_SECONDS, 0) AS MAKING_SECONDS, NVL(l.PICKING_SECONDS, 0) AS PICKING_SECONDS, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS i WHERE i.LIST_ID = l.LIST_ID) AS ITEM_COUNT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS i WHERE i.LIST_ID = l.LIST_ID AND NVL(i.QTY_PICKED, 0) >= i.QTY_ADDED) AS PICKED_COUNT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS i WHERE i.LIST_ID = l.LIST_ID AND NVL(i.QTY_PICKED, 0) > 0 AND NVL(i.QTY_PICKED, 0) < i.QTY_ADDED) AS PARTIAL_COUNT FROM RETAIL_LISTS l ORDER BY l.STARTED_AT DESC` )); try { const listRes = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT LIST_ID, LIST_NAME, STATUS, CREATED_BY, PICKED_BY, STARTED_AT, FINALIZED_AT, PICK_STARTED_AT, CLOSED_AT, NVL(MAKING_SECONDS, 0) AS MAKING_SECONDS, NVL(PICKING_SECONDS, 0) AS PICKING_SECONDS FROM RETAIL_LISTS WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { id } )); ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME, sh.SHELF_NAME, a.ROW_NAME) FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS li JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = li.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE li.STOCKID = i.STOCKID), (SELECT LISTAGG(z.ZONE_NAME || ' / ' || sh.SHELF_NAME || ' / Row ' || a.ROW_NAME, '; ') WITHIN GROUP (ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME, sh.SHELF_NAME, a.ROW_NAME) FROM LOCATION_ROW_ITEMS li JOIN LOCATION_ROWS a ON a.ROW_ID = li.ROW_ID JOIN LOCATION_SHELVES sh ON sh.SHELF_ID = a.SHELF_ID JOIN LOCATION_ZONES z ON z.ZONE_ID = sh.ZONE_ID WHERE li.STOCKID IS NULL AND li.ITEM_NAME = i.ITEM_NAME) ) AS LOCATION FROM RETAIL_LIST_ITEMS i LEFT JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = i.STOCKID WHERE i.LIST_ID = :id ORDER BY i.ADDED_AT`, { id } )); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: reopen a finalized list back into Making List (undo an accidental finalize) ---- app.post('/api/retail/lists/:id/reopen', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: mark picking as started (sets the picking timer's zero point, once) and note who's picking ---- app.post('/api/retail/lists/:id/start-picking', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { picked_by } = req.body || {}; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETAIL_LISTS SET PICK_STARTED_AT = COALESCE(PICK_STARTED_AT, SYSTIMESTAMP), PICKED_BY = COALESCE(:picked_by, PICKED_BY) WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { id, picked_by: (picked_by && picked_by.trim()) || null }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: add elapsed *active* seconds to making or picking time ---- // The frontend calls this every ~20s while a list is actually open on screen (and flushes on // close/hide/tab-away), so MAKING_SECONDS / PICKING_SECONDS reflect real working time, not // wall-clock time since the list was first created. app.post('/api/retail/lists/:id/track-time', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; const { phase, seconds } = req.body || {}; const secs = Math.max(0, Math.min(3600, Math.floor(Number(seconds) || 0))); // sanity cap per call if (secs <= 0 || (phase !== 'making' && phase !== 'picking')) { return res.json({ ok: true, skipped: true }); } const col = phase === 'making' ? 'MAKING_SECONDS' : 'PICKING_SECONDS'; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETAIL_LISTS SET ${col} = NVL(${col}, 0) + :secs WHERE LIST_ID = :id RETURNING ${col} INTO :new_total`, { id, secs, new_total: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); const total = result.outBinds.new_total ? result.outBinds.new_total[0] : null; res.json({ ok: true, total_seconds: total }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: close (end of the making session, or end of picking) ---- app.post('/api/retail/lists/:id/close', async (req, res) => { const { id } = req.params; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETAIL_LISTS SET STATUS = 'CLOSED', CLOSED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* =========================================================== STAFF PORTAL API (Phase 1: auth, leave, lateness, illness) =========================================================== */ function legacyHash(password, salt) { return crypto.createHash('sha256').update(String(password) + String(salt)).digest('hex'); } // Role is never taken from the client anymore — it's derived from whatever // department is actually on file for the employee in staff_employees. This // removes any need for the login/signup forms to ask "what role/access are // you signing in as" and removes the old cross-check that used to exist to // stop someone picking a role their department didn't allow — now there's // nothing for them to pick. 'staff' covers everyone not in a Manager/HR // department; the old separate 'rota_editor' role never granted anything // beyond 'staff' at the token level (real per-department edit rights come // from the Access tab in schedule.html, keyed by rota_login_id), so it's // folded into 'staff' here. function deriveRoleFromDepartment(department) { const dept = (department || '').toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ''); if (dept.includes('manage')) return 'manager'; if (dept.includes('hr')) return 'hr'; return 'staff'; } // Case/whitespace-insensitive exact match, used to compare what the person // typed (name, department) against what HR has on file. const normalizeText = s => String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\s+/g, ' '); function staffAuthMiddleware(req, res, next) { const header = req.headers.authorization || ''; const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : null; if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: 'Not signed in' }); try { req.staffUser = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET); next(); } catch (e) { return res.status(401).json({ ok: false, error: 'Session expired, please sign in again' }); } } function requireStaffRole(...roles) { return (req, res, next) => { if (!req.staffUser || !roles.includes(req.staffUser.role)) { return res.status(403).json({ ok: false, error: 'Not permitted for this role' }); } next(); }; } // ---- Departments list (public — used to populate the sign-up dropdown // so people pick a real department instead of retyping HR's exact // spelling) ---- app.get('/api/staff/departments', async (req, res) => { try { const departments = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute( `SELECT DISTINCT department FROM staff_employees WHERE active = 1 AND department IS NOT NULL ORDER BY department` ); return lower(rs.rows).map(r => r.department); }); res.json({ ok: true, departments }); } catch (err) { console.error('departments list error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not load departments' }); } }); app.get('/api/staff/health', (req, res) => res.json({ status: 'ok', area: 'staff-portal' })); /* --------------------------------------------------------- STAFF LOGIN 2FA (email code) — same two-step pattern as the Portal login above (PORTAL_USERS / PORTAL_LOGIN_OTP): step 1 checks the password and, if correct, emails a 6-digit code instead of handing back a token straight away; step 2 (/api/staff/login/verify) checks that code and only then issues the JWT. This applies uniformly to /api/staff/login, /api/staff/signup and /api/staff/forgot-password — all three now end by calling beginStaffOtp() instead of signing a token directly, so there is exactly one place (verify, below) that ever hands out a Rota session. staff_employees already has an EMAIL column (used elsewhere in the Staff Portal), so no schema change is needed there — this reuses whatever email HR already has on file for that Employee ID. If it's ever blank, login is refused with a clear message rather than silently skipping 2FA. Requires one new Oracle table — run this once (e.g. via DBeaver): CREATE TABLE STAFF_LOGIN_OTP ( OTP_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, EMPLOYEE_ID VARCHAR2(50) NOT NULL, CODE_HASH VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, EXPIRES_AT TIMESTAMP NOT NULL, ATTEMPTS NUMBER(2) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP ); --------------------------------------------------------- */ const STAFF_OTP_TTL_MINUTES = 10; const STAFF_OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS = 5; const STAFF_OTP_RESEND_COOLDOWN_MS = 30 * 1000; const STAFF_OTP_MAX_PER_DAY = 8; // Sends the 6-digit code for `emp` (a lower-cased staff_employees row) and // returns the { ok:true, requiresOtp:true, employeeId } shape every one of // login/signup/forgot-password now responds with. Throws (caller should // wrap in try/catch) if the email fails to send, since a code that never // arrives would otherwise strand the person mid-login. async function beginStaffOtp(conn, emp, pendingPasswordHash) { if (!emp.email) { return { ok: false, error: 'No email on file for this account — ask HR to add one to your Employee record before you can sign in.' }; } // Rate-limit how often a fresh code can be requested for this account — // covers both a normal resend and a repeated forgot-password/signup // call, all of which land here. Without this, resend-code had no limit // at all: no password needed, unlimited fresh codes, which floods the // account owner's inbox and gives an attacker unlimited guesses overall // (5 per code) even though each individual code only allows 5. const recent = await conn.execute( `SELECT CREATED_AT FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE EMPLOYEE_ID = :id ORDER BY OTP_ID DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY`, { id: emp.employee_id } ); if (recent.rows.length) { const lastCreated = new Date(lower(recent.rows)[0].created_at).getTime(); if (Date.now() - lastCreated < STAFF_OTP_RESEND_COOLDOWN_MS) { return { ok: false, error: `Please wait a few seconds before requesting another code.` }; } } const todayCount = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS CNT FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE EMPLOYEE_ID = :id AND CREATED_AT > SYSTIMESTAMP - 1`, { id: emp.employee_id } ); if (lower(todayCount.rows)[0].cnt >= STAFF_OTP_MAX_PER_DAY) { return { ok: false, error: 'Too many code requests for this account today — please try again tomorrow, or contact HR.' }; } const code = generateOtpCode(); const codeHash = await bcrypt.hash(code, 10); await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE EMPLOYEE_ID = :id`, { id: emp.employee_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO STAFF_LOGIN_OTP (EMPLOYEE_ID, CODE_HASH, EXPIRES_AT, PENDING_PASSWORD_HASH) VALUES (:id, :codeHash, CURRENT_TIMESTAMP + INTERVAL '${STAFF_OTP_TTL_MINUTES}' MINUTE, :pendingHash)`, { id: emp.employee_id, codeHash, pendingHash: pendingPasswordHash || null }, { autoCommit: true } ); try { await sendMail({ to: emp.email, subject: pendingPasswordHash ? 'Hallan Rota — confirm your password reset' : 'Hallan Rota — your sign-in code', text: pendingPasswordHash ? `Someone requested a password reset for this account. Your confirmation code is ${code}. It expires in ${STAFF_OTP_TTL_MINUTES} minutes. If this wasn't you, ignore this email and your password will stay unchanged.` : `Your sign-in code is ${code}. It expires in ${STAFF_OTP_TTL_MINUTES} minutes. If this wasn't you, ignore this email.` }); } catch (mailErr) { console.error('staff OTP email failed', mailErr); return { ok: false, error: "Couldn't send the code email — check BREVO_API_KEY/MAIL_FROM, or try again" }; } return { ok: true, requiresOtp: true, employeeId: emp.employee_id }; } // Shared by login/signup/forgot-password/verify — the token + emp payload // shape the front end has always expected. function issueStaffToken(emp) { const role = deriveRoleFromDepartment(emp.department); const isKiosk = KIOSK_EMPLOYEE_IDS.has(String(emp.employee_id)); const token = jwt.sign( { employeeId: emp.employee_id, role, name: emp.full_name }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: isKiosk ? KIOSK_TOKEN_TTL : TOKEN_TTL } ); return { ok: true, token, emp: { id: emp.employee_id, name: emp.full_name, email: emp.email, department: emp.department, position: emp.position, phone: emp.phone, holidayAllocation: emp.holiday_allocation, holidayTaken: emp.holiday_taken, holidayBalance: emp.holiday_balance }, role, isAdmin: role === 'hr', isManager: role === 'manager' }; } // ---- Login, step 1: { employeeId, password } -> emails a 6-digit code. // Role comes entirely from the employee's real department on file — // nothing about role or department is ever asked at login. ---- app.post('/api/staff/login', async (req, res) => { const { employeeId, password } = req.body || {}; if (!employeeId || !password) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Employee ID and password are required' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const empRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id AND active = 1`, { id: employeeId } ); if (empRs.rows.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Employee not found' }; const emp = lower(empRs.rows)[0]; const authRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT password_hash, legacy_salt, legacy_hash FROM staff_auth WHERE employee_id = :id`, { id: employeeId } ); if (authRs.rows.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'No password set for this account yet' }; const auth = lower(authRs.rows)[0]; let passwordOk = false; if (auth.password_hash) { passwordOk = await bcrypt.compare(password, auth.password_hash); } if (!passwordOk && auth.legacy_hash && auth.legacy_salt) { if (legacyHash(password, auth.legacy_salt) === auth.legacy_hash) { passwordOk = true; const newHash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10); await conn.execute( `UPDATE staff_auth SET password_hash = :h, legacy_salt = NULL, legacy_hash = NULL, updated_at = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE employee_id = :id`, { h: newHash, id: employeeId } ); await conn.commit(); } } if (!passwordOk) return { ok: false, error: 'Incorrect password' }; return beginStaffOtp(conn, emp); }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(401).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff login error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Login failed, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Login, step 2: { employeeId, code } -> JWT. Completes login, signup // AND forgot-password alike, since all three now funnel through // beginStaffOtp() above and land here to actually get a session. ---- app.post('/api/staff/login/verify', async (req, res) => { const { employeeId, code } = req.body || {}; const enteredCode = String(code || '').trim(); if (!employeeId || !enteredCode) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Enter the code from your email' }); try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const otpResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT OTP_ID, CODE_HASH, EXPIRES_AT, ATTEMPTS, PENDING_PASSWORD_HASH FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE EMPLOYEE_ID = :id ORDER BY OTP_ID DESC`, { id: employeeId } ); const otpRows = lower(otpResult.rows); const otp = otpRows[0]; if (!otp) return { ok: false, error: 'No code pending — please sign in again' }; if (otpRows.length > 1) { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE EMPLOYEE_ID = :id AND OTP_ID != :keepId`, { id: employeeId, keepId: otp.otp_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); } if (new Date(otp.expires_at).getTime() < Date.now()) { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE OTP_ID = :id`, { id: otp.otp_id }, { autoCommit: true }); return { ok: false, error: 'Code expired — please sign in again' }; } if (otp.attempts >= STAFF_OTP_MAX_ATTEMPTS) { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE OTP_ID = :id`, { id: otp.otp_id }, { autoCommit: true }); return { ok: false, error: 'Too many attempts — please sign in again' }; } const codeOk = await bcrypt.compare(enteredCode, otp.code_hash); if (!codeOk) { await conn.execute( `UPDATE STAFF_LOGIN_OTP SET ATTEMPTS = ATTEMPTS + 1 WHERE OTP_ID = :id`, { id: otp.otp_id }, { autoCommit: true } ); return { ok: false, error: 'Incorrect code' }; } const empRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id AND active = 1`, { id: employeeId } ); if (empRs.rows.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Employee not found' }; const emp = lower(empRs.rows)[0]; // Only now — after the emailed code has actually been confirmed — // does a pending forgot-password reset (if any) get applied. // Previously this write happened the moment forgot-password was // called, before any code was checked at all. if (otp.pending_password_hash) { const existingAuth = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id FROM staff_auth WHERE employee_id = :id`, { id: employeeId } ); if (existingAuth.rows.length === 0) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO staff_auth (employee_id, password_hash) VALUES (:id, :hash)`, { id: employeeId, hash: otp.pending_password_hash }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE staff_auth SET password_hash = :hash, legacy_salt = NULL, legacy_hash = NULL, updated_at = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE employee_id = :id`, { hash: otp.pending_password_hash, id: employeeId }, { autoCommit: true } ); } } await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM STAFF_LOGIN_OTP WHERE OTP_ID = :id`, { id: otp.otp_id }, { autoCommit: true }); return issueStaffToken(emp); }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(401).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff login verify error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Verification failed, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Resend code: { employeeId } -> re-sends a fresh code for a login // already in progress (e.g. the first email got lost/delayed). Deliberately // does NOT re-check the password — step 1 already proved that — only that // this employee is still a real, active account. ---- app.post('/api/staff/login/resend-code', async (req, res) => { const { employeeId } = req.body || {}; if (!employeeId) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Missing employee ID' }); try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const empRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id AND active = 1`, { id: employeeId } ); if (empRs.rows.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'Employee not found' }; const emp = lower(empRs.rows)[0]; return beginStaffOtp(conn, emp); }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(400).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff resend-code error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not resend the code, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Sign up: { employeeId, department, password } ---- // This only ever creates the staff_auth row (the password) for an employee // HR has ALREADY added to staff_employees. There's no password yet at this // point to prove who someone is, so we require the typed department to // match what HR has on file for that Employee ID before a password can be // set. Role is derived from the real department on file, never from // anything the client sends. // If staff_auth already has a row for that ID — including one created by // /api/staff/seed-default-passwords, whose default password is just the // employee's own ID — signup is refused rather than silently overwritten. // That existing password still logs them in; /api/staff/change-password // below is the correct way to change it from there. app.post('/api/staff/signup', async (req, res) => { const { employeeId, department, password } = req.body || {}; if (!employeeId || !department || !password) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Employee ID, department and password are required' }); } if (String(password).length < 6) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Password must be at least 6 characters' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const empRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id AND active = 1`, { id: employeeId } ); if (empRs.rows.length === 0) { return { ok: false, error: 'Employee ID not found — check it with HR and try again.' }; } const emp = lower(empRs.rows)[0]; if (normalizeText(department) !== normalizeText(emp.department)) { return { ok: false, error: "That department doesn't match our records for this Employee ID — check with HR." }; } const existingAuth = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id FROM staff_auth WHERE employee_id = :id`, { id: employeeId } ); if (existingAuth.rows.length > 0) { return { ok: false, error: 'An account already exists for this Employee ID. Log in instead, or ask HR to reset your password.' }; } const hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10); await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO staff_auth (employee_id, password_hash) VALUES (:id, :hash)`, { id: employeeId, hash }, { autoCommit: true } ); // Account created — same 2FA step as a normal login rather than an // immediate token, so a brand-new account is protected by the email // code from the very first sign-in. return beginStaffOtp(conn, emp); }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(400).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff signup error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Sign up failed, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Forgot password: { employeeId, department, password } ---- // For someone who already has an account but has forgotten the password // (this is the "no old password" counterpart to /change-password above, // which needs the old password and so can't help here). Uses the exact // same trust model as signup: proves who they are by typing the // department HR has on file for that Employee ID, not by knowing the // old password. If that matches, the existing password is overwritten // with the new one. Role/department are never taken from the client — // always re-derived from staff_employees. app.post('/api/staff/forgot-password', async (req, res) => { const { employeeId, department, password } = req.body || {}; if (!employeeId || !department || !password) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Employee ID, department and new password are required' }); } if (String(password).length < 6) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Password must be at least 6 characters' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const empRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id AND active = 1`, { id: employeeId } ); if (empRs.rows.length === 0) { return { ok: false, error: 'Employee ID not found — check it with HR and try again.' }; } const emp = lower(empRs.rows)[0]; if (normalizeText(department) !== normalizeText(emp.department)) { return { ok: false, error: "That department doesn't match our records for this Employee ID — check with HR." }; } const hash = await bcrypt.hash(password, 10); // The password is NOT written yet. It travels as PENDING_PASSWORD_HASH // on the OTP row and is only applied once the emailed code is // confirmed at /api/staff/login/verify — see beginStaffOtp and the // verify route. Previously this route wrote straight to staff_auth // here, before any code was checked, so Employee ID + department // (department list is public) was enough on its own to overwrite — // and so lock out — any employee's password. return beginStaffOtp(conn, emp, hash); }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(400).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff forgot-password error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not reset password, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Change password (must already be signed in): { old_password, new_password } ---- app.post('/api/staff/change-password', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { old_password, new_password } = req.body || {}; if (!old_password || !new_password) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Current and new password are required' }); } if (String(new_password).length < 6) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'New password must be at least 6 characters' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const authRs = await conn.execute( `SELECT password_hash, legacy_salt, legacy_hash FROM staff_auth WHERE employee_id = :id`, { id: req.staffUser.employeeId } ); if (authRs.rows.length === 0) return { ok: false, error: 'No password set for this account yet' }; const auth = lower(authRs.rows)[0]; let passwordOk = false; if (auth.password_hash) { passwordOk = await bcrypt.compare(old_password, auth.password_hash); } if (!passwordOk && auth.legacy_hash && auth.legacy_salt) { passwordOk = legacyHash(old_password, auth.legacy_salt) === auth.legacy_hash; } if (!passwordOk) return { ok: false, error: 'Current password is incorrect' }; const newHash = await bcrypt.hash(new_password, 10); await conn.execute( `UPDATE staff_auth SET password_hash = :h, legacy_salt = NULL, legacy_hash = NULL, updated_at = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE employee_id = :id`, { h: newHash, id: req.staffUser.employeeId }, { autoCommit: true } ); return { ok: true }; }); if (!result.ok) return res.status(400).json(result); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff change-password error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not change password, please try again' }); } }); // ---- Rota app state (shared across every device/browser) ---- // Everything the Schedule/Rota page needs — employees, shift types, // shifts, time off, and Access-tab grants — used to live only in // whichever browser's localStorage happened to set it, which is why data // looked "gone" on another device and Access-tab grants only applied on // the device that created them. It now lives as one JSON blob in a // single row (ID = 1) of ROTA_STATE, so every device reads and writes // the same thing. Run this once against the Oracle DB before deploying // this version of server.js: // // CREATE TABLE ROTA_STATE ( // ID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, // DATA CLOB NOT NULL, // UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, // UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(50) // ); // // Saves use optimistic locking: the client sends back the updatedAt it // last loaded, and the save is rejected with 409 if someone else has // saved since then — so two people editing the rota on different // devices at the same time can't silently overwrite each other's // changes. The whole state is one blob rather than separate tables per // entity, which is the fastest way to close the "nothing syncs" gap // right now; splitting it into proper relational tables (EMPLOYEES, // SHIFTS, TIME_OFF, ACCESS_GRANTS) would be the sturdier long-term // shape if this keeps growing. app.get('/api/staff/rota-state', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute(`SELECT DATA, UPDATED_AT FROM ROTA_STATE WHERE ID = 1`); if (rs.rows.length === 0) return { data: null, updatedAt: null }; const row = lower(rs.rows)[0]; const dataText = typeof row.data === 'string' ? row.data : await row.data.getData(); return { data: JSON.parse(dataText), updatedAt: row.updated_at }; }); res.json({ ok: true, data: result.data, updatedAt: result.updatedAt }); } catch (err) { console.error('rota-state get error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not load the rota' }); } }); app.put('/api/staff/rota-state', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { data, expectedUpdatedAt } = req.body || {}; if (!data) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'data is required' }); try { const result = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute(`SELECT UPDATED_AT FROM ROTA_STATE WHERE ID = 1`); const current = rs.rows.length ? lower(rs.rows)[0].updated_at : null; const currentTime = current ? new Date(current).getTime() : null; const expectedTime = expectedUpdatedAt ? new Date(expectedUpdatedAt).getTime() : null; if (currentTime !== expectedTime) { // Someone else saved since this client last loaded — refuse the // overwrite instead of silently losing their change. return { conflict: true }; } const dataText = JSON.stringify(data); if (rs.rows.length === 0) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO ROTA_STATE (ID, DATA, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (1, :data, SYSTIMESTAMP, :updatedBy)`, { data: { val: dataText, type: oracledb.CLOB }, updatedBy: req.staffUser.employeeId }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE ROTA_STATE SET DATA = :data, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY = :updatedBy WHERE ID = 1`, { data: { val: dataText, type: oracledb.CLOB }, updatedBy: req.staffUser.employeeId }, { autoCommit: true } ); } const after = await conn.execute(`SELECT UPDATED_AT FROM ROTA_STATE WHERE ID = 1`); return { conflict: false, updatedAt: lower(after.rows)[0].updated_at }; }); if (result.conflict) { return res.status(409).json({ ok: false, error: 'Someone else saved changes to the rota since you last loaded it. Reload to get the latest version, then re-apply your change.' }); } res.json({ ok: true, updatedAt: result.updatedAt }); } catch (err) { console.error('rota-state put error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not save the rota' }); } }); // ---- Timesheet clock-in/out data (shared across every device/browser) ---- // This used to live only in a 'hallan_timesheet_v1' localStorage key, on // the assumption that the Time Clock kiosk (clockin.html) and this // Schedule/Timesheet page would always be used on the same device. In // practice staff clock in from their own phones while managers view the // Timesheet tab elsewhere, so punches never left the phone they were // made on. This moves each day's punch to its own row in Oracle instead, // so every device sees the same data. One row per employee per day // (rather than one big JSON blob like ROTA_STATE) so one person clocking // in never has to fight another person's edit for the same lock. Run // this once against the Oracle DB before deploying this version of // server.js: // // CREATE TABLE TIMESHEET_ENTRIES ( // EMPLOYEE_ID VARCHAR2(50) NOT NULL, // ENTRY_DATE VARCHAR2(10) NOT NULL, -- 'YYYY-MM-DD' // CLOCK_IN VARCHAR2(5), -- 'HH:MM' 24hr, or NULL // BREAK_START VARCHAR2(5), // BREAK_END VARCHAR2(5), // CLOCK_OUT VARCHAR2(5), // REMARKS VARCHAR2(500), // UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, // UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(50), // CONSTRAINT TIMESHEET_ENTRIES_PK PRIMARY KEY (EMPLOYEE_ID, ENTRY_DATE) // ); // // Returns the same { days: { employeeId: { dateStr: {...} } } } shape the // front end already used for its localStorage copy, so schedule.html only // needed its load/save plumbing changed, not every call site. app.get('/api/staff/timesheet-state', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const days = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute( `SELECT EMPLOYEE_ID, ENTRY_DATE, CLOCK_IN, BREAK_START, BREAK_END, CLOCK_OUT, REMARKS FROM TIMESHEET_ENTRIES` ); const out = {}; lower(rs.rows).forEach(r => { if (!out[r.employee_id]) out[r.employee_id] = {}; out[r.employee_id][r.entry_date] = { in: r.clock_in || null, breakStart: r.break_start || null, breakEnd: r.break_end || null, out: r.clock_out || null, comment: r.remarks || '' }; }); return out; }); res.json({ ok: true, days }); } catch (err) { console.error('timesheet-state get error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not load the timesheet' }); } }); // ---- Upsert one employee's one day: { employeeId, date, in, breakStart, breakEnd, out, comment } ---- // Used both by the Timesheet tab's per-row Save button and by the Time // Clock kiosk on each clock-in/break/clock-out button press. app.put('/api/staff/timesheet-entry', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { employeeId, date, in: clockIn, breakStart, breakEnd, out: clockOut, comment } = req.body || {}; if (!employeeId || !date) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'employeeId and date are required' }); } if (!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(date)) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'date must be YYYY-MM-DD' }); } try { await withConnection(async (conn) => { await conn.execute( `MERGE INTO TIMESHEET_ENTRIES t USING (SELECT :employeeId AS employee_id, :entryDate AS entry_date FROM dual) src ON (t.EMPLOYEE_ID = src.employee_id AND t.ENTRY_DATE = src.entry_date) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET CLOCK_IN = :clockIn, BREAK_START = :breakStart, BREAK_END = :breakEnd, CLOCK_OUT = :clockOut, REMARKS = :remarks, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY = :updatedBy WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (EMPLOYEE_ID, ENTRY_DATE, CLOCK_IN, BREAK_START, BREAK_END, CLOCK_OUT, REMARKS, UPDATED_AT, UPDATED_BY) VALUES (:employeeId, :entryDate, :clockIn, :breakStart, :breakEnd, :clockOut, :remarks, SYSTIMESTAMP, :updatedBy)`, { employeeId, entryDate: date, clockIn: clockIn || null, breakStart: breakStart || null, breakEnd: breakEnd || null, clockOut: clockOut || null, remarks: comment || null, updatedBy: req.staffUser.employeeId }, { autoCommit: true } ); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (err) { console.error('timesheet-entry put error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: 'Could not save the timesheet entry' }); } }); app.get('/api/staff/me', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const emp = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute( `SELECT employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, phone, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance FROM staff_employees WHERE employee_id = :id`, { id: req.staffUser.employeeId } ); return lower(rs.rows)[0]; }); res.json({ ok: true, emp, role: req.staffUser.role }); } catch (err) { console.error('staff me error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: err.message }); } }); // ---- Dashboard summary ---- app.get('/api/staff/dashboard-summary', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const summary = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = await conn.execute(` SELECT status, COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM staff_leave_requests GROUP BY status`); const rows = lower(rs.rows); const out = { pending: 0, approved: 0, declined: 0, partial: 0, totalRequests: 0 }; for (const r of rows) { out[r.status] = Number(r.cnt); out.totalRequests += Number(r.cnt); } return out; }); res.json({ ok: true, ...summary }); } catch (err) { console.error('staff dashboard-summary error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: err.message }); } }); /* ---- Leave / holiday requests ---- The HR Holiday Requests admin view adds a "Partial" decision on top of Approve/Decline, which needs two new columns on the existing staff_leave_requests table — run this once (e.g. via DBeaver) if they don't already exist: ALTER TABLE staff_leave_requests ADD ( PARTIAL_DAYS NUMBER(4,1), DECISION_NOTE VARCHAR2(500) ); */ /* --------------------------------------------------------- STAFF HOLIDAY DETAIL / HISTORY / EXCEL UPLOAD Tables/columns are created automatically on boot by ensureStaffHolidayTables() (ORA-00955 / ORA-01430 / ORA-01408 = "already there" and are swallowed) — nothing to run by hand. --------------------------------------------------------- */ async function ensureStaffHolidayTables() { const statements = [ `ALTER TABLE staff_employees ADD (WEEKLY_WORKING_DAYS NUMBER(3,1))`, `ALTER TABLE staff_leave_requests ADD (PARTIAL_DAYS NUMBER(4,1))`, `ALTER TABLE staff_leave_requests ADD (DECISION_NOTE VARCHAR2(500))`, `CREATE TABLE STAFF_HOLIDAY_HISTORY ( HISTORY_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, EMPLOYEE_ID VARCHAR2(50) NOT NULL, EMPLOYEE_NAME VARCHAR2(200), START_DATE DATE NOT NULL, END_DATE DATE NOT NULL, DAYS NUMBER(6,1), HOLIDAY_TYPE VARCHAR2(50) DEFAULT 'Holiday', NOTE VARCHAR2(500), APPROVED_BY VARCHAR2(100), SOURCE VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'upload', REQUEST_ID NUMBER, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX STAFF_HOL_HIST_EMP_IX ON STAFF_HOLIDAY_HISTORY (EMPLOYEE_ID, START_DATE)`, `CREATE INDEX STAFF_HOL_HIST_REQ_IX ON STAFF_HOLIDAY_HISTORY (REQUEST_ID)` ]; for (const sql of statements) { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true })); } catch (e) { if (![955, 1430, 1408].includes(e.errorNum)) { console.error('[staff-holiday] setup error:', e.message, '\n while running:', sql.split('\n')[0]); } } } } function staffToNum(v) { if (v === null || v === undefined || v === '') return null; const n = Number(String(v).replace(/,/g, '').trim()); return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : null; } // Accepts only a real calendar date in YYYY-MM-DD form (the browser // normalises whatever is in the Excel file into this before sending). function staffIsoDate(v) { const s = String(v || '').trim(); const m = s.match(/^(\d{4})-(\d{2})-(\d{2})$/); if (!m) return null; const d = new Date(Date.UTC(+m[1], +m[2] - 1, +m[3])); if (d.getUTCFullYear() !== +m[1] || d.getUTCMonth() !== +m[2] - 1 || d.getUTCDate() !== +m[3]) return null; return s; } // Working days between two dates (inclusive). 7 = every day, 6 = Mon–Sat, // anything else = Mon–Fri. Only used as a fallback when "days" isn't given. function staffCountWorkingDays(start, end, weeklyDays) { const s = new Date(start), e = new Date(end); if (isNaN(s) || isNaN(e) || e < s) return null; const wd = Number(weeklyDays) || 5; let n = 0; const d = new Date(Date.UTC(s.getUTCFullYear(), s.getUTCMonth(), s.getUTCDate())); const last = Date.UTC(e.getUTCFullYear(), e.getUTCMonth(), e.getUTCDate()); while (d.getTime() <= last) { const dow = d.getUTCDay(); if (wd >= 7) n++; else if (wd >= 6) { if (dow !== 0) n++; } else if (dow !== 0 && dow !== 6) n++; d.setUTCDate(d.getUTCDate() + 1); } return n; } app.get('/api/staff/leave-requests', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const isHRorManager = ['hr', 'manager'].includes(req.staffUser.role); const rows = await withConnection(async (conn) => { const rs = isHRorManager ? await conn.execute(` SELECT r.request_id, r.employee_id, e.full_name, r.start_date, r.end_date, r.days_requested, r.status, r.reason, r.created_at, r.partial_days, r.decision_note, e.holiday_allocation, e.holiday_taken, e.holiday_balance, e.weekly_working_days, e.department FROM staff_leave_requests r LEFT JOIN staff_employees e ON e.employee_id = r.employee_id ORDER BY r.created_at DESC`) : await conn.execute(` SELECT request_id, employee_id, start_date, end_date, days_requested, status, reason, created_at, partial_days, decision_note FROM staff_leave_requests WHERE employee_id = :id ORDER BY created_at DESC`, { id: req.staffUser.employeeId }); // ---- Holiday history. HR/manager: ?employeeId=… ---- app.get('/api/staff/holiday-history', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const canSeeOthers = ['hr', 'manager'].includes(req.staffUser.role); const target = canSeeOthers && req.query.employeeId ? The browser reads the .xlsx/.csv itself (SheetJS) // and posts clean JSON rows, so no extra server package is needed. // mode 'employees' -> allocation / taken / days left / weekly working days // mode 'history' -> one row per past holiday app.post('/api/staff/holiday-upload', staffAuthMiddleware, requireStaffRole('hr'), async (req, res) => { const { mode, rows, createMissing, replaceHistory } = req.body || {}; if (!['employees', 'history'].includes(mode)) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid upload type' }); } if (!Array.isArray(rows) || rows.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'No rows to upload' }); } if (rows.length > 5000) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Too many rows (max 5000 per upload)' }); } const result = { ok: true, mode, total: rows.length, updated: 0, created: 0, inserted: 0, duplicates: 0, skipped: 0, errors: [], warnings: [] }; try { await withConnection(async (conn) => { if (mode === 'employees') { for (const r of rows) { const rowNo = r.row || '?'; const id = String(r.employeeId ?? '').trim(); if (!id) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: 'Missing Staff ID' }); continue; } const alloc = staffToNum(r.allocation); const taken = staffToNum(r.taken); const weekly = staffToNum(r.weeklyDays); let bal = staffToNum(r.balance); if (bal === null && alloc !== null) bal = alloc - (taken || 0); const binds = { id, name: r.name || null, dept: r.department || null, pos: r.position || null, email: r.email || null, alloc, taken, bal, wd: weekly }; try { const up = await conn.execute( `UPDATE staff_employees SET full_name = NVL(:name, full_name), department = NVL(:dept, department), position = NVL(:pos, position), email = NVL(:email, email), holiday_allocation = NVL(:alloc, holiday_allocation), holiday_taken = NVL(:taken, holiday_taken), holiday_balance = NVL(:bal, holiday_balance), weekly_working_days = NVL(:wd, weekly_working_days) WHERE TO_CHAR(employee_id) = :id`, binds); if (up.rowsAffected > 0) { result.updated++; continue; } if (!createMissing) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: `Staff ID ${id} not found (tick "Create employees that don't exist yet" to add them)` }); continue; } await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO staff_employees (employee_id, full_name, email, department, position, holiday_allocation, holiday_taken, holiday_balance, weekly_working_days, active) VALUES (:id, :name, :email, :dept, :pos, :alloc, :taken, :bal, :wd, 1)`, binds); result.created++; } catch (e) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: e.message }); } } } else { const ids = [...new Set(rows.map(r => String(r.employeeId ?? '').trim()).filter(Boolean))]; const weeklyCache = {}; for (const id of ids) { const rs = await conn.execute( `SELECT weekly_working_days FROM staff_employees WHERE TO_CHAR(employee_id) = :id`, { id }); const found = lower(rs.rows)[0]; weeklyCache[id] = found ? found.weekly_working_days : undefined; if (!found) result.warnings.push(`Staff ID ${id} isn't in the employee list yet — their history was still saved.`); } if (replaceHistory) { for (const id of ids) { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM staff_holiday_history WHERE employee_id = :id AND source = 'upload'`, { id }); } } for (const r of rows) { const rowNo = r.row || '?'; const id = String(r.employeeId ?? '').trim(); const sd = staffIsoDate(r.startDate); const ed = staffIsoDate(r.endDate || r.startDate); if (!id) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: 'Missing Staff ID' }); continue; } if (!sd || !ed) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: 'Start/End date missing or not a valid date' }); continue; } if (ed < sd) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: 'End date is before start date' }); continue; } const days = staffToNum(r.days) ?? staffCountWorkingDays(sd, ed, weeklyCache[id]); try { const dup = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS cnt FROM staff_holiday_history WHERE employee_id = :id AND start_date = TO_DATE(:sd,'YYYY-MM-DD') AND end_date = TO_DATE(:ed,'YYYY-MM-DD')`, { id, sd, ed }); if (lower(dup.rows)[0].cnt > 0) { result.duplicates++; continue; } await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO staff_holiday_history (employee_id, employee_name, start_date, end_date, days, holiday_type, note, approved_by, source) VALUES (:id, :name, TO_DATE(:sd,'YYYY-MM-DD'), TO_DATE(:ed,'YYYY-MM-DD'), :days, :type, :note, :by, 'upload')`, { id, name: r.name ? String(r.name).slice(0, 200) : null, sd, ed, days, type: String(r.type || 'Holiday').slice(0, 50), note: r.note ? String(r.note).slice(0, 500) : null, by: String(req.staffUser.employeeId).slice(0, 100) }); result.inserted++; } catch (e) { result.skipped++; result.errors.push({ row: rowNo, error: e.message }); } } } await conn.commit(); }); if (result.errors.length > 200) result.errors = result.errors.slice(0, 200); res.json(result); } catch (err) { console.error('staff holiday-upload error', err); res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: err.message }); } }); app.post('/api/staff/leave-requests', staffAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { startDate, endDate, daysRequested, reason } = req.body || {}; if (!startDate || !endDate) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Start and end date are required' }); } }); app.patch('/api/staff/leave-requests/:id/status', staffAuthMiddleware, requireStaffRole('hr', 'manager'), async (req, res) => { const { status, partialDays, note } = req.body || {}; // 'partial' covers requests only part-approved (e.g. 3 of 5 days). if (!['approved', 'declined', 'pending', 'partial'].includes(status)) { return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: 'Invalid status' }); } await conn.commit(); return { ok: true }; }); if (out.notFound) return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: 'Request not found' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (err) { console.error('staff leave-requests status error', err); } }); /* =========================================================== RESTAURANT ORDERS A separate, lightweight ordering flow for wholesale restaurant customers: they sign in with a short passcode (not a staff login), then build an order across three steps — what they bought in the last week, what they bought in the last 3 months, and what similar customers are buying — before reviewing and submitting. Requires these tables (see restaurant_orders_schema.sql): RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS — admin-managed: customer_id, name, passcode RESTAURANT_ORDERS — submitted order headers RESTAURANT_ORDER_ITEMS — submitted order lines VACUSTOMERS — synced from Firebird like VESALES/VESTOCK (TRANSID, CUSTID, CUST_NAME) — this is what links a sale (VESALES.TRANSID) to the customer who bought it. Until your Firebird sync (test_oracle.py) populates this, every customer's order history will come back empty — that's a data problem, not a bug in these endpoints. =========================================================== */ // ---- Restaurant customer auth: passcode -> short-lived JWT (separate // token "type" from staff tokens, so one can't be used as the other) ---- function restaurantAuthMiddleware(req, res, next) { const header = req.headers.authorization || ''; const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : null; if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Not signed in' }); try { const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET); if (payload.type !== 'restaurant') throw new Error('wrong token type'); req.restaurantUser = payload; next(); } catch (e) { return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Session expired — please enter your passcode again' }); } } app.post('/api/restaurant/login', async (req, res) => { const { passcode } = req.body || {}; if (!passcode || !passcode.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Enter your passcode' }); try { const customer = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT CUSTOMER_ID, CUSTOMER_NAME FROM RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS WHERE PASSCODE = :passcode AND ACTIVE = 1`, { passcode: passcode.trim() } ); const rows = lower(result.rows); return rows.length ? rows[0] : null; }); if (!customer) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Passcode not recognised — check with Hallan if you think this is wrong' }); const token = jwt.sign( { type: 'restaurant', customerId: customer.customer_id, customerName: customer.customer_name }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '8h' } ); res.json({ ok: true, token, customer_id: customer.customer_id, customer_name: customer.customer_name }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Admin: manage restaurant customers & their passcodes (password-gated, see above) ---- app.get('/api/restaurant/admin/customers', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT CUSTOMER_ID, CUSTOMER_NAME, PASSCODE, ACTIVE FROM RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS ORDER BY CUSTOMER_NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/restaurant/admin/customers', async (req, res) => { // Upsert by customer_id — same screen adds a new customer and edits an existing one's // name/passcode. customer_id should match VACUSTOMERS.CUSTID so order history joins up. const { customer_id, customer_name, passcode, active } = req.body || {}; if (!customer_id || !customer_name || !customer_name.trim() || !passcode || !passcode.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'customer_id, customer_name and passcode are required' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS r USING (SELECT :id AS k FROM dual) d ON (r.CUSTOMER_ID = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET r.CUSTOMER_NAME = :name, r.PASSCODE = :passcode, r.ACTIVE = :active, r.UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (CUSTOMER_ID, CUSTOMER_NAME, PASSCODE, ACTIVE) VALUES (:id, :name, :passcode, :active)`, { id: customer_id, name: customer_name.trim(), passcode: passcode.trim(), active: active === false ? 0 : 1 }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/restaurant/admin/customers/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM RESTAURANT_CUSTOMERS WHERE CUSTOMER_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Shared helpers for building an order-step item list ---- async function customerHistoryItems(conn, customerId, sinceDays) { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, st.NAME AS ITEM_NAME, SUM(s.SELL_QTY) AS QTY FROM VACUSTOMERS c JOIN VESALES s ON s.TRANSID = c.TRANSID JOIN VESTOCK st ON st.STOCKID = s.STOCKID WHERE c.CUSTID = :customerId AND s.TRANS_DATE >= (SYSDATE - :sinceDays) GROUP BY s.STOCKID, st.NAME ORDER BY st.NAME`, { customerId, sinceDays } ); return lower(result.rows); } async function otherCustomersPopularItems(conn, customerId, sinceDays, limit) { // Excludes anything this same customer has already bought in the same window, // so this step surfaces genuinely new suggestions rather than repeating steps 1/2. const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, st.NAME AS ITEM_NAME, SUM(s.SELL_QTY) AS QTY, COUNT(DISTINCT c.CUSTID) AS CUSTOMER_COUNT FROM VACUSTOMERS c JOIN VESALES s ON s.TRANSID = c.TRANSID JOIN VESTOCK st ON st.STOCKID = s.STOCKID WHERE c.CUSTID != :customerId AND s.TRANS_DATE >= (SYSDATE - :sinceDays) AND s.STOCKID NOT IN ( SELECT s2.STOCKID FROM VACUSTOMERS c2 JOIN VESALES s2 ON s2.TRANSID = c2.TRANSID WHERE c2.CUSTID = :customerId AND s2.TRANS_DATE >= (SYSDATE - :sinceDays) ) GROUP BY s.STOCKID, st.NAME ORDER BY CUSTOMER_COUNT DESC, QTY DESC FETCH FIRST :lim ROWS ONLY`, { customerId, sinceDays, lim: limit } ); return lower(result.rows); } // Adds department + Trade 3 price and whether it's in stock right now (used to // flag a substitute prompt — the actual quantity is never shown to the // customer, only whether it's zero or not). PRICE_NET is only corrected // (÷1.2) for the rows where PRICE_VAT = 0 despite VATID = 1 — see the fuller // explanation on the /api/wholesale/pricing query above, same underlying // data issue. // Department comes from two places: WHOLESALE_PRICING (matched by name) for // items in the Wholesale catalog — those also carry a Trade 3 price — and, // for everything else, VESTOCK.DEPTID -> VEDEPTS.NAME (every product has a // real department there, whether or not it's on the Wholesale price list). // is_wholesale lets the frontend list Wholesale departments before everything // else, since those are the departments this customer base actually shops. async function enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, items) { if (items.length === 0) return items; const idBinds = {}; const idPlaceholders = items.map((it, i) => { idBinds['id' + i] = it.stockid; return ':id' + i; }).join(','); const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.QUANTITY, d.NAME AS DEPT_NAME, wp.DEPARTMENT_NAME AS WHOLESALE_DEPT_NAME, CASE WHEN wp.VATID = 1 AND wp.PRICE_VAT = 0 THEN wp.PRICE_NET / 1.2 ELSE wp.PRICE_NET END AS PRICE_NET, wp.VATID, CASE WHEN si.STOCKID IS NOT NULL AND si.IMAGEDATA IS NOT NULL THEN 1 ELSE 0 END AS HAS_IMAGE FROM VESTOCK s LEFT JOIN VEDEPTS d ON d.DEPTID = s.DEPTID LEFT JOIN WHOLESALE_PRICING wp ON UPPER(TRIM(wp.STOCK_NAME)) = UPPER(TRIM(s.NAME)) LEFT JOIN STOCK_IMAGES si ON si.STOCKID = s.STOCKID WHERE s.STOCKID IN (${idPlaceholders})`, idBinds ); const meta = new Map(lower(result.rows).map(r => [r.stockid, r])); return items.map(it => { const m = meta.get(it.stockid) || {}; return { stockid: it.stockid, item_name: it.item_name, qty_last_bought: it.qty, customer_count: it.customer_count || null, department_name: m.wholesale_dept_name || m.dept_name || 'Other', is_wholesale: !!m.wholesale_dept_name, trade_price: (m.price_net === undefined || m.price_net === null) ? null : Number(m.price_net), is_vat: m.vatid === 1, in_stock: Number(m.quantity || 0) > 0, image_url: m.has_image ? ('/api/stock-images/' + it.stockid) : null }; }); } // ---- Product photo for one item, synced from Vector (Firebird VESTOCKIMAGES // + VEIMAGES) into Oracle STOCK_IMAGES by test_oracle.py. Served plain // (no restaurantAuthMiddleware) so it can be used directly as an , which can't send an Authorization header — the photo itself // isn't sensitive, only the ordering flow around it is passcode-gated. // Cached hard since MD5HASH-driven sync means the same bytes only ever // change when the actual photo does. ---- app.get('/api/stock-images/:stockid', async (req, res) => { try { const row = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGEDATA, MD5HASH FROM STOCK_IMAGES WHERE STOCKID = :stockid`, { stockid: req.params.stockid } ); return result.rows.length ? result.rows[0] : null; }); if (!row || !row.IMAGEDATA) return res.status(404).end(); if (row.MD5HASH) res.set('ETag', '"' + row.MD5HASH + '"'); res.set('Content-Type', 'image/jpeg'); res.set('Cache-Control', 'public, max-age=604800, immutable'); res.send(row.IMAGEDATA); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).end(); } }); app.get('/api/restaurant/order/week', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const items = await withConnection(async conn => { const raw = await customerHistoryItems(conn, req.restaurantUser.customerId, 7); return enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, raw); }); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.get('/api/restaurant/order/three-month', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const items = await withConnection(async conn => { const raw = await customerHistoryItems(conn, req.restaurantUser.customerId, 90); return enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, raw); }); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.get('/api/restaurant/order/suggested', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const items = await withConnection(async conn => { const raw = await otherCustomersPopularItems(conn, req.restaurantUser.customerId, 90, 40); return enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, raw); }); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Substitute suggestions for an out-of-stock item: reuses the same lenient, // word-based fuzzy matcher as the main Stock Info search, restricted to // items that are actually in stock right now. ---- app.get('/api/restaurant/order/substitutes', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const q = (req.query.item || '').trim(); if (!q) return res.json({ items: [] }); try { const words = q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const binds = {}; const matchExprs = words.map((w, i) => { binds['w' + i] = w; return `CASE WHEN UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`; }); const orClauses = words.map((w, i) => `UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%'`).join(' OR '); const matchCountExpr = matchExprs.join(' + '); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, NAME FROM ( SELECT STOCKID, NAME, (${matchCountExpr}) AS MATCH_COUNT FROM VESTOCK WHERE QUANTITY > 0 AND UPPER(NAME) != UPPER(:fullQ) AND (${orClauses}) ) ORDER BY MATCH_COUNT DESC FETCH FIRST 200 ROWS ONLY`, { ...binds, fullQ: q } )); const candidates = lower(result.rows); const scored = candidates .map(row => ({ row, score: scoreMatch(q, row.name) })) .filter(x => x.score !== null) .sort((a, b) => b.score.matchedCount - a.score.matchedCount || a.score.distance - b.score.distance) .slice(0, 4) .map(x => ({ stockid: x.row.stockid, item_name: x.row.name })); const items = await withConnection(conn => enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, scored.map(s => ({ stockid: s.stockid, item_name: s.item_name, qty: null })))); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Step 4, "Search & Add Items": free search across the whole catalog (not // scoped to this customer's history), for anything the first three steps // didn't happen to surface. Same lenient word-matching as substitutes // above, but without the QUANTITY > 0 filter — an out-of-stock result // here still shows up with the same "may be out of stock" flag the other // steps use, rather than being hidden entirely. ---- app.get('/api/restaurant/order/search', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const q = (req.query.q || '').trim(); if (!q || q.length < 2) return res.json({ items: [] }); try { const words = q.toLowerCase().split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean); const binds = {}; const matchExprs = words.map((w, i) => { binds['w' + i] = w; return `CASE WHEN UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END`; }); const orClauses = words.map((w, i) => `UPPER(NAME) LIKE '%' || UPPER(:w${i}) || '%'`).join(' OR '); const matchCountExpr = matchExprs.join(' + '); const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, NAME FROM ( SELECT STOCKID, NAME, (${matchCountExpr}) AS MATCH_COUNT FROM VESTOCK WHERE (${orClauses}) ) ORDER BY MATCH_COUNT DESC FETCH FIRST 300 ROWS ONLY`, binds )); const candidates = lower(result.rows); const scored = candidates .map(row => ({ row, score: scoreMatch(q, row.name) })) .filter(x => x.score !== null) .sort((a, b) => b.score.matchedCount - a.score.matchedCount || a.score.distance - b.score.distance) .slice(0, 30) .map(x => ({ stockid: x.row.stockid, item_name: x.row.name, qty: null })); const items = await withConnection(conn => enrichWithWholesaleMeta(conn, scored)); res.json({ items }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Submit the finished order: one header row + one line per item ---- app.post('/api/restaurant/order/submit', restaurantAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { items } = req.body || {}; if (!Array.isArray(items) || items.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Add at least one item before submitting' }); } try { const orderId = await withConnection(async conn => { const headerResult = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RESTAURANT_ORDERS (CUSTOMER_ID, CUSTOMER_NAME) VALUES (:customerId, :customerName) RETURNING ORDER_ID INTO :new_id`, { customerId: req.restaurantUser.customerId, customerName: req.restaurantUser.customerName, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } } ); const newOrderId = headerResult.outBinds.new_id[0]; for (const it of items) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RESTAURANT_ORDER_ITEMS (ORDER_ID, STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, QTY, TRADE_PRICE, SOURCE, NOTES) VALUES (:orderId, :stockid, :itemName, :qty, :tradePrice, :source, :notes)`, { orderId: newOrderId, stockid: it.stockid || null, itemName: it.item_name, qty: it.qty, tradePrice: (it.trade_price === undefined || it.trade_price === null) ? null : it.trade_price, source: it.source || 'OTHER', notes: it.note || null } ); } await conn.commit(); return newOrderId; }); // No FTP push here anymore — the order sits in Oracle with // XML_EXPORTED = 0 (its default) until the shop PC's puller script // picks it up via GET /api/restaurant/orders/pending-xml below. res.json({ ok: true, order_id: orderId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Pull-based XML export, called by order-xml-puller.js running on the // shop PC (NOT by the browser/customer). Returns every order not yet // exported, each with its items and pre-built XML. Locked down with a // shared secret header rather than the customer JWT middleware, since // the caller here is a local script, not a signed-in customer. ---- app.get('/api/restaurant/orders/pending-xml', requireLocalSyncSecret, async (req, res) => { try { const orders = await withConnection(async conn => { const headerResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT ORDER_ID, CUSTOMER_ID, CUSTOMER_NAME FROM RESTAURANT_ORDERS WHERE NVL(XML_EXPORTED, 0) = 0 ORDER BY ORDER_ID` ); const headers = lower(headerResult.rows); const out = []; for (const h of headers) { const itemsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, QTY, TRADE_PRICE, SOURCE, NOTES FROM RESTAURANT_ORDER_ITEMS WHERE ORDER_ID = :orderId`, { orderId: h.order_id } ); const items = lower(itemsResult.rows); const xml = buildOrderXml(h.order_id, h.customer_id, h.customer_name, items); out.push({ order_id: h.order_id, file_name: `restaurant-order-${h.order_id}.xml`, xml }); } return out; }); res.json({ orders }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Marks one order as exported once order-xml-puller.js has // successfully written its XML file to the shop PC's local disk, so // it isn't handed back again on the next poll. ---- app.post('/api/restaurant/orders/:id/mark-exported', requireLocalSyncSecret, async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `UPDATE RESTAURANT_ORDERS SET XML_EXPORTED = 1 WHERE ORDER_ID = :orderId`, { orderId: req.params.id } ); await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* =========================================================== MANAGEMENT — shelf-check checklist A separate, simple named-login area (not the staff picking login, not the Wholesale/Restaurant single shared passwords) for managers to walk the whole store, tick off each location as checked, and see who checked it and when. Deliberately NOT linked to LOCATION_ZONES / LOCATION_SHELVES / LOCATION_ROWS (the Store Locations feature used for shelf replenishment) — those cover specific shelving for stock picking; this covers the whole store for a walk-through checklist, a different purpose with its own simpler two-level Zone -> Location shape (no shelves/rows). Requires MANAGEMENT_USERS, CHECKLIST_ZONES, CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS, CHECKLIST_CHECKS — see management_schema.sql, which also seeds the first login (name "Hallan", password "hallan2026"). =========================================================== */ function managementAuthMiddleware(req, res, next) { const header = req.headers.authorization || ''; const token = header.startsWith('Bearer ') ? header.slice(7) : null; if (!token) return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Not signed in' }); try { const payload = jwt.verify(token, JWT_SECRET); if (payload.type !== 'management') throw new Error('wrong token type'); req.managementUser = payload; next(); } catch (e) { return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Session expired — please sign in again' }); } } app.post('/api/management/login', async (req, res) => { const { name, password } = req.body || {};
