if (!name || !password) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name and password are required' }); try { const user = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT USER_ID, NAME, PASSWORD_HASH FROM MANAGEMENT_USERS WHERE UPPER(NAME) = UPPER(:name)`, { name: name.trim() } ); const rows = lower(result.rows); return rows.length ? rows[0] : null; }); if (!user || !verifyPasswordAgainstStored(password, user.password_hash)) { return res.status(401).json({ error: 'Name or password is incorrect' }); } // Seeded/legacy rows may still be plaintext (no ':') — upgrade to a proper // hash the moment they successfully log in with it, same as elsewhere in this file. if (!user.password_hash.includes(':')) { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE MANAGEMENT_USERS SET PASSWORD_HASH = :hash WHERE USER_ID = :id`, { hash: hashPassword(password), id: user.user_id }, { autoCommit: true } )); } const token = jwt.sign({ type: 'management', userId: user.user_id, name: user.name }, JWT_SECRET, { expiresIn: '12h' }); res.json({ ok: true, token, name: user.name }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Add another manager login, once already signed in ---- app.post('/api/management/users', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { name, password } = req.body || {}; if (!name || !name.trim() || !password || !password.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Name and password are required' }); } try { const existing = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT USER_ID FROM MANAGEMENT_USERS WHERE UPPER(NAME) = UPPER(:name)`, { name: name.trim() } )); if (existing.rows.length > 0) { return res.status(409).json({ error: 'A user with that name already exists' }); } await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO MANAGEMENT_USERS (NAME, PASSWORD_HASH) VALUES (:name, :hash)`, { name: name.trim(), hash: hashPassword(password.trim()) }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.get('/api/management/users', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT USER_ID, NAME, CREATED_AT FROM MANAGEMENT_USERS ORDER BY NAME` )); res.json({ users: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Zones ---- app.get('/api/management/zones', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT z.ZONE_ID, z.ZONE_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS l WHERE l.ZONE_ID = z.ZONE_ID) AS LOCATION_COUNT FROM CHECKLIST_ZONES z ORDER BY z.ZONE_NAME` )); res.json({ zones: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/management/zones', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { zone_name } = req.body || {}; if (!zone_name || !zone_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'zone_name is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO CHECKLIST_ZONES (ZONE_NAME) VALUES (:name)`, { name: zone_name.trim() }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/management/zones/:id', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM CHECKLIST_CHECKS WHERE LOCATION_ID IN (SELECT LOCATION_ID FROM CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS WHERE ZONE_ID = :id)`, { id: req.params.id } ); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS WHERE ZONE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CHECKLIST_ZONES WHERE ZONE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Locations within a zone, each with its most recent check (if any) ---- app.get('/api/management/zones/:zoneId/locations', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.LOCATION_ID, l.LOCATION_NAME, (SELECT c.CHECKED_BY FROM CHECKLIST_CHECKS c WHERE c.LOCATION_ID = l.LOCATION_ID ORDER BY c.CHECKED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY) AS LAST_CHECKED_BY, (SELECT c.CHECKED_AT FROM CHECKLIST_CHECKS c WHERE c.LOCATION_ID = l.LOCATION_ID ORDER BY c.CHECKED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY) AS LAST_CHECKED_AT FROM CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS l WHERE l.ZONE_ID = :zoneId ORDER BY l.LOCATION_NAME`, { zoneId: req.params.zoneId } )); res.json({ locations: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/management/zones/:zoneId/locations', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { const { location_name } = req.body || {}; if (!location_name || !location_name.trim()) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'location_name is required' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS (ZONE_ID, LOCATION_NAME) VALUES (:zoneId, :name)`, { zoneId: req.params.zoneId, name: location_name.trim() }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/management/locations/:id', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CHECKLIST_CHECKS WHERE LOCATION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM CHECKLIST_LOCATIONS WHERE LOCATION_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }); await conn.commit(); }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Tick a location as checked: logs a new event (who + UK time via // SYSTIMESTAMP, which is already UK time — see process.env.TZ at the // top of this file) rather than overwriting a single "checked" flag, // so the check history is never lost, only ever added to. ---- app.post('/api/management/locations/:id/check', managementAuthMiddleware, async (req, res) => { try { const checkedAt = await withConnection(async conn => { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO CHECKLIST_CHECKS (LOCATION_ID, CHECKED_BY) VALUES (:id, :checkedBy)`, { id: req.params.id, checkedBy: req.managementUser.name }, { autoCommit: true } ); const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT CHECKED_AT FROM CHECKLIST_CHECKS WHERE LOCATION_ID = :id ORDER BY CHECKED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 1 ROW ONLY`, { id: req.params.id } ); return lower(result.rows)[0].checked_at; }); res.json({ ok: true, checked_by: req.managementUser.name, checked_at: checkedAt }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- WHATSAPP BROADCAST — saved contact lists + bulk send via Meta's own WhatsApp Business Cloud API. Sending directly through Meta (rather than a paid reseller like Twilio/360dialog) means there's no extra per-message markup on top of Meta's own rate — the cheapest way to send WhatsApp messages programmatically. Requires these tables (contacts now belong to a list, so a saved number can be organised into e.g. "General" / "VIP customers" / "Newsletter" instead of one flat address book): CREATE TABLE WHATSAPP_LISTS ( LIST_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); CREATE TABLE WHATSAPP_CONTACTS ( CONTACT_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, LIST_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES WHATSAPP_LISTS(LIST_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE, NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, PHONE VARCHAR2(30) NOT NULL, -- full international number, digits only (e.g. 447911123456) CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); MIGRATING AN EXISTING WHATSAPP_CONTACTS TABLE (one that predates lists): CREATE TABLE WHATSAPP_LISTS ( LIST_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); ALTER TABLE WHATSAPP_CONTACTS ADD LIST_ID NUMBER; INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_LISTS (NAME) VALUES ('General'); UPDATE WHATSAPP_CONTACTS SET LIST_ID = (SELECT LIST_ID FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS WHERE NAME = 'General') WHERE LIST_ID IS NULL; ALTER TABLE WHATSAPP_CONTACTS MODIFY LIST_ID NOT NULL; ALTER TABLE WHATSAPP_CONTACTS ADD CONSTRAINT FK_WA_CONTACTS_LIST FOREIGN KEY (LIST_ID) REFERENCES WHATSAPP_LISTS(LIST_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE; COMMIT; Also requires this table, one row per flyer, holding the saved default image (as a full data URL, e.g. "data:image/jpeg;base64,...") the Image message type falls back to when nothing is attached for that send: CREATE TABLE WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES ( FLYER_KEY VARCHAR2(50) PRIMARY KEY, -- 'colour' or 'veg' IMAGE_DATA CLOB NOT NULL, FILENAME VARCHAR2(300), UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); Also requires this table, one row per genuine incoming customer message — the webhook below writes to it so replies can be browsed in the portal's "Replies" tab instead of only ever appearing in the Render console log: CREATE TABLE WHATSAPP_INBOUND ( INBOUND_ID NUMBER GENERATED BY DEFAULT AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, PHONE VARCHAR2(30) NOT NULL, MSG_TYPE VARCHAR2(30) NOT NULL, -- WhatsApp's own type: 'text', 'image', 'document', 'button', etc. BODY VARCHAR2(4000), -- the text itself, or a bracketed placeholder like '[image]' for non-text messages RECEIVED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, IS_READ NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0 NOT NULL ); Requires these env vars — see developers.facebook.com/docs/whatsapp/cloud-api for how to get them (Meta Business Manager > WhatsApp > API Setup): WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN — a permanent access token for a Meta System User with the whatsapp_business_messaging permission (the default token shown in API Setup expires in 24h — swap it for a permanent one before going live) WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID — the "Phone number ID" of your sending number WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_TEXT — approved template name for a text-only send (body has one {{1}} variable for the message) WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_DOCUMENT — approved template name with a DOCUMENT header plus the same {{1}} body variable WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_COLOUR — approved template name for the Wholesale Colour Flyer image send. Falls back to the older WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE if this isn't set, so an existing setup keeps working. WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_VEG — approved template name for the Wholesale Veg Flyer image send (e.g. "veg_flyer"). WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE — legacy/back-compat name for the colour flyer template — only read when WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_COLOUR isn't set. WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE — language code the templates were submitted in, e.g. "en_GB" (defaults to en_GB below). Used for every template type unless overridden per-type below. WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_TEXT / _DOCUMENT / _IMAGE_COLOUR / _IMAGE_VEG — optional per-type overrides for the line above (WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE is still read as a fallback for the colour flyer). Set one of these only if that particular template was approved under a different language than the others (Meta requires an exact language match per template — a mismatch fails with error #132001 "Template name does not exist in the translation", even though the template itself exists). WHY TEMPLATES, NOT FREE-TEXT: WhatsApp only allows free-form (non-template) messages to a contact who has messaged your business number within the last 24 hours — that rule applies no matter how much you're willing to pay, it's an anti-spam rule Meta enforces on every account. An approved message template is Meta's own workaround: once a template is approved, you can send it to anyone, anytime, with no window requirement, and Meta simply bills you per delivered message. So every send below goes out as a template, with the typed message filling the template's {{1}} variable and (if there's an attachment) the file filling the template's media header. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Multi-number config ---- // Two WhatsApp numbers can be connected to this app at once: // 'api' — the original Cloud-API-only number, used for broadcasts to // non-regular customers. WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN / WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID. // 'coex' — the coexistence number, linked via the Embedded Signup button // (/whatsapp-connect), also used day-to-day in the WhatsApp // Business app on a phone. WHATSAPP_COEX_ACCESS_TOKEN / // WHATSAPP_COEX_PHONE_NUMBER_ID. // Every existing call site defaults to 'api' when no channel is passed, so // broadcast sends and the wholesale schedule runner behave exactly as // before with no changes needed at their call sites. const WHATSAPP_CHANNELS = { api: { accessToken: () => process.env.WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN, phoneNumberId: () => process.env.WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID }, coex: { accessToken: () => process.env.WHATSAPP_COEX_ACCESS_TOKEN, phoneNumberId: () => process.env.WHATSAPP_COEX_PHONE_NUMBER_ID } }; function whatsappConfigured(channel = 'api') { const ch = WHATSAPP_CHANNELS[channel]; return !!(ch && ch.accessToken() && ch.phoneNumberId()); } // Given an inbound webhook's phone_number_id, works out which configured // channel ('api' or 'coex') it belongs to, so a reply goes out from the // same number the message came in on. Returns null if it matches neither // (e.g. a channel that isn't configured yet). function whatsappChannelForPhoneNumberId(phoneNumberId) { for (const [name, ch] of Object.entries(WHATSAPP_CHANNELS)) { if (phoneNumberId && ch.phoneNumberId() === phoneNumberId) return name; } return null; } async function whatsappApi(pathSuffix, options, channel = 'api') { const ch = WHATSAPP_CHANNELS[channel]; if (!ch || !ch.accessToken() || !ch.phoneNumberId()) { throw new Error(`WhatsApp channel "${channel}" is not configured (missing access token or phone number ID env vars)`); } const url = `https://graph.facebook.com/v20.0/${ch.phoneNumberId()}${pathSuffix}`; const r = await fetch(url, { ...options, headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${ch.accessToken()}`, ...(options.headers || {}) } }); const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!r.ok) { const msg = (data && data.error && data.error.message) || `WhatsApp API error (${r.status})`; throw new Error(msg); } return data; } // ---- Lists: list all, with a live contact count per list ---- app.get('/api/whatsapp/lists', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT L.LIST_ID, L.NAME, COUNT(C.CONTACT_ID) AS CONTACT_COUNT FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS L LEFT JOIN WHATSAPP_CONTACTS C ON C.LIST_ID = L.LIST_ID GROUP BY L.LIST_ID, L.NAME ORDER BY L.NAME` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: create ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/lists', async (req, res) => { const { name } = req.body || {}; if (!name || !name.trim()) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'A list name is required' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_LISTS (NAME) VALUES (:name) RETURNING LIST_ID INTO :new_id`, { name: name.trim(), new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Lists: delete (contacts in it go too — see ON DELETE CASCADE on the FK) ---- app.delete('/api/whatsapp/lists/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHATSAPP_LISTS WHERE LIST_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Contacts: list, optionally scoped to one list ---- app.get('/api/whatsapp/contacts', async (req, res) => { const listId = req.query.list_id; try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( listId ? `SELECT CONTACT_ID, LIST_ID, NAME, PHONE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE LIST_ID = :listId ORDER BY NAME` : `SELECT CONTACT_ID, LIST_ID, NAME, PHONE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS ORDER BY NAME`, listId ? { listId } : {} )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Contacts: add (saved permanently until explicitly removed) ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/contacts', async (req, res) => { const { name, phone, list_id } = req.body || {}; const cleanPhone = (phone || '').replace(/[^\d]/g, ''); if (!name || !name.trim() || !cleanPhone || cleanPhone.length < 8 || !list_id) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'name, a valid international phone number, and list_id are required' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_CONTACTS (LIST_ID, NAME, PHONE) VALUES (:listId, :name, :phone) RETURNING CONTACT_ID INTO :new_id`, { listId: list_id, name: name.trim(), phone: cleanPhone, new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ new_id: result.outBinds.new_id[0] }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Contacts: bulk import (CSV upload on the frontend, parsed there into // [{name, phone}, ...]) — inserted one row at a time so a handful of bad // or duplicate rows don't sink the whole file; the response reports back // exactly what happened to each row. ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/contacts/bulk', async (req, res) => { const { list_id, contacts } = req.body || {}; if (!list_id || !Array.isArray(contacts) || contacts.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'list_id and a non-empty contacts array are required' }); } try { const existingResult = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT PHONE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE LIST_ID = :list_id`, { list_id } )); const existingPhones = new Set(lower(existingResult.rows).map(r => r.phone)); let inserted = 0; const skipped = []; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const raw of contacts) { const name = ((raw && raw.name) || '').toString().trim(); const phone = ((raw && raw.phone) || '').toString().replace(/[^\d]/g, ''); if (!name || !phone || phone.length < 8) { skipped.push({ name: (raw && raw.name) || '', phone: (raw && raw.phone) || '', reason: 'invalid name or phone' }); continue; } if (existingPhones.has(phone)) { skipped.push({ name, phone, reason: 'already in this list' }); continue; } await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_CONTACTS (LIST_ID, NAME, PHONE) VALUES (:list_id, :name, :phone)`, { list_id, name, phone }, { autoCommit: true } ); existingPhones.add(phone); inserted++; } }); res.json({ inserted, skipped }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Contacts: remove ---- app.delete('/api/whatsapp/contacts/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE CONTACT_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Webhook verification (Meta calls this once, when you click "Verify and save") ---- app.get('/api/whatsapp/webhook', (req, res) => { const mode = req.query['hub.mode']; const token = req.query['hub.verify_token']; const challenge = req.query['hub.challenge']; if (mode === 'subscribe' && token === process.env.WHATSAPP_VERIFY_TOKEN) { return res.status(200).send(challenge); } res.sendStatus(403); }); // ---- Webhook events (delivery statuses, incoming replies, template approval updates) ---- // Nobody actively monitors this inbox, so every genuine incoming message gets // an instant auto-reply pointing the customer to a number that IS watched. // This is a plain free-text send, not a template — WhatsApp allows any // business-initiated free text within 24h of the customer's own message, no // Meta approval needed, so it goes out immediately with zero review delay. // // The text itself lives in APP_SETTINGS (key WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT) instead // of being hardcoded here, so it can be edited from the portal's Replies tab // without a redeploy. If the row is missing (fresh install, or nobody has // edited it yet), this default is used and is also what the portal shows as // the starting point. const DEFAULT_WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT = 'Thanks for your message! This number isn\'t regularly monitored — for queries please contact us on WhatsApp: https://wa.me/447718041859'; async function getWhatsappAutoreplyText(conn) { const stored = await getStoredSetting(conn, 'WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT'); return stored || DEFAULT_WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT; } // GET returns the current text (or the default, if never customized) so the // portal can show it in an editable box. app.get('/api/whatsapp/autoreply-text', async (req, res) => { try { const text = await withConnection(conn => getWhatsappAutoreplyText(conn)); res.json({ text }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // POST saves a new auto-reply text. Reuses the same APP_SETTINGS MERGE // pattern as the wholesale/restaurant passwords above. app.post('/api/whatsapp/autoreply-text', async (req, res) => { const text = (req.body && req.body.text || '').trim(); if (!text) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'text is required' }); if (text.length > 900) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Message is too long (max 900 characters)' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO APP_SETTINGS s USING (SELECT 'WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT' AS k, :text AS v FROM dual) d ON (s.SETTING_KEY = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.SETTING_VALUE = d.v WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SETTING_KEY, SETTING_VALUE) VALUES (d.k, d.v)`, { text }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Forward-to number: nobody carries a phone with the API number's // WhatsApp app installed, so genuine incoming messages are also pushed out // as a notification to a real, actively-carried WhatsApp number, so a // person actually sees them arrive in their own WhatsApp app instead of // relying on someone remembering to open the portal's Replies tab. The // number itself is stored in APP_SETTINGS (key WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE) so // it can be changed from the portal without a redeploy, same pattern as // the auto-reply text above. Falls back to the WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE env // var, then to the number Vahi asked for, if nobody has set it via the UI. const DEFAULT_WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE = '447398511060'; async function getWhatsappForwardPhone(conn) { const stored = await getStoredSetting(conn, 'WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE'); const raw = stored || process.env.WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE || DEFAULT_WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE || ''; return raw.replace(/[^\d]/g, ''); } app.get('/api/whatsapp/forward-phone', async (req, res) => { try { const phone = await withConnection(conn => getWhatsappForwardPhone(conn)); res.json({ phone }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/whatsapp/forward-phone', async (req, res) => { const phone = ((req.body && req.body.phone) || '').replace(/[^\d]/g, ''); if (!phone) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'phone is required' }); if (phone.length < 8 || phone.length > 15) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'That doesn\'t look like a valid number — digits only, with country code, e.g. 447398511060' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `MERGE INTO APP_SETTINGS s USING (SELECT 'WHATSAPP_FORWARD_PHONE' AS k, :phone AS v FROM dual) d ON (s.SETTING_KEY = d.k) WHEN MATCHED THEN UPDATE SET s.SETTING_VALUE = d.v WHEN NOT MATCHED THEN INSERT (SETTING_KEY, SETTING_VALUE) VALUES (d.k, d.v)`, { phone }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // Pulls a human-readable line out of whatever shape a given WhatsApp message // type happens to use — most customer replies are plain 'text', but button // taps and list-menu replies (from any template that has them) use their own // nested shapes instead of a top-level .text.body. function extractInboundBody(msg) { const type = msg.type || 'unknown'; if (type === 'text' && msg.text && msg.text.body) return msg.text.body; if (type === 'button' && msg.button && msg.button.text) return msg.button.text; if (type === 'interactive' && msg.interactive) { const br = msg.interactive.button_reply; const lr = msg.interactive.list_reply; if (br && br.title) return br.title; if (lr && lr.title) return lr.title; } return `[${type}]`; } // Saves one genuine incoming customer message so it shows up in the portal's // Replies tab. Best-effort: a DB hiccup here must never stop the auto-reply // below from going out, so failures are only logged, not thrown. async function saveInboundMessage(phone, msg, channel) { try { const msgType = (msg.type || 'unknown').slice(0, 30); const body = extractInboundBody(msg).slice(0, 4000); await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_INBOUND (PHONE, MSG_TYPE, BODY, CHANNEL) VALUES (:phone, :msgType, :body, :channel)`, { phone, msgType, body, channel: channel || 'api' }, { autoCommit: true } )); } catch (e) { console.error('Failed to save inbound WhatsApp message from', phone, ':', e.message); } } // Looks up a saved display name for a phone number from WHATSAPP_CONTACTS, // if it happens to be in there (same lookup the Replies tab itself uses). async function lookupContactName(phone) { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT MIN(NAME) AS NAME FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE PHONE = :phone`, { phone } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); return (rows.length && rows[0].name) || null; } catch (e) { console.error('lookupContactName failed for', phone, ':', e.message); return null; } } // Forwards one genuine incoming message on the (unmonitored) API number to // a real WhatsApp number that IS carried/monitored, so it shows up in that // person's own WhatsApp app in real time rather than only in the portal's // Replies tab. Best-effort, same as saveInboundMessage above — a forwarding // hiccup must never throw back into the webhook handler. // // This is a business-initiated message to a number that may not have // messaged this WhatsApp Business Account recently, so the normal 24-hour // free-form messaging window is often closed for it. This tries a plain // free-text send first (works for free whenever that window happens to be // open) and falls back to an approved message TEMPLATE if one is // configured — WhatsApp requires a template for any business-initiated // message sent outside that window, no way around it. To make delivery // reliable rather than "only when the window happens to be open": // 1. In Meta Business Manager > WhatsApp Manager > Message Templates, // create a UTILITY-category template (Utility, not Marketing — those // get much faster review and no extra throttling), e.g. named // "inbox_forward" with a body such as: // New message from {{1}} ({{2}}) at {{3}}: // {{4}} // and wait for Meta's approval (usually minutes to a few hours). // 2. Set these as env vars on the server: // WHATSAPP_FORWARD_TEMPLATE — the approved template's name // WHATSAPP_FORWARD_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE — the language it was submitted // under (defaults to // WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE, // then 'en_GB') // Until that template exists, forwarding will only get through on messages // where the destination number has messaged this WhatsApp Business Account // itself within the last 24 hours — otherwise it's logged (Render logs) // and silently skipped, exactly like any other best-effort side effect // here. async function forwardInboundMessage(from, msg, waProfileName) { try { const forwardTo = await withConnection(conn => getWhatsappForwardPhone(conn)); if (!forwardTo || forwardTo === from) return; const savedName = await lookupContactName(from); const name = savedName || waProfileName || 'Unknown'; const body = extractInboundBody(msg); const time = new Date().toLocaleString('en-GB', { timeZone: 'Europe/London', dateStyle: 'short', timeStyle: 'short' }); const text = `New message from ${name} (${from}) at ${time}:\n${body}`; try { await whatsappApi('/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: forwardTo, type: 'text', text: { body: text } }) }, 'api'); return; } catch (freeTextError) { const templateName = process.env.WHATSAPP_FORWARD_TEMPLATE; if (!templateName) { console.error('Forward to', forwardTo, 'failed (no open 24h window) and WHATSAPP_FORWARD_TEMPLATE isn\'t set, so there\'s no fallback:', freeTextError.message); return; } const languageCode = process.env.WHATSAPP_FORWARD_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE || process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE || 'en_GB'; await whatsappApi('/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: forwardTo, type: 'template', template: { name: templateName, language: { code: languageCode }, components: [{ type: 'body', parameters: [ { type: 'text', text: name }, { type: 'text', text: from }, { type: 'text', text: time }, { type: 'text', text: body } ] }] } }) }, 'api'); } } catch (e) { console.error('forwardInboundMessage failed for', from, ':', e.message); } } // Verifies Meta's HMAC-SHA256 signature over the raw request body. Without // this, anyone who finds the webhook URL can post fake events — which // would write junk into WHATSAPP_INBOUND and trigger a real auto-reply // from the business number to any phone number of their choosing. function requireWhatsappSignature(req, res, next) { const secret = process.env.WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET; if (!secret) { console.error('[whatsapp] WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET is not set — webhook rejected'); return res.status(500).end(); } const header = req.headers['x-hub-signature-256'] || ''; const expected = 'sha256=' + crypto.createHmac('sha256', secret).update(req.rawBody || Buffer.alloc(0)).digest('hex'); const headerBuf = Buffer.from(header); const expectedBuf = Buffer.from(expected); const signatureOk = headerBuf.length === expectedBuf.length && crypto.timingSafeEqual(headerBuf, expectedBuf); if (!signatureOk) { console.warn('[whatsapp] webhook rejected: missing/invalid X-Hub-Signature-256 (is WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET set and correct in both Render and the Meta App Dashboard?)'); return res.status(403).end(); } next(); } app.post('/api/whatsapp/webhook', requireWhatsappSignature, (req, res) => { console.log('WhatsApp webhook event:', JSON.stringify(req.body)); // Respond 200 immediately — Meta retries (and eventually disables the // subscription) if this doesn't return fast. The save + auto-reply send // below happen after, fire-and-forget, so neither delays this response. res.sendStatus(200); try { const entry = (req.body.entry || [])[0]; const change = (entry && entry.changes || [])[0]; const value = change && change.value; const messages = (value && value.messages) || []; // Which of our two numbers this message actually arrived on — Meta // includes this on every message so one shared webhook URL can serve // both the API-only number and the coexistence number at once. const incomingPhoneNumberId = value && value.metadata && value.metadata.phone_number_id; const channel = whatsappChannelForPhoneNumberId(incomingPhoneNumberId) || 'api'; // Meta includes the sender's own WhatsApp display name alongside the // messages array (matched by wa_id), which is nicer to forward on than // a bare phone number when there's no saved contact for it. const contacts = (value && value.contacts) || []; // `messages` is only present on genuine incoming customer messages — // delivery/read status updates and template approval events use a // different payload shape (`statuses`, not `messages`), so this // condition alone keeps the save + auto-reply from firing on those. for (const msg of messages) { const from = msg.from; if (!from) continue; saveInboundMessage(from, msg, channel).catch(e => console.error('saveInboundMessage error:', e.message)); // Auto-reply, and forwarding to a real monitored number, only on the // API-only number. The coexistence number is actively used by a // person in the WhatsApp Business app, so it's genuinely monitored — // sending the "not monitored" bounce-back (or a forward notification) // there would be pointless. Messages to the coexistence number are // still saved above so they show up in the portal's Replies tab. if (channel === 'api') { const contactEntry = contacts.find(c => c.wa_id === from); const waProfileName = contactEntry && contactEntry.profile && contactEntry.profile.name; forwardInboundMessage(from, msg, waProfileName).catch(e => console.error('forwardInboundMessage error:', e.message)); withConnection(conn => getWhatsappAutoreplyText(conn)) .catch(e => { console.error('Failed to load autoreply text, using default:', e.message); return DEFAULT_WHATSAPP_AUTOREPLY_TEXT; }) .then(autoreplyText => whatsappApi('/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: from, type: 'text', text: { body: autoreplyText } }) }, channel)) .catch(e => console.error('Auto-reply send failed for', from, e.message)); } } } catch (e) { // Never let a malformed/unexpected webhook payload crash the process — // this whole block is best-effort on top of the 200 already sent above. console.error('Auto-reply handling error:', e.message); } }); // ---- Inbound: list recent customer replies, newest first, with the saved // contact name attached wherever the phone matches a saved contact. Capped // at the most recent 200 — plenty for a "did anyone reply" check; older // messages are still in the table and in Render's logs if ever needed. ---- app.get('/api/whatsapp/inbound', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT i.INBOUND_ID, i.PHONE, i.MSG_TYPE, i.BODY, i.RECEIVED_AT, i.IS_READ, i.CHANNEL, (SELECT MIN(c.NAME) FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS c WHERE c.PHONE = i.PHONE) AS CONTACT_NAME FROM WHATSAPP_INBOUND i ORDER BY i.RECEIVED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 200 ROWS ONLY` )); const items = lower(result.rows); res.json({ items, unread_count: items.filter(m => !m.is_read).length }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Inbound: mark a single reply as read ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/inbound/:id/read', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WHATSAPP_INBOUND SET IS_READ = 1 WHERE INBOUND_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Inbound: mark every unread reply as read in one go, for one number ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/inbound/read-all', async (req, res) => { const channel = ['api', 'coex'].includes(req.body && req.body.channel) ? req.body.channel : 'api'; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WHATSAPP_INBOUND SET IS_READ = 1 WHERE IS_READ = 0 AND CHANNEL = :channel`, { channel }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Embedded Signup code exchange — used once per number, from the // /whatsapp-connect page, to link coexistence for a new number. Exchanges // the short-lived code (30 second TTL) for a permanent business token via // Meta's own oauth endpoint. Doesn't touch Oracle or the existing broadcast // feature at all — it only logs the result so you can copy the token and // phone_number_id into WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN / WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID // yourself. Requires WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET as an env var (App Dashboard > // App settings > Basic > App secret — different from WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN). app.post('/api/whatsapp/embedded-signup/exchange', async (req, res) => { const { code, phone_number_id, waba_id } = req.body || {}; if (!code) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Missing code' }); if (!process.env.WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET) { return res.status(500).json({ error: 'WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET is not set as an environment variable yet.' }); } try { const params = new URLSearchParams({ client_id: '1834320277742646', client_secret: process.env.WHATSAPP_APP_SECRET, redirect_uri: 'https://portal.hallans.co.uk/picking-sheets/', code }); const r = await fetch(`https://graph.facebook.com/v26.0/oauth/access_token?${params.toString()}`); const data = await r.json().catch(() => ({})); if (!r.ok) { const msg = (data && data.error && data.error.message) || `Token exchange failed (${r.status})`; console.error('WhatsApp embedded signup exchange error:', data); return res.status(400).json({ error: msg }); } console.log('=== WhatsApp Embedded Signup — coexistence number connected ==='); console.log('Copy these into Render as env vars on this service:'); console.log('WHATSAPP_COEX_PHONE_NUMBER_ID =', phone_number_id || '(not sent)'); console.log('WHATSAPP_COEX_WABA_ID =', waba_id || '(not sent)'); console.log('WHATSAPP_COEX_ACCESS_TOKEN =', data.access_token); console.log('================================================================'); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error('WhatsApp embedded signup exchange error:', e.message); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Flyer images: the saved default image per flyer ('colour' / 'veg') // that the Image message type sends when nothing new is attached that // time. Stored as a data URL (mirrors the WHOLESALE_FLYER_IMAGES pattern // above) so the frontend can drop it straight into an or // reuse it as the outgoing attachment without a round trip. ---- app.get('/api/whatsapp/flyer-images', async (req, res) => { try { const images = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute(`SELECT FLYER_KEY, IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME FROM WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES`); const rows = lower(result.rows); const out = {}; for (const r of rows) { out[r.flyer_key] = { data: typeof r.image_data === 'string' ? r.image_data : await r.image_data.getData(), filename: r.filename || null }; } return out; }); res.json({ images }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/whatsapp/flyer-images', async (req, res) => { const { flyer_key, image_data, filename } = req.body || {}; if (!flyer_key || !image_data) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'flyer_key and image_data are required' }); } try { await withConnection(async conn => { const existing = await conn.execute( `SELECT FLYER_KEY FROM WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES WHERE FLYER_KEY = :flyer_key`, { flyer_key } ); if (existing.rows.length === 0) { await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES (FLYER_KEY, IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME, UPDATED_AT) VALUES (:flyer_key, :image_data, :filename, SYSTIMESTAMP)`, { flyer_key, image_data: { val: image_data, type: oracledb.CLOB }, filename: filename || null }, { autoCommit: true } ); } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES SET IMAGE_DATA = :image_data, FILENAME = :filename, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE FLYER_KEY = :flyer_key`, { image_data: { val: image_data, type: oracledb.CLOB }, filename: filename || null, flyer_key }, { autoCommit: true } ); } }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Send: uploads the attachment (if any) to Meta once, then fires one // approved-template message per contact across every selected list, and // reports back a per-contact result so the sender can see exactly who it // reached and who it didn't. ---- app.post('/api/whatsapp/send', async (req, res) => { const channel = ['api', 'coex'].includes(req.body && req.body.channel) ? req.body.channel : 'api'; if (!whatsappConfigured(channel)) { return res.status(500).json({ error: channel === 'coex' ? 'The coexistence number is not configured on the server yet — add WHATSAPP_COEX_ACCESS_TOKEN and WHATSAPP_COEX_PHONE_NUMBER_ID as environment variables.' : 'WhatsApp is not configured on the server yet — add WHATSAPP_ACCESS_TOKEN and WHATSAPP_PHONE_NUMBER_ID as environment variables.' }); } const { message, attachment, list_ids, messageType, flyer } = req.body || {}; const text = (message || '').trim(); if (!text) { // The approved templates below all have a {{1}} body variable that the // typed message fills — Meta rejects a template send with that // parameter left blank, so a message is required even when there's // an attachment (a short caption line works fine). return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Write a message — it fills the template, so it\'s required even when attaching a file' }); } const listIds = Array.isArray(list_ids) ? list_ids.filter(Boolean) : []; if (listIds.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Select at least one contact list to send to' }); } // messageType picks which approved template (and header type) is used. // 'text' ignores any attachment; 'image'/'document' require one and it // must match the chosen type, so a stray file can't slip through as the // wrong header kind. const kind = ['text', 'image', 'document'].includes(messageType) ? messageType : 'text'; if (kind !== 'text' && !(attachment && attachment.data)) { return res.status(400).json({ error: `Attach a ${kind} to send a ${kind} message, or switch the message type to Text` }); } if (kind === 'image' && attachment && !((attachment.mimeType || '').startsWith('image/'))) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'The attached file is not an image — attach an image or switch message type' }); } // An image send is always one of two approved flyers — 'colour' // (wholesale_colour_flyer) or 'veg' (veg_flyer) — each its own Meta // template, so the image and the template it's declared under can // never end up mismatched. const flyerKind = kind === 'image' ? (['colour', 'veg'].includes(flyer) ? flyer : null) : null; if (kind === 'image' && !flyerKind) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Choose which flyer to send — Wholesale Colour Flyer or Wholesale Veg Flyer' }); } const mediaKind = kind === 'text' ? null : kind; const templateEnvKey = flyerKind === 'colour' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_COLOUR' : flyerKind === 'veg' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE_VEG' : mediaKind === 'document' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_DOCUMENT' : 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_TEXT'; // The colour flyer's template env var predates the colour/veg split — an // existing setup that only ever set WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE keeps working // without needing a new env var added for that flyer. const templateName = process.env[templateEnvKey] || (flyerKind === 'colour' ? process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_IMAGE : undefined); if (!templateName) { return res.status(500).json({ error: `No approved template configured for this send — set the ${templateEnvKey} environment variable to your approved template name.` }); } // Each template can be approved under its own language in WhatsApp Manager // (e.g. one submitted as "English (UK)", another as plain "English") — a // single shared language code would silently mismatch whichever template // wasn't submitted in that exact language and fail with Meta error // #132001 "Template name does not exist in the translation". So each // template type gets its own optional override, falling back to the // shared WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE (itself defaulting to en_GB) when a // per-type one isn't set. const languageEnvKey = flyerKind === 'colour' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE_COLOUR' : flyerKind === 'veg' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE_VEG' : mediaKind === 'document' ? 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_DOCUMENT' : 'WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_TEXT'; const languageCode = process.env[languageEnvKey] || (flyerKind === 'colour' ? process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE_IMAGE : undefined) || process.env.WHATSAPP_TEMPLATE_LANGUAGE || 'en_GB'; try { const placeholders = listIds.map((_, i) => `:id${i}`).join(','); const binds = {}; listIds.forEach((id, i) => { binds[`id${i}`] = id; }); const contactsResult = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT CONTACT_ID, NAME, PHONE FROM WHATSAPP_CONTACTS WHERE LIST_ID IN (${placeholders}) ORDER BY NAME`, binds )); // De-duplicate by phone in JS (not in SQL) so the same number saved in // two selected lists is only messaged, and billed, once. const seenPhones = new Set(); const contacts = lower(contactsResult.rows).filter(c => { if (seenPhones.has(c.phone)) return false; seenPhones.add(c.phone); return true; }); if (contacts.length === 0) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No saved contacts in the selected list(s) to send to' }); } // Upload once and reuse the same media_id for every recipient, instead // of re-uploading the same file per contact. let mediaId = null; if (mediaKind) { const buffer = Buffer.from(attachment.data, 'base64'); const form = new FormData(); form.append('messaging_product', 'whatsapp'); form.append( 'file', new Blob([buffer], { type: attachment.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream' }), attachment.filename || 'attachment' ); const uploadData = await whatsappApi('/media', { method: 'POST', body: form }, channel); mediaId = uploadData.id; } const components = []; if (mediaId && mediaKind === 'image') { components.push({ type: 'header', parameters: [{ type: 'image', image: { id: mediaId } }] }); } else if (mediaId && mediaKind === 'document') { components.push({ type: 'header', parameters: [{ type: 'document', document: { id: mediaId, filename: attachment.filename || 'document' } }] }); } components.push({ type: 'body', parameters: [{ type: 'text', text }] }); const results = []; for (const c of contacts) { try { const payload = { messaging_product: 'whatsapp', to: c.phone, type: 'template', template: { name: templateName, language: { code: languageCode }, components } }; await whatsappApi('/messages', { method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify(payload) }, channel); results.push({ contact_id: c.contact_id, name: c.name, phone: c.phone, ok: true }); } catch (e) { results.push({ contact_id: c.contact_id, name: c.name, phone: c.phone, ok: false, error: e.message }); } } res.json({ results }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- RETURNS (supplier returns — scan a product in, pick which supplier it's going back to, track it through Pending for Return -> Closed List -> Awaiting Credit Note -> Returned Goods). STATUS FLOW: PENDING (open list — every scan for that supplier is added straight into it) -> CLOSED_LIST ("Close List": no more scanning into it, the next scan starts a fresh PENDING list) -> AWAITING_CREDIT ("Items Returned", which also records Returned by / Accepted by / date / signature) -> RETURNED ("Credit Notes Received") -> CLOSED (archived). One RETURNS_LISTS row is the "batch" that's returned to a supplier together. Only ever one PENDING batch per supplier at a time — scanning always adds into that same batch until "Items Returned" is tapped, which locks it and moves it to AWAITING_CREDIT. The next scan for that supplier then opens a brand new PENDING batch. "Credit Notes Received" moves an AWAITING_CREDIT batch on to RETURNED. Nothing is ever deleted automatically — items only leave via the explicit delete endpoint below (correcting a mis-scan). Requires these tables: CREATE TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ( RETURN_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, SUPPID NUMBER, SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, STATUS VARCHAR2(30) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, -- PENDING | AWAITING_CREDIT | RETURNED CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, RETURNED_AT TIMESTAMP, -- set when it moves to AWAITING_CREDIT CREDIT_NOTE_AT TIMESTAMP -- set when it moves to RETURNED ); CREATE TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ( ITEM_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, RETURN_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES RETURNS_LISTS(RETURN_ID), STOCKID NUMBER, ITEM_NAME VARCHAR2(300) NOT NULL, BARCODE VARCHAR2(100), COST_PRICE NUMBER, -- always stored NET of VAT IS_VAT NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0, -- 1 = standard-rated, VAT added on top for display/totals QUANTITY NUMBER DEFAULT 1, LOCATION VARCHAR2(300), ADDED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP ); VAT: COST_PRICE is always the ex-VAT (net) cost. IS_VAT flags whether standard-rate VAT (RETURNS_VAT_RATE below) applies on top of it — set by hand when the item is added (there's no VATID reliably available to this endpoint's data sources, same limitation noted elsewhere for SUPPLIER_STOCK), and editable afterwards. Every total below is reported as three figures — net (ex VAT), VAT, and gross (inc VAT) — never a single blended number, so Bin/Charity/Returns analytics reports actual wastage cost accurately either way. --------------------------------------------------------- */ const RETURNS_VAT_RATE = 0.20; // UK standard rate, matches wholesaleIncVatPriceServer() above function returnsLineVat(costPrice, quantity, isVat) { if (!isVat || costPrice === null || costPrice === undefined) return 0; return Number(costPrice) * Number(quantity || 0) * RETURNS_VAT_RATE; } // ---- Best-effort VAT default for the Returns "VAT-rated" checkbox. // Every item added to a return used to start with the checkbox // unticked no matter what, which is why VAT-rated items were showing // up with £0.00 VAT — staff had to remember to tick it by hand every // single time. VESTOCK.VATID is the real, reliable source for this // (1 = VAT-rated, 2 = not VAT-rated, covers every stock item since // it's the master table — unlike WHOLESALE_PRICING, which only has // rows for items priced through the wholesale flyer). This pre-ticks // the checkbox from that; it's still just a default and stays fully // editable either way. Returns null (not false) when VATID is // anything other than 1 or 2 (e.g. not set), so the caller can tell // "known not VAT" from "unknown" if it ever needs to. */ function returnsDefaultVatFromVatid(vatid) { if (vatid === 1) return true; if (vatid === 2) return false; return null; } // ---- Scan a barcode: returns the product plus every supplier it's linked // to (from SUPPLIER_STOCK), preferred supplier first, so the scanner // screen can offer "this supplier, or one of these others". ---- app.get('/api/returns/scan-lookup/:code', async (req, res) => { const code = (req.params.code || '').trim(); if (!code) return res.json({ item: null }); try { const item = await withConnection(async conn => { const stockResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, s.VATID, s.QUANTITY, b.BARCODE FROM VEBARCODES b JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = b.STOCKID WHERE b.BARCODE = :code`, { code } ); const stockRows = lower(stockResult.rows); if (!stockRows.length) return null; const stock = stockRows[0]; const suppResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT sup.SUPPID, sup.NAME AS SUPPLIER_NAME, ss.COST_NET, NVL(ss.PREFERRED,0) AS PREFERRED FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN SUPPLIERS sup ON sup.SUPPID = ss.SUPPID WHERE ss.STOCKID = :stockid ORDER BY NVL(ss.PREFERRED,0) DESC, sup.NAME`, { stockid: stock.stockid } ); stock.suppliers = lower(suppResult.rows); stock.default_vat = returnsDefaultVatFromVatid(stock.vatid); return stock; }); res.json({ item }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Suppliers linked to a stock item, by stockid — used by "Search by // name" in Returns Scan, so a product picked by name search shows the // same preferred-supplier-first list a barcode scan would have shown. ---- app.get('/api/returns/stock-suppliers/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const stockid = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!stockid) return res.json({ suppliers: [] }); try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT sup.SUPPID, sup.NAME AS SUPPLIER_NAME, ss.COST_NET, NVL(ss.PREFERRED,0) AS PREFERRED FROM SUPPLIER_STOCK ss JOIN SUPPLIERS sup ON sup.SUPPID = ss.SUPPID WHERE ss.STOCKID = :stockid ORDER BY NVL(ss.PREFERRED,0) DESC, sup.NAME`, { stockid } ); // Name search and Stock Adjusted (Vector) both land here with only a // stockid in hand — look up VATID directly off VESTOCK the same way // the barcode-scan path does. const vatResult = await conn.execute(`SELECT VATID, QUANTITY FROM VESTOCK WHERE STOCKID = :stockid`, { stockid }); const vatRows = lower(vatResult.rows); const defaultVat = returnsDefaultVatFromVatid(vatRows.length ? vatRows[0].vatid : null); return { suppliers: lower(result.rows), default_vat: defaultVat, quantity: vatRows.length ? vatRows[0].quantity : null }; }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Recently received: every goods-received line for one stock item in // the last 12 months (which supplier it came from, how many, and the // POSTDATE it was posted), newest first. Shown under the supplier list // in the Returns supplier picker so staff can see who it really came // from before choosing where it goes back to. Notes that haven't been // posted yet (POSTDATE IS NULL) are not counted as received. ---- app.get('/api/returns/recent-receipts/:stockid', async (req, res) => { const stockid = Number(req.params.stockid); if (!stockid) return res.json({ receipts: [] }); try { const receipts = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT g.SUPPID, g.SUPPLIER_NAME, g.RECNOTEREF, g.POSTDATE, g.QTY FROM GOODS_RECEIVED_NOTES g WHERE g.STOCKID = :stockid AND g.POSTDATE IS NOT NULL AND g.POSTDATE >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE), -12) ORDER BY g.POSTDATE DESC, g.RECNOTEID DESC FETCH FIRST 100 ROWS ONLY`, { stockid } ); return lower(result.rows); }); res.json({ receipts }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Add a scanned item straight onto a supplier's return list. Finds the // supplier's current PENDING batch (creating one if this is the first // item scanned for them since the last "Items Returned") and inserts // the item into it in the same call — the scanner screen never has to // juggle a separate "create list" step. ---- app.post('/api/returns/scan-add', async (req, res) => { const { suppid, supplier_name, stockid, item_name, barcode, cost_price, is_vat, quantity, return_reason, created_by, stockadjustid, damage_id, destination, location } = req.body || {}; // Set when the item is being added from Returns > Stock Adjusted (Vector): // that write-off is then marked dismissed in the same transaction as the // return item insert, so it can't be left behind on the list or added twice. const adjId = Number(stockadjustid) > 0 ? Number(stockadjustid) : null; // Set when the item is being added from Returns > Warehouse Damages: that // WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS row (and its photos, via ON DELETE CASCADE) is // deleted in the same transaction as the return item insert, so it can't // be left behind on the Damaged List or added twice. const damageId = Number(damage_id) > 0 ? Number(damage_id) : null; // Optional: send it straight to Charity/Bin instead of the supplier's // pending list, chosen up front in the same modal as qty/reason (rather // than the older two-step add-then-move). Still lands in RETURNS_ITEMS // under the usual supplier list — DESTINATION is just set immediately — // so it behaves exactly like moveReturnsItem() below, just in one step. const dest = (destination === 'CHARITY' || destination === 'BIN') ? destination : null; if (!supplier_name || !item_name) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'supplier_name and item_name are required' }); } const supplierName = String(supplier_name).slice(0, 200); try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const existing = suppid ? await conn.execute( `SELECT RETURN_ID FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID = :suppid FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { suppid: Number(suppid) } ) : await conn.execute( `SELECT RETURN_ID FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID IS NULL AND SUPPLIER_NAME = :supplier_name FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { supplier_name: supplierName } ); const existingRows = lower(existing.rows); let returnId; if (existingRows.length) { returnId = existingRows[0].return_id; } else { const created = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RETURNS_LISTS (SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, STATUS, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :supplier_name, 'PENDING', :created_by) RETURNING RETURN_ID INTO :out_id`, { suppid: { val: suppid ? Number(suppid) : null, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, supplier_name: supplierName, created_by: { val: created_by || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); returnId = created.outBinds.out_id[0]; } const itemResult = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RETURNS_ITEMS (RETURN_ID, STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, BARCODE, COST_PRICE, IS_VAT, QUANTITY, RETURN_REASON, DESTINATION, MOVED_AT, LOCATION) VALUES (:return_id, :stockid, :item_name, :barcode, :cost_price, :is_vat, :quantity, :return_reason, :destination, :moved_at, :location) RETURNING ITEM_ID INTO :out_id`, { return_id: returnId, stockid: { val: stockid === undefined || stockid === null ? null : Number(stockid), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, item_name: String(item_name).slice(0, 300), barcode: { val: barcode ? String(barcode).slice(0, 100) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, cost_price: { val: (cost_price === undefined || cost_price === null || cost_price === '') ? null : Number(cost_price), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, is_vat: { val: is_vat ? 1 : 0, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, quantity: { val: Number(quantity) > 0 ? Number(quantity) : 1, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, return_reason: { val: return_reason ? String(return_reason).slice(0, 200) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, destination: { val: dest, type: oracledb.STRING }, moved_at: { val: dest ? new Date() : null, type: oracledb.DATE }, location: { val: location ? String(location).slice(0, 300) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: !(adjId || damageId) } // with a write-off/damage entry attached, commit only after it is cleared below ); const newItemId = itemResult.outBinds.out_id[0]; let remainingQty = null; // set when only part of a write-off / damage entry was sent on const sentQty = Number(quantity) > 0 ? Number(quantity) : 1; if (adjId) { try { // Partial send: if fewer units were sent on than the write-off holds, // the write-off stays on the Stock Adjusted list with what's left // (ADJUST_QTY keeps its sign, only the size shrinks). Only when // everything has gone is it dismissed, same as before. const cur = await conn.execute( `SELECT ADJUST_QTY FROM STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS WHERE STOCKADJUSTID = :adj_id AND DISMISSED = 'N'`, { adj_id: adjId } ); const curRow = lower(cur.rows)[0]; const curAbs = curRow ? Math.abs(Number(curRow.adjust_qty)) : 0; const left = Math.round((curAbs - sentQty) * 10000) / 10000; if (curRow && left > 0.0001) { const sign = Number(curRow.adjust_qty) < 0 ? -1 : 1; await conn.execute( `UPDATE STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS SET ADJUST_QTY = :new_qty WHERE STOCKADJUSTID = :adj_id AND DISMISSED = 'N'`, { new_qty: sign * left, adj_id: adjId }, { autoCommit: true } // commits the item insert above and this update together ); remainingQty = left; } else { await conn.execute( `UPDATE STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS SET DISMISSED = 'Y', DISMISSED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, DISMISSED_ACTION = 'ADDED_TO_RETURN', RETURN_ITEM_ID = :item_id WHERE STOCKADJUSTID = :adj_id AND DISMISSED = 'N'`, { item_id: newItemId, adj_id: adjId }, { autoCommit: true } // commits the item insert above and this update together ); remainingQty = 0; } } catch (e) { await conn.rollback(); throw e; } } if (damageId) { try { // Partial send: if fewer units were sent on than were logged, the // damage entry (and its photos) stays on the Warehouse Damages list // with the remainder. Only when everything has gone is the row // deleted outright (ON DELETE CASCADE takes its photos with it — // the client has already copied them onto the new return item). const cur = await conn.execute( `SELECT QUANTITY FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :damage_id`, { damage_id: damageId } ); const curRow = lower(cur.rows)[0]; const curQty = curRow ? Number(curRow.quantity) : 0; const left = Math.round((curQty - sentQty) * 10000) / 10000; if (curRow && left > 0.0001) { await conn.execute( `UPDATE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS SET QUANTITY = :new_qty WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :damage_id`, { new_qty: left, damage_id: damageId }, { autoCommit: true } ); remainingQty = left; } else { await conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :damage_id`, { damage_id: damageId }, { autoCommit: true } // commits the item insert above and this delete together ); remainingQty = 0; } } catch (e) { await conn.rollback(); throw e; } } return { return_id: returnId, item_id: newItemId, remaining_qty: remainingQty }; }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Suppliers that have any return activity at all, with a count of // batches in each stage — used to populate the Lists screen before // the person has typed a search. ---- // ---- Suppliers that have any return activity at all, with a count of // batches in each stage — used to populate the Lists screen before // the person has typed a search. PENDING is counted in *items*, not // batches — a batch can exist with 0 items in it once its last item // is moved to Bin/Charity, and that shouldn't still read as "pending" // here when the supplier's own detail screen already says nothing is. ---- app.get('/api/returns/suppliers', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.SUPPID, l.SUPPLIER_NAME, SUM(CASE WHEN l.STATUS = 'PENDING' THEN (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i WHERE i.RETURN_ID = l.RETURN_ID AND i.DESTINATION IS NULL) ELSE 0 END) AS PENDING_ITEMS, SUM(CASE WHEN l.STATUS = 'CLOSED_LIST' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS CLOSED_LISTS, SUM(CASE WHEN l.STATUS = 'AWAITING_CREDIT' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS AWAITING_LISTS, SUM(CASE WHEN l.STATUS = 'RETURNED' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS RETURNED_LISTS FROM RETURNS_LISTS l GROUP BY l.SUPPID, l.SUPPLIER_NAME ORDER BY l.SUPPLIER_NAME` )); res.json({ suppliers: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Every return batch for one supplier (any status) — the client groups // these into the three Pending / Awaiting / Returned blocks. ---- app.get('/api/returns/lists', async (req, res) => { const suppid = req.query.suppid || null; const supplierName = (req.query.supplier_name || '').trim() || null; if (!suppid && !supplierName) return res.json({ lists: [] }); try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT l.RETURN_ID, l.SUPPID, l.SUPPLIER_NAME, l.STATUS, l.CREATED_BY, l.CREATED_AT, l.CLOSED_AT, l.RETURNED_AT, l.CREDIT_NOTE_AT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i WHERE i.RETURN_ID = l.RETURN_ID AND i.DESTINATION IS NULL) AS ITEM_COUNT FROM RETURNS_LISTS l WHERE ${suppid ? 'l.SUPPID = :key' : 'l.SUPPLIER_NAME = :key'} ORDER BY l.CREATED_AT DESC`, { key: suppid ? Number(suppid) : supplierName } )); res.json({ lists: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- One batch, with its items. Items already moved to Bin or Charity // (DESTINATION set) are left out — once moved they belong to the // Bin/Charity lists instead, not to the batch they started in. ---- app.get('/api/returns/lists/:id', async (req, res) => { try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const listResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT RETURN_ID, SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, STATUS, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT, CLOSED_AT, RETURNED_AT, CREDIT_NOTE_AT, RETURNED_BY, ACCEPTED_BY, TO_CHAR(SIGNOFF_DATE, 'YYYY-MM-DD') AS SIGNOFF_DATE, SIGNATURE FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE RETURN_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id } ); const list = lower(listResult.rows)[0] || null; // SIGNATURE is a CLOB (PNG data URL) — same handling as the item photos. if (list) list.signature = list.signature ? await clobToString(list.signature) : null; const itemsResult = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ID, i.RETURN_ID, i.STOCKID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.BARCODE, i.COST_PRICE, i.IS_VAT, i.QUANTITY, i.LOCATION, i.RETURN_REASON, i.ADDED_AT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES ri WHERE ri.ITEM_ID = i.ITEM_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i WHERE i.RETURN_ID = :id AND i.DESTINATION IS NULL ORDER BY i.ADDED_AT`, { id: req.params.id } ); return { list, items: lower(itemsResult.rows) }; }); res.json(data); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Edit one item: quantity, cost price, or its location (scanned or // typed in by hand — both just land in the same LOCATION column). ---- app.put('/api/returns/items/:id', async (req, res) => { const { quantity, cost_price, is_vat, location, return_reason } = req.body || {}; try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_ITEMS SET QUANTITY = COALESCE(:quantity, QUANTITY), COST_PRICE = :cost_price, IS_VAT = COALESCE(:is_vat, IS_VAT), LOCATION = :location, RETURN_REASON = COALESCE(:return_reason, RETURN_REASON) WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { quantity: { val: Number(quantity) > 0 ? Number(quantity) : null, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, cost_price: { val: (cost_price === undefined || cost_price === null || cost_price === '') ? null : Number(cost_price), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, // is_vat is a tri-state on the wire: true/false explicitly sets it, // undefined/null leaves the stored flag untouched (COALESCE above). is_vat: { val: (is_vat === undefined || is_vat === null) ? null : (is_vat ? 1 : 0), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, location: { val: location || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, return_reason: { val: return_reason ? String(return_reason).slice(0, 200) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Remove a mis-scanned item from a batch ---- app.delete('/api/returns/items/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM RETURNS_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Move an item off a supplier return onto the Bin List or Charity // List. The item stays in RETURNS_ITEMS (so it's still linked back to // the supplier batch it came from for reference) but DESTINATION being // set is what excludes it from that batch's item list and item count // from here on — it now belongs to /api/returns/bin-charity instead. ---- app.post('/api/returns/items/:id/move', async (req, res) => { const destination = (req.body && req.body.destination || '').toUpperCase(); if (destination !== 'BIN' && destination !== 'CHARITY') { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'destination must be BIN or CHARITY' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_ITEMS SET DESTINATION = :destination, MOVED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { destination, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Everything currently sitting in the Bin List or Charity List, newest // first, with the supplier it originally came from for context. ---- app.get('/api/returns/bin-charity/:destination', async (req, res) => { const destination = (req.params.destination || '').toUpperCase(); if (destination !== 'BIN' && destination !== 'CHARITY') { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'destination must be BIN or CHARITY' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ID, i.RETURN_ID, i.STOCKID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.BARCODE, i.COST_PRICE, i.IS_VAT, i.QUANTITY, i.LOCATION, i.RETURN_REASON, i.MOVED_AT, l.SUPPLIER_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES ri WHERE ri.ITEM_ID = i.ITEM_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i JOIN RETURNS_LISTS l ON l.RETURN_ID = i.RETURN_ID WHERE i.DESTINATION = :destination AND i.DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID IS NULL ORDER BY i.MOVED_AT DESC`, { destination } )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Close the current Bin List / Charity List: everything on it right now is // stamped into a closed batch (DISPOSAL_CLOSURES) and the open list // starts again empty. Items keep DESTINATION, so Analytics still counts // them — they just stop showing on the open list and can be looked up // again under "View closed lists". ---- app.post('/api/returns/bin-charity/:destination/close', async (req, res) => { const destination = (req.params.destination || '').toUpperCase(); if (destination !== 'BIN' && destination !== 'CHARITY') { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'destination must be BIN or CHARITY' }); } const closedBy = String((req.body && req.body.closed_by) || '').slice(0, 200) || null; try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const cnt = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS N FROM RETURNS_ITEMS WHERE DESTINATION = :destination AND DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID IS NULL`, { destination } ); if (!Number(lower(cnt.rows)[0].n)) { throw Object.assign(new Error('This list has no items to close'), { status: 400 }); } try { const created = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO DISPOSAL_CLOSURES (DESTINATION, CLOSED_BY) VALUES (:destination, :closed_by) RETURNING CLOSE_ID INTO :out_id`, { destination, closed_by: { val: closedBy, type: oracledb.STRING }, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } } ); const closeId = created.outBinds.out_id[0]; await conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_ITEMS SET DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID = :close_id WHERE DESTINATION = :destination AND DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID IS NULL`, { close_id: closeId, destination }, { autoCommit: true } // commits the closure row and the item stamp together ); return { ok: true, close_id: closeId }; } catch (e) { await conn.rollback(); throw e; } }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Every closed Bin / Charity list, newest first, with item count and value. ---- app.get('/api/returns/bin-charity/:destination/closed', async (req, res) => { const destination = (req.params.destination || '').toUpperCase(); if (destination !== 'BIN' && destination !== 'CHARITY') { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'destination must be BIN or CHARITY' }); } try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT c.CLOSE_ID, c.DESTINATION, c.CLOSED_AT, c.CLOSED_BY, COUNT(i.ITEM_ID) AS ITEM_COUNT, NVL(SUM(i.QUANTITY), 0) AS TOTAL_QTY, NVL(SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY), 0) AS NET_VALUE, NVL(SUM(CASE WHEN i.IS_VAT = 1 THEN NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY * :vatRate ELSE 0 END), 0) AS VAT_VALUE FROM DISPOSAL_CLOSURES c LEFT JOIN RETURNS_ITEMS i ON i.DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID = c.CLOSE_ID WHERE c.DESTINATION = :destination GROUP BY c.CLOSE_ID, c.DESTINATION, c.CLOSED_AT, c.CLOSED_BY ORDER BY c.CLOSED_AT DESC`, { destination, vatRate: RETURNS_VAT_RATE } )); res.json({ lists: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- One closed Bin / Charity list with its items. ---- app.get('/api/returns/bin-charity-closed/:id', async (req, res) => { try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const c = await conn.execute( `SELECT CLOSE_ID, DESTINATION, CLOSED_AT, CLOSED_BY FROM DISPOSAL_CLOSURES WHERE CLOSE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id } ); const closure = lower(c.rows)[0] || null; if (!closure) throw Object.assign(new Error('Closed list not found'), { status: 404 }); const items = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.ITEM_ID, i.RETURN_ID, i.STOCKID, i.ITEM_NAME, i.BARCODE, i.COST_PRICE, i.IS_VAT, i.QUANTITY, i.LOCATION, i.RETURN_REASON, i.MOVED_AT, l.SUPPLIER_NAME, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES ri WHERE ri.ITEM_ID = i.ITEM_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i JOIN RETURNS_LISTS l ON l.RETURN_ID = i.RETURN_ID WHERE i.DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID = :id ORDER BY i.MOVED_AT DESC`, { id: req.params.id } ); return { closure, items: lower(items.rows) }; }); res.json(data); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Move an item back out of Bin/Charity (undo a mis-tap) ---- app.post('/api/returns/items/:id/unmove', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_ITEMS SET DESTINATION = NULL, MOVED_AT = NULL WHERE ITEM_ID = :id AND DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID IS NULL`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Send an item back to ⏳ Pending for Return: takes one item off a // Closed List / Awaiting Credit Note / Returned Goods batch and puts it // onto that supplier's open PENDING list (a new one is created if the // supplier has none open). Everything on the item (qty, cost, VAT, // reason, location, photos) is kept — only RETURN_ID changes. If that // leaves the old batch with nothing on it, the batch is archived // (STATUS 'CLOSED', same as the Returned Goods "✕") so an empty list // doesn't sit in Closed / Awaiting / Returned. All in one transaction. ---- app.post('/api/returns/items/:id/send-to-pending', async (req, res) => { const createdBy = String((req.body && req.body.created_by) || '').slice(0, 200) || null; try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const cur = await conn.execute( `SELECT i.RETURN_ID, i.DESTINATION, l.SUPPID, l.SUPPLIER_NAME, l.STATUS FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i JOIN RETURNS_LISTS l ON l.RETURN_ID = i.RETURN_ID WHERE i.ITEM_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id } ); const row = lower(cur.rows)[0]; if (!row) throw Object.assign(new Error('Item not found'), { status: 404 }); if (row.destination) throw Object.assign(new Error('This item is on the Bin / Charity list — undo that first'), { status: 409 }); if (row.status === 'PENDING') throw Object.assign(new Error('This item is already in Pending for Return'), { status: 409 }); if (row.status !== 'CLOSED_LIST' && row.status !== 'AWAITING_CREDIT' && row.status !== 'RETURNED') { throw Object.assign(new Error('This item belongs to an archived list and cannot be moved'), { status: 409 }); } try { const hasSuppId = row.suppid !== null && row.suppid !== undefined; const existing = hasSuppId ? await conn.execute( `SELECT RETURN_ID FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID = :k FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { k: row.suppid } ) : await conn.execute( `SELECT RETURN_ID FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID IS NULL AND SUPPLIER_NAME = :k FETCH FIRST 1 ROWS ONLY`, { k: row.supplier_name } ); const existingRows = lower(existing.rows); let pendingId; if (existingRows.length) { pendingId = existingRows[0].return_id; } else { const created = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RETURNS_LISTS (SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME, STATUS, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:suppid, :supplier_name, 'PENDING', :created_by) RETURNING RETURN_ID INTO :out_id`, { suppid: { val: hasSuppId ? Number(row.suppid) : null, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, supplier_name: row.supplier_name, created_by: { val: createdBy, type: oracledb.STRING }, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } } ); pendingId = created.outBinds.out_id[0]; } await conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_ITEMS SET RETURN_ID = :new_id WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { new_id: pendingId, id: req.params.id } ); const left = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS N FROM RETURNS_ITEMS WHERE RETURN_ID = :old_id AND DESTINATION IS NULL`, { old_id: row.return_id } ); let archived = false; if (!Number(lower(left.rows)[0].n)) { await conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'CLOSED' WHERE RETURN_ID = :old_id AND STATUS IN ('CLOSED_LIST','AWAITING_CREDIT','RETURNED')`, { old_id: row.return_id } ); archived = true; } await conn.commit(); return { ok: true, pending_return_id: pendingId, list_archived: archived }; } catch (e) { await conn.rollback(); throw e; } }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Returns item photos: evidence photos (damaged goods, out-of-date // labels, etc.) attached to a RETURNS_ITEMS row. Added right when the // item is created (Scan Returns and Stock Adjusted share the same // quantity+reason modal — a photo taken there is uploaded here the // moment the item itself is saved and gets its ITEM_ID) or any time // afterwards from a return list / Charity List / Bin List, for // anything missed at the time. The client always sends JPEG data URLs // (it downscales/compresses in-browser before sending, same as the // wholesale flyer image upload elsewhere in this file), but PNG/WebP // are accepted too in case that ever changes. ---- const RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES = 8 * 1024 * 1024; // generous — client already downscales/compresses const RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_PER_UPLOAD = 8; const RETURNS_IMAGE_DATA_URL_RE = /^data:image\/(jpeg|jpg|png|webp);base64,/i; app.get('/api/returns/items/:id/images', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGE_ID, FILENAME, IMAGE_DATA, CREATED_AT FROM RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES WHERE ITEM_ID = :id ORDER BY CREATED_AT`, { id: req.params.id } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); const images = await Promise.all(rows.map(async r => ({ image_id: r.image_id, filename: r.filename, created_at: r.created_at, data: await clobToString(r.image_data) }))); res.json({ images }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/returns/items/:id/images', async (req, res) => { const itemId = Number(req.params.id); const images = Array.isArray(req.body && req.body.images) ? req.body.images : []; if (!itemId) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'invalid item id' }); if (!images.length) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No photos supplied' }); if (images.length > RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_PER_UPLOAD) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Up to ' + RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_PER_UPLOAD + ' photos at a time' }); } try { const created = await withConnection(async conn => { // Confirm the item exists first so a typo'd/deleted item id fails // clearly instead of silently inserting orphaned rows (the FK would // also catch this, but this gives a clearer error message). const itemCheck = await conn.execute(`SELECT 1 FROM RETURNS_ITEMS WHERE ITEM_ID = :id`, { id: itemId }); if (!itemCheck.rows.length) throw Object.assign(new Error('Return item not found'), { status: 404 }); const out = []; for (const img of images) { const dataUrl = img && img.data; if (typeof dataUrl !== 'string' || !RETURNS_IMAGE_DATA_URL_RE.test(dataUrl)) { throw Object.assign(new Error('One of the photos was not a valid image'), { status: 400 }); } const approxBytes = Math.ceil((dataUrl.length - dataUrl.indexOf(',') - 1) * 3 / 4); if (approxBytes > RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES) { throw Object.assign(new Error('One of the photos is too large'), { status: 400 }); } const filename = img.filename ?
