String(img.filename).slice(0, 300) : null; const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES (ITEM_ID, IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME) VALUES (:item_id, :image_data, :filename) RETURNING IMAGE_ID INTO :out_id`, { item_id: itemId, image_data: { val: dataUrl, type: oracledb.CLOB }, filename, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); out.push({ image_id: result.outBinds.out_id[0], filename }); } return out; }); res.json({ images: created }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/returns/item-images/:imageId', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES WHERE IMAGE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.imageId }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Close List": ends the open (PENDING) list for a supplier. From here // on scanning for that supplier starts a brand new PENDING list, and this // one sits in "Closed Lists" (between Pending for Return and Awaiting // Credit Note) until "Items Returned" is tapped. An empty list can't be // closed — there'd be nothing to send back. ---- app.post('/api/returns/lists/:id/close-list', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const cnt = await conn.execute( `SELECT COUNT(*) AS N FROM RETURNS_ITEMS WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND DESTINATION IS NULL`, { id: req.params.id } ); if (!Number(lower(cnt.rows)[0].n)) { throw Object.assign(new Error('This list has no items — add at least one before closing it'), { status: 400 }); } const r = await conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'CLOSED_LIST', CLOSED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'PENDING'`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } ); if (!r.rowsAffected) throw Object.assign(new Error('Only an open list can be closed'), { status: 409 }); return { ok: true }; }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Reopen list": undo a mis-tapped Close List. Only allowed while the // supplier has no other open list (there is only ever one PENDING list // per supplier at a time). ---- app.post('/api/returns/lists/:id/reopen', async (req, res) => { try { const out = await withConnection(async conn => { const cur = await conn.execute( `SELECT SUPPID, SUPPLIER_NAME FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'CLOSED_LIST'`, { id: req.params.id } ); const row = lower(cur.rows)[0]; if (!row) throw Object.assign(new Error('Only a closed list can be reopened'), { status: 409 }); const clash = row.suppid !== null && row.suppid !== undefined ? await conn.execute(`SELECT 1 FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID = :k`, { k: row.suppid }) : await conn.execute(`SELECT 1 FROM RETURNS_LISTS WHERE STATUS = 'PENDING' AND SUPPID IS NULL AND SUPPLIER_NAME = :k`, { k: row.supplier_name }); if (clash.rows.length) { throw Object.assign(new Error('This supplier already has an open list — close or finish that one first'), { status: 409 }); } await conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'PENDING', CLOSED_AT = NULL WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'CLOSED_LIST'`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } ); return { ok: true }; }); res.json(out); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Items Returned": only for a CLOSED_LIST. Records who returned the // goods, who accepted them, the date, and the acceptor's signature (PNG // data URL drawn on screen), then moves the list to Awaiting Credit Note. ---- const RETURNS_SIGNATURE_MAX_BYTES = 2 * 1024 * 1024; app.post('/api/returns/lists/:id/items-returned', async (req, res) => { const b = req.body || {}; const returnedBy = String(b.returned_by || '').trim().slice(0, 200); const acceptedBy = String(b.accepted_by || '').trim().slice(0, 200); const signoffDate = /^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(String(b.signoff_date || '')) ? String(b.signoff_date) : null; const signature = typeof b.signature === 'string' ? b.signature : ''; // Sign-off details are optional — "Items Returned" goes through with or // without names / date / signature. If a signature IS sent it must be a // PNG data URL of a sensible size. if (signature && !/^data:image\/png;base64,/i.test(signature)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Signature must be a PNG image' }); if (signature && signature.length * 3 / 4 > RETURNS_SIGNATURE_MAX_BYTES) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Signature image is too large' }); try { const r = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'AWAITING_CREDIT', RETURNED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, RETURNED_BY = :returned_by, ACCEPTED_BY = :accepted_by, SIGNOFF_DATE = CASE WHEN :signoff_date IS NULL THEN NULL ELSE TO_DATE(:signoff_date, 'YYYY-MM-DD') END, SIGNATURE = :signature WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'CLOSED_LIST'`, { returned_by: { val: returnedBy || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, accepted_by: { val: acceptedBy || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, signoff_date: { val: signoffDate, type: oracledb.STRING }, signature: { val: signature || null, type: oracledb.CLOB }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (!r.rowsAffected) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'Close the list first — only a closed list can be marked as returned' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Credit Notes Received": moves the batch on to Returned Goods ---- app.post('/api/returns/lists/:id/credit-note-received', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'RETURNED', CREDIT_NOTE_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'AWAITING_CREDIT'`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- "Close": archives a finished RETURNED batch so it drops off the // Returned Goods screen. Deliberately a status change rather than a // DELETE — the return record and its items stay in the database for // auditing, they just stop cluttering the list. ---- app.post('/api/returns/lists/:id/close', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE RETURNS_LISTS SET STATUS = 'CLOSED' WHERE RETURN_ID = :id AND STATUS = 'RETURNED'`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- RETURNS > ANALYTICS (wastage reporting) One screen answering "what is wastage actually costing us": how much stock value is going to the Bin vs Charity vs actually getting credited back from a supplier, which items and suppliers show up most in Bin/ Charity, and how that's trending month to month. Every value is split net / VAT / gross using RETURNS_VAT_RATE and each item's IS_VAT flag — see the RETURNS_ITEMS comment above for what that means. --------------------------------------------------------- */ app.get('/api/returns/analytics', async (req, res) => { try { const data = await withConnection(async conn => { const bucketSql = ` SELECT CASE WHEN i.DESTINATION IS NOT NULL THEN i.DESTINATION ELSE l.STATUS END AS BUCKET, COUNT(*) AS ITEM_COUNT, SUM(i.QUANTITY) AS TOTAL_QTY, SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) AS NET_VALUE, SUM(CASE WHEN i.IS_VAT = 1 THEN NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY * :vatRate ELSE 0 END) AS VAT_VALUE FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i JOIN RETURNS_LISTS l ON l.RETURN_ID = i.RETURN_ID GROUP BY CASE WHEN i.DESTINATION IS NOT NULL THEN i.DESTINATION ELSE l.STATUS END `; const topItemsSql = ` SELECT i.ITEM_NAME, COUNT(*) AS TIMES, SUM(i.QUANTITY) AS TOTAL_QTY, SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) AS NET_VALUE, SUM(CASE WHEN i.IS_VAT = 1 THEN NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY * :vatRate ELSE 0 END) AS VAT_VALUE FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i WHERE i.DESTINATION IN ('BIN','CHARITY') GROUP BY i.ITEM_NAME ORDER BY SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) DESC FETCH FIRST 10 ROWS ONLY `; const topSuppliersSql = ` SELECT l.SUPPLIER_NAME, COUNT(*) AS ITEM_COUNT, SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) AS NET_VALUE, SUM(CASE WHEN i.IS_VAT = 1 THEN NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY * :vatRate ELSE 0 END) AS VAT_VALUE FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i JOIN RETURNS_LISTS l ON l.RETURN_ID = i.RETURN_ID WHERE i.DESTINATION IN ('BIN','CHARITY') GROUP BY l.SUPPLIER_NAME ORDER BY SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) DESC FETCH FIRST 10 ROWS ONLY `; const trendSql = ` SELECT TO_CHAR(i.MOVED_AT, 'YYYY-MM') AS MONTH, i.DESTINATION, SUM(NVL(i.COST_PRICE,0) * i.QUANTITY) AS NET_VALUE FROM RETURNS_ITEMS i WHERE i.DESTINATION IN ('BIN','CHARITY') AND i.MOVED_AT >= ADD_MONTHS(TRUNC(SYSDATE,'MM'), -5) GROUP BY TO_CHAR(i.MOVED_AT, 'YYYY-MM'), i.DESTINATION ORDER BY 1 `; const binds = { vatRate: RETURNS_VAT_RATE }; const [buckets, topItems, topSuppliers, trend] = await Promise.all([ conn.execute(bucketSql, binds), conn.execute(topItemsSql, binds), conn.execute(topSuppliersSql, binds), // trendSql has no :vatRate placeholder (it doesn't do a VAT calc), // so it must be run with no binds — passing the shared `binds` // object here caused NJS-098 ("0 bind placeholders were used in // the SQL statement but 1 bind values were provided"), which is // what made the whole analytics endpoint 500 ("Could not load // analytics"). conn.execute(trendSql) ]); return { buckets: lower(buckets.rows), topItems: lower(topItems.rows), topSuppliers: lower(topSuppliers.rows), trend: lower(trend.rows) }; }); // Normalise into named buckets so the frontend never has to guess which // statuses/destinations exist — every key is always present, zeroed if // there's no data for it yet. const empty = () => ({ item_count: 0, total_qty: 0, net_value: 0, vat_value: 0, gross_value: 0 }); const named = { bin: empty(), charity: empty(), pending: empty(), closed_list: empty(), awaiting_credit: empty(), returned: empty(), closed: empty() }; const keyFor = b => ({ BIN: 'bin', CHARITY: 'charity', PENDING: 'pending', CLOSED_LIST: 'closed_list', AWAITING_CREDIT: 'awaiting_credit', RETURNED: 'returned', CLOSED: 'closed' }[b]); for (const row of data.buckets) { const key = keyFor(row.bucket); if (!key) continue; const net = Number(row.net_value) || 0; const vat = Number(row.vat_value) || 0; named[key] = { item_count: Number(row.item_count) || 0, total_qty: Number(row.total_qty) || 0, net_value: net, vat_value: vat, gross_value: net + vat }; } const wastage = { // Bin + Charity together = stock that never earned anything back net_value: named.bin.net_value + named.charity.net_value, vat_value: named.bin.vat_value + named.charity.vat_value, gross_value: named.bin.gross_value + named.charity.gross_value, item_count: named.bin.item_count + named.charity.item_count }; const backToSupplier = { net_value: named.pending.net_value + named.closed_list.net_value + named.awaiting_credit.net_value + named.returned.net_value + named.closed.net_value, vat_value: named.pending.vat_value + named.closed_list.vat_value + named.awaiting_credit.vat_value + named.returned.vat_value + named.closed.vat_value, gross_value: named.pending.gross_value + named.closed_list.gross_value + named.awaiting_credit.gross_value + named.returned.gross_value + named.closed.gross_value, item_count: named.pending.item_count + named.closed_list.item_count + named.awaiting_credit.item_count + named.returned.item_count + named.closed.item_count }; const topItems = data.topItems.map(r => { const net = Number(r.net_value) || 0, vat = Number(r.vat_value) || 0; return { item_name: r.item_name, times: Number(r.times) || 0, total_qty: Number(r.total_qty) || 0, net_value: net, vat_value: vat, gross_value: net + vat }; }); const topSuppliers = data.topSuppliers.map(r => { const net = Number(r.net_value) || 0, vat = Number(r.vat_value) || 0; return { supplier_name: r.supplier_name, item_count: Number(r.item_count) || 0, net_value: net, vat_value: vat, gross_value: net + vat }; }); // Pivot the trend rows into { month, bin_net, charity_net } per month. const trendMap = new Map(); for (const row of data.trend) { if (!trendMap.has(row.month)) trendMap.set(row.month, { month: row.month, bin_net: 0, charity_net: 0 }); const entry = trendMap.get(row.month); if (row.destination === 'BIN') entry.bin_net = Number(row.net_value) || 0; else if (row.destination === 'CHARITY') entry.charity_net = Number(row.net_value) || 0; } const trend = Array.from(trendMap.values()).sort((a, b) => a.month.localeCompare(b.month)); res.json({ buckets: named, wastage, back_to_supplier: backToSupplier, top_items: topItems, top_suppliers: topSuppliers, trend, vat_rate: RETURNS_VAT_RATE }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- RETURNS > STOCK ADJUSTED (VECTOR) Write-offs (negative stock adjustments) synced from Vector into STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS by test_oracle.py, starting from 23 Sept 2026. The sync only ever INSERTS new rows; DISMISSED is owned by this app, so a row that has been cleared (or turned into a supplier return) stays gone. DISMISSED_ACTION records how: 'DELETED' (cross + confirm) or 'ADDED_TO_RETURN' (set inside /api/returns/scan-add above). Paths deliberately start with /api/returns/ so the existing 'returns' module permission covers them. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Every write-off still waiting to be dealt with, newest first. The // time is formatted by Oracle (TO_CHAR) rather than sent as a Date so // it shows exactly as Vector recorded it, with no timezone shift. ---- app.get('/api/returns/stock-adjustments', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT STOCKADJUSTID, STOCKID, STOCK_NAME, ADJUST_QTY, TO_CHAR(ADJUST_DATETIME, 'DD Mon YYYY HH24:MI') AS ADJUST_DATETIME_TXT, REASON FROM STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS WHERE DISMISSED = 'N' ORDER BY ADJUST_DATETIME DESC FETCH FIRST 500 ROWS ONLY` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Cross + confirm on the screen: clears one write-off from the list. // Soft delete on purpose — the row stays (DISMISSED = 'Y') so the sync // knows it has already been seen and never re-adds it. ---- app.post('/api/returns/stock-adjustments/:id/dismiss', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'invalid id' }); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS SET DISMISSED = 'Y', DISMISSED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, DISMISSED_ACTION = 'DELETED' WHERE STOCKADJUSTID = :id AND DISMISSED = 'N'`, { id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- WAREHOUSE LIST > DAMAGE LOGS Staff-logged damage found in the warehouse — separate from STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS above (that's write-offs synced from Vector). A barcode/name-search lookup creates an entry with quantity, reason and photos; entries sit on the Damaged List until edited/deleted there, or picked up from Returns > Warehouse Damages, which turns one into a supplier return (or Charity/Bin) and deletes the entry (and its photos, via ON DELETE CASCADE) in the same transaction — see damageId handling inside /api/returns/scan-add above. --------------------------------------------------------- */ // ---- Scan a barcode for a Damage Log entry — just the product, no supplier // list needed (unlike /api/returns/scan-lookup) since this doesn't pick // a supplier until it's later turned into a return from Warehouse // Damages). ---- app.get('/api/warehouse/damage-scan-lookup/:code', async (req, res) => { const code = (req.params.code || '').trim(); if (!code) return res.json({ item: null }); try { const item = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `SELECT s.STOCKID, s.NAME, b.BARCODE FROM VEBARCODES b JOIN VESTOCK s ON s.STOCKID = b.STOCKID WHERE b.BARCODE = :code`, { code } ); const rows = lower(result.rows); return rows.length ? rows[0] : null; }); res.json({ item }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Every damage log entry, newest first, with a photo count for the // Damaged List's "Photos" column. Used both by the Warehouse List side // (Damaged List / hub count) and by Returns > Warehouse Damages, which // reads the exact same rows. ---- app.get('/api/warehouse/damage-logs', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT d.DAMAGE_ID, d.STOCKID, d.ITEM_NAME, d.BARCODE, d.QUANTITY, d.REASON, d.CREATED_BY, d.REDUCED_PRICE, d.CURRENT_PRICE, d.LOCATION, TO_CHAR(d.CREATED_AT, 'DD Mon YYYY HH24:MI') AS CREATED_AT_TXT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES wi WHERE wi.DAMAGE_ID = d.DAMAGE_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS d WHERE d.WRITEOFF_AT IS NULL ORDER BY d.CREATED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 1000 ROWS ONLY` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Returns > Warehouse Damages reads through this alias so its client // code lives alongside the rest of Returns (same reasoning as the // /api/returns/ prefix on /api/returns/stock-adjustments above) — same // underlying table and shape as /api/warehouse/damage-logs. // REDUCED_PRICE/CURRENT_PRICE: set via PUT .../reduced-price below when // staff choose "Reduced Price" instead of "Return to Supplier" — the // entry stays in this same list, just with the price it was marked // down to (and what it was reduced from) shown alongside it. ---- app.get('/api/returns/warehouse-damages', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT d.DAMAGE_ID, d.STOCKID, d.ITEM_NAME, d.BARCODE, d.QUANTITY, d.REASON, d.REDUCED_PRICE, d.CURRENT_PRICE, d.LOCATION, TO_CHAR(d.CREATED_AT, 'DD Mon YYYY HH24:MI') AS CREATED_AT_TXT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES wi WHERE wi.DAMAGE_ID = d.DAMAGE_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS d WHERE d.WRITEOFF_AT IS NULL ORDER BY d.CREATED_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 500 ROWS ONLY` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Returns > Write-off List. A damage entry is moved here from Warehouse // Damages ("Move to Write-off List" in the choice popup). It is the // same WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS row (same photos, quantity, reason and // location) with WRITEOFF_AT set — which hides it from Warehouse // Damages and the warehouse Damaged List and shows it here instead. // From this list it can be added to a supplier's return list through // the exact same POST /api/returns/scan-add { damage_id } path as // Warehouse Damages (that deletes the row, or keeps the remainder on // this list after a partial send), or cleared with the existing // DELETE /api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id. ---- app.put('/api/returns/warehouse-damages/:id/writeoff', async (req, res) => { const by = (req.portalUser && req.portalUser.name) ? String(req.portalUser.name).slice(0, 200) : null; try { const r = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS SET WRITEOFF_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP, WRITEOFF_BY = :written_by WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id AND WRITEOFF_AT IS NULL`, { written_by: { val: by, type: oracledb.STRING }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); if (!r.rowsAffected) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'This entry is not on the Warehouse Damages list (it may already have been moved)' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.get('/api/returns/writeoff-list', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT d.DAMAGE_ID, d.STOCKID, d.ITEM_NAME, d.BARCODE, d.QUANTITY, d.REASON, d.LOCATION, d.REDUCED_PRICE, d.CURRENT_PRICE, d.WRITEOFF_BY, TO_CHAR(d.CREATED_AT, 'DD Mon YYYY HH24:MI') AS CREATED_AT_TXT, TO_CHAR(d.WRITEOFF_AT, 'DD Mon YYYY HH24:MI') AS WRITEOFF_AT_TXT, (SELECT COUNT(*) FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES wi WHERE wi.DAMAGE_ID = d.DAMAGE_ID) AS IMAGE_COUNT FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS d WHERE d.WRITEOFF_AT IS NOT NULL ORDER BY d.WRITEOFF_AT DESC FETCH FIRST 500 ROWS ONLY` )); res.json({ items: lower(result.rows) }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Set (or clear) the reduced price on a Warehouse Damages entry — the // "Reduced Price" alternative to "Return to Supplier": rather than // moving to a supplier's return list, the entry just stays here with // REDUCED_PRICE/CURRENT_PRICE recorded so the list can show what it was // marked down to. CURRENT_PRICE is a snapshot of the retail price at // the moment it was reduced (client fetches it from // /api/stock/:stockid/retail-price), not a live lookup, so the "was // £X" note stays accurate even if the retail price changes later. ---- app.put('/api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id/reduced-price', async (req, res) => { const { reduced_price, current_price } = req.body || {}; if (reduced_price === undefined || reduced_price === null || reduced_price === '' || isNaN(Number(reduced_price))) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'reduced_price is required' }); } try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS SET REDUCED_PRICE = :reduced_price, CURRENT_PRICE = :current_price WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id`, { reduced_price: Number(reduced_price), current_price: { val: (current_price === undefined || current_price === null || current_price === '') ? null : Number(current_price), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Create a damage log entry — scanned or name-searched product plus // quantity and reason. Photos (if any) are uploaded separately right // after, via the images endpoint below, once this returns damage_id. ---- app.post('/api/warehouse/damage-logs', async (req, res) => { const { stockid, item_name, barcode, quantity, reason, created_by, location } = req.body || {}; if (!item_name) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'item_name is required' }); try { const damageId = await withConnection(async conn => { const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS (STOCKID, ITEM_NAME, BARCODE, QUANTITY, REASON, CREATED_BY, LOCATION) VALUES (:stockid, :item_name, :barcode, :quantity, :reason, :created_by, :location) RETURNING DAMAGE_ID INTO :out_id`, { stockid: { val: stockid === undefined || stockid === null ? null : Number(stockid), type: oracledb.NUMBER }, item_name: String(item_name).slice(0, 300), barcode: { val: barcode ? String(barcode).slice(0, 100) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, quantity: { val: Number(quantity) > 0 ? Number(quantity) : 1, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, reason: { val: reason ? String(reason).slice(0, 200) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, created_by: { val: created_by || null, type: oracledb.STRING }, location: { val: location ? String(location).slice(0, 300) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return result.outBinds.out_id[0]; }); res.json({ damage_id: damageId }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Edit quantity/reason from the Damaged List ---- app.put('/api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id', async (req, res) => { const { quantity, reason, location } = req.body || {}; const hasLocation = Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(req.body || {}, 'location'); try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `UPDATE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS SET QUANTITY = :quantity, REASON = :reason, LOCATION = CASE WHEN :has_location = 1 THEN :location ELSE LOCATION END WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id`, { quantity: { val: Number(quantity) > 0 ? Number(quantity) : 1, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, reason: { val: reason ? String(reason).slice(0, 200) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, has_location: { val: hasLocation ? 1 : 0, type: oracledb.NUMBER }, location: { val: location ? String(location).slice(0, 300) : null, type: oracledb.STRING }, id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Delete a damage log entry outright — from the ✕ on the Damaged List, // the ✕ on Returns > Warehouse Damages, or (with ON DELETE CASCADE // taking its images) as part of turning it into a return — see // damageId handling inside /api/returns/scan-add above. ---- app.delete('/api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.id }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); // ---- Photos on a damage log entry — same data-URL-in-a-CLOB pattern, and // the same client-side downscale/compress before upload, as // RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES above. ---- app.get('/api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id/images', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT IMAGE_ID, FILENAME, IMAGE_DATA, CREATED_AT FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id ORDER BY CREATED_AT`, { id: req.params.id } )); const rows = lower(result.rows); const images = await Promise.all(rows.map(async r => ({ image_id: r.image_id, filename: r.filename, created_at: r.created_at, data: await clobToString(r.image_data) }))); res.json({ images }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/warehouse/damage-logs/:id/images', async (req, res) => { const damageId = Number(req.params.id); const images = Array.isArray(req.body && req.body.images) ? req.body.images : []; if (!damageId) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'invalid damage log id' }); if (!images.length) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'No photos supplied' }); if (images.length > RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_PER_UPLOAD) { return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Up to ' + RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_PER_UPLOAD + ' photos at a time' }); } try { const created = await withConnection(async conn => { const check = await conn.execute(`SELECT 1 FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS WHERE DAMAGE_ID = :id`, { id: damageId }); if (!check.rows.length) throw Object.assign(new Error('Damage log entry not found'), { status: 404 }); const out = []; for (const img of images) { const dataUrl = img && img.data; if (typeof dataUrl !== 'string' || !RETURNS_IMAGE_DATA_URL_RE.test(dataUrl)) { throw Object.assign(new Error('One of the photos was not a valid image'), { status: 400 }); } const approxBytes = Math.ceil((dataUrl.length - dataUrl.indexOf(',') - 1) * 3 / 4); if (approxBytes > RETURNS_IMAGE_MAX_BYTES) { throw Object.assign(new Error('One of the photos is too large'), { status: 400 }); } const filename = img.filename ? String(img.filename).slice(0, 300) : null; const result = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES (DAMAGE_ID, IMAGE_DATA, FILENAME) VALUES (:damage_id, :image_data, :filename) RETURNING IMAGE_ID INTO :out_id`, { damage_id: damageId, image_data: { val: dataUrl, type: oracledb.CLOB }, filename, out_id: { dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT, type: oracledb.NUMBER } }, { autoCommit: true } ); out.push({ image_id: result.outBinds.out_id[0], filename }); } return out; }); res.json({ images: created }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(e.status || 500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/warehouse/damage-images/:imageId', async (req, res) => { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `DELETE FROM WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES WHERE IMAGE_ID = :id`, { id: req.params.imageId }, { autoCommit: true } )); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); /* --------------------------------------------------------- STARTUP --------------------------------------------------------- */ // Creates the two RETURNS tables if they're not there yet. Runs on every // boot: ORA-00955 ("name is already used") just means a previous boot // already made it, so that one error is swallowed and anything else is // logged loudly. This is why you don't have to run any SQL by hand — // deploy the server once and the tables appear. async function ensureReturnsTables() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ( RETURN_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, SUPPID NUMBER, SUPPLIER_NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, STATUS VARCHAR2(30) DEFAULT 'PENDING' NOT NULL, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, RETURNED_AT TIMESTAMP, CREDIT_NOTE_AT TIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ( ITEM_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, RETURN_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES RETURNS_LISTS(RETURN_ID), STOCKID NUMBER, ITEM_NAME VARCHAR2(300) NOT NULL, BARCODE VARCHAR2(100), COST_PRICE NUMBER, QUANTITY NUMBER DEFAULT 1, LOCATION VARCHAR2(300), RETURN_REASON VARCHAR2(200), ADDED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX RETURNS_LISTS_SUPP_IX ON RETURNS_LISTS (SUPPID, STATUS)`, `CREATE INDEX RETURNS_ITEMS_LIST_IX ON RETURNS_ITEMS (RETURN_ID)`, // Added after the first release — existing installs already have the // table, so this runs as an ALTER. ORA-01430 ("column already exists") // is swallowed below in the same way ORA-00955 is. `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ADD (RETURN_REASON VARCHAR2(200))`, // VAT support — see the RETURNS_ITEMS comment near the top of this // section for what IS_VAT means and how it's used. `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ADD (IS_VAT NUMBER(1) DEFAULT 0)`, // Bin/Charity support — DESTINATION/MOVED_AT are read and written all // over the RETURNS > STOCK ADJUSTED / ANALYTICS code below (moveReturnsItem // on the client, the analytics bucket/top-items/trend queries, the Charity // List / Bin List screens) but the columns were never added here, so every // query touching them failed with ORA-00904 ("invalid identifier") — // that's what "Could not load analytics" was. Adding them now. `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ADD (DESTINATION VARCHAR2(20))`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ADD (MOVED_AT TIMESTAMP)`, // Evidence photos for a return item (damaged goods, out-of-date labels, // etc.) — staff can attach these when the item is first added (Scan // Returns / Stock Adjusted share the same modal) or any time afterwards // from a return list / Charity List / Bin List, for anything missed at // the time. Stored as a data URL in a CLOB, same pattern as the other // in-browser image uploads in this file (WHATSAPP_FLYER_IMAGES etc.) — // this is staff phone photos, not the Vector-synced product shots in // STOCK_IMAGES, which is a different table for a different purpose. `CREATE TABLE RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES ( IMAGE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, ITEM_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES RETURNS_ITEMS(ITEM_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE, IMAGE_DATA CLOB NOT NULL, FILENAME VARCHAR2(300), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES_ITEM_IX ON RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES (ITEM_ID)`, // "Close List" + sign-off support. CLOSED_AT is set when a list moves // PENDING -> CLOSED_LIST. RETURNED_BY / ACCEPTED_BY / SIGNOFF_DATE / // SIGNATURE are filled in when "Items Returned" is tapped (signature is // a PNG data URL held in a CLOB, same pattern as the item photos). `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ADD (CLOSED_AT TIMESTAMP)`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ADD (RETURNED_BY VARCHAR2(200))`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ADD (ACCEPTED_BY VARCHAR2(200))`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ADD (SIGNOFF_DATE DATE)`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_LISTS ADD (SIGNATURE CLOB)`, // Bin List / Charity List "Close list": each close creates a // DISPOSAL_CLOSURES row and stamps the open items with its CLOSE_ID. // DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID IS NULL = still on the open list. `CREATE TABLE DISPOSAL_CLOSURES ( CLOSE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, DESTINATION VARCHAR2(20) NOT NULL, CLOSED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, CLOSED_BY VARCHAR2(200) )`, `ALTER TABLE RETURNS_ITEMS ADD (DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID NUMBER)`, `CREATE INDEX RETURNS_ITEMS_DCLOSE_IX ON RETURNS_ITEMS (DESTINATION, DISPOSAL_CLOSE_ID)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[returns] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (table/index made on an earlier boot) // 1430 = column already exists (ALTER already applied) if (e.errorNum !== 955 && e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[returns] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // Creates STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS (Vector write-offs, filled by test_oracle.py) if // it isn't there yet. Same pattern as ensureReturnsTables: ORA-00955 just // means an earlier boot (or the manual SQL script) already made it. // test_oracle.py reads MAX(ADJUST_DATETIME) from this table on every run, so // the table must exist before that script's next run — booting this server // once, or running create_stock_adjustments.sql, both do that. async function ensureStockAdjustmentsTable() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS ( STOCKADJUSTID NUMBER PRIMARY KEY, STOCKID NUMBER, STOCK_NAME VARCHAR2(300), ADJUST_QTY NUMBER, ADJUST_DATETIME TIMESTAMP, REASON VARCHAR2(200), DISMISSED CHAR(1) DEFAULT 'N' NOT NULL, DISMISSED_AT TIMESTAMP, DISMISSED_ACTION VARCHAR2(20), RETURN_ITEM_ID NUMBER, SYNCED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX STOCK_ADJ_DISMISSED_IX ON STOCK_ADJUSTMENTS (DISMISSED, ADJUST_DATETIME)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[stock-adjustments] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (table/index made on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[stock-adjustments] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // Creates the Warehouse List > Damage Logs tables if they're not there yet. // Same pattern as ensureReturnsTables: ORA-00955 ("name is already used") // just means an earlier boot already made it. async function ensureWarehouseDamageTables() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS ( DAMAGE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, STOCKID NUMBER, ITEM_NAME VARCHAR2(300) NOT NULL, BARCODE VARCHAR2(100), QUANTITY NUMBER DEFAULT 1, REASON VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS_AT_IX ON WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS (CREATED_AT)`, // Evidence photos for a damage log entry — same data-URL-in-a-CLOB // pattern as RETURNS_ITEM_IMAGES. ON DELETE CASCADE means turning an // entry into a return (or just deleting it from the Damaged List) never // leaves orphaned photo rows behind. `CREATE TABLE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES ( IMAGE_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, DAMAGE_ID NUMBER NOT NULL REFERENCES WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS(DAMAGE_ID) ON DELETE CASCADE, IMAGE_DATA CLOB NOT NULL, FILENAME VARCHAR2(300), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE INDEX WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES_DMG_IX ON WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_IMAGES (DAMAGE_ID)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[warehouse-damage] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (table/index made on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[warehouse-damage] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // Adds REDUCED_PRICE / CURRENT_PRICE to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS — used by // Returns > Warehouse Damages' "Reduced Price" option (as an alternative to // "Return to Supplier"): the entry stays on the Warehouse Damages list // rather than being turned into a return, just carrying the price it was // marked down to (and the price it was reduced from, for display). Same // ORA-01430 ("column already exists") pattern as ensureWhatsappInboundChannelColumn. async function ensureWarehouseDamagePriceColumns() { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `ALTER TABLE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS ADD (REDUCED_PRICE NUMBER, CURRENT_PRICE NUMBER)`, {}, { autoCommit: true } )); console.log('[warehouse-damage] added REDUCED_PRICE/CURRENT_PRICE columns to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS'); } catch (e) { // 1430 = column already exists (migration already applied on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[warehouse-damage] price column migration error:', e.message); } } // Adds LOCATION to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS — where the damaged stock is sitting, // entered (typed or scanned) right after the product is scanned. It carries // across onto the return item when a damage entry is added to a supplier // return. Same ORA-01430 ("column already exists") pattern as above. async function ensureWarehouseDamageLocationColumn() { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `ALTER TABLE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS ADD (LOCATION VARCHAR2(300))`, {}, { autoCommit: true } )); console.log('[warehouse-damage] added LOCATION column to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS'); } catch (e) { // 1430 = column already exists (migration already applied on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[warehouse-damage] location column migration error:', e.message); } } // Adds WRITEOFF_AT / WRITEOFF_BY to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS — set when a damage // entry is moved to Returns > Write-off List (NULL = still on Warehouse // Damages). Same ORA-01430 ("column already exists") pattern as above. async function ensureWarehouseDamageWriteoffColumns() { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `ALTER TABLE WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS ADD (WRITEOFF_AT TIMESTAMP, WRITEOFF_BY VARCHAR2(200))`, {}, { autoCommit: true } )); console.log('[warehouse-damage] added WRITEOFF_AT/WRITEOFF_BY columns to WAREHOUSE_DAMAGE_LOGS'); } catch (e) { // 1430 = column already exists (migration already applied on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 1430) console.error('[warehouse-damage] write-off column migration error:', e.message); } } // Adds the CHANNEL column to WHATSAPP_INBOUND (which number — 'api' or // 'coex' — a reply came in on) if it isn't there yet. Runs on every boot, // same pattern as ensureReturnsTables above: ORA-01430 ("column already // exists") just means a previous boot already applied it, so that one error // is swallowed and anything else is logged loudly. Nothing to run by hand. async function ensureWhatsappInboundChannelColumn() { try { await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `ALTER TABLE WHATSAPP_INBOUND ADD (CHANNEL VARCHAR2(20) DEFAULT 'api' NOT NULL)`, {}, { autoCommit: true } )); console.log('[whatsapp] added CHANNEL column to WHATSAPP_INBOUND'); } catch (e) { // 1430 = column already exists (migration already applied on an earlier boot) // 942 = table doesn't exist yet (WHATSAPP_INBOUND is created manually — // see the comment above its CREATE TABLE further up this file; // nothing to do here until that table exists) if (e.errorNum !== 1430 && e.errorNum !== 942) console.error('[whatsapp] CHANNEL column migration error:', e.message); } } /* --------------------------------------------------------- LABEL PRINTER (home tile "Label Printer") Custom labels: a size in mm plus any number of text and barcode elements, each with its own position and size. Every saved label is stored as one row; the element list is kept as JSON in CONFIG so the label can be re-opened and edited exactly as it was saved. Table (created automatically on boot by ensureLabelTemplatesTable): LABEL_TEMPLATES (LABEL_ID, NAME, NAME_KEY, WIDTH_MM, HEIGHT_MM, CONFIG CLOB, CREATED_BY/AT, UPDATED_BY/AT) NAME_KEY is the lower-cased name with a unique index, so two labels cannot share a name (ignoring upper/lower case). Endpoints (all gated by the 'labels' portal module): GET /api/labels -> { labels: [...] } newest-edited first POST /api/labels -> create { name, width_mm, height_mm, config } PUT /api/labels/:id -> overwrite an existing label (rename too) DELETE /api/labels/:id -> delete --------------------------------------------------------- */ async function ensureLabelTemplatesTable() { const statements = [ `CREATE TABLE LABEL_TEMPLATES ( LABEL_ID NUMBER GENERATED ALWAYS AS IDENTITY PRIMARY KEY, NAME VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, NAME_KEY VARCHAR2(200) NOT NULL, WIDTH_MM NUMBER(7,2) NOT NULL, HEIGHT_MM NUMBER(7,2) NOT NULL, CONFIG CLOB NOT NULL, CREATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), CREATED_AT TIMESTAMP DEFAULT SYSTIMESTAMP, UPDATED_BY VARCHAR2(200), UPDATED_AT TIMESTAMP )`, `CREATE UNIQUE INDEX LABEL_TEMPLATES_NAME_UX ON LABEL_TEMPLATES (NAME_KEY)` ]; await withConnection(async conn => { for (const sql of statements) { try { await conn.execute(sql, {}, { autoCommit: true }); console.log('[labels] applied:', sql.trim().split(/\s+/).slice(0, 3).join(' ')); } catch (e) { // 955 = object already exists (made on an earlier boot) if (e.errorNum !== 955) console.error('[labels] schema error:', e.message); } } }); } // Validates a create/edit body. Returns { error } or the cleaned values. function parseLabelBody(body) { body = body || {}; const name = String(body.name || '').trim().slice(0, 200); if (!name) return { error: 'Enter a name for the label' }; const w = Number(body.width_mm); const h = Number(body.height_mm); if (!Number.isFinite(w) || !Number.isFinite(h) || w < 5 || h < 5 || w > 500 || h > 500) { return { error: 'Label width and height must be between 5 and 500 mm' }; } const cfg = body.config; if (!cfg || !Array.isArray(cfg.elements)) return { error: 'The label design is missing' }; if (cfg.elements.length > 60) return { error: 'A label can have at most 60 items' }; const json = JSON.stringify({ elements: cfg.elements }); if (json.length > 200000) return { error: 'The label design is too large' }; return { name, nameKey: name.toLowerCase(), width: Math.round(w * 100) / 100, height: Math.round(h * 100) / 100, json }; } app.get('/api/labels', async (req, res) => { try { const result = await withConnection(conn => conn.execute( `SELECT LABEL_ID, NAME, WIDTH_MM, HEIGHT_MM, CONFIG, CREATED_BY, CREATED_AT, UPDATED_BY, UPDATED_AT FROM LABEL_TEMPLATES ORDER BY NVL(UPDATED_AT, CREATED_AT) DESC, LABEL_ID DESC`, {}, { fetchInfo: { CONFIG: { type: oracledb.STRING } } } )); const labels = lower(result.rows).map(r => { let elements = []; try { elements = (JSON.parse(r.config || '{}').elements) || []; } catch (_) { elements = []; } return { label_id: r.label_id, name: r.name, width_mm: Number(r.width_mm), height_mm: Number(r.height_mm), elements, created_by: r.created_by, updated_by: r.updated_by || r.created_by, updated_at: r.updated_at || r.created_at }; }); res.json({ labels }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.post('/api/labels', async (req, res) => { const p = parseLabelBody(req.body); if (p.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: p.error }); try { const newId = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute( `INSERT INTO LABEL_TEMPLATES (NAME, NAME_KEY, WIDTH_MM, HEIGHT_MM, CONFIG, CREATED_BY) VALUES (:lbl_name, :lbl_key, :lbl_w, :lbl_h, :lbl_config, :lbl_user) RETURNING LABEL_ID INTO :lbl_new_id`, { lbl_name: { val: p.name, type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_key: { val: p.nameKey, type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_w: p.width, lbl_h: p.height, lbl_config: { val: p.json, type: oracledb.CLOB }, lbl_user: { val: cnUserName(req), type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_new_id: { type: oracledb.NUMBER, dir: oracledb.BIND_OUT } }, { autoCommit: true } ); return r.outBinds.lbl_new_id[0]; }); res.json({ ok: true, label_id: newId }); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum === 1) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'A label with that name already exists — choose a different name.' }); console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.put('/api/labels/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!Number.isInteger(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad label id' }); const p = parseLabelBody(req.body); if (p.error) return res.status(400).json({ error: p.error }); try { const rows = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute( `UPDATE LABEL_TEMPLATES SET NAME = :lbl_name, NAME_KEY = :lbl_key, WIDTH_MM = :lbl_w, HEIGHT_MM = :lbl_h, CONFIG = :lbl_config, UPDATED_BY = :lbl_user, UPDATED_AT = SYSTIMESTAMP WHERE LABEL_ID = :lbl_id`, { lbl_name: { val: p.name, type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_key: { val: p.nameKey, type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_w: p.width, lbl_h: p.height, lbl_config: { val: p.json, type: oracledb.CLOB }, lbl_user: { val: cnUserName(req), type: oracledb.STRING }, lbl_id: id }, { autoCommit: true } ); return r.rowsAffected; }); if (!rows) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'That label no longer exists.' }); res.json({ ok: true, label_id: id }); } catch (e) { if (e.errorNum === 1) return res.status(409).json({ error: 'A label with that name already exists — choose a different name.' }); console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); app.delete('/api/labels/:id', async (req, res) => { const id = Number(req.params.id); if (!Number.isInteger(id)) return res.status(400).json({ error: 'Bad label id' }); try { const rows = await withConnection(async conn => { const r = await conn.execute(`DELETE FROM LABEL_TEMPLATES WHERE LABEL_ID = :lbl_id`, { lbl_id: id }, { autoCommit: true }); return r.rowsAffected; }); if (!rows) return res.status(404).json({ error: 'That label no longer exists.' }); res.json({ ok: true }); } catch (e) { console.error(e); res.status(500).json({ error: e.message }); } }); initPool() .then(async () => { await ensureReturnsTables(); await ensureSupplierCreditNoteTables(); await ensureLabelTemplatesTable(); await ensureStockAdjustmentsTable(); await ensureOnlineDataChecksTable(); await ensureShopifySyncTable(); await ensureShopifyContentTable(); await ensureWooTables(); await ensureInvoiceSupplierChecksTable(); await ensureNewItemListsTable(); await ensureWarehouseDamageTables(); await ensureWarehouseDamagePriceColumns(); await ensureWarehouseDamageLocationColumn(); await ensureWarehouseDamageWriteoffColumns(); await ensureWhatsappInboundChannelColumn(); await ensureStaffHolidayTables(); await ensureWholesaleWhatsappScheduleTable(); await ensureWholesaleFlyerWebpageScheduleTable(); // Closes off any WhatsApp schedule left mid-send by a restart/redeploy — // see recoverStaleWholesaleWhatsappRuns. Best-effort: a hiccup here must // never stop the server from starting. try { await recoverStaleWholesaleWhatsappRuns(); } catch (e) { console.error('[wholesale-wa-schedule] stale-run recovery failed:', e.message); } try { await recoverStaleWholesaleFlyerWebpageRuns(); } catch (e) { console.error('[wholesale-flyer-web-schedule] stale-run recovery failed:', e.message); } app.listen(PORT, () => console.log('Hallan picking backend listening on port ' + PORT)); // Wholesale Pricing schedule runner — only starts once the pool exists, // so it never fires against an undefined pool during startup. setInterval(pollWholesaleSchedules, WHOLESALE_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS); // Wholesale WhatsApp flyer schedule runner — same reasoning as above. setInterval(pollWholesaleWhatsappSchedules, WHOLESALE_WA_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS); // Wholesale flyer webpage publish schedule runner — same reasoning as above. setInterval(pollWholesaleFlyerWebpageSchedules, WHOLESALE_FLYER_WEB_SCHEDULE_POLL_MS); // Shopify continuous stock sync (EPOS quantity -> Shopify). Needs SHOPIFY_SYNC_STOCK=1 + SHOPIFY_LOCATION_ID. startShopifyStockSync(); }) .catch(err => { console.error('Failed to create Oracle pool:', err); process.exit(1); });
